import type {Icon} from '@primer/octicons-react'
import {Label} from '@primer/react'

import styles from './CommandPill.module.css'

type CommandPillProps = React.ComponentProps<'button'> & {
  children: string
  icon: Icon
  isNew?: boolean
}

export const CommandPill = ({children, icon: Icon, isNew = false, ...buttonProps}: CommandPillProps) => {
  return (
    <button className={styles.button} {...buttonProps}>
      <Icon />
      <span className={styles.text}>{children}</span>

      {isNew && (
        <Label size="small" variant="accent">
          New
        </Label>
      )}
    </button>
  )
}

import type {Icon} from '@primer/octicons-react'
import {Label} from '@primer/react'

import styles from './CommandPill.module.css'

type CommandPillProps = React.ComponentProps<'button'> & {
  children: string
  icon: Icon
  isNew?: boolean
}

export const CommandPill = ({children, icon: Icon, isNew = false, ...buttonProps}: CommandPillProps) => {
  return (
    <button className={styles.button} {...buttonProps}>
      <Icon />
      <span className={styles.text}>{children}</span>

      {isNew && (
        <Label size="small" variant="accent">
          New
        </Label>
      )}
    </button>
  )
}

import {type AvailableCommand, useAvailableCommands} from '@github-ui/copilot-chat/hooks/use-available-commands'
import {ModeSupportName, type StarterEntry} from '@github-ui/copilot-chat/utils/copilot-chat-types'

import {CommandPill} from './CommandPill'
import styles from './CommandStarters.module.css'
import {getStarterGroups} from './starter-groups'
import {StarterGroupWithMenu} from './StarterGroupWithMenu'

type CommandsProps = {
  onSelect: (command: AvailableCommand) => void
  onIceBreakerSelect: (command: StarterEntry) => void
  iceBreakersExperimentEnabled?: boolean
  paidUser?: boolean
  activeModeName?: string
}

export const CommandStarters = ({
  onSelect,
  onIceBreakerSelect,
  iceBreakersExperimentEnabled,
  paidUser,
  activeModeName,
}: CommandsProps) => {
  const {availableCommands} = useAvailableCommands({enabled: true})

  // Legacy starters to show in the icebreakers experiment
  // show only 'git' and 'pull request' for paid users, and also 'write code' for non-paid users
  const legacyStarters = getStarterGroups(true, activeModeName === ModeSupportName.Task).filter(group => {
    if (group.id === 'git-group' || group.id === 'pull-requests-group') return true
    if (!paidUser && group.id === 'write-code-group') return true
    if (activeModeName === ModeSupportName.Task) return true
    return false
  })

  // Filter commands based on feature flags and user plan
  const filteredCommands = iceBreakersExperimentEnabled
    ? availableCommands.filter(command => {
        if (activeModeName === ModeSupportName.Task) return false
        if (command.key === 'ask') return false
        if (!paidUser && command.key === 'spark') return false
        return true
      })
    : availableCommands

  if (filteredCommands.length === 0 && legacyStarters.length === 0) {
    return null
  }

  return (
    <div className={styles.container}>
      <ul className={styles.list}>
        {filteredCommands.map(command => (
          <li key={command.key}>
            <CommandPill icon={command.icon} onClick={() => onSelect(command)} isNew={command.isNew}>
              {command.name}
            </CommandPill>
          </li>
        ))}
        {/* When the experiment FF is enabled, we want to render the legacy icebreakers */}
        {iceBreakersExperimentEnabled &&
          legacyStarters.map(group => (
            <StarterGroupWithMenu key={group.id} group={group} onEntrySelect={onIceBreakerSelect} />
          ))}
      </ul>
    </div>
  )
}

import type {ChatInputRef} from '@github-ui/copilot-chat/components/ChatInput'
import {type AvailableCommand, useAvailableCommands} from '@github-ui/copilot-chat/hooks/use-available-commands'
import {extractChatCommandType} from '@github-ui/copilot-chat/utils/chat-commands-helpers'
import type {CopilotChatCommandType, StarterEntry} from '@github-ui/copilot-chat/utils/copilot-chat-types'
import {sendEvent} from '@github-ui/hydro-analytics'
import {useNavigate} from '@github-ui/use-navigate'

import {CommandStarters} from './CommandStarters'
import {CommandSuggestions} from './CommandSuggestions'

interface CommandStartersAndSuggestionsProps {
  chatCommand: CopilotChatCommandType | null
  chatInputRef: React.RefObject<ChatInputRef | null>
  textAreaRef: React.RefObject<HTMLTextAreaElement | null>
  containerRef: React.RefObject<HTMLElement | null>
  iceBreakersExperimentEnabled?: boolean
  paidUser?: boolean
  activeModeName?: string
}

export function CommandStartersAndSuggestions({
  chatCommand,
  chatInputRef,
  containerRef,
  textAreaRef,
  iceBreakersExperimentEnabled,
  paidUser,
  activeModeName,
}: CommandStartersAndSuggestionsProps) {
  const {availableCommands, availableCommandKeys} = useAvailableCommands({enabled: true})
  const navigate = useNavigate()

  const handleCommandSelect = (command: AvailableCommand) => {
    sendEvent('dotcom_chat.activate', {target: 'COMMAND_STARTER_SELECTED', mode: 'immersive', command: command.key})
    if (command.enabled?.() === false && command.disabledAction) {
      command.disabledAction(navigate)
      return
    }
    chatInputRef.current?.setText(text => {
      // Replace the existing command with the new one
      const foundCommand = extractChatCommandType(text, availableCommandKeys)

      if (foundCommand) {
        return text.replace(`/${foundCommand}`, `/${command.key}`)
      }

      return `/${command.key} ${text}`
    })

    textAreaRef.current?.focus()
  }

  const handleSuggestionSelect = (command: CopilotChatCommandType, suggestion: string) => {
    sendEvent('dotcom_chat.activate', {
      target: 'COMMAND_SUGGESTION_SELECTED',
      mode: 'immersive',
      command,
      suggestion,
      iceBreakerFFEnabled: iceBreakersExperimentEnabled,
    })
    chatInputRef.current?.setText(`/${command} ${suggestion}`.trim())
    textAreaRef.current?.focus()
  }

  const handleIceBreakerSelect = (command: StarterEntry) => {
    sendEvent('dotcom_chat.activate', {
      target: 'COMMAND_SUGGESTION_SELECTED',
      mode: 'immersive',
      command: command.id,
      suggestion: command.message,
      iceBreakerFFEnabled: iceBreakersExperimentEnabled,
    })
    chatInputRef.current?.setText(`${command.message}`.trim())
    textAreaRef.current?.focus()
  }

  const suggestions = chatCommand ? availableCommands.find(command => command.key === chatCommand)?.suggestions : null
  const hasSuggestions = suggestions && suggestions.length > 0

  return (
    <>
      {/* only show suggestions if they exist, otherwise default to command starters */}
      {chatCommand && hasSuggestions && (
        <CommandSuggestions
          key={chatCommand}
          chatCommand={chatCommand}
          suggestions={suggestions}
          onSelect={handleSuggestionSelect}
          anchorRef={containerRef}
          textAreaRef={textAreaRef}
        />
      )}
      <>
        <h2 className="sr-only">Chat Commands</h2>
        <CommandStarters
          onSelect={handleCommandSelect}
          onIceBreakerSelect={handleIceBreakerSelect}
          iceBreakersExperimentEnabled={iceBreakersExperimentEnabled}
          paidUser={paidUser}
          activeModeName={activeModeName}
        />
      </>
    </>
  )
}

import {iconForReference} from '@github-ui/copilot-chat/components/ReferenceToken'
import {referenceID, referenceName} from '@github-ui/copilot-chat/utils/copilot-chat-helpers'
import {
  type CopilotChatReference,
  type DraftIssueProjectReference,
  type DraftIssueReference,
  type ExistingIssueReference,
  type FileDiffReference,
  type FileReference,
  type IssueReference,
  type JobReference,
  NullMessageId,
  type ThreadScopedFileReference,
} from '@github-ui/copilot-chat/utils/copilot-chat-types'
import {
  deserializeDraftIssueProjects,
  serializeDraftIssueProjects,
} from '@github-ui/copilot-chat/utils/draft-issue-serialization'
import type {RefObject} from 'react'
import type React from 'react'

export interface ContentPreviewContext {
  /** All items by ID, regardless of visibility. */
  items: ItemsMap
  /**
   * Given a base ID, get all the versions associated with it. For unversioned items this will be a single-element
   * array. Versions are sorted by timestamp.
   */
  versionedItems: VersionedItemsMap
  /**
   * Visible item IDs, in order. If the pane is closed, this will be an empty array.
   * Do not call this from a render method, as the component will not get updated when the items change.
   */
  getOpenItems: () => readonly PreviewableContentIdentifier[]
  /**
   * Open items, in order, even if the content preview pane is closed.
   */
  allOpenItems: readonly PreviewableContentIdentifier[]
  /** Item IDs that were visible before switching subthreads. Used to restore open files after new subthread loads */
  openItemsBeforeSubthreadChange: readonly PreviewableContentIdentifier[]
  /** Currently selected item. */
  selectedItem: PreviewableContentIdentifier | undefined
  /** Whether the loading UI is visible */
  showLoadingState: boolean
  /** Reference to the content preview container element */
  contentPreviewRef: RefObject<HTMLDivElement | null>
  /** Reference to the element that triggered opening the preview pane, used for returning focus */
  returnFocusElementRef: RefObject<HTMLElement | null>

  /** Add or update an item. */
  updateItem: (item: PreviewableContent) => void
  /** Set the items to restore after a subthread change. */
  setOpenItemsBeforeSubthreadChange: (items: readonly PreviewableContentIdentifier[]) => void
  /** Open the item, selecting it if specified. Does **not** open the pane if the pane is closed. */
  openItem: (id: PreviewableContentIdentifier, selectItem?: boolean) => void
  /** Close (hide) the item. */
  closeItem: (id: PreviewableContentIdentifier) => void
  /** Close (hide) the items with the given IDs, or all if undefined  */
  closeItems: (ids?: PreviewableContentIdentifier[]) => void
  /** Close the entire preview pane. Preserves open items for when reopened. */
  closePreviewPane: () => void
  /** Open the preview pane and restore any previously open items. Optionally specify an element to return focus to when closed. */
  openPreviewPane: (returnFocusElement?: HTMLElement | null) => void
  /** Close the resource panel. */
  closeResourcePanel: () => void
  /** Open the resource panel.  */
  openResourcePanel: () => void
  /** Removes the items and openItems with the given IDs, or all if undefined  */
  removeItems: (ids?: PreviewableContentIdentifier[]) => void
  /** Shows the loading UI */
  enableLoadingState: () => void
  /** Hides the loading UI */
  disableLoadingState: () => void
  /** Callback to open the Create PR dialog, if available. */
  createPrDialogOpener?: (() => void) | null
  /** Register/unregister the Create PR dialog opener callback. */
  setCreatePrDialogOpener?: (opener: (() => void) | null) => void
}

export type PreviewableContentTypes =
  | 'file'
  | 'issue'
  | 'new-issue'
  | 'existing-issue'
  | 'update-issue'
  | 'image'
  | 'file-reference'
  | 'file-diff'
  | 'task-diff'
  | 'plugin-content'
  | 'job-result'

export type PreviewableContentIdentifier = `${PreviewableContentTypes}:${string}`

export type VersionedPreviewableContentIdentifier = `${PreviewableContentIdentifier}#${string}`

export interface PreviewableContentBase {
  messageId: string
  name: string
  id: PreviewableContentIdentifier
  type: PreviewableContentTypes
  disableCloseButton?: boolean
  trailingComponent?: React.ReactNode
}

export interface VersionedPreviewableContentBase extends PreviewableContentBase {
  id: VersionedPreviewableContentIdentifier
  /** Indicates whether the user has edited this unsaved/draft content */
  isUserEdited: boolean
  /** If present, indicates this item is a duplicate of another item with this ID (used to prevent version bumping) */
  duplicateOf?: PreviewableContentIdentifier
}

export interface File extends VersionedPreviewableContentBase {
  id: `file:${string}#${number}.${number}` | `file:${string}#${number}`
  language: string
  value: string
  type: 'file'
  name: string
  isStreaming: boolean
  timestamp: Date
  /** if this file is from a reference (i.e. not generated by Copilot) this will be set */
  reference?: FileReference | ThreadScopedFileReference
}

export interface FileDiff extends VersionedPreviewableContentBase {
  type: 'file-diff'
  id: `file-diff:${string}#${number}`
  baseFile: File
  headFile: File
  name: string
  language: string
  isStreaming: boolean
  timestamp: Date
  reference: FileDiffReference
}

export interface Issue extends PreviewableContentBase {
  number: number
  owner: string
  repo: string
  href: string
  type: 'issue'
  id: `issue:${string}`
  state?: 'open' | 'closed'
}

// A specific view of an existing issue, with additional
// properties to support use in drafting issue trees.
// The counterpart to a `DraftIssue` below.
export interface ExistingIssue extends PreviewableContentBase {
  type: 'existing-issue'
  id: `${ExistingIssue['type']}:${string}`
  tag: string
  parentTag?: string
  repository: string
  number: number
  state: 'open' | 'closed'
  href: string
  blockedByTags?: string[]
  isBlockingTags?: string[]
}

export interface DraftIssue extends VersionedPreviewableContentBase {
  /** The issue number/ID is not known until the issue is created, so we ask Copilot to create an identifier for us called a tag */
  tag: string
  parentTag?: string
  repository?: string
  type: 'new-issue' | 'update-issue'
  id: `${DraftIssue['type']}:${string}#${number}`
  template?: string
  body?: string
  bodyVersion?: string
  assignees?: string[]
  labels?: string[]
  issueType?: string
  projects?: DraftIssueProjectReference[]
  rawProjectsInput?: string[] // The raw, unfiltered list of projects from payload. TODO: https://github.com/github/copilot-voyager/issues/786
  milestone?: string
  isStreaming?: boolean
  defaultValuesById?: Record<string, string>
  /** If this field is present, the draft issue represents an update to an existing issue */
  issueNumber?: number
  blockedByTags?: string[]
  isBlockingTags?: string[]
}

export type DraftOrExistingIssue = DraftIssue | ExistingIssue

export interface Image extends PreviewableContentBase {
  altText: string
  type: 'image'
  url: string
  id: `image:${string}`
}

export interface PluginContent extends PreviewableContentBase {
  type: 'plugin-content'
  reference: CopilotChatReference
  icon?: React.ComponentType
}

export interface JobResult extends PreviewableContentBase {
  type: 'job-result'
  id: `job-result:${string}`
  jobId: number
  workflowId?: number
  repoOwner: string
  repoName: string
}

export type VersionedPreviewableContent = File | DraftIssue | FileDiff

export type PreviewableContent = Issue | ExistingIssue | Image | VersionedPreviewableContent | PluginContent | JobResult

/**
 * A map of unversioned identifiers to a list of versioned identifiers.
 */
export type VersionedItemsMap = ReadonlyMap<PreviewableContentIdentifier, PreviewableContentIdentifier[]>

/**
 * A map of identifiers to the content they identify.
 */
export type ItemsMap = ReadonlyMap<PreviewableContentIdentifier, PreviewableContent>

export function stripVersionFromId<T extends PreviewableContentIdentifier>(id: T): T {
  return id.split('#')[0] as T
}

export function getRevisionNumber(
  id: PreviewableContentIdentifier,
  versionedItems: VersionedItemsMap,
  items: ItemsMap,
): number | null {
  // If this item is marked as a duplicate, resolve to the original item's version
  const item = items.get(id)
  if (item && 'duplicateOf' in item && item.duplicateOf) {
    return getRevisionNumber(item.duplicateOf, versionedItems, items)
  }

  const baseId = stripVersionFromId(id)
  const versions = versionedItems.get(baseId) ?? []
  const versionIndex = versions.indexOf(id)
  return versions.length > 1 && versionIndex !== -1 ? versionIndex + 1 : null
}

export function makeReferenceFromFile(item: File): ThreadScopedFileReference {
  return {
    type: 'thread-scoped-file',
    name: item.name,
    language: item.language,
    text: item.value,
  }
}

export function getItemIdFromThreadScopedFileReference(
  ref: ThreadScopedFileReference,
  messageIndex: number,
): File['id'] {
  return `file:${ref.name}#${messageIndex}` // TODO: this should be messageId once we have a stable value for that
}

export function getItemVersionForReference(
  ref: CopilotChatReference,
  messageIndex: number,
  versionedItems: VersionedItemsMap,
  items: ItemsMap,
): number | undefined {
  switch (ref.type) {
    case 'thread-scoped-file':
      return (
        getRevisionNumber(getItemIdFromThreadScopedFileReference(ref, messageIndex), versionedItems, items) ?? undefined
      )
    case 'draft-issue':
    case 'draft-update-issue':
      return (
        getRevisionNumber(makeVersionedItemFromReference({ref, messageIndex}).id, versionedItems, items) ?? undefined
      )
  }
}

export function makeItemFromThreadScopedFileReference(
  ref: ThreadScopedFileReference,
  messageIndex: number,
  messageId = NullMessageId,
  timestamp?: string,
): File {
  return {
    type: 'file',
    id: getItemIdFromThreadScopedFileReference(ref, messageIndex),
    reference: ref,
    messageId,
    name: ref.name,
    language: ref.language,
    value: ref.text,
    timestamp: timestamp ? new Date(timestamp) : new Date(),
    isStreaming: false,
    // If no message ID is provided, it's because the file has been edited and the message does not yet exist
    isUserEdited: messageId === NullMessageId,
  }
}

export function makeItemFromFileReference(ref: FileReference, messageId?: string, timestamp?: string): File {
  return {
    type: 'file',
    id: `file:${referenceID(ref)}#0`,
    reference: ref,
    messageId: messageId ?? NullMessageId,
    name: ref.path,
    language: ref.languageName ?? '',
    value: '',
    isStreaming: true,
    isUserEdited: false,
    timestamp: timestamp ? new Date(timestamp) : new Date(),
  }
}

export function makeItemFromFileDiffReference(
  ref: FileDiffReference,
  messageId?: string,
  timestamp?: string,
): FileDiff {
  // Create a default empty file for new/deleted files
  const createEmptyFile = (path: string): File => ({
    type: 'file',
    id: `file:empty:${path}#${Date.now()}`,
    messageId: messageId ?? NullMessageId,
    name: path,
    language: '',
    value: '',
    isStreaming: false,
    isUserEdited: false,
    timestamp: timestamp ? new Date(timestamp) : new Date(),
  })

  const baseFile = ref.baseFile ? makeItemFromFileReference(ref.baseFile) : createEmptyFile(ref.headFile?.path || '')
  const headFile = ref.headFile ? makeItemFromFileReference(ref.headFile) : createEmptyFile(ref.baseFile?.path || '')

  // Use the file that exists for metadata
  const existingFile = ref.headFile || ref.baseFile
  const filePath = existingFile?.path || ''

  return {
    type: 'file-diff',
    id: `file-diff:${referenceID(ref)}#0`,
    baseFile,
    headFile,
    name: filePath,
    language: (ref.headFile ? headFile : baseFile).language,
    isStreaming: true,
    isUserEdited: false,
    timestamp: timestamp ? new Date(timestamp) : new Date(),
    messageId: messageId ?? NullMessageId,
    reference: ref,
  }
}

export function makeItemFromIssueReference(ref: IssueReference, messageId?: string): Issue {
  return {
    type: 'issue',
    id: `issue:${ref.number}`,
    messageId: messageId ?? NullMessageId,
    name: ref.title ?? '',
    number: ref.number,
    owner: ref.repository.owner,
    repo: ref.repository.name,
    href: ref.url,
  }
}

export function makeItemFromJobReference(ref: JobReference, messageId?: string): JobResult {
  const {repoOwner, repoName, id} = ref
  return {
    type: 'job-result',
    id: `job-result:${repoOwner}-${repoName}-${id}`,
    messageId: messageId ?? NullMessageId,
    name: `Job #${id} in ${repoOwner}/${repoName}`,
    jobId: Number(id),
    workflowId: ref.workflow?.id,
    repoOwner,
    repoName,
  }
}

function makeReferenceFromDraftIssue(item: DraftIssue): DraftIssueReference {
  return {
    type: item.type === 'update-issue' ? 'draft-update-issue' : 'draft-issue',
    tag: item.tag,
    parentTag: item.parentTag,
    title: item.name,
    description: item.body,
    bodyVersion: item.bodyVersion,
    repository: item.repository,
    template: item.template,
    assignees: item.assignees,
    labels: item.labels,
    issueType: item.issueType,
    projects: serializeDraftIssueProjects(item.projects),
    rawProjectsInput: item.rawProjectsInput,
    milestone: item.milestone,
    templateFormValuesById: item.defaultValuesById,
    issueNumber: item.issueNumber,
    blockedByTags: item.blockedByTags,
    isBlockingTags: item.isBlockingTags,
  }
}

function makeItemFromDraftIssueReference({
  ref,
  messageId = NullMessageId,
  messageIndex,
}: {
  ref: DraftIssueReference
  messageIndex: number
  messageId?: string
}): DraftIssue {
  const type = ref.issueNumber ? 'update-issue' : 'new-issue'
  return {
    type,
    id: `${type}:${ref.tag}#${messageIndex}`,
    tag: ref.tag,
    parentTag: ref.parentTag,
    repository: ref.repository,
    name: ref.title,
    body: ref.description,
    bodyVersion: ref.bodyVersion,
    assignees: ref.assignees,
    labels: ref.labels,
    issueType: ref.issueType,
    projects: deserializeDraftIssueProjects(ref.projects),
    rawProjectsInput: ref.rawProjectsInput,
    milestone: ref.milestone,
    isUserEdited: messageId === NullMessageId, // If no message ID is provided, it's because the file has been edited and the message does not yet exist
    messageId,
    isStreaming: false,
    template: ref.template,
    defaultValuesById: ref.templateFormValuesById ?? {},
    issueNumber: ref.issueNumber,
    blockedByTags: ref.blockedByTags ?? [],
    isBlockingTags: ref.isBlockingTags ?? [],
  }
}

export function makeItemForPluginSupportedReference(
  reference: CopilotChatReference,
  messageId = NullMessageId,
): PluginContent {
  return {
    type: 'plugin-content',
    id: `plugin-content:${reference.type}:${referenceID(reference)}`,
    name: referenceName(reference),
    icon: iconForReference(reference),
    messageId,
    reference,
  }
}

export function makeReferenceFromExistingIssue(item: ExistingIssue): ExistingIssueReference {
  return {
    type: item.type,
    tag: item.tag,
    parentTag: item.parentTag,
    repository: item.repository,
    number: item.number,
    title: item.name,
    state: item.state,
    url: item.href,
    blockedByTags: item.blockedByTags ?? [],
    isBlockingTags: item.isBlockingTags ?? [],
  }
}

export function makeReferenceFromVersionedItem(item: File): ThreadScopedFileReference
export function makeReferenceFromVersionedItem(item: DraftIssue): DraftIssueReference
export function makeReferenceFromVersionedItem(item: PreviewableContent): CopilotChatReference | null
export function makeReferenceFromVersionedItem(item: PreviewableContent): CopilotChatReference | null {
  switch (item.type) {
    case 'file':
      return makeReferenceFromFile(item)
    case 'new-issue':
    case 'update-issue':
      return makeReferenceFromDraftIssue(item)
    default:
      return null
  }
}

export function makeVersionedItemFromReference({
  ref,
  messageIndex,
  messageId,
  timestamp,
}: {
  ref: ThreadScopedFileReference
  messageIndex: number
  messageId?: string
  timestamp?: string
}): File
export function makeVersionedItemFromReference({
  ref,
  messageIndex,
  messageId,
}: {
  ref: DraftIssueReference
  messageIndex: number
  messageId?: string
}): DraftIssue
export function makeVersionedItemFromReference({
  ref,
  messageIndex,
}: {
  ref: CopilotChatReference
  messageIndex: number
  messageId?: string
  timestamp?: string
}): VersionedPreviewableContent | null
export function makeVersionedItemFromReference({
  ref,
  messageIndex,
  messageId,
  timestamp,
}: {
  ref: CopilotChatReference
  messageIndex: number
  messageId?: string
  timestamp?: string
}): VersionedPreviewableContent | null {
  switch (ref.type) {
    case 'thread-scoped-file':
      return makeItemFromThreadScopedFileReference(ref, messageIndex, messageId, timestamp)
    case 'draft-issue':
    case 'draft-update-issue':
      return makeItemFromDraftIssueReference({ref, messageIndex, messageId})
    default:
      return null
  }
}

export function getPreviousVersion<T extends PreviewableContent>(
  content: PreviewableContent,
  items: ItemsMap,
  versionedItems: VersionedItemsMap,
): T | undefined {
  const unversionedId = stripVersionFromId(content.id)
  const versions = versionedItems.get(unversionedId)
  const currentIdx = versions?.findIndex(v => v === content.id) ?? -1
  const previousVersionId = versions?.[currentIdx - 1]
  return previousVersionId && (items.get(previousVersionId) as T | undefined)
}

// Compares two draft issues to determine if they have meaningful content changes.
// Ignores fields that change on every render like messageId, isStreaming, and id.
export function areDraftIssuesEqual(a: DraftIssue, b: DraftIssue): boolean {
  const normalize = (issue: DraftIssue) => ({
    tag: issue.tag,
    parentTag: issue.parentTag,
    repository: issue.repository,
    type: issue.type,
    template: issue.template,
    name: issue.name,
    body: issue.body,
    bodyVersion: issue.bodyVersion,
    isUserEdited: issue.isUserEdited,
    issueType: issue.issueType,
    milestone: issue.milestone,
    issueNumber: issue.issueNumber,
    assignees: issue.assignees?.slice().sort(),
    labels: issue.labels?.slice().sort(),
    projects: issue.projects?.slice().sort((projectA, projectB) => {
      const aKey = `${projectA.id || ''}:${projectA.title || ''}`
      const bKey = `${projectB.id || ''}:${projectB.title || ''}`
      return aKey.localeCompare(bKey)
    }),
    rawProjectsInput: issue.rawProjectsInput?.slice().sort(),
    defaultValuesById: issue.defaultValuesById,
    blockedByTags: issue.blockedByTags?.slice().sort(),
    isBlockingTags: issue.isBlockingTags?.slice().sort(),
  })

  return JSON.stringify(normalize(a)) === JSON.stringify(normalize(b))
}

import {useChatStateValues} from '@github-ui/copilot-chat/CopilotChatContext'
import {useChatManager} from '@github-ui/copilot-chat/CopilotChatManagerContext'
import {useIsScrolledToEnd} from '@github-ui/copilot-chat/hooks/use-is-scrolled-to-end'
import {isFileReference, referencesAreEqual} from '@github-ui/copilot-chat/utils/copilot-chat-helpers'
import {copilotFeatureFlags} from '@github-ui/copilot-chat/utils/copilot-feature-flags'
import {useGetChatState} from '@github-ui/copilot-chat/utils/ObservableChatContext'
import {isFeatureEnabled} from '@github-ui/feature-flags'
import {sendEvent} from '@github-ui/hydro-analytics'
import {useLayoutContext} from '@github-ui/pacer/Layout/layout-context'
import {ErrorBoundary} from '@github-ui/react-core/error-boundary'
import {ScrollContainer} from '@github-ui/scroll-container'
import {usePreviousValue} from '@github-ui/use-previous-value'
import {useStableCallback} from '@github-ui/use-stable-callback'
import {FocusKeys} from '@primer/behaviors'
import {
  FileZipIcon,
  KebabHorizontalIcon,
  ListUnorderedIcon,
  MaximizeIcon,
  MinimizeIcon,
  XIcon,
} from '@primer/octicons-react'
import {ActionList, ActionMenu, Banner, IconButton, useFocusZone} from '@primer/react'
import {downloadZip} from 'client-zip'
import {clsx} from 'clsx'
import {type RefObject, startTransition, useCallback, useEffect, useId, useRef, type WheelEvent} from 'react'

import {useCreatePr} from '../../hooks/use-create-pr/use-create-pr'
import type {CreatePrState} from '../../hooks/use-create-pr/use-create-pr-util'
import {CreatePrDialog} from '../create-pr-dialog/CreatePrDialog'
import {ErrorFallback} from '../ErrorFallback'
import styles from './ContentPreview.module.css'
import {useContentPreview, useContentPreviewOpen, useResourcePanelOpen} from './ContentPreviewContext'
import {ContentPreviewItem} from './ContentPreviewItem'
import {ContentPreviewTab} from './ContentPreviewTab'
import {downloadFile} from './download-file'
import {EmptyState} from './EmptyState'
import {LoadingState} from './LoadingState'
import {sendContentPreviewEvent} from './telemetry'

interface ContentPreviewProps {
  /** Element to focus on close. */
  returnFocusRef: RefObject<HTMLElement | null>
  /** Disable the resource panel feature. */
  disableResourcePanel?: boolean
  disableActionsMenu?: boolean
  /** Hide the expand workbench button */
  hideExpandButton?: boolean
}

/** Hook to close preview pane and collapse secondary column if expanded */
function useClosePreviewPane() {
  const {closePreviewPane} = useContentPreview()
  const {secondaryColumnFullWidth, toggleSecondaryColumnFullWidth, setDesktopLayoutState} = useLayoutContext()

  return useCallback(() => {
    if (secondaryColumnFullWidth) {
      toggleSecondaryColumnFullWidth()
      setDesktopLayoutState(prev => ({...prev, fullWidth: false}))
    }
    closePreviewPane()
  }, [closePreviewPane, secondaryColumnFullWidth, toggleSecondaryColumnFullWidth, setDesktopLayoutState])
}

function ContentPreviewInner(props: ContentPreviewProps) {
  return isFeatureEnabled('copilot_create_pr') ? (
    <ContentPreviewInnerWithCreatePr {...props} />
  ) : (
    <ContentPreviewInnerCore {...props} createPr={null} />
  )
}

function ContentPreviewInnerWithCreatePr(props: ContentPreviewProps) {
  const createPr = useCreatePr()
  return <ContentPreviewInnerCore {...props} createPr={createPr} />
}

function ContentPreviewInnerCore({
  returnFocusRef,
  disableResourcePanel = false,
  disableActionsMenu = false,
  hideExpandButton = false,
  createPr,
}: ContentPreviewProps & {createPr: CreatePrState | null}) {
  const isOpen = useContentPreviewOpen()
  const isResourcePanelOpen = useResourcePanelOpen()
  const {selectedThreadID} = useChatStateValues('selectedThreadID')
  const getChatState = useGetChatState()
  const manager = useChatManager()
  const prevSelectedThreadID = usePreviousValue(selectedThreadID)
  const {
    items,
    allOpenItems: openItems,
    selectedItem,
    openItem,
    closeItem,
    closeItems,
    showLoadingState,
    openResourcePanel,
    returnFocusElementRef,
    setCreatePrDialogOpener,
  } = useContentPreview()
  const handleClosePreviewPane = useClosePreviewPane()
  const prevIsOpen = usePreviousValue(isOpen)
  const isOpening = isOpen && !prevIsOpen
  const isClosing = prevIsOpen && !isOpen
  const openFiles = openItems.map(itemId => items.get(itemId)).filter(item => item?.type === 'file')

  const activeTabId = useId()
  const selectedTabPanelId = useId()
  const closeTabControlHintId = useId()

  const openItemInstances = openItems.map(key => items.get(key)).filter(item => !!item)
  const selectedItemInstance = openItemInstances.find(item => item.id === selectedItem) ?? openItemInstances[0]
  const previousSelectedItem = usePreviousValue(selectedItem)

  const resourcePanelSupported = copilotFeatureFlags.copilotResourcePanel && !disableResourcePanel
  const isFocusingFirstTimeRef = useRef(true)

  const createPrError = createPr?.error ?? null
  const clearError = createPr?.clearError
  const openPullRequestDialog = createPr?.openPullRequestDialog
  const dialogProps = createPr?.dialogProps ?? null

  const createPrDialogOpener = useStableCallback(() => {
    openPullRequestDialog?.()
  })

  useEffect(() => {
    setCreatePrDialogOpener?.(openPullRequestDialog ? createPrDialogOpener : null)
    return () => {
      setCreatePrDialogOpener?.(null)
    }
  }, [openPullRequestDialog, createPrDialogOpener, setCreatePrDialogOpener])

  useEffect(() => {
    // Return focus when:
    // 1. Closing the preview pane, or
    // 2. After workbench has opened and a file is loaded (if feature flag is enabled)
    if (
      isClosing ||
      (copilotFeatureFlags.focusTextAreaInsteadOfWorkbench &&
        isFocusingFirstTimeRef.current &&
        selectedItemInstance &&
        selectedItemInstance.type === 'file' &&
        !isOpening)
    ) {
      isFocusingFirstTimeRef.current = false
      // Use the stored returnFocusElement if available, otherwise fall back to returnFocusRef
      const returnFocusElement = returnFocusElementRef.current
      if (returnFocusElement) {
        returnFocusElement.focus()
      } else {
        returnFocusRef.current?.focus()
      }
    }
  }, [isClosing, isOpening, returnFocusRef, selectedItemInstance, returnFocusElementRef])

  useEffect(() => {
    // close preview pane when changing threads
    if (prevSelectedThreadID != null && prevSelectedThreadID !== selectedThreadID) {
      handleClosePreviewPane()
    }
  }, [handleClosePreviewPane, prevSelectedThreadID, selectedThreadID])

  useEffect(() => {
    // keep selected tab in view when changing tabs
    if (!selectedItem || !isOpen) return

    const index = openItemInstances.findIndex(item => item.id === selectedItem)
    const tab = tabsRef.current[index]

    tab?.scrollIntoView({block: 'nearest', inline: 'nearest'})
  }, [selectedItem, openItemInstances, isOpen])

  useEffect(() => {
    // open resource panel if an eligible item is selected
    if (
      resourcePanelSupported &&
      selectedItem &&
      selectedItem !== previousSelectedItem &&
      !isResourcePanelOpen &&
      selectedItemInstance?.type === 'file' &&
      isFileReference(selectedItemInstance.reference)
    ) {
      openResourcePanel()
    }
  }, [
    isResourcePanelOpen,
    openResourcePanel,
    previousSelectedItem,
    resourcePanelSupported,
    selectedItem,
    selectedItemInstance,
  ])

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!copilotFeatureFlags.copilotResourcePanel) return
    if (selectedItem === previousSelectedItem) return
    const {currentReferences, implicitReferences} = getChatState()
    // automatically add implicit reference to selected tab.
    if (selectedItem && selectedItemInstance?.type === 'file') {
      const reference = selectedItemInstance.reference
      if (isFileReference(reference) && !currentReferences.some(ref => referencesAreEqual(ref, reference))) {
        if (implicitReferences.size > 0) {
          manager.clearImplicitReferences()
        }
        manager.addReference(reference, 'ContentPreview', {implicit: true})
      }
    } else if (implicitReferences.size > 0) {
      manager.clearImplicitReferences()
    }
  }, [getChatState, manager, previousSelectedItem, selectedItem, selectedItemInstance])

  const downloadAll = async () => {
    const files = openFiles.map(item => new File([item.value], item.name))
    const blob = await downloadZip(files).blob()
    downloadFile(new File([blob], 'files.zip'))
  }

  const handleClose = useCallback(
    (closePreview = false) => {
      if (selectedItemInstance) {
        const shouldClosePreview = closePreview && openItemInstances.length === 1
        closeItem(selectedItemInstance.id)
        if (shouldClosePreview) {
          handleClosePreviewPane()
          sendEvent('dotcom_chat.activate', {target: 'BROWSER_CLOSE', mode: 'immersive'})
        }
      }
    },

    [closeItem, handleClosePreviewPane, openItemInstances.length, selectedItemInstance],
  )

  const tabsRef = useRef<Array<HTMLDivElement | null>>([])

  const tablistRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null)
  useFocusZone(
    {
      containerRef: tablistRef,
      focusableElementFilter: el => el.role === 'tab',
      bindKeys: FocusKeys.ArrowHorizontal | FocusKeys.HomeAndEnd,
      focusInStrategy: 'first',
      focusOutBehavior: 'wrap',
    },
    [openItemInstances],
  )
  // We don't need that when we use the new ScrollContainer component
  const tabsHasOverflow = !useIsScrolledToEnd(tablistRef) && !copilotFeatureFlags.scrollPreviewTabs

  const onTabsWheel = (event: WheelEvent<HTMLDivElement>) => {
    if (!event.deltaY) return
    event.preventDefault()
    event.currentTarget.scrollLeft += event.deltaY
  }

  const closeTab = (i: number) => {
    const item = openItemInstances[i]
    if (!item) return

    const nextFocusTab = tabsRef.current[i + 1] ?? tabsRef.current[i - 1] ?? tablistRef.current
    const nextFocusTarget = nextFocusTab?.querySelector('[role="tab"]') as HTMLElement | null
    nextFocusTarget?.focus()

    const isOnly = openItemInstances.length === 1
    closeItem(item.id)
    if (isOnly) handleClosePreviewPane()
    sendContentPreviewEvent('filePreviewTab.close', item)
  }

  const closeAll = () => {
    closeItems()
    handleClosePreviewPane()
    sendEvent('dotcom_chat.activate', {target: 'BROWSER_CLOSE_ALL_TABS', mode: 'immersive'})
  }

  return (
    <>
      {createPrError && (
        <Banner
          aria-atomic="true"
          role="alert"
          variant="critical"
          title="Failed to create pull request"
          hideTitle
          description={createPrError ?? ''}
          onDismiss={() => {
            clearError?.()
          }}
        />
      )}
      <div
        className={clsx(styles.toolbar, copilotFeatureFlags.copilotWorkbenchSlimLineTopTabs && styles.slimLineTopTabs)}
      >
        {resourcePanelSupported && !isResourcePanelOpen && (
          <div className={clsx(styles.actions, styles.rightDivider)}>
            <IconButton
              icon={ListUnorderedIcon}
              aria-label="Open resource panel"
              onClick={openResourcePanel}
              variant="invisible"
            />
          </div>
        )}
        <TabsContainer tabsListRef={tablistRef} onTabsWheel={onTabsWheel}>
          {openItemInstances.map((item, i) => (
            <ContentPreviewTab
              id={item.id === selectedItem ? activeTabId : undefined}
              tabPanelId={item.id === selectedItem ? selectedTabPanelId : undefined}
              controlHintId={closeTabControlHintId}
              key={item.id}
              isActive={item.id === selectedItem}
              onClick={() => {
                if (copilotFeatureFlags.filePreviewKeepMounted) {
                  startTransition(() => openItem(item.id))
                } else {
                  openItem(item.id)
                }
                sendContentPreviewEvent('filePreviewTab.click', item)
              }}
              onClose={() => closeTab(i)}
              item={item}
              ref={el => {
                tabsRef.current[i] = el
              }}
            />
          ))}
          <span id={closeTabControlHintId} aria-hidden="true" className="sr-only">
            Press Delete to close.
          </span>
          <span />
        </TabsContainer>
        <div className={clsx(styles.actions, tabsHasOverflow && styles.leftDivider)}>
          {!disableActionsMenu && openItems.length > 0 && (
            <ActionMenu>
              <ActionMenu.Anchor>
                <IconButton icon={KebabHorizontalIcon} aria-label="More options" variant="invisible" />
              </ActionMenu.Anchor>
              <ActionMenu.Overlay>
                <ActionList>
                  {openFiles.length > 0 && (
                    <ActionList.Item onSelect={downloadAll}>
                      <ActionList.LeadingVisual>
                        <FileZipIcon />
                      </ActionList.LeadingVisual>
                      Download all files
                    </ActionList.Item>
                  )}
                  <ActionList.Item onSelect={closeAll}>
                    <ActionList.LeadingVisual>
                      <XIcon />
                    </ActionList.LeadingVisual>
                    Close all tabs
                  </ActionList.Item>
                </ActionList>
              </ActionMenu.Overlay>
            </ActionMenu>
          )}
          <CloseContentPreviewButton hideExpandButton={hideExpandButton} />
        </div>
      </div>

      <div
        className={clsx(styles.content, 'content-preview-content')}
        role="tabpanel"
        id={selectedTabPanelId}
        aria-labelledby={activeTabId}
      >
        {showLoadingState ? (
          <LoadingState />
        ) : selectedItemInstance ? (
          <ContentPreviewItem isOpening={isOpening} previewItem={selectedItemInstance} onClose={handleClose} />
        ) : (
          <EmptyState isOpening={isOpening} />
        )}
      </div>

      {dialogProps ? <CreatePrDialog {...dialogProps} /> : null}
    </>
  )
}

function CloseContentPreviewButton({hideExpandButton = false}: {hideExpandButton?: boolean}) {
  const handleClosePreviewPane = useClosePreviewPane()
  const {secondaryColumnFullWidth, toggleSecondaryColumnFullWidth} = useLayoutContext()

  return (
    <div className="d-flex gap-1">
      {!hideExpandButton && (
        <IconButton
          variant="invisible"
          icon={secondaryColumnFullWidth ? MinimizeIcon : MaximizeIcon}
          aria-label={secondaryColumnFullWidth ? 'Collapse workbench' : 'Expand workbench'}
          onClick={() => {
            toggleSecondaryColumnFullWidth()
          }}
          className={styles.onlyShowOnLargeViewports}
        />
      )}
      <IconButton
        variant="invisible"
        icon={XIcon}
        aria-label="Close workbench"
        onClick={() => {
          handleClosePreviewPane()
          sendEvent('dotcom_chat.activate', {target: 'CLOSE_PREVIEW_PANE_BUTTON', mode: 'immersive'})
        }}
      />
    </div>
  )
}

function ContentPreviewFallback() {
  return (
    <>
      <div className={styles.toolbar}>
        <div className={clsx(styles.actions, styles.rightDivider)}>
          <CloseContentPreviewButton />
        </div>
      </div>
      <div className={clsx(styles.content)}>
        <ErrorFallback regionName="The workbench" />
      </div>
    </>
  )
}

// We put as much logic into the Inner component as possible so that errors are always caught. We still need some
// container styles around the error boundary though to contain it if it does appear.
export function ContentPreview({
  returnFocusRef,
  disableResourcePanel = false,
  disableActionsMenu = false,
  hideExpandButton = false,
  className,
}: ContentPreviewProps & {className?: string}) {
  const labelId = useId()
  const {contentPreviewRef} = useContentPreview()
  const contentPreviewOpen = useContentPreviewOpen()
  const isResourcePanelOpen = useResourcePanelOpen()

  return (
    <div
      aria-labelledby={labelId}
      className={clsx(
        styles.container,
        copilotFeatureFlags.copilotWorkbenchSlimLineTopTabs && styles.slimLineTopTabs,
        isResourcePanelOpen && styles.resourcePanelOpen,
        className,
      )}
      tabIndex={-1}
      data-testid="content-preview"
      role="dialog"
      aria-modal="false"
      ref={contentPreviewOpen ? contentPreviewRef : undefined}
    >
      <h2 id={labelId} className="sr-only">
        Workbench
      </h2>
      <ErrorBoundary fallback={<ContentPreviewFallback />}>
        <ContentPreviewInner
          returnFocusRef={returnFocusRef}
          disableResourcePanel={disableResourcePanel}
          disableActionsMenu={disableActionsMenu}
          hideExpandButton={hideExpandButton}
        />
      </ErrorBoundary>
    </div>
  )
}

interface TabsContainerProps {
  tabsListRef: React.RefObject<HTMLDivElement | null>
  onTabsWheel: (event: React.WheelEvent<HTMLDivElement>) => void
  children: React.ReactNode
}

const TabsContainer = ({tabsListRef, onTabsWheel, children}: TabsContainerProps) => {
  const scrollContainerEnabled = copilotFeatureFlags.scrollPreviewTabs

  if (scrollContainerEnabled) {
    return (
      <ScrollContainer
        classNameForInnerDiv={styles.scrollContainerTabs}
        ref={tabsListRef}
        direction="horizontal"
        onWheel={onTabsWheel}
        innerDivProps={{
          role: 'tablist',
          'aria-label': 'Workbench items',
        }}
      >
        {children}
      </ScrollContainer>
    )
  }

  return (
    <div className={styles.tabsContainer}>
      <div
        className={styles.tabs}
        role="tablist"
        aria-label="Workbench items"
        ref={tabsListRef}
        tabIndex={-1}
        onWheel={onTabsWheel}
      >
        {children}
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

// eslint-disable-next-line no-restricted-imports
import {useChatState, useChatStateLens} from '@github-ui/copilot-chat/CopilotChatContext'
import {useNavigableRef} from '@github-ui/navigable-ref'
import type {Observable} from '@github-ui/observable'
import {useObservableValue, useObservedState} from '@github-ui/react-observable'
import {usePreviousValue} from '@github-ui/use-previous-value'
import {getSessionStorageItemsForKeyPrefix} from '@github-ui/use-safe-storage/session-storage'
import {useStableCallback} from '@github-ui/use-stable-callback'
import {createContext, use, useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState} from 'react'

import {useBooleanState} from '../../hooks/use-boolean-state'
import {useLoadDiffReferences} from '../../hooks/use-load-diff-reference'
import {
  areDraftIssuesEqual,
  type ContentPreviewContext as ContentPreviewContextType,
  type DraftIssue,
  type DraftOrExistingIssue,
  type ItemsMap,
  type PreviewableContent,
  type PreviewableContentIdentifier,
  stripVersionFromId,
} from './content-preview-types'
import {useSyncEditedItemsWithCurrentReferences} from './use-sync-items-with-current-references'

export type ContentPreviewContext = ContentPreviewContextType
type ContentPreviewContextInternal = ContentPreviewContext & {
  isContentPreviewOpen: Observable<boolean>
  isResourcePanelOpen: Observable<boolean>
}
export const ContentPreviewContext = createContext<ContentPreviewContextInternal | undefined>(undefined)

const emptyItems: ItemsMap = new Map()
const emptyOpenItems: ReturnType<ContentPreviewContext['getOpenItems']> = []

export function ContentPreviewProvider({children}: {children: React.ReactNode}) {
  const isContentPreviewOpen = useObservableValue(false)
  const isResourcePanelOpen = useObservableValue(false)
  const contentPreviewRef = useNavigableRef<HTMLDivElement>()
  const returnFocusElementRef = useRef<HTMLElement | null>(null)
  const [createPrDialogOpener, setCreatePrDialogOpenerState] = useState<(() => void) | null>(null)
  const setCreatePrDialogOpener = useCallback((opener: (() => void) | null) => {
    setCreatePrDialogOpenerState(prev => (prev === opener ? prev : opener))
  }, [])
  const [items, setItems] = useState(emptyItems)
  const [openItems, setOpenItems] = useState(emptyOpenItems)
  const [openItemsBeforeSubthreadChange, setOpenItemsBeforeSubthreadChange] = useState(emptyOpenItems)
  const [selectedItem, setSelectedItem] = useState<PreviewableContentIdentifier | undefined>(undefined)
  const [showLoadingState, enableLoadingState, disableLoadingState] = useBooleanState(false)

  const previousThreadId = usePreviousValue(useChatStateLens(s => s.selectedThreadID))
  const threadId = useChatStateLens(s => s.selectedThreadID)
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!previousThreadId || previousThreadId === threadId) return
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/set-state-in-effect
    setItems(emptyItems)
    setOpenItems(emptyOpenItems)
    setSelectedItem(undefined)
    if (threadId === null) isContentPreviewOpen.setValue(false)
  }, [threadId, previousThreadId, isContentPreviewOpen])

  const openItem = useCallback((id: PreviewableContentIdentifier, selectItem: boolean = true) => {
    setOpenItems(prevVisibleItems => {
      // Item already visible, no-op
      if (prevVisibleItems.includes(id)) return prevVisibleItems

      // Only one version of an item can ever be open
      const unversionedId = stripVersionFromId(id)
      const replaceIndex = prevVisibleItems.findIndex(visibleItem => stripVersionFromId(visibleItem) === unversionedId)

      const newVisibleItems = [...prevVisibleItems]

      if (replaceIndex === -1) newVisibleItems.push(id)
      else newVisibleItems.splice(replaceIndex, 1, id)

      return newVisibleItems
    })

    setSelectedItem(prevSelectedItem =>
      // If we open a different version of the currently selected item, we must update the selected item or nothing will be selected
      selectItem || prevSelectedItem === undefined || stripVersionFromId(prevSelectedItem) === stripVersionFromId(id)
        ? id
        : prevSelectedItem,
    )
  }, [])

  const updateItem = useCallback((item: PreviewableContent) => {
    setItems(prevItems => {
      const isStreaming = 'isStreaming' in item ? item.isStreaming : false
      let duplicateOfId: PreviewableContentIdentifier | undefined

      // For draft issues, check for duplicates once streaming is complete
      if (!isStreaming && (item.type === 'new-issue' || item.type === 'update-issue')) {
        const unversionedId = stripVersionFromId(item.id)

        for (const [existingId, existingItem] of prevItems) {
          if (
            existingId !== item.id && // Don't compare an item with itself
            stripVersionFromId(existingId) === unversionedId &&
            (existingItem.type === 'new-issue' || existingItem.type === 'update-issue') &&
            // Don't consider items that are themselves duplicates - they'll be filtered out of versionedItems
            !existingItem.duplicateOf
          ) {
            if (areDraftIssuesEqual(item, existingItem)) {
              // Because we wait until streaming is complete to check for duplicates, the item may already exist.
              // To prevent unnecessary/infinite updates, we return early if the item hasn't changed and is already
              // marked as a dupe.
              const currentItemInPrevItems = prevItems.get(item.id)
              if (
                currentItemInPrevItems && // does the item already exist?
                areDraftIssuesEqual(item, currentItemInPrevItems as DraftIssue) && // is it still the same as the existing content?
                'duplicateOf' in currentItemInPrevItems &&
                currentItemInPrevItems.duplicateOf === existingId // is it already marked as a duplicate?
              ) {
                return prevItems
              }

              duplicateOfId = existingId
              break
            }
          }
        }
      }

      const newItems = new Map(prevItems)
      if (duplicateOfId && (item.type === 'new-issue' || item.type === 'update-issue')) {
        newItems.set(item.id, {...item, duplicateOf: duplicateOfId} as DraftOrExistingIssue)
      } else {
        newItems.set(item.id, item)
      }
      return newItems
    })
  }, [])

  const closeItem = useStableCallback((id: PreviewableContentIdentifier) => {
    const index = openItems.indexOf(id)
    if (index === -1) return // Item not open, no-op
    const newOpenItems = openItems.toSpliced(index, 1)
    if (selectedItem === id) setSelectedItem(newOpenItems[index - 1] ?? newOpenItems[0])
    setOpenItems(newOpenItems)
  })

  const closeItems = useStableCallback((ids?: PreviewableContentIdentifier[]) => {
    if (ids) {
      let newOpenItems = openItems
      for (const id of ids) {
        const index = newOpenItems.indexOf(id)
        if (index === -1) continue // Item not open, no-op
        newOpenItems = newOpenItems.toSpliced(index, 1)
        if (selectedItem === id) {
          setSelectedItem(newOpenItems[index - 1] ?? newOpenItems[0])
        }
      }
      setOpenItems(newOpenItems)
    } else {
      setOpenItems(emptyOpenItems)
      setSelectedItem(undefined)
    }
  })

  const versionedItems = useMemo(() => {
    const result = new Map<PreviewableContentIdentifier, PreviewableContentIdentifier[]>()

    // collect version sets
    for (const [versionedId, item] of items) {
      const unversionedId = stripVersionFromId(versionedId)
      const versions = result.get(unversionedId) ?? []

      // Skip items marked as duplicates to prevent version bumping
      if ('duplicateOf' in item && item.duplicateOf) {
        continue
      }

      // Skip streaming items to prevent version strobing while content is being generated
      // Once streaming completes, the item will either be marked as a duplicate or appear as a new version
      if ('isStreaming' in item && item.isStreaming) {
        continue
      }

      versions.push(item.id)
      result.set(unversionedId, versions)
    }

    // sort each set
    for (const versions of result.values())
      versions.sort((idA, idB) => {
        const a = items.get(idA)!
        const b = items.get(idB)!
        return 'timestamp' in a && 'timestamp' in b ? a.timestamp.valueOf() - b.timestamp.valueOf() : 0
      })

    return result
  }, [items])

  const removeItems = useStableCallback((ids?: PreviewableContentIdentifier[]) => {
    if (ids) {
      for (const id of ids) {
        setItems(prevItems => {
          const newItems = new Map(prevItems)
          // If the item was not found, return the previous items
          return newItems.delete(id) ? newItems : prevItems
        })

        // If this item is versioned and open, roll the user to the previous version. Otherwise, close the item
        if (openItems.includes(id)) {
          const unversionedId = stripVersionFromId(id)
          const versions = versionedItems.get(unversionedId)
          const versionIndex = versions?.indexOf(id) ?? -1
          const previousVersion = versions?.[versionIndex - 1]
          if (previousVersion) openItem(previousVersion, false)
          else closeItem(id)
        }
      }
    } else {
      setItems(emptyItems)
      setOpenItems(emptyOpenItems)
      setSelectedItem(undefined)
    }
  })

  const getOpenItems = useStableCallback(() => (isContentPreviewOpen.value ? openItems : emptyOpenItems))
  const openPreviewPane = useCallback(
    (newReturnFocusElement?: HTMLElement | null) => {
      isContentPreviewOpen.setValue(true)
      // Move focus to the workbench dialog so keyboard users land inside it even if it was already visible.
      queueMicrotask(() => {
        contentPreviewRef.current?.focus()
      })
      // Store the element that should receive focus when the pane closes
      // Do this after queuing the focus to avoid state update interference
      if (newReturnFocusElement !== undefined) {
        returnFocusElementRef.current = newReturnFocusElement
      }
    },
    [isContentPreviewOpen, contentPreviewRef, returnFocusElementRef],
  )
  const closePreviewPane = useCallback(() => {
    isContentPreviewOpen.setValue(false)
    isResourcePanelOpen.setValue(false)
  }, [isContentPreviewOpen, isResourcePanelOpen])
  const openResourcePanel = useCallback(() => isResourcePanelOpen.setValue(true), [isResourcePanelOpen])
  const closeResourcePanel = useCallback(() => isResourcePanelOpen.setValue(false), [isResourcePanelOpen])

  const value = useMemo(
    (): ContentPreviewContextInternal => ({
      items,
      versionedItems,
      contentPreviewRef,
      isContentPreviewOpen,
      isResourcePanelOpen,
      getOpenItems,
      allOpenItems: openItems,
      openItemsBeforeSubthreadChange,
      selectedItem,
      createPrDialogOpener,
      setCreatePrDialogOpener,
      updateItem,
      setOpenItemsBeforeSubthreadChange,
      openItem,
      closeItem,
      closeItems,
      openPreviewPane,
      closePreviewPane,
      openResourcePanel,
      closeResourcePanel,
      removeItems,
      showLoadingState,
      enableLoadingState,
      disableLoadingState,
      returnFocusElementRef,
    }),
    [
      items,
      versionedItems,
      contentPreviewRef,
      isContentPreviewOpen,
      isResourcePanelOpen,
      getOpenItems,
      openItems,
      openItemsBeforeSubthreadChange,
      selectedItem,
      createPrDialogOpener,
      setCreatePrDialogOpener,
      updateItem,
      openItem,
      closeItem,
      closeItems,
      openPreviewPane,
      closePreviewPane,
      openResourcePanel,
      closeResourcePanel,
      removeItems,
      showLoadingState,
      enableLoadingState,
      disableLoadingState,
      returnFocusElementRef,
    ],
  )

  useLoadDiffReferences(value)
  useEditedIssueWithSessionStorage(value)
  useSyncEditedItemsWithCurrentReferences(value)

  return <ContentPreviewContext value={value}>{children}</ContentPreviewContext>
}

const useEditedIssueWithSessionStorage = ({updateItem}: ContentPreviewContext) => {
  const {messagesLoading, selectedThreadID} = useChatState()
  const storageKeyPrefix = `edited-${selectedThreadID}`

  useEffect(() => {
    const editedIssues = getSessionStorageItemsForKeyPrefix<DraftIssue>(storageKeyPrefix)
    if (editedIssues && messagesLoading.state === 'loaded') {
      for (const editedIssue of editedIssues) {
        updateItem(editedIssue)
      }
    }
  }, [storageKeyPrefix, updateItem, messagesLoading.state])
}

export function useContentPreview(): ContentPreviewContext {
  const context = use(ContentPreviewContext)
  if (!context) {
    throw new Error('useContentPreview must be used within ContentPreviewContextProvider')
  }
  return context
}

/**
 * Returns true if the content preview pane is open, false otherwise.
 */
export function useContentPreviewOpen() {
  const context = use(ContentPreviewContext)
  if (!context) {
    throw new Error('useContentPreviewOpen must be used within ContentPreviewContextProvider')
  }
  return useObservedState(context.isContentPreviewOpen)
}

/**
 * Returns true if the content preview pane is open, false otherwise.
 */
export function useResourcePanelOpen() {
  const context = use(ContentPreviewContext)
  if (!context) {
    throw new Error('useResourcePanelOpen must be used within ContentPreviewContextProvider')
  }
  return useObservedState(context.isResourcePanelOpen)
}

import {usePlugins} from '@github-ui/copilot-chat/plugin/registry'
import {ErrorBoundary} from '@github-ui/react-core/error-boundary'

import {ErrorFallback} from '../ErrorFallback'
import type {PreviewableContent} from './content-preview-types'
import {CreateIssuePreview} from './create-issue-preview/CreateIssuePreview'
import {ExistingIssuePreview} from './create-issue-preview/ExistingIssuePreview'
import {UpdateIssuePreview} from './create-issue-preview/UpdateIssuePreview'
import {DiffPreview} from './DiffPreview'
import {FilePreview} from './FilePreview'
import {ImagePreview} from './ImagePreview'
import {IssuePreview} from './IssuePreview'
import {JobResultPreview} from './JobResultPreview'

interface ContentPreviewItemProps {
  isOpening: boolean
  onClose: (closePreview?: boolean) => void
  previewItem: PreviewableContent
}

export function ContentPreviewItem({isOpening, onClose, previewItem}: ContentPreviewItemProps) {
  const plugins = usePlugins()
  let item = null
  switch (previewItem.type) {
    case 'file':
      item = <FilePreview isPreviewOpening={isOpening} file={previewItem} onClose={onClose} />
      break
    case 'file-diff':
      item = <DiffPreview fileDiff={previewItem} />
      break
    case 'issue':
      item = <IssuePreview isPreviewOpening={isOpening} issue={previewItem} onClose={onClose} />
      break
    case 'new-issue':
      item = <CreateIssuePreview isPreviewOpening={isOpening} issue={previewItem} onClose={onClose} />
      break
    case 'update-issue':
      item = <UpdateIssuePreview isPreviewOpening={isOpening} issue={previewItem} onClose={onClose} />
      break
    case 'existing-issue':
      item = <ExistingIssuePreview isPreviewOpening={isOpening} issue={previewItem} onClose={onClose} />
      break
    case 'image':
      item = <ImagePreview image={previewItem} />
      break
    case 'plugin-content':
      for (const plugin of plugins) {
        const PluginPreviewComponent = plugin.previewComponents?.[previewItem.reference.type]
        if (PluginPreviewComponent) {
          item = <PluginPreviewComponent reference={previewItem.reference} />
          break
        }
      }
      break
    case 'job-result':
      item = <JobResultPreview jobResult={previewItem} />
      break
  }

  return item ? (
    <ErrorBoundary fallback={<ErrorFallback regionName="This workbench item" />}>{item}</ErrorBoundary>
  ) : null
}

import {copilotFeatureFlags} from '@github-ui/copilot-chat/utils/copilot-feature-flags'
import {sendEvent} from '@github-ui/hydro-analytics'
import {Tooltip} from '@github-ui/pacer/Tooltip'
import {
  CodeIcon,
  FileDiffIcon,
  ImageIcon,
  IssueClosedIcon,
  IssueDraftIcon,
  IssueOpenedIcon,
  MarkdownIcon,
  XIcon,
} from '@primer/octicons-react'
import {IconButton, Spinner} from '@primer/react'
import {clsx} from 'clsx'
import type React from 'react'
import {useLayoutEffect, useRef, useState} from 'react'

import type {File, PreviewableContent} from './content-preview-types'
import styles from './ContentPreviewTab.module.css'

interface ContentPreviewTabProps extends React.HTMLAttributes<HTMLButtonElement> {
  isActive?: boolean
  onClose?: () => void
  item: PreviewableContent
  controlHintId: string
  tabPanelId?: string
  ref?: React.Ref<HTMLDivElement>
}

function CodeTabIcon({file, className}: {file: File; className?: string}) {
  if (file.isStreaming) {
    return <Spinner size="small" />
  }
  switch (file.language) {
    case 'markdown':
      return <MarkdownIcon className={className} />
    default:
      return <CodeIcon className={className} />
  }
}

function TabIcon({item}: {item: PreviewableContent}) {
  const className = clsx({[styles.activeTabIcon]: copilotFeatureFlags.copilotWorkbenchSlimLineTopTabs})

  switch (item.type) {
    case 'file':
      return <CodeTabIcon className={className} file={item} />
    case 'file-diff':
      return <FileDiffIcon className={className} />
    case 'image':
      return <ImageIcon className={className} />
    case 'issue':
      return item.state === 'closed' ? (
        <IssueClosedIcon className={'fgColor-done'} />
      ) : (
        <IssueOpenedIcon className={item.state === 'open' ? 'fgColor-open' : undefined} />
      )
    case 'new-issue':
      return <IssueDraftIcon />
    case 'update-issue':
      return <IssueDraftIcon />
    case 'existing-issue':
      return <IssueOpenedIcon />
    case 'plugin-content':
      return item.icon ? <item.icon /> : null
    default:
      return null
  }
}

export function ContentPreviewTab({
  ref,
  onClick,
  onClose,
  isActive,
  item,
  controlHintId,
  tabPanelId,
  ...rest
}: ContentPreviewTabProps) {
  const [isTitleTruncated, setIsTitleTruncated] = useState(false)
  const titleRef = useRef<HTMLSpanElement>(null)
  const showCloseButton = !item.disableCloseButton

  const closeTab = () => {
    if (onClose) onClose()
    sendEvent('dotcom_chat.activate', {target: 'BROWSER_TAB_CLOSE', mode: 'immersive'})
  }

  const closeButtonRef = useRef<HTMLButtonElement>(null)

  const title = item.name

  const checkTruncation = () => {
    const element = titleRef.current
    if (!element) return

    const isOverflowing = element.scrollWidth > element.clientWidth
    setIsTitleTruncated(prev => (isOverflowing !== prev ? isOverflowing : prev))
  }

  useLayoutEffect(() => {
    checkTruncation()
  }, [title])

  const titleSpan = (
    <span
      className={clsx(styles.title, {[styles.isClipped]: isTitleTruncated})}
      data-title={title}
      data-testid={`content-preview-tab-${item.id}`}
      ref={titleRef}
      onMouseEnter={checkTruncation}
    >
      {title}
    </span>
  )

  return (
    <div ref={ref} className={styles.tabContainer}>
      <div
        className={clsx(styles.tabContent, {
          [styles.slimLineTopTabs]: copilotFeatureFlags.copilotWorkbenchSlimLineTopTabs,
          [styles.isActive]: isActive,
        })}
      >
        <button
          className={styles.mainButton}
          type="button"
          role="tab"
          aria-selected={isActive ? 'true' : undefined}
          aria-describedby={controlHintId}
          aria-controls={tabPanelId}
          onClick={e => {
            if (onClick) onClick(e)
            sendEvent('dotcom_chat.activate', {target: 'BROWSER_TAB_SELECT', mode: 'immersive'})
          }}
          onKeyDown={e => {
            switch (e.key.toLowerCase()) {
              case 'delete':
              case 'backspace':
                if (showCloseButton) closeTab()
                break
              case 'tab':
                if (e.shiftKey) break
                if (showCloseButton) {
                  e.preventDefault()
                  closeButtonRef.current?.focus()
                }
            }
          }}
          onAuxClick={e => {
            // middle mouse button
            if (e.button === 1 && showCloseButton) closeTab()
          }}
          {...rest}
        >
          <span className={styles.icon}>
            <TabIcon item={item} />
          </span>
          {/* The data-title attribute and respective CSS is required to set the width of the title using a semibold
          font-weight. This ensures that switching between tabs with different font-weight won't change the width
          of a tab because a thicker font-weight adds to the width of the element. */}
          {isTitleTruncated ? (
            <Tooltip text={title} position="bottom">
              {titleSpan}
            </Tooltip>
          ) : (
            titleSpan
          )}
          {item.trailingComponent ? <div className={styles.trailingComponent}>{item.trailingComponent}</div> : null}
        </button>
        {showCloseButton && (
          <div className={styles.closeButton}>
            {/* eslint-disable-next-line primer-react/a11y-remove-disable-tooltip */}
            <IconButton
              icon={XIcon}
              size="small"
              variant="invisible"
              aria-label={`Close ${title}`}
              aria-hidden
              tabIndex={-1}
              unsafeDisableTooltip
              onClick={closeTab}
              ref={closeButtonRef}
            />
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

export const CREATE_PR_DIALOG_IDS = {
  dialogTitle: 'open-pull-request-dialog-title',
  repositorySelect: 'open-pull-request-repository',
  branchInput: 'open-pull-request-branch',
  commitMessageInput: 'open-pull-request-commit-message',
  commitDescriptionInput: 'open-pull-request-commit-description',
  pullRequestTitleInput: 'open-pull-request-title',
  filesToIncludeFieldset: 'open-pull-request-files-to-include',
  filesToIncludeError: 'open-pull-request-files-to-include-error',
} as const

export const CREATE_PR_DIALOG_STRINGS = {
  title: 'Open a pull request',
  cancelButton: 'Cancel',
  createButton: 'Create pull request',
  filesToIncludeLegend: 'Files to include',
  filesToIncludeEmptyError: 'Select at least one file to include in the pull request.',
} as const

export type CreatePrDraft = {
  repositoryNwo: string
  /** Optional. When provided, used to call backend PR creation endpoint. */
  repositoryId?: number
  /** Optional. Base branch name (e.g. "main"). */
  baseBranch?: string
  /** Optional. Base commit OID/SHA that changes are based on. */
  baseCommitOid?: string
  /** Optional. Selected reviewer user ids. */
  reviewerUserIds?: number[]
  /** Optional. Selected reviewer team ids. */
  reviewerTeamIds?: number[]
  /** Optional. Selected assignee logins. */
  assigneeLogins?: string[]
  /** Optional. Selected label names. */
  labelNames?: string[]
  /** Optional. Selected project V2 ids (GraphQL ids). */
  projectIds?: string[]
  /** Optional. Selected milestone title. */
  milestoneTitle?: string
  /** Optional. Full file contents by path. */
  files?: Record<string, string>
  /** File paths to exclude from the created branch/commit/PR. */
  excludedFilePaths: string[]
  /**
   * If true, the branch name was explicitly specified by the user.
   * If false/undefined, the branch name was suggested by Copilot and may be adjusted to avoid conflicts.
   */
  branchNameWasUserProvided?: boolean
  branchName: string
  commitMessage: string
  commitDescription: string
  pullRequestTitle: string
  pullRequestBody: string
}

export type CreatePrRepositoryOption = {nameWithOwner: string}

export type CreatePrDialogDisabledFields = Partial<{
  repository: boolean
  branchName: boolean
  commitMessage: boolean
  commitDescription: boolean
  pullRequestTitle: boolean
  pullRequestBody: boolean
}>

export type CreatePrDialogProps = {
  isOpen: boolean
  repositories: CreatePrRepositoryOption[]
  availableFilePaths?: string[]
  value: CreatePrDraft
  onChange: (next: CreatePrDraft) => void
  onDismiss: () => void
  onConfirm: (draft: CreatePrDraft) => void
  isSubmitting?: boolean
  errorMessage?: string | null
  /** Disable inputs while waiting for their initial values. */
  disabledFields?: CreatePrDialogDisabledFields
}

/** Sort two strings lexicographically. */
function sortStrings(a: string, b: string) {
  return a.localeCompare(b)
}

/** Build a stable list of repo `owner/name` options, including the currently selected NWO. */
export function buildRepositoryOptions(repositories: CreatePrRepositoryOption[], selectedNwo: string): string[] {
  const unique = new Set(repositories.map(r => r.nameWithOwner).filter(Boolean))
  if (selectedNwo) unique.add(selectedNwo)
  return Array.from(unique).sort(sortStrings)
}

/** Convert an excluded file path array to a lookup Set. */
export function buildExcludedSet(excludedFilePaths: string[] | undefined | null): Set<string> {
  return new Set(excludedFilePaths ?? [])
}

/** Build a unique, sorted list of file paths for rendering. */
export function buildSortedFilePaths(availableFilePaths: string[] | undefined): string[] {
  if (!availableFilePaths) return []
  const unique = new Set(availableFilePaths.filter(Boolean))
  return Array.from(unique).sort(sortStrings)
}

/** Count the number of selected files given the available paths and an excluded set. */
export function countSelectedFiles(availableFilePaths: string[] | undefined, excludedSet: Set<string>): number | null {
  if (!availableFilePaths) return null

  let count = 0
  for (const path of availableFilePaths) {
    if (!excludedSet.has(path)) count++
  }

  return count
}

/** Compute whether the dialog's Create action should be disabled. */
export function isCreateDisabled(args: {
  value: Pick<CreatePrDraft, 'repositoryNwo' | 'branchName' | 'pullRequestTitle'>
  noFilesSelected: boolean
  isSubmitting: boolean
}): boolean {
  const {value, noFilesSelected, isSubmitting} = args
  return !value.repositoryNwo || !value.branchName || !value.pullRequestTitle || noFilesSelected || isSubmitting
}

/** Toggle a single path in `excludedFilePaths` based on checkbox state. */
export function toggleExcludedFilePath(args: {
  value: CreatePrDraft
  path: string
  nextChecked: boolean
}): CreatePrDraft {
  const {value, path, nextChecked} = args

  const nextExcluded = new Set(value.excludedFilePaths ?? [])
  if (nextChecked) {
    nextExcluded.delete(path)
  } else {
    nextExcluded.add(path)
  }

  return {...value, excludedFilePaths: Array.from(nextExcluded).sort(sortStrings)}
}

import {useIssueCreateDataContext} from '@github-ui/issue-create/IssueCreateDataContext'
import type {IssueCreatePayload} from '@github-ui/issue-create/Model'
import type {IssueFormRef} from '@github-ui/issue-form/Types'
import {CommandButton} from '@github-ui/ui-commands'
import {Dialog} from '@primer/react'
import React, {type RefObject, useCallback} from 'react'

import type {DraftOrExistingIssue} from '../content-preview-types'
import {BulkCreateButton} from './BulkCreateButton'
import type {TreeNode} from './use-draft-issue-tree-map'
import type {SingleIssueCreateError} from './use-issue-creation-handler'
import {useIssueCreationHandler} from './use-issue-creation-handler'

export type CreateIssueButtonProps = {
  issueTemplate: IssueCreatePayload | null | undefined
  issueNode: TreeNode<DraftOrExistingIssue> | undefined
  issueFormRef: RefObject<IssueFormRef | null>
  noRepoAccess: boolean
  onClose: () => void
  onError?: (error: SingleIssueCreateError) => void
  onClearError?: () => void
}

export function CreateIssueButton({
  issueTemplate,
  issueNode,
  issueFormRef,
  noRepoAccess = false,
  onClose,
  onError,
  onClearError,
}: CreateIssueButtonProps) {
  const [dialogOpen, setDialogOpen] = React.useState(false)
  const buttonRef = React.useRef<HTMLButtonElement>(null)
  const [isSubmitting, setIsSubmitting] = React.useState(false)

  const {repository: formRepository} = useIssueCreateDataContext()
  const templateRequired = formRepository && !issueTemplate && !formRepository.isBlankIssuesEnabled

  const {clearSessionData} = useIssueCreateDataContext()

  const onClearSession = useCallback(() => {
    clearSessionData()
    issueFormRef.current?.clearSessionStorage()
  }, [clearSessionData, issueFormRef])

  // Clears session storage and closes the content preview window.
  // This mirrors the cleanup logic that we pass into CreateIssueForm.
  const clearOnCreate = useCallback(() => {
    onClearSession()
    onClose?.()
    setIsSubmitting(false)
  }, [onClearSession, onClose])

  // Handle error callback
  const handleError = useCallback(
    (error: SingleIssueCreateError) => {
      onError?.(error)
      setIsSubmitting(false)
    },
    [onError],
  )

  // Use the unified issue creation handler
  const {handleCreate: handleSingleCreate} = useIssueCreationHandler({
    issueNode,
    onComplete: clearOnCreate,
    onError: handleError,
    issueFormRef,
    issueTemplate,
    formRepository,
  })

  // If this issue is part of a tree (root or otherwise),
  // save is handled in a confirmation/blocking dialog.
  if (issueNode?.parent || issueNode?.children.length) {
    return <BulkCreateButton issueNode={issueNode} disabled={noRepoAccess} onSuccess={clearOnCreate} />
  }

  // Don't render button if no repository is selected or if issues are disabled
  if (!formRepository || !formRepository.hasIssuesEnabled) {
    return null
  }

  if (templateRequired) {
    return (
      <>
        <CommandButton
          commandId="github:submit-form"
          variant="primary"
          data-testid="create-issue-dialog-button"
          ref={buttonRef}
          showKeybindingHint
          onClick={e => {
            e.preventDefault()
            setDialogOpen(true)
          }}
        >
          Create
        </CommandButton>
        {dialogOpen && (
          <Dialog
            title="Select a template"
            onClose={() => setDialogOpen(false)}
            footerButtons={[
              {
                buttonType: 'primary',
                content: 'OK',
                onClick: () => setDialogOpen(false),
              },
            ]}
            returnFocusRef={buttonRef}
          >
            This repository requires all issues to have a template. To create this issue, first select the appropriate
            template from the template dropdown.
          </Dialog>
        )}
      </>
    )
  }

  // Handle single creation with bulk job
  return (
    <>
      <CommandButton
        commandId="github:submit-form"
        variant="primary"
        data-testid="single-create-issue-button"
        ref={buttonRef}
        inactive={isSubmitting}
        showKeybindingHint
        onClick={e => {
          e.preventDefault()
          onClearError?.() // Reset error state on resubmission
          setIsSubmitting(true)
          handleSingleCreate()
        }}
      >
        Create
      </CommandButton>
    </>
  )
}

// eslint-disable-next-line no-restricted-imports
import {useChatState} from '@github-ui/copilot-chat/CopilotChatContext'
import {CreateIssueForm as InnerCreateIssueForm} from '@github-ui/issue-create/CreateIssueForm'
import {useIssueCreateDataContext} from '@github-ui/issue-create/IssueCreateDataContext'
import {instanceOfIssueFormData, type IssueCreatePayload} from '@github-ui/issue-create/Model'
import type {ChangeTrackingProps, IssueFormRef} from '@github-ui/issue-form/Types'
import {IssueViewerLoading} from '@github-ui/issue-viewer/IssueViewerLoading'
import {noop} from '@github-ui/noop'
import {IssueDraftIcon} from '@primer/octicons-react'
import {type RefObject, useCallback, useEffect, useState} from 'react'

import {userMessageIsTimelineEvent} from '../../timeline-events/TimelineEvents'
import type {DraftIssue} from '../content-preview-types'
import {FailureState} from '../FailureState'
import {RelationshipsSection} from './relationships-section/RelationshipsSection'
import {useDraftIssueAssignees} from './use-draft-issue-assignees'
import {useDraftIssueLabels} from './use-draft-issue-labels'
import {useDraftIssueMilestone} from './use-draft-issue-milestone'
import {useDraftIssueProjects} from './use-draft-issue-projects'
import type {TreeNode} from './use-draft-issue-tree-map'
import {useDraftIssueType} from './use-draft-issue-type'
import {useParentMessageRetryCallback} from './use-parent-message-retry-callback'

type CreateIssueFormProps = {
  draftIssue: DraftIssue
  issueNode?: TreeNode<DraftIssue>
  template?: IssueCreatePayload
  issueFormRef: RefObject<IssueFormRef | null>
  loadingDraftIssueRepo?: boolean
  onChange?: (issue: Partial<DraftIssue>) => void
  onClose?: () => void
  onBeforeCreate?: (issueBody: string) => Promise<string>
} & ChangeTrackingProps

export function CreateIssueForm({
  draftIssue,
  issueNode,
  template,
  issueFormRef,
  loadingDraftIssueRepo,
  onChange,
  onClose,
  onBeforeCreate,
  onFieldChange,
}: CreateIssueFormProps) {
  const {name: initTitle, body: initDescription = '', defaultValuesById} = draftIssue

  const {repository, clearSessionData} = useIssueCreateDataContext()
  const [title, setTitle] = useState(initTitle)
  const [description, setDescription] = useState(initDescription)

  const onRetry = useParentMessageRetryCallback()
  const state = useChatState()

  const onClearSession = useCallback(() => {
    // Copied from packages/issue-create/hooks/use-safe-close.ts
    clearSessionData()
    issueFormRef.current?.clearSessionStorage()
  }, [clearSessionData, issueFormRef])

  const onClearAndClose = useCallback(() => {
    onClearSession()
    onClose?.()
  }, [onClearSession, onClose])

  useEffect(() => {
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/set-state-in-effect
    setTitle(initTitle)
    setDescription(initDescription)
  }, [initDescription, initTitle])

  useDraftIssueAssignees(draftIssue)
  useDraftIssueLabels(draftIssue)
  useDraftIssueType(draftIssue)
  useDraftIssueMilestone(draftIssue)
  useDraftIssueProjects(draftIssue)

  if (repository == null) {
    if ((draftIssue.repository === undefined && draftIssue.isStreaming) || loadingDraftIssueRepo) {
      return <IssueViewerLoading optionConfig={{useViewportQueries: false}} />
    }
    return (
      <FailureState
        title="Repository required"
        description="Please select a repository above to continue creating your issue."
        icon={IssueDraftIcon}
      />
    )
  }

  // Render loading skeleton when switching templates and repos until Copilot responds with the updated data.
  // The old repo/template/form is no longer applicaple to the pending draft, and the user may not realize they're
  // waiting on Copilot if they don't see the timeline event
  const latestMessage = state.messages.at(-1)
  if (latestMessage && userMessageIsTimelineEvent(latestMessage, ['switch-issue-template', 'switch-issue-repo'])) {
    return <IssueViewerLoading optionConfig={{useViewportQueries: false}} />
  }

  // It's possible for Copilot to fail to return data in the right format, in which case we allow one retry.
  // We only want to do a retry if the following conditions are met:
  // - The template is a Form
  // - The message is from Copilot
  // - defaultValuesById is empty (Copilot should return Form field values)
  // - The message is complete (not still streaming)
  // - The message is the most recent message (don't retry old messages)
