  // - The parent message is not a Retry (limit to one retry)
  const message = state.messages.find(m => m.id === draftIssue.messageId)
  const parentMessage =
    message?.parentMessageIndex !== undefined ? state.messages[message.parentMessageIndex] : undefined
  if (message && parentMessage) {
    const isIssueForm = template && instanceOfIssueFormData(template.data)
    const isNoData = Object.keys(defaultValuesById || {}).length === 0
    const messageCompleted = !state.isWaitingOnCopilot
    const messageIsLastMessage = message.messageIndex === state.messages.length - 1
    const isCopilotMessage = message.role === 'assistant'
    const hasRetried = userMessageIsTimelineEvent(parentMessage, ['automatic-retry'])
    if (isIssueForm && isNoData) {
      if (!messageCompleted) {
        return <IssueViewerLoading optionConfig={{useViewportQueries: false}} />
      } else if (messageIsLastMessage && isCopilotMessage && !hasRetried) {
        void onRetry(
          message,
          'Copilot failed to use the issue template, retrying…',
          "Force a retry of the user's request and make sure to generate template form values properly. Do not send the same response as the previous attempt.",
        )
        return <IssueViewerLoading optionConfig={{useViewportQueries: false}} />
      } else {
        return (
          <FailureState
            title="Copilot failed to create an issue"
            description="Copilot failed to use the issue template. Please retry or choose a different template."
            icon={IssueDraftIcon}
          />
        )
      }
    }
  }

  return (
    <>
      <InnerCreateIssueForm
        repository={repository}
        title={title}
        body={description}
        setTitle={newTitle => {
          setTitle(newTitle)
          onChange?.({name: newTitle})
        }}
        setBody={newDescription => {
          setDescription(newDescription)
          onChange?.({body: newDescription})
        }}
        clearOnCreate={onClearAndClose}
        issueFormRef={issueFormRef}
        onCreateSuccess={noop}
        onCreateError={noop}
        onCancel={onClearAndClose}
        onBeforeCreate={onBeforeCreate}
        defaultValuesById={defaultValuesById}
        selectedTemplate={template}
        onFieldChange={onFieldChange}
      />
      {issueNode && <RelationshipsSection issueNode={issueNode} />}
    </>
  )
}

// eslint-disable-next-line no-restricted-imports
import {useChatState} from '@github-ui/copilot-chat/CopilotChatContext'
import {getSafeConfig} from '@github-ui/issue-create/getSafeConfig'
import {IssueCreateContextProvider} from '@github-ui/issue-create/IssueCreateContext'
import type {PreselectedDataTypes} from '@github-ui/issue-create/IssueCreateDataContext'
import type {IssueFormRef} from '@github-ui/issue-form/Types'
import {IssueViewerLoading} from '@github-ui/issue-viewer/IssueViewerLoading'
import type {RepositoryPickerRepository} from '@github-ui/item-picker/RepositoryPicker'
import {testIdProps} from '@github-ui/test-id-props'
import {IssueDraftIcon} from '@primer/octicons-react'
import {Banner, Stack} from '@primer/react'
import {AriaAlert} from '@primer/react/experimental'
import {clsx} from 'clsx'
import {useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState, useSyncExternalStore} from 'react'

import {BROWSER_ANIMATION_DURATION} from '../../../utils/constants'
import type {DraftIssue} from '../content-preview-types'
import {useContentPreview} from '../ContentPreviewContext'
import {FailureState} from '../FailureState'

import issuePreviewStyles from '../IssuePreview.module.css'

import {RepositoryPicker} from './RepositoryPicker'
import {TemplatePicker} from './TemplatePicker'

import {useOnChangeCallback} from './use-on-change-callback'
import {useOnFieldChangeCallback} from './use-on-field-change-callback'
import {useRepoQuery} from './use-repo-query'
import {useDraftIssueFormTemplateQuery} from './use-template-query'
import {useTopReposQuery} from './use-top-repos-query'

export const METADATA_FOOTER_BREAKPOINT = 1024

export type CreateIssuePreviewProps = {
  issue: DraftIssue
  isPreviewOpening: boolean
  onClose: () => void
}

export function CreateIssuePreview({isPreviewOpening, issue: draftIssue, onClose}: CreateIssuePreviewProps) {
  const {openItem} = useContentPreview()

  // We know the current node is a DraftIssue;
  // we're fetching it using the tag from our DraftIssue.
  const treeNodes = useDraftIssueTreeMap()
  const currentNode = treeNodes.get(draftIssue.tag) as TreeNode<DraftIssue> | undefined

  // There are rendering issues if we draw the form while animating the workbench opening, so we show loading until the animation is done.
  const [isAnimating, setIsAnimating] = useState(isPreviewOpening)
  useEffect(() => {
    if (isAnimating) {
      const timer = setTimeout(() => setIsAnimating(false), BROWSER_ANIMATION_DURATION)
      return () => clearTimeout(timer)
    }
  }, [isAnimating])

  // Error state for issue creation
  const [creationError, setCreationError] = useState<SingleIssueCreateError | null>(null)
  const errorBanner = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null)

  const {selectedThreadID} = useChatState()

  // Load repositories
  const {isLoading: loadingTopRepos, data: topRepos} = useTopReposQuery()
  const [owner, repo] = draftIssue.repository?.split('/') ?? []
  const {isLoading: loadingDraftIssueRepo, data: draftIssueRepo} = useRepoQuery({owner, repo})
  const [selectedRepo, setSelectedRepo] = useState(draftIssueRepo)

  useEffect(() => {
    if (draftIssueRepo) {
      // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/set-state-in-effect
      setSelectedRepo(draftIssueRepo)
    }
  }, [draftIssueRepo])

  const noRepoAccess = useMemo(() => {
    return (
      selectedRepo != null &&
      (!selectedRepo.hasIssuesEnabled ||
        (selectedRepo.viewerInteractionLimitReasonHTML != null &&
          selectedRepo.viewerInteractionLimitReasonHTML?.length > 0))
    )
  }, [selectedRepo])

  // Error callback to handle creation errors
  const onCreateError = useCallback((error: SingleIssueCreateError) => {
    setCreationError(error)
  }, [])

  // Clear error when form changes
  const clearError = useCallback(() => {
    setCreationError(null)
  }, [])

  // Focus error banner when error appears
  useEffect(() => {
    if (creationError && errorBanner?.current) {
      errorBanner.current.focus()
    }
  }, [creationError])

  const onChange = useOnChangeCallback(draftIssue)
  const onFieldChange = useOnFieldChangeCallback(draftIssue, onChange)

  const containerRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null)
  const renderNarrowView = useRenderNarrowView(containerRef)
  const shouldRenderSubIssueIndicator = currentNode?.parent != null

  const issueFormRef = useRef<IssueFormRef>(null)

  const {isLoading: loadingTemplate, data: templateData} = useDraftIssueFormTemplateQuery({
    repoId: draftIssueRepo?.id ?? '',
    filename: draftIssue.template || '',
  })

  const preselectedData: PreselectedDataTypes = {
    repository: draftIssueRepo ?? undefined,
  }

  if (isAnimating || loadingTopRepos || loadingTemplate) {
    return <IssueViewerLoading optionConfig={{useViewportQueries: false}} />
  }

  return (
    <IssueCreateContextProvider
      optionConfig={getSafeConfig({
        storageKeyPrefix: `${draftIssue.id}@${selectedThreadID!}`,
        insidePortal: renderNarrowView,
        disableCopilotAssignmentModal: true,
        isCopilotImmersive: true,
      })}
      preselectedData={preselectedData}
      {...testIdProps('create-issue-preview')}
    >
      <div className={clsx(issuePreviewStyles.container, styles.container)} ref={containerRef}>
        <div className={filePreviewStyles.toolbar}>
          <div className={clsx(filePreviewStyles.toolbarLeft, styles.toolbarLeft)}>
            <div>
              <VersionSelector item={draftIssue} />
            </div>
            {shouldRenderSubIssueIndicator && (
              <div>
                <ParentSelector node={currentNode} onItemSelected={node => openItem(node.item.id, true)} />
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
          <div className={filePreviewStyles.toolbarActions}>
            <CreateIssueButton
              issueTemplate={templateData}
              issueNode={currentNode}
              issueFormRef={issueFormRef}
              noRepoAccess={noRepoAccess}
              onClose={onClose}
              onError={onCreateError}
              onClearError={clearError}
            />
          </div>
        </div>
        <div className={styles.form}>
          <Stack direction={'vertical'} align={'start'} gap={'condensed'} className="py-2">
            <RepositoryPicker
              draftIssue={draftIssue}
              resolvedDraftIssueRepo={draftIssueRepo}
              loadingDraftIssueRepo={loadingDraftIssueRepo}
              topRepos={topRepos || []}
            />
            <TemplatePicker draftIssue={draftIssue} />
          </Stack>
          {creationError && (
            <Banner
              ref={errorBanner}
              hideTitle
              title={creationError.bannerTitle}
              description={<AriaAlert>{creationError.message}</AriaAlert>}
              variant={creationError.bannerVariant}
              role="alert"
              data-testid="create-issue-error-banner"
            />
          )}
          {noRepoAccess && renderBlankslate(draftIssueRepo!)}
          {!noRepoAccess && (
            <CreateIssueForm
              draftIssue={draftIssue}
              issueNode={currentNode}
              template={templateData ?? undefined}
              issueFormRef={issueFormRef}
              loadingDraftIssueRepo={loadingDraftIssueRepo}
              onChange={onChange}
              onClose={onClose}
              onFieldChange={onFieldChange}
            />
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    </IssueCreateContextProvider>
  )
}

function renderBlankslate(repository: RepositoryPickerRepository) {
  if (repository && !repository.hasIssuesEnabled) {
    return (
      <FailureState
        title="Issues are disabled"
        description="Issues are disabled on this repository. Please select a different repository above to continue creating your issue."
        icon={IssueDraftIcon}
      />
    )
  }

  if (repository && repository.viewerInteractionLimitReasonHTML) {
    return (
      <FailureState
        title="You cannot create issues in this repository"
        description={repository.viewerInteractionLimitReasonHTML}
        icon={IssueDraftIcon}
      />
    )
  }
}

function useRenderNarrowView<T extends HTMLElement>(elementRef: React.RefObject<T | null>) {
  const subscribe = (notify: () => void) => {
    const resizeObserver = new ResizeObserver(notify)
    resizeObserver.observe(document.documentElement)
    return () => {
      resizeObserver.unobserve(document.documentElement)
      resizeObserver.disconnect()
    }
  }

  const onChange = () => {
    if (!elementRef.current) return true

    return elementRef.current.clientWidth < METADATA_FOOTER_BREAKPOINT
  }

  return useSyncExternalStore(subscribe, onChange)
}

import {CommentBox} from '@github-ui/comment-box/CommentBox'
import type {Subject as CommentBoxSubject} from '@github-ui/comment-box/subject'
import {type AssigneePickerAssignee, AssigneeRepositoryPicker} from '@github-ui/item-picker-assignee-tsq'
import {LabelPicker, type LabelPickerLabel} from '@github-ui/item-picker-label-tsq'
import type {MilestonePickerMilestone} from '@github-ui/item-picker-milestone'
import {MilestonePicker} from '@github-ui/item-picker-milestone-tsq'
import {ProjectPicker} from '@github-ui/item-picker-project-tsq'
import {FileAddedIcon, MilestoneIcon, PeopleIcon, PersonIcon, ProjectIcon, TagIcon} from '@primer/octicons-react'
import {
  ActionList,
  ActionMenu,
  Button,
  Checkbox,
  Flash,
  FormControl,
  Select,
  SkeletonBox,
  TextInput,
} from '@primer/react'

import {
  buildExcludedSet,
  buildRepositoryOptions,
  buildSortedFilePaths,
  countSelectedFiles,
  CREATE_PR_DIALOG_IDS,
  CREATE_PR_DIALOG_STRINGS,
  type CreatePrDialogProps,
  isCreateDisabled,
  toggleExcludedFilePath,
} from './create-pr-dialog-util'

export type CreatePrDraft = CreatePrDialogProps['value']

type ReviewerMenuItem = {
  id: number
  type: 'user' | 'team'
  login: string
  name: string
  selected?: boolean
  disabled?: boolean
}

type FetchReviewersMenuResult = {
  ok: boolean
  items: ReviewerMenuItem[]
}

async function fetchReviewersMenu(args: {
  owner: string
  repo: string
  range: string
  query?: string
}): Promise<FetchReviewersMenuResult> {
  const {owner, repo, range, query} = args
  const url = new URL(`/${owner}/${repo}/pull/new/review-requests/${encodeURIComponent(range)}`, window.location.origin)
  url.searchParams.set('typeAhead', '1')
  url.searchParams.set('q', query ?? '')

  // ReviewRequestsController#menu requires an XHR request.
  const res = await fetch(url.href, {
    method: 'GET',
    credentials: 'same-origin',
    headers: {
      Accept: 'application/json',
      'X-Requested-With': 'XMLHttpRequest',
    },
  })

  if (!res.ok) return {ok: false, items: []}
  const json = (await res.json()) as {users?: ReviewerMenuItem[]}
  return {ok: true, items: Array.isArray(json.users) ? json.users : []}
}

function countLines(text: string): number {
  // JS split() returns [''] for empty strings; that matches the expected 1-line display.
  return text.split(/\r\n|\r|\n/).length
}

function formatBytes(bytes: number): string {
  if (!Number.isFinite(bytes) || bytes < 0) return ''
  if (bytes < 1024) return `${bytes}B`

  const kb = Math.max(1, Math.round(bytes / 1024))
  if (kb < 1024) return `${kb}KB`

  const mb = Math.max(1, Math.round(kb / 1024))
  return `${mb}MB`
}

function formatFileMeta(args: {content: string}): string {
  const {content} = args
  const lines = countLines(content)
  const bytes = new TextEncoder().encode(content).length
  const size = formatBytes(bytes)
  const lineText = `${lines} line${lines === 1 ? '' : 's'}`
  return size ? `${lineText} · ${size}` : lineText
}

export function CreatePrDialog({
  isOpen,
  repositories,
  availableFilePaths,
  value,
  onChange,
  onDismiss,
  onConfirm,
  isSubmitting,
  errorMessage,
  disabledFields,
}: CreatePrDialogProps) {
  const [reviewersOpen, setReviewersOpen] = useState(false)
  const [reviewersQuery, setReviewersQuery] = useState('')
  const [reviewersLoading, setReviewersLoading] = useState(false)
  const [reviewersItems, setReviewersItems] = useState<ReviewerMenuItem[]>([])
  const [reviewersError, setReviewersError] = useState<string | null>(null)

  const repoOptions = useMemo(
    () => buildRepositoryOptions(repositories, value.repositoryNwo),
    [repositories, value.repositoryNwo],
  )

  const [ownerLogin, repoName] = useMemo(() => {
    const [owner, repo] = value.repositoryNwo.split('/')
    return [owner ?? '', repo ?? '']
  }, [value.repositoryNwo])

  const reviewersRange = useMemo(() => {
    // The reviewers menu endpoint expects a compare range. We don't have a real head branch yet,
    // so use a stable, always-valid range based on the base branch.
    const base = value.baseBranch?.trim() || 'HEAD'
    return `${base}...${base}`
  }, [value.baseBranch])

  const openReviewers = useCallback(async () => {
    if (!ownerLogin || !repoName) return
    setReviewersError(null)
    setReviewersLoading(true)
    try {
      const result = await fetchReviewersMenu({owner: ownerLogin, repo: repoName, range: reviewersRange, query: ''})
      setReviewersItems(result.items)
      if (!result.ok) setReviewersError('Failed to load reviewers. Please try again.')
    } catch {
      setReviewersItems([])
      setReviewersError('Failed to load reviewers. Please try again.')
    } finally {
      setReviewersLoading(false)
    }
  }, [ownerLogin, repoName, reviewersRange])

  const searchReviewers = useCallback(
    async (nextQuery: string) => {
      if (!ownerLogin || !repoName) return
      setReviewersQuery(nextQuery)
      setReviewersError(null)
      setReviewersLoading(true)
      try {
        const result = await fetchReviewersMenu({
          owner: ownerLogin,
          repo: repoName,
          range: reviewersRange,
          query: nextQuery,
        })
        setReviewersItems(result.items)
        if (!result.ok) setReviewersError('Failed to load reviewers. Please try again.')
      } catch {
        setReviewersItems([])
        setReviewersError('Failed to load reviewers. Please try again.')
      } finally {
        setReviewersLoading(false)
      }
    },
    [ownerLogin, repoName, reviewersRange],
  )

  const toggleReviewer = useCallback(
    (item: ReviewerMenuItem) => {
      const nextUserIds = new Set(value.reviewerUserIds ?? [])
      const nextTeamIds = new Set(value.reviewerTeamIds ?? [])

      if (item.type === 'user') {
        if (nextUserIds.has(item.id)) nextUserIds.delete(item.id)
        else nextUserIds.add(item.id)
      } else {
        if (nextTeamIds.has(item.id)) nextTeamIds.delete(item.id)
        else nextTeamIds.add(item.id)
      }

      onChange({...value, reviewerUserIds: Array.from(nextUserIds), reviewerTeamIds: Array.from(nextTeamIds)})
    },
    [onChange, value],
  )

  const pullRequestBodyLoading = disabledFields?.pullRequestBody === true
  const pullRequestBodySubject = useMemo<CommentBoxSubject | undefined>(() => {
    if (typeof value.repositoryId !== 'number') return undefined

    return {
      type: 'pull_request',
      repository: {
        databaseId: value.repositoryId,
        nwo: value.repositoryNwo,
        slashCommandsEnabled: false,
      },
    }
  }, [value.repositoryId, value.repositoryNwo])

  const excludedSet = useMemo(() => buildExcludedSet(value.excludedFilePaths), [value.excludedFilePaths])
  const sortedFilePaths = useMemo(() => buildSortedFilePaths(availableFilePaths), [availableFilePaths])

  const selectedFileCount = useMemo(
    () => countSelectedFiles(sortedFilePaths, excludedSet),
    [sortedFilePaths, excludedSet],
  )

  const noFilesSelected = selectedFileCount === 0

  const createDisabled = isCreateDisabled({
    value,
    noFilesSelected,
    isSubmitting: isSubmitting === true,
  })

  const toggleExcluded = (path: string, nextChecked: boolean) => {
    onChange(toggleExcludedFilePath({value, path, nextChecked}))
  }

  if (!isOpen) return null

  return (
    <Dialog
      onClose={() => onDismiss()}
      aria-labelledby={CREATE_PR_DIALOG_IDS.dialogTitle}
      title={CREATE_PR_DIALOG_STRINGS.title}
      width="xlarge"
      renderFooter={() => (
        <div className="d-flex flex-justify-end gap-2 width-full tmp-pb-3 tmp-pr-3">
          <Button onClick={onDismiss} disabled={isSubmitting === true}>
            {CREATE_PR_DIALOG_STRINGS.cancelButton}
          </Button>
          <Button variant="primary" onClick={() => onConfirm(value)} disabled={createDisabled}>
            {CREATE_PR_DIALOG_STRINGS.createButton}
          </Button>
        </div>
      )}
    >
      {errorMessage ? (
        <div className="tmp-mt-3">
          <Flash variant="danger">{errorMessage}</Flash>
        </div>
      ) : null}
      <div className="d-flex flex-column flex-sm-row gap-3">
        <div className="flex-1">
          <FormControl id={CREATE_PR_DIALOG_IDS.repositorySelect} disabled={disabledFields?.repository === true}>
            <FormControl.Label>Repository</FormControl.Label>
            {disabledFields?.repository === true ? (
              <SkeletonBox height="32px" width="100%" />
            ) : (
              <Select
                block
                value={value.repositoryNwo}
                onChange={e => onChange({...value, repositoryNwo: e.target.value})}
              >
                {repoOptions.map(nwo => (
                  <Select.Option key={nwo} value={nwo}>
                    {nwo}
                  </Select.Option>
                ))}
              </Select>
            )}
          </FormControl>
        </div>

        <div className="flex-1">
          <FormControl id={CREATE_PR_DIALOG_IDS.branchInput} disabled={disabledFields?.branchName === true}>
            <FormControl.Label>Branch</FormControl.Label>
            {disabledFields?.branchName === true ? (
              <SkeletonBox height="32px" width="100%" />
            ) : (
              <TextInput
                block
                value={value.branchName}
                onChange={e => onChange({...value, branchName: e.target.value, branchNameWasUserProvided: true})}
              />
            )}
          </FormControl>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="tmp-mt-3">
        <FormControl id={CREATE_PR_DIALOG_IDS.commitMessageInput} disabled={disabledFields?.commitMessage === true}>
          <FormControl.Label>Commit message</FormControl.Label>
          {disabledFields?.commitMessage === true ? (
            <SkeletonBox height="32px" width="100%" />
          ) : (
            <TextInput
              block
              value={value.commitMessage}
              onChange={e => onChange({...value, commitMessage: e.target.value})}
            />
          )}
        </FormControl>
      </div>

      <div className="tmp-mt-3">
        <FormControl
          id={CREATE_PR_DIALOG_IDS.commitDescriptionInput}
          disabled={disabledFields?.commitDescription === true}
        >
          <FormControl.Label>Extended commit description</FormControl.Label>
          {disabledFields?.commitDescription === true ? (
            <SkeletonBox height="32px" width="100%" />
          ) : (
            <TextInput
              block
              value={value.commitDescription}
              onChange={e => onChange({...value, commitDescription: e.target.value})}
            />
          )}
        </FormControl>
      </div>

      <hr className="tmp-my-3 border-0 border-top borderColor-muted" />

      <div className="tmp-mt-3">
        <FormControl
          id={CREATE_PR_DIALOG_IDS.pullRequestTitleInput}
          disabled={disabledFields?.pullRequestTitle === true}
        >
          <FormControl.Label>Pull request title</FormControl.Label>
          {disabledFields?.pullRequestTitle === true ? (
            <SkeletonBox height="32px" width="100%" />
          ) : (
            <TextInput
              block
              value={value.pullRequestTitle}
              onChange={e => onChange({...value, pullRequestTitle: e.target.value})}
            />
          )}
        </FormControl>
      </div>

      <div className="tmp-mt-3">
        <CommentBox
          label="Pull request description"
          aria-label="Pull request description"
          showLabel={false}
          subject={pullRequestBodySubject}
          value={value.pullRequestBody}
          placeholder="Add a description"
          onChange={next => onChange({...value, pullRequestBody: next})}
          generatingText={pullRequestBodyLoading}
        />
      </div>

      <div className="mt-2 d-flex flex-wrap gap-2">
        <ActionMenu
          open={reviewersOpen}
          onOpenChange={open => {
            setReviewersOpen(open)
            if (open) void openReviewers()
          }}
        >
          <ActionMenu.Anchor>
            <Button leadingVisual={PeopleIcon} disabled={!ownerLogin || !repoName}>
              Reviewers
            </Button>
          </ActionMenu.Anchor>
          <ActionMenu.Overlay width="medium">
            <div className="p-2 border-bottom borderColor-muted">
              <TextInput
                block
                placeholder="Search reviewers"
                value={reviewersQuery}
                onChange={e => {
                  void searchReviewers(e.target.value)
                }}
              />
            </div>

            {reviewersError ? (
              <div className="p-2">
                <Flash variant="danger">{reviewersError}</Flash>
              </div>
            ) : null}

            {reviewersLoading ? (
              <div className="p-2">
                <SkeletonBox height="24px" width="100%" />
              </div>
            ) : (
              <ActionList>
                {reviewersItems.map(item => {
                  const isSelected =
                    item.type === 'user'
                      ? (value.reviewerUserIds ?? []).includes(item.id)
                      : (value.reviewerTeamIds ?? []).includes(item.id)

                  return (
                    <ActionList.Item
                      key={`${item.type}:${item.id}`}
                      disabled={item.disabled === true}
                      selected={isSelected}
                      onSelect={() => toggleReviewer(item)}
                    >
                      {item.login}
                    </ActionList.Item>
                  )
                })}
                {reviewersItems.length === 0 ? <ActionList.Item disabled>No reviewers found</ActionList.Item> : null}
              </ActionList>
            )}
          </ActionMenu.Overlay>
        </ActionMenu>

        <AssigneeRepositoryPicker
          owner={ownerLogin}
          repo={repoName}
          assigneeTokens={value.assigneeLogins ?? []}
          includeAssignableBots
          includeAuthorableBots={false}
          viewer={null}
          onSelectionChange={(assignees: AssigneePickerAssignee[]) => {
            onChange({...value, assigneeLogins: assignees.map(a => a.login)})
          }}
          anchorElement={(anchorProps: HTMLAttributes<HTMLElement>, ref?: RefObject<HTMLButtonElement | null>) => (
            <Button ref={ref} leadingVisual={PersonIcon} {...anchorProps}>
              Assignee
            </Button>
          )}
        />

        <LabelPicker
          owner={ownerLogin}
          repo={repoName}
          labels={[]}
          canCreateLabel={false}
          labelNames={value.labelNames ?? []}
          selectionVariant="multiple"
          onSelectionChange={(labels: LabelPickerLabel[]) => {
            onChange({...value, labelNames: labels.map(l => l.name)})
          }}
          anchorElement={(anchorProps: HTMLAttributes<HTMLElement>, ref?: RefObject<HTMLButtonElement | null>) => (
            <Button ref={ref} leadingVisual={TagIcon} {...anchorProps}>
              Label
            </Button>
          )}
        />

        <ProjectPicker
          pickerId="copilot-create-pr-project-picker"
          owner={ownerLogin}
          repo={repoName}
          selectedProjects={(value.projectIds ?? []).map(id => ({
            id,
            title: '',
            closed: false,
            number: 0,
            url: '',
            viewerCanUpdate: true,
            hasReachedItemsLimit: false,
          }))}
          onSave={(projects: Array<{id: string}>) => {
            onChange({...value, projectIds: projects.map(p => p.id)})
          }}
          anchorElement={(anchorProps: HTMLAttributes<HTMLElement>) => (
            <Button leadingVisual={ProjectIcon} {...anchorProps}>
              Project
            </Button>
          )}
          title="Projects"
        />

        <MilestonePicker
          owner={ownerLogin}
          repo={repoName}
          canCreateMilestone={false}
          activeMilestone={
            value.milestoneTitle
              ? ({
                  id: '',
                  title: value.milestoneTitle,
                  closed: false,
                  closedAt: null,
                  dueOn: null,
                  progressPercentage: 0,
                  url: '',
                } satisfies MilestonePickerMilestone)
              : null
          }
          onSelectionChange={(milestones: Array<{title: string}>) => {
            const picked = milestones[0]
            onChange({...value, milestoneTitle: picked?.title})
          }}
          selectionVariant="single"
          anchorElement={(anchorProps: HTMLAttributes<HTMLElement>, ref?: RefObject<HTMLButtonElement | null>) => (
            <Button ref={ref} leadingVisual={MilestoneIcon} {...anchorProps}>
              Milestone
            </Button>
          )}
        />
      </div>

      {availableFilePaths ? (
        <>
          <hr className="tmp-my-3 border-0 border-top borderColor-muted" />
          <fieldset
            className="tmp-mt-3"
            aria-describedby={noFilesSelected ? CREATE_PR_DIALOG_IDS.filesToIncludeError : undefined}
          >
            <legend className="sr-only">{CREATE_PR_DIALOG_STRINGS.filesToIncludeLegend}</legend>
            <div className="mt-2" id={CREATE_PR_DIALOG_IDS.filesToIncludeFieldset}>
              {sortedFilePaths.map((path, index) => {
                const checked = !excludedSet.has(path)
                const fileContent = value.files?.[path]
                const fileMeta = fileContent ? formatFileMeta({content: fileContent}) : null
                const showRowDivider = index !== sortedFilePaths.length - 1

                const rowClassName = [
                  'd-flex flex-items-center gap-2 px-2 py-2',
                  showRowDivider ? 'border-bottom borderColor-muted' : null,
                ]
                  .filter(Boolean)
                  .join(' ')

                return (
                  <div key={path} className={rowClassName}>
                    <Checkbox
                      checked={checked}
                      onChange={e => toggleExcluded(path, e.target.checked)}
                      aria-label={path}
                    />
                    <FileAddedIcon className="fgColor-muted flex-shrink-0" />
                    <div className="d-flex flex-wrap flex-items-baseline min-width-0">
                      <span className="text-semibold wb-break-word">{path}</span>
                      {fileMeta ? (
                        <span className="text-small fgColor-muted ml-2">{fileMeta}</span>
                      ) : (
                        <span className="text-small fgColor-muted ml-2">—</span>
                      )}
                    </div>
                  </div>
                )
              })}
            </div>

            {noFilesSelected ? (
              <div className="tmp-mt-3" id={CREATE_PR_DIALOG_IDS.filesToIncludeError}>
                <Flash variant="danger">{CREATE_PR_DIALOG_STRINGS.filesToIncludeEmptyError}</Flash>
              </div>
            ) : null}
          </fieldset>
        </>
      ) : null}
    </Dialog>
  )
}

function DiceIcon() {
  return (
    <svg aria-hidden="true" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="16" height="16" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 16 16">
      <path
        fill="currentColor"
        fillRule="evenodd"
        d="M13 .5H3A2.5 2.5 0 0 0 .5 3v10A2.5 2.5 0 0 0 3 15.5h10a2.5 2.5 0 0 0 2.5-2.5V3A2.5 2.5 0 0 0 13 .5M2 3a1 1 0 0 1 1-1h10a1 1 0 0 1 1 1v10a1 1 0 0 1-1 1H3a1 1 0 0 1-1-1zm6 6.25a1.25 1.25 0 1 0 0-2.5 1.25 1.25 0 0 0 0 2.5M12.25 5a1.25 1.25 0 1 1-2.5 0 1.25 1.25 0 0 1 2.5 0M5 12.25a1.25 1.25 0 1 0 0-2.5 1.25 1.25 0 0 0 0 2.5"
        clipRule="evenodd"
      />
    </svg>
  )
}

export default DiceIcon

import {CopyToClipboardButton} from '@github-ui/copy-to-clipboard/Button'
import {sendEvent} from '@github-ui/hydro-analytics'
import {DownloadIcon, LinkExternalIcon} from '@primer/octicons-react'
import {IconButton} from '@primer/react'
import {useState} from 'react'

import {useHydrateDiffFile} from '../../hooks/use-hydrate-diff-file'
import {useFileInfo} from '../../utils/content-preview-utils'
import type {FileDiff} from './content-preview-types'
import {useContentPreview} from './ContentPreviewContext'
import styles from './DiffPreview.module.css'
import {DiffView} from './DiffView'
import {DiffViewSettings} from './DiffViewSettings'
import {downloadFile} from './download-file'

interface DiffPreviewProps {
  fileDiff: FileDiff
}

export function DiffPreview({fileDiff}: DiffPreviewProps) {
  const {updateItem} = useContentPreview()

  const baseFile = fileDiff.baseFile
  const headFile = fileDiff.headFile
  const [isUnifiedDiff, setIsUnifiedDiff] = useState(true)

  const isDiffable = baseFile.value && headFile.value
  const isNewFile = !baseFile.value && headFile.value
  const isDeletedFile = baseFile.value && !headFile.value

  const {linesOfCode, size} = useFileInfo(isNewFile || isDiffable ? headFile : baseFile)
  const lineLabel = `${isNewFile ? 'Added: ' : isDeletedFile ? 'Deleted: ' : ''} ${linesOfCode} `

  useHydrateDiffFile(fileDiff, updateItem, 'base')
  useHydrateDiffFile(fileDiff, updateItem, 'head')

  return (
    <div className={styles.container}>
      <div className={styles.toolbar}>
        <div className={styles.toolbarLeft}>
          <p className={styles.toolbarLoC}>
            {lineLabel} {linesOfCode === 1 ? 'line' : 'lines'} · {size}
          </p>
        </div>
        <div className={styles.toolbarActions}>
          {baseFile.reference && 'url' in baseFile.reference && (
            <IconButton
              as="a"
              aria-label="Open file in a new tab"
              icon={LinkExternalIcon}
              variant="invisible"
              href={baseFile.reference.url}
              target="_blank"
              rel="noopener noreferrer"
            />
          )}
          {/* Both copy and download return the head file */}
          <CopyToClipboardButton
            textToCopy={headFile.value}
            ariaLabel={'Copy code'}
            onCopy={() => sendEvent('dotcom_chat.activate', {target: 'BROWSER_FILE_COPY', mode: 'immersive'})}
          />
          <IconButton
            aria-label="Download code"
            icon={DownloadIcon}
            variant="invisible"
            onClick={() => {
              const fileName = headFile.name
              downloadFile(new File([headFile.value], fileName))
              sendEvent('dotcom_chat.activate', {target: 'BROWSER_FILE_DOWNLOAD', mode: 'immersive'})
            }}
          />
          {isDiffable && <DiffViewSettings setIsUnifiedDiff={setIsUnifiedDiff} isUnifiedDiff={isUnifiedDiff} />}
        </div>
      </div>
      <DiffView left={baseFile} right={headFile} view={isUnifiedDiff ? 'unified' : 'split'} />
    </div>
  )
}

import {CodeMirrorDiff} from '@github-ui/code-mirror/CodeMirrorDiff'
import {useId} from 'react'

import {useGuessCodeMirrorSettings} from '../../hooks/use-code-mirror-config'
import type {File} from './content-preview-types'

export function DiffView({left, right, view}: {left: File; right: File; view: 'split' | 'unified'}) {
  const leftLabelId = useId()
  const rightLabelId = useId()
  const unifiedLabelId = useId()
  const codeMirrorOptions = useGuessCodeMirrorSettings(left)

  return (
    <div>
      <span id={leftLabelId} className="sr-only">
        {left.name} original file contents
      </span>
      <span id={rightLabelId} className="sr-only">
        {right.name} modified file contents
      </span>
      <span id={unifiedLabelId} className="sr-only">
        {left.name} and {right.name} unified diff view
      </span>

      <CodeMirrorDiff
        fileName={left.name}
        left={left.value}
        leftLabelledBy={leftLabelId}
        right={right.value}
        rightLabelledBy={rightLabelId}
        spacing={codeMirrorOptions}
        unifiedLabelledBy={unifiedLabelId}
        view={view}
      />
    </div>
  )
}

import {GearIcon} from '@primer/octicons-react'
import {ActionList, ActionMenu, IconButton} from '@primer/react'

interface DiffViewSettingsProps {
  isUnifiedDiff: boolean
  setIsUnifiedDiff: (isUnified: boolean) => void
}

export function DiffViewSettings({isUnifiedDiff, setIsUnifiedDiff}: DiffViewSettingsProps) {
  return (
    <ActionMenu>
      <ActionMenu.Anchor>
        <IconButton icon={GearIcon} aria-label="Open diff view settings" variant="invisible" />
      </ActionMenu.Anchor>
      <ActionMenu.Overlay>
        <ActionList>
          <ActionList.Group selectionVariant="single">
            <ActionList.GroupHeading>Layout</ActionList.GroupHeading>
            <ActionList.Item selected={isUnifiedDiff} onSelect={() => setIsUnifiedDiff(true)}>
              Unified
            </ActionList.Item>
            <ActionList.Item selected={!isUnifiedDiff} onSelect={() => setIsUnifiedDiff(false)}>
              Split
            </ActionList.Item>
          </ActionList.Group>
        </ActionList>
      </ActionMenu.Overlay>
    </ActionMenu>
  )
}

export const downloadFile = (file: File) => {
  const url = URL.createObjectURL(file)
  const a = document.createElement('a')
  a.href = url
  a.download = file.name || 'file'
  a.click()
  URL.revokeObjectURL(url)
}

// eslint-disable-next-line no-restricted-imports
import {useChatState} from '@github-ui/copilot-chat/utils/CopilotChatContext'
import {useChatManager} from '@github-ui/copilot-chat/utils/CopilotChatManagerContext'
import {getSelectedThread} from '@github-ui/copilot-chat/utils/get-selected-thread'
import {sendEvent} from '@github-ui/hydro-analytics'
import {useAppPayload} from '@github-ui/react-core/use-app-payload'
import {useLocation} from '@github-ui/react-router'
import {BrowserIcon} from '@primer/octicons-react'
import {Button} from '@primer/react'
import {useEffect, useRef, useState} from 'react'

import type {CopilotImmersivePayload, Icebreaker} from '../../routes/payloads'
import DiceIcon from '../Icons/DiceIcon'
import styles from './EmptyState.module.css'

interface EmptyStateProps {
  isOpening: boolean
}

type IcebreakerType = 'functional' | 'instructional' | 'interactional'

interface IcebreakerData {
  type: IcebreakerType
  data: Icebreaker[]
}

function isIcebreakerDataArray(value: unknown): value is IcebreakerData[] {
  return Array.isArray(value) && value.every(item => 'type' in item && 'data' in item)
}

function getRandomIcebreaker(icebreakers: Icebreaker[]): Icebreaker {
  const randomIndex = Math.floor(Math.random() * icebreakers.length)
  const randomIcebreaker = icebreakers[randomIndex]
  if (!randomIcebreaker) {
    throw new Error('No icebreaker found')
  }
  return randomIcebreaker
}

export function EmptyState({isOpening}: EmptyStateProps) {
  const manager = useChatManager()
  const state = useChatState()
  const location = useLocation()
  const buttonRef = useRef<HTMLButtonElement>(null)

  const currentThread = getSelectedThread(state)
  const payload = useAppPayload<CopilotImmersivePayload>()

  const [interactionalSuggestions, setInteractionalSuggestions] = useState<Icebreaker[]>([])

  useEffect(() => {
    if (isIcebreakerDataArray(payload.icebreakers)) {
      const icebreakers = payload.icebreakers.find(ib => ib.type === 'interactional')
      if (icebreakers && icebreakers.data.length > 0) {
        // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/set-state-in-effect
        setInteractionalSuggestions(icebreakers.data)
      }
    }
  }, [payload.icebreakers, location])

  // Focus the button when isOpening becomes true
  useEffect(() => {
    if (isOpening && buttonRef.current) {
      // Use requestAnimationFrame to ensure focus happens after render
      window.requestAnimationFrame(() => {
        buttonRef.current?.focus({preventScroll: true})
      })
    }
  }, [isOpening])

  const handleButtonClick = () => {
    if (interactionalSuggestions.length === 0) return

    const randomSuggestion = getRandomIcebreaker(interactionalSuggestions)
    const thread = state.messages.length === 0 ? currentThread : getSelectedThread(state)
    const currentUserLogin = state.currentUserLogin
    const message = randomSuggestion.message

    void manager.sendChatMessage({
      thread,
      content: message.replaceAll('$$USERNAME$$', currentUserLogin),
      references: state.currentReferences,
      topic: state.currentTopic,
      context: state.context,
      customInstructions: state.customInstructions,
      model: state.model,
    })

    sendEvent('dotcom_chat.activate', {
      target: 'CONTENT_PREVIEW_EMPTY_STATE_ICEBREAKER_BUTTON',
      topic: state.currentTopic?.name,
      mode: 'immersive',
      suggestionId: randomSuggestion.id,
    })
  }

  return (
    <div className={styles.container}>
      <BrowserIcon size={24} className={styles.icon} />
      <h1 className="sr-only">Content preview panel</h1>
      <h2 className={styles.heading}>View files directly within Copilot</h2>
      <p className={styles.subtitle}>Ask to generate a file or app, or use the button below to try it yourself.</p>
      <Button ref={buttonRef} leadingVisual={DiceIcon} onClick={handleButtonClick} disabled={state.isWaitingOnCopilot}>
        I’m feeling lucky
      </Button>
    </div>
  )
}

import {IssueViewer} from '@github-ui/issue-viewer/IssueViewer'
import {IssueViewerContextProvider} from '@github-ui/issue-viewer/IssueViewerContextProvider'
import {IssueViewerLoading} from '@github-ui/issue-viewer/IssueViewerLoading'
import type {OptionConfig} from '@github-ui/issue-viewer/OptionConfig'
import type {ItemIdentifier} from '@github-ui/issue-viewer/Types'
import {noop} from '@github-ui/noop'
import {LinkExternalIcon} from '@primer/octicons-react'
import {IconButton} from '@primer/react'
import {clsx} from 'clsx'
import {useEffect, useMemo, useState} from 'react'

import {BROWSER_ANIMATION_DURATION} from '../../../utils/constants'
import type {ExistingIssue} from '../content-preview-types'
import {useContentPreview} from '../ContentPreviewContext'
import filePreviewStyles from '../FilePreview.module.css'
import issuePreviewStyles from '../IssuePreview.module.css'
import {BulkCreateButton} from './BulkCreateButton'
import createIssueStyles from './CreateIssuePreview.module.css'
import styles from './ExistingIssuePreview.module.css'
import {ParentSelector} from './ParentSelector'
import {RelationshipsSection} from './relationships-section/RelationshipsSection'
import {useDraftIssueTreeMap} from './use-draft-issue-tree-map'

export function ExistingIssuePreview({
  isPreviewOpening,
  issue,
  onClose,
}: {
  isPreviewOpening: boolean
  issue: ExistingIssue
  onClose: () => void
}) {
  const [showLoadingState, setShowLoadingState] = useState(isPreviewOpening)

  const {openItem} = useContentPreview()
  const currentNode = useDraftIssueTreeMap().get(issue.tag)!

  // issue viewer causes lag if we try to render it while animating, so we show a loading until the animation is done.
  useEffect(() => {
    if (showLoadingState) {
      const timer = setTimeout(() => setShowLoadingState(false), BROWSER_ANIMATION_DURATION)
      return () => clearTimeout(timer)
    }
  }, [showLoadingState])

  const openIssueInNewTabButton = useMemo(() => {
    return (
      <IconButton
        icon={LinkExternalIcon}
        variant="invisible"
        aria-label="Open issue in new tab"
        onClick={() => {
          window.open(issue.href, '_blank')
        }}
      />
    )
  }, [issue.href])

  const optionConfig: OptionConfig = useMemo(
    () => ({
      // We don't want the user to manage the issue from here, regardless of permissions. The intent is to
      // provide a preview of the existing issue as part of the draft issue graph. Any edits that happen in
      // this preview would be outside the context of the draft issue flow, causing confusion.
      isReadonly: true,
      // We also hide the timeline, including comments, to eliminate lengthy scrolling between issue body and metadata.
      hideTimeline: true,
      navigate: noop,
      insideSidePanel: true,
      shouldSkipSetDocumentTitle: true,
      useViewportQueries: false,
      onIssueDelete: onClose,
      onRemovedFromProject: noop,
      onIssueUpdate: noop,
      additionalHeaderActions: openIssueInNewTabButton,
      showRepositoryPill: true,
    }),
    [onClose, openIssueInNewTabButton],
  )

  const itemIdentifier: ItemIdentifier | undefined = useMemo(() => {
    const [owner, repo] = issue.repository.split('/')
    if (!owner || !repo) return

    return {
      owner,
      repo,
      number: issue.number,
      type: 'Issue',
    }
  }, [issue.number, issue.repository])

  if (showLoadingState || !itemIdentifier) {
    return <IssueViewerLoading optionConfig={optionConfig} />
  }

  return (
    <div className={clsx(issuePreviewStyles.container, createIssueStyles.container)}>
      <div className={filePreviewStyles.toolbar}>
        <div className={clsx(filePreviewStyles.toolbarLeft, createIssueStyles.toolbarLeft)}>
          {currentNode.parent && (
            <ParentSelector node={currentNode} onItemSelected={node => openItem(node.item.id, true)} />
          )}
        </div>
        <div className={filePreviewStyles.toolbarActions}>
          <BulkCreateButton issueNode={currentNode} onSuccess={onClose} />
        </div>
      </div>
      <div className={clsx(createIssueStyles.viewerContainer, styles.viewerContainer)}>
        <IssueViewerContextProvider>
          <IssueViewer itemIdentifier={itemIdentifier} optionConfig={optionConfig} />
        </IssueViewerContextProvider>
        <div className={styles.relationshipsContainer}>
          <RelationshipsSection issueNode={currentNode} />
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

import type {Icon} from '@primer/octicons-react'

import styles from './FailureState.module.css'

interface FailureStateProps {

    </div>
  )
}

import CodeMirror from '@github-ui/code-mirror'
import type {SelectionChange} from '@github-ui/code-mirror/extensions/selection-listener'
// eslint-disable-next-line no-restricted-imports
import {useChatState} from '@github-ui/copilot-chat/CopilotChatContext'
// We now interact with the chat manager directly for selection attachments (no event indirection)
import type {ThreadScopedSnippetReference} from '@github-ui/copilot-chat/utils/copilot-chat-types'
import {NullMessageId} from '@github-ui/copilot-chat/utils/copilot-chat-types'
import {copilotFeatureFlags} from '@github-ui/copilot-chat/utils/copilot-feature-flags'
import {CopilotTextAttacher} from '@github-ui/copilot-chat/utils/copilot-text-attacher'
import {useChatManager} from '@github-ui/copilot-chat/utils/CopilotChatManagerContext'
import {CopyToClipboardButton} from '@github-ui/copy-to-clipboard/Button'
import {sendEvent} from '@github-ui/hydro-analytics'
import {useStableCallback} from '@github-ui/use-stable-callback'
import {CodeIcon, DownloadIcon, EyeIcon, FileDiffIcon, type IconProps, LinkExternalIcon} from '@primer/octicons-react'
import {IconButton, Link, SegmentedControl} from '@primer/react'
import debounce from 'lodash-es/debounce'
import {startTransition, useCallback, useEffect, useId, useMemo, useRef, useState} from 'react'

import {useGuessCodeMirrorSettings} from '../../hooks/use-code-mirror-config'
import {formatFilePath, parseGitHubUrl, useFileInfo} from '../../utils/content-preview-utils'
import {useHydrateReference} from '../../utils/use-hydrate-reference'
import {
  type File,
  getPreviousVersion,
  type PreviewableContent,
  stripVersionFromId,
  type VersionedItemsMap,
} from './content-preview-types'
import {useContentPreview} from './ContentPreviewContext'
import {DiffView} from './DiffView'
import {DiffViewSettings} from './DiffViewSettings'
import {downloadFile} from './download-file'
import styles from './FilePreview.module.css'
import {HtmlPreview} from './HtmlPreview'
import {MarkdownPreview} from './MarkdownPreview'
import {VersionSelector} from './VersionSelector'

interface FilePreviewProps {
  isPreviewOpening: boolean
  file: File
  onClose: () => void
}

type ViewMode = 'code' | 'preview' | 'diff'
const iconMap: Record<ViewMode, React.FunctionComponent<IconProps>> = {
  code: CodeIcon,
  preview: EyeIcon,
  diff: FileDiffIcon,
}

export function FilePreview({isPreviewOpening, file, onClose}: FilePreviewProps) {
  const {updateItem, items, versionedItems} = useContentPreview()
  const state = useChatState()
  const fileMessage = file.isStreaming
    ? state.messages[state.messages.length - 1]
    : state.messages.find(message => message.id === file.messageId)
  const {linesOfCode, isPreviewable, size} = useFileInfo(file)
  useHydrateReference(file, updateItem)

  const keepMounted = copilotFeatureFlags.filePreviewKeepMounted
  const [viewMode, setViewMode] = useState<ViewMode>('code')
  const [mountedViews, setMountedViews] = useState<ReadonlySet<ViewMode>>(() => new Set<ViewMode>(['code']))
  const [isUnifiedDiff, setIsUnifiedDiff] = useState(true)
  const [selectedVersion, setSelectedVersion] = useState<string | undefined>(undefined)

  const handleVersionChange = useCallback((versionLabel: string) => {
    setSelectedVersion(versionLabel)
  }, [])

  const previousVersion = getPreviousVersion<File>(file, items, versionedItems)
  const isDiffable = !!previousVersion

  useEffect(() => {
    if (fileMessage?.error?.type === 'publicCode' || fileMessage?.error?.type === 'filtered') {
      onClose()
    }
  }, [fileMessage?.error, onClose])

  const availableModes: Array<[ViewMode, string]> = [['code', 'Code']]
  let effectiveViewMode: ViewMode = 'code'
  if (isPreviewable) {
    availableModes.unshift(['preview', 'Preview'])
    effectiveViewMode = viewMode === 'preview' ? 'preview' : effectiveViewMode
  }
  if (isDiffable) {
    availableModes.push(['diff', 'Diff'])
    effectiveViewMode = viewMode === 'diff' ? 'diff' : effectiveViewMode
  }

  return (
    <div className={styles.container}>
      <div
        className={`${styles.toolbar}${
          copilotFeatureFlags.copilotWorkbenchSlimLineTopTabs ? ` ${styles.slimLineTopTabs}` : ''
        }`}
      >
        <div className={styles.toolbarLeft}>
          {(isPreviewable || isDiffable) && (
            <SegmentedControl
              aria-label="File view"
              size="small"
              onChange={i => {
                const newViewMode = availableModes[i]?.[0] || 'code'
                sendEvent('dotcom_chat.activate', {target: 'BROWSER_FILE_VIEW_OPTION', option: newViewMode})
                if (keepMounted) {
                  startTransition(() => {
                    setViewMode(newViewMode)
                    setMountedViews(prev => {
                      if (prev.has(newViewMode)) return prev
                      const next = new Set(prev)
                      next.add(newViewMode)
                      return next
                    })
                  })
                } else {
                  setViewMode(newViewMode)
                }
              }}
            >
              {availableModes.map(([mode, label]) => {
                const IconComponent = iconMap[mode]

                return (
                  <SegmentedControl.IconButton
                    key={mode}
                    aria-label={label}
                    icon={<IconComponent size="small" />}
                    selected={viewMode === mode}
                  />
                )
              })}
            </SegmentedControl>
          )}
          <VersionSelector item={file} onVersionSelect={handleVersionChange} />
          <FileLink file={file} />
          {(!file.reference || !('url' in file.reference)) && (
            <p className={styles.toolbarLoC}>
              {linesOfCode} {linesOfCode === 1 ? 'line' : 'lines'} · {size}
            </p>
          )}
        </div>
        <div className={styles.toolbarActions}>
          {file.reference && 'url' in file.reference && (
            <IconButton
              as="a"
              type={undefined} // Removes type: 'button' as it's invalid on anchor elements
              aria-label="Open file in a new tab"
              icon={LinkExternalIcon}
              variant="invisible"
              href={file?.reference?.url}
              target="_blank"
              rel="noopener noreferrer"
              size="small"
            />
          )}
          <CopyToClipboardButton
            textToCopy={file.value}
            ariaLabel={'Copy code'}
            onCopy={() => sendEvent('dotcom_chat.activate', {target: 'BROWSER_FILE_COPY', mode: 'immersive'})}
            size="small"
          />
          <IconButton
            aria-label="Download code"
            icon={DownloadIcon}
            variant="invisible"
            onClick={() => {
              const fileName = getFileName(file, selectedVersion)
              downloadFile(new File([file.value], fileName))
              sendEvent('dotcom_chat.activate', {target: 'BROWSER_FILE_DOWNLOAD', mode: 'immersive'})
            }}
            size="small"
          />
          {effectiveViewMode === 'diff' && (
            <DiffViewSettings isUnifiedDiff={isUnifiedDiff} setIsUnifiedDiff={setIsUnifiedDiff} />
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
      <div className={styles.content}>
        {keepMounted ? (
          <>
            {isPreviewable && mountedViews.has('preview') && (
              <div style={{display: effectiveViewMode === 'preview' ? 'contents' : 'none'}}>
                <PreviewComponent file={file} />
              </div>
            )}
            {previousVersion && mountedViews.has('diff') && (
              <div style={{display: effectiveViewMode === 'diff' ? 'contents' : 'none'}}>
                <DiffView left={previousVersion} right={file} view={isUnifiedDiff ? 'unified' : 'split'} />
              </div>
            )}
            <div style={{display: effectiveViewMode === 'code' ? 'contents' : 'none'}}>
              <CodeMirrorView file={file} isPreviewOpening={isPreviewOpening} />
            </div>
          </>
        ) : effectiveViewMode === 'preview' ? (
          <PreviewComponent file={file} />
        ) : effectiveViewMode === 'diff' && previousVersion ? (
          <DiffView left={previousVersion} right={file} view={isUnifiedDiff ? 'unified' : 'split'} />
        ) : (
          <CodeMirrorView file={file} isPreviewOpening={isPreviewOpening} />
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

function getFileName(file: File, selectedVersion: string | undefined): string {
  const extensionIndex = file.name.lastIndexOf('.')
  if (extensionIndex > 0) {
    const baseName = file.name.substring(0, extensionIndex)
    const extension = file.name.substring(extensionIndex)
    return selectedVersion !== undefined ? `${baseName}_${selectedVersion}${extension}` : file.name
  }
  return selectedVersion !== undefined ? `${file.name}_${selectedVersion}` : file.name
}

const saveChanges = debounce(
  (file: File, fileValue: string, updateItem: (file: File) => void) => {
    const updatedFile = {...file, value: fileValue}
    updateItem(updatedFile)
  },
  500,
  {leading: false, trailing: true},
)

function CodeMirrorView({file, isPreviewOpening}: {file: File; isPreviewOpening: boolean}) {
  const {openItem, updateItem, versionedItems} = useContentPreview()
  const manager = useChatManager()
  const focusNextRenderRef = useRef<boolean>(false)
  const isLatest = isLatestVersion(versionedItems, file)
  const isEditableFileType = !file.reference || file.reference.type === 'thread-scoped-file'
  const isEditable = isEditableFileType && !file.isStreaming && isLatest
  const labelId = useId()
  const codeMirrorOptions = useGuessCodeMirrorSettings(file)
  const [editedValue, setEditedValue] = useState<{value: string; id: string}>()
  const fileValue = editedValue?.id === file.id ? editedValue.value : file.value
  // Force loading state on first render
  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(true)
  const {messages, currentReferences} = useChatState()

  // Track last attached selection signature and reference to support replace/remove semantics
  const lastSelectionSigRef = useRef<string>('')
  const lastSelectionRef = useRef<ThreadScopedSnippetReference | null>(null)

  // Build selection handlers and debounce for CodeMirror
  const [selectionAttachmentRange, setSelectionAttachmentRange] = useState<
    {from: number; to: number} | null | undefined
  >(undefined)

  // Clear visual selection when attachment chip is dismissed
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!currentReferences) return

    // Check if there are any thread-scoped-snippet references for this file
    const hasThreadScopedSnippetReference = currentReferences.some(
      ref => ref.type === 'thread-scoped-snippet' && ref.name === file.name,
    )

    if (!hasThreadScopedSnippetReference) {
      // No thread-scoped-snippet references for this file - clear visual selection
      // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/set-state-in-effect
      setSelectionAttachmentRange(null)
    }
  }, [currentReferences, file.name])

  const selectionListenerOptions = useMemo(() => {
    // Only enable selection attachments if the feature flag is enabled
    if (!copilotFeatureFlags.selectionAttachments) {
      return undefined
    }

    const sizeLimit = CopilotTextAttacher.getAttachmentSizeLimit()
    return {
      // Remove debounce for immediate visual feedback
      debounceMs: 0,
      onSelection: ({view, text, fromLine, toLine}: SelectionChange) => {
        if (!text || text.trim() === '') return
        if (!manager) return

        // Snap selection to full line boundaries using CodeMirror's line API
        const firstLine = view.state.doc.line(fromLine)
        const lastLine = view.state.doc.line(toLine)

        const snappedFrom = firstLine.from
        const snappedTo = lastLine.to

        // Extract the snapped text
        const snappedText = view.state.doc.sliceString(snappedFrom, snappedTo)

        const signature = `${file.id}:${snappedFrom}-${snappedTo}:${file.value.length}`
        if (lastSelectionSigRef.current === signature) return

        const bytes = new Blob([snappedText]).size
        if (bytes > sizeLimit) return

        const name = file.name

        const reference: ThreadScopedSnippetReference = {
          type: 'thread-scoped-snippet',
          name,
          snippetText: snappedText,
          language: file.language || 'text',
          range: {
            start: fromLine,
            end: toLine,
          },
        }
        // Replace previous selection reference if it exists; otherwise add
        if (lastSelectionRef.current) {
          manager.replaceReference(lastSelectionRef.current, reference)
        } else {
          manager.addReference(reference, 'immersive-selection')
        }
        lastSelectionRef.current = reference
        setSelectionAttachmentRange({from: snappedFrom, to: snappedTo})
        lastSelectionSigRef.current = signature
      },
      onClear: () => {
        if (!lastSelectionSigRef.current) return
        if (manager && lastSelectionRef.current) {
          manager.removeReference(lastSelectionRef.current)
        }
        lastSelectionSigRef.current = ''
        lastSelectionRef.current = null
        setSelectionAttachmentRange(null)
      },
    }
  }, [file.id, file.name, file.value, file.language, manager])

  // Clear selection attachments when file ID changes
  useEffect(() => {
    if (manager && lastSelectionRef.current) {
      manager.removeReference(lastSelectionRef.current)
    }
    lastSelectionRef.current = null
    lastSelectionSigRef.current = ''
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/set-state-in-effect
    setSelectionAttachmentRange(null)
  }, [file.id, manager])

  // Cleanup any lingering selection attachment when the file preview is closed/unmounted.
  useEffect(() => {
    return () => {
      if (manager && lastSelectionRef.current) {
        manager.removeReference(lastSelectionRef.current)
      }
      lastSelectionRef.current = null
      lastSelectionSigRef.current = ''
    }
  }, [manager])

  useEffect(() => {
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/set-state-in-effect
    setIsLoading(false)
  }, [])

  useEffect(() => {
    if (isPreviewOpening) {
      focusNextRenderRef.current = true
    }
  }, [isPreviewOpening])

  const onChange = useStableCallback(
    useCallback(
      (value: string) => {
        if (!isEditable || value === file.value) return
        if (!file.isUserEdited) {
          const newFile: File = {
            ...file,
            messageId: NullMessageId,
            id: `file:${file.name}#${messages.length}`,
            timestamp: new Date(),
            isUserEdited: true,
            value,
          }
          updateItem(newFile)
          openItem(newFile.id)
          setEditedValue({value, id: newFile.id})
        } else {
          // we want to re-render this component though, so as to keep CodeMirror in sync with its props
          setEditedValue({value, id: file.id})
          saveChanges(file, value, updateItem)
        }
      },
      [file, isEditable, messages.length, openItem, updateItem],
    ),
  )

  return (
    <>
      <span id={labelId} className="sr-only">
        {file.name} file contents
      </span>
      <CodeMirror
        ariaLabelledBy={labelId}
        containerClassName={styles.codeMirrorContainer}
        fileName={file.name}
        height="100%"
        isReadOnly={!isEditable}
        spacing={codeMirrorOptions}
        value={isLoading ? '' : fileValue}
        onChange={onChange}
        focusNextRenderRef={focusNextRenderRef}
        placeholder="Loading..."
        hideHelp={false}
        selectionListenerOptions={selectionListenerOptions}
        selectionAttachmentRange={selectionAttachmentRange}
      />
      {isEditableFileType && !isLatest && (
        <div className={styles.footer}>Switch to the latest version to edit this file.</div>
      )}
    </>
  )
}

function PreviewComponent({file}: {file: File}) {
  if (file.language === 'markdown') {
    return (
      <div className={styles.previewContainer}>
        <MarkdownPreview content={file} />
      </div>
    )
  } else if (file.language === 'html') {
    return (
      <div className={styles.previewContainer}>
        <HtmlPreview file={file} />
      </div>
    )
  } else {
    return null
  }
}

function isLatestVersion(versionedItems: VersionedItemsMap, file: PreviewableContent) {
  const versions = versionedItems.get(stripVersionFromId(file.id)) ?? []
  return versions[versions.length - 1] === file.id
}

export function FileLink({file}: {file: File}) {
  const reference = file.reference

  if (!reference || reference.type !== 'file' || !reference.url) {
    return null
  }

  // parseGitHubUrl validates URL security
  const repoInfo = parseGitHubUrl(reference.url)

  if (!repoInfo) return null

  const {repoOwner, repoName, filePath} = repoInfo

  return (
    <div className={styles.fileLinkContainer}>
      <Link className={styles.filePath} href={reference.url} rel="noopener noreferrer">
        {formatFilePath(repoOwner, repoName, filePath)}
      </Link>
    </div>
  )
}

import {CopilotAnimation} from '@github-ui/copilot-animation'
import {useAppPayload} from '@github-ui/react-core/use-app-payload'
import {SandboxView} from '@github-ui/sandbox-view'
import {useEffect, useState} from 'react'

import type {CopilotImmersivePayload} from '../../routes/payloads'
import type {File, ItemsMap, PreviewableContentIdentifier, VersionedItemsMap} from './content-preview-types'
import {useContentPreview} from './ContentPreviewContext'
import styles from './HtmlPreview.module.css'

/**
 * Extracts the referenced style file names from the HTML content.
 */
function getReferencedStyles(htmlContent: string) {
  // grab all link tags
  const styleTags = Array.from(htmlContent.matchAll(/<link([^>]+)>/g))
    .map(tag => tag[1])
    .filter(tag => tag !== undefined)

  const styleReferences = []
  for (const style of styleTags) {
    // filter out non-stylesheet links
    const rel = /rel\s*=\s*"([^"]+)"/.exec(style)?.[1]
    if (rel !== 'stylesheet') continue

    // grab the href
    const href = /href\s*=\s*"([^"]+)"/.exec(style)?.[1]
    if (!href) continue

    styleReferences.push(href)
  }

  return styleReferences
}

/**
 * Takes a file reference and maps it to the latest version of the file's content.
 */
function mapReferenceToFile(reference: string, items: ItemsMap, versionedItems: VersionedItemsMap): File | null {
  const version: PreviewableContentIdentifier = `file:${reference}`
  if (!versionedItems.has(version)) return null

  const itemVersions = versionedItems.get(version) ?? []
  const lastVersion = itemVersions[itemVersions.length - 1]
  if (!lastVersion) return null

  const itemToAdd = items.get(lastVersion)
  if (!itemToAdd || itemToAdd.type !== 'file') return null

  return itemToAdd
}

/**
 * Iterate through referenced scripts/styles and add the latest version and the contents of each into a single string.
 * Returns a string of all scripts and files, as well as a list of files referenced. Null values in the list of files
 * are references to files that do not (yet) exist.
 */
function extractReferencedResourcesContent(htmlContent: string, items: ItemsMap, versionedItems: VersionedItemsMap) {
  const referencedScriptNames = Array.from(htmlContent.matchAll(/<script.*?src="(.*?)"/g))
    .map(script => script[1])
    .filter(script => script !== undefined)
  const referencedScriptFiles = referencedScriptNames.map(script => mapReferenceToFile(script, items, versionedItems))

  const referencedStyleNames = getReferencedStyles(htmlContent) // /<link rel="stylesheet" href="([^"]+)"/g.exec(htmlContent) ?? []
  const referencedStyleFiles = referencedStyleNames.map(style => mapReferenceToFile(style, items, versionedItems))

  const inlineScripts = Array.from(htmlContent.matchAll(/<script(?![^>]*\bsrc=).*?>(.*?)<\/script>/gms))
    .map(script => script[1])
    .filter(script => script !== undefined)

  const scripts = [
    ...referencedScriptFiles.map(file => file?.value ?? ''),
    ...inlineScripts,
    `document.dispatchEvent(new Event("DOMContentLoaded"))`,
  ].join('\n')

  return {
    scripts,
    styles: referencedStyleFiles.map(file => file?.value ?? '').join('\n'),
    files: referencedScriptFiles.concat(referencedStyleFiles),
  }
}

function extractReferencedExternalResources(htmlContent: string) {
  const externalScripts = Array.from(htmlContent.matchAll(/<script.*?src="(https:\/\/.*?)"/g))
    .map(script => script[1])
    .filter(script => script !== undefined)

  const headScripts = []
  const bodyScripts = []

  for (const script of externalScripts) {
    // eslint-disable-next-line github/unescaped-html-literal
    const headContents = htmlContent.substring(htmlContent.indexOf('<head'), htmlContent.indexOf('</head>'))
    if (headContents.includes(script)) {
      headScripts.push(script)
    } else {
      bodyScripts.push(script)
    }
  }

  const externalStyles = Array.from(htmlContent.matchAll(/<link rel="stylesheet" href="(https:\/\/.*?)"/g))
    .map(style => style[1])
    .filter(style => style !== undefined)

  return {
    headScripts,
    bodyScripts,
    styles: externalStyles,
  }
}

export function HtmlPreview({file}: {file: File}) {
  const {previewUrl} = useAppPayload<CopilotImmersivePayload>()
  const {items, versionedItems} = useContentPreview()
  const {scripts, styles: stylesText, files} = extractReferencedResourcesContent(file.value, items, versionedItems)
  const [forceShow, setForceShow] = useState(false)
  const isLoading = (file.isStreaming || files.some(f => !f || f.isStreaming)) && !forceShow

  // purposefully run this on every render to reset the timer
  useEffect(() => {
    if (isLoading) {
      // if we go 3 seconds with no progress, go ahead and show the content
      const id = setTimeout(() => {
        setForceShow(true)
      }, 3000)
      return () => clearTimeout(id)
    }
  })

  const {headScripts, bodyScripts, styles: externalStyles} = extractReferencedExternalResources(file.value)

  if (isLoading) {
    return (
      <div className={styles.loadingContainer}>
        <div className={styles.loadingHead}>
          <CopilotAnimation animationType="thinking" loopAnimation />
        </div>
        <p className={styles.loadingText}>Preparing your preview&hellip;</p>
      </div>
    )
  } else {
    return (
      <SandboxView
        key={file.id}
        inlineScripts={scripts}
        inlineStyles={stylesText}
        headScripts={headScripts}
        bodyScripts={bodyScripts}
        styles={externalStyles}
        content={file.value}
        viewscreenUrl={previewUrl}
      />
    )
  }
}

import type {Image} from './content-preview-types'
import styles from './ImagePreview.module.css'

export function ImagePreview({image}: {image: Image}) {
  return (
    <div className={styles.container}>
      <img className={styles.imageElement} src={image.url} alt={image.altText} />
    </div>
  )
}

import {IssueClosedIcon, IssueDraftIcon, IssueOpenedIcon} from '@primer/octicons-react'

import type {DraftIssue, ExistingIssue} from '../content-preview-types'

type IssueIconProps = Pick<DraftIssue, 'type'> | Pick<ExistingIssue, 'type' | 'state'>

export function IssueIcon(props: IssueIconProps) {
  if (props.type === 'existing-issue') {
    if (props.state === 'open') {
      return <IssueOpenedIcon className="fgColor-open" />
    }
    if (props.state === 'closed') {
      return <IssueClosedIcon className="fgColor-done" />
    }
  }

  return <IssueDraftIcon className="fgColor-draft" />
}

import {IssueViewer} from '@github-ui/issue-viewer/IssueViewer'
import {IssueViewerContextProvider} from '@github-ui/issue-viewer/IssueViewerContextProvider'
import {IssueViewerLoading} from '@github-ui/issue-viewer/IssueViewerLoading'
import type {OptionConfig} from '@github-ui/issue-viewer/OptionConfig'
import type {ItemIdentifier} from '@github-ui/issue-viewer/Types'
import {noop} from '@github-ui/noop'
import {RelayCachePreheatContextProvider} from '@github-ui/relay-query-cache/contexts/RelayCachePreheatContext'
import {LinkExternalIcon} from '@primer/octicons-react'
import {IconButton} from '@primer/react'
import {memo, useEffect, useMemo, useState} from 'react'

import {BROWSER_ANIMATION_DURATION} from '../../utils/constants'
import type {Issue} from './content-preview-types'
import styles from './IssuePreview.module.css'

export const IssuePreview = memo(function IssuePreview({
  isPreviewOpening,
  issue,
  onClose,
}: {
  isPreviewOpening: boolean
  issue: Issue
  onClose: () => void
}) {
  const [showLoadingState, setShowLoadingState] = useState(isPreviewOpening)

  // issue viewer causes lag if we try to render it while animating, so we show a loading until the animation is done.
  useEffect(() => {
    if (showLoadingState) {
      const timer = setTimeout(() => setShowLoadingState(false), BROWSER_ANIMATION_DURATION)
      return () => clearTimeout(timer)
    }
  }, [showLoadingState])

  const openIssueInNewTabButton = useMemo(() => {
    return (
      <IconButton
        icon={LinkExternalIcon}
        variant="invisible"
        aria-label="Open issue in new tab"
        onClick={() => {
          window.open(issue.href, '_blank')
        }}
      />
    )
  }, [issue.href])

  const optionConfig: OptionConfig = useMemo(
    () => ({
      navigate: noop,
      insideSidePanel: true,
      shouldSkipSetDocumentTitle: true,
      useViewportQueries: false,
      onIssueDelete: onClose,
      onRemovedFromProject: noop,
      onIssueUpdate: noop,
      additionalHeaderActions: openIssueInNewTabButton,
      showRepositoryPill: true,
    }),
    [onClose, openIssueInNewTabButton],
  )
  const itemIdentifier: ItemIdentifier = useMemo(
    () => ({
      owner: issue.owner,
      repo: issue.repo,
      number: issue.number,
      type: 'Issue',
    }),
    [issue.number, issue.owner, issue.repo],
  )

  if (showLoadingState) return <IssueViewerLoading optionConfig={optionConfig} />

  return (
    <div className={styles.container}>
      <IssueViewerContextProvider>
        <RelayCachePreheatContextProvider>
          <IssueViewer itemIdentifier={itemIdentifier} optionConfig={optionConfig} />
        </RelayCachePreheatContextProvider>
      </IssueViewerContextProvider>
    </div>
  )
})

import {BlockedIcon} from '@primer/octicons-react'
import {CounterLabel, Link, Token} from '@primer/react'
import {clsx} from 'clsx'
import React from 'react'

import styles from './IssueTree.module.css'

const TreeContext = React.createContext<{
  hasTrailingVisuals: boolean | undefined
}>({
  hasTrailingVisuals: undefined,
})

const ItemContext = React.createContext<{
  level: number
}>({
  level: 0,
})

type TreeProps = React.PropsWithChildren<{
  subIssueCount: number
  subIssueHeaderText?: string
  hasTrailingVisuals?: boolean
}>

function Tree({subIssueCount, subIssueHeaderText = 'Sub-issues', hasTrailingVisuals = false, children}: TreeProps) {
  const childrenArray = React.Children.toArray(children)
  const rootItem = childrenArray.find(
    (child): child is React.ReactElement<React.ComponentProps<typeof TreeRootItem>> =>
      React.isValidElement(child) && child.type === TreeRootItem,
  )
  const items = childrenArray.filter(
    (child): child is React.ReactElement<React.ComponentProps<typeof TreeItem>> =>
      React.isValidElement(child) && child.type === TreeItem,
  )

  // Mark the last item to handle rounded corners and padding
  const lastItemsIndex = items.length - 1
  if (lastItemsIndex >= 0 && React.isValidElement(items[lastItemsIndex])) {
    items[lastItemsIndex] = React.cloneElement(items[lastItemsIndex], {isLastBranch: true})
  }

  const providerProps = React.useMemo(() => ({hasTrailingVisuals}), [hasTrailingVisuals])

  return (
    <TreeContext value={providerProps}>
      <div className={styles.container} data-has-trailing-visual={hasTrailingVisuals || undefined}>
        {rootItem}

        {items.length > 0 && (
          <>
            <div className={styles.subHeader}>
              {subIssueHeaderText}
              <CounterLabel>{subIssueCount}</CounterLabel>
            </div>
            <ul role="tree" className={styles.tree}>
              {items}
            </ul>
          </>
        )}
      </div>
    </TreeContext>
  )
}

type TreeRootItemProps = TreeItemContentProps

function TreeRootItem(props: TreeRootItemProps) {
  const providerProps = React.useMemo(() => ({level: 0}), [])

  return (
    <ItemContext value={providerProps}>
      <div className={clsx(styles.item, styles.rootItem)}>
        <TreeItemContent {...props} />
      </div>
    </ItemContext>
  )
}

type TreeItemProps = React.PropsWithChildren<TreeItemContentProps> & {
  /**
   * Indicates this as the last branch at its level. A data attribute will be applied
   * to the deepest item in this branch (either this item if it has no children, or
   * recursively to its last child) for visual styling.
   */
  isLastBranch?: boolean
}

function TreeItem({children, isLastBranch, ...props}: TreeItemProps) {
  const childrenArray = React.Children.toArray(children)
  const nestedItems = childrenArray.filter(
    (child): child is React.ReactElement<React.ComponentProps<typeof TreeItem>> =>
      React.isValidElement(child) && child.type === TreeItem,
  )
  const contentChildren = childrenArray.filter(child => !(React.isValidElement(child) && child.type === TreeItem))

  const {level} = React.use(ItemContext)
  const providerProps = React.useMemo(() => ({level: level + 1}), [level])

  const isLastRendered = isLastBranch && nestedItems.length === 0

  // If this item has nested items, the last nested item should get the prop
  const lastNestedIndex = nestedItems.length - 1
  if (isLastBranch && lastNestedIndex >= 0 && React.isValidElement(nestedItems[lastNestedIndex])) {
    nestedItems[lastNestedIndex] = React.cloneElement(nestedItems[lastNestedIndex], {isLastBranch: true})
  }

  return (
    <ItemContext value={providerProps}>
      <li
        role="treeitem"
        aria-selected="false"
        className={styles.item}
        data-is-last-rendered={isLastRendered || undefined} // Mark the last rendered item for styling
      >
        <TreeItemContent {...props}>{contentChildren}</TreeItemContent>
        {nestedItems.length > 0 && (
          <ul role="group" className={styles.tree}>
            {nestedItems}
          </ul>
        )}
      </li>
    </ItemContext>
  )
}

type TreeItemContentProps = {
  leadingVisual?: React.ReactNode
  title: string
  description?: React.ReactNode
  trailingBadge?: React.ReactNode
  trailingVisual?: React.ReactNode
  href?: string
  onClick?: (event: React.MouseEvent) => void
  blockedBy?: BlockedByDependencies[]
}
function TreeItemContent({
  leadingVisual,
  title,
  description,
  trailingBadge,
  trailingVisual,
  href,
  onClick,
  blockedBy,
}: TreeItemContentProps) {
  const {level} = React.use(ItemContext)
  const {hasTrailingVisuals} = React.use(TreeContext)
  const hasDependencies = blockedBy != null && blockedBy.length > 0

  return (
    <>
      <div className={styles.grid} style={{'--level': level}} data-has-onclick={onClick ? true : undefined}>
        {level > 0 && <div className={styles.spacer} />}
        <div className={styles.leadingVisual}>{leadingVisual}</div>
        <div className={styles.content}>
          <Link as={href ? 'a' : onClick ? 'button' : 'span'} className={styles.title} href={href} onClick={onClick}>
            {title}
          </Link>
          {trailingBadge}
        </div>
        {(description != null || hasDependencies) && (
          <div className={styles.description}>
            {hasDependencies && <IssueDependencies dependencyType="Blocked by" blockedBy={blockedBy} />}
            {description}
          </div>
        )}
        {hasTrailingVisuals && <div className={styles.trailingVisual}>{trailingVisual}</div>}
      </div>
    </>
  )
}

export type BlockedByDependencies = {
  title: string
  href?: string
  onClick?: (event: React.MouseEvent) => void
}

interface IssueDependenciesProps {
  dependencyType: 'Blocked by'
  blockedBy: BlockedByDependencies[]
}
function IssueDependencies({dependencyType, blockedBy}: IssueDependenciesProps) {
  if (!blockedBy.length) return null

  const blockedByIssueLink = (
    <Link
      data-testid="blocked-by-link"
      className={styles.blockedBy}
      muted
      as={blockedBy[0]?.href ? 'a' : blockedBy[0]?.onClick ? 'button' : 'span'}
      href={blockedBy[0]?.href}
      onClick={blockedBy[0]?.onClick}
      title={blockedBy[0]?.title} // Add full title as tooltip
    >
      {blockedBy[0]?.title}
    </Link>
  )

  return (
    <Token
      size="medium"
      className="mr-2"
      leadingVisual={() => <BlockedIcon data-testid="blocked-by-icon" className="fgColor-danger" size={14} />}
      text={
        blockedBy.length > 1 ? (
          <>
            <span className={styles.tokenContainer}>
              {dependencyType} {blockedBy.length}
            </span>
          </>
        ) : (
          <>
            <span className={styles.tokenContainer}>
              {dependencyType}: {blockedByIssueLink}
            </span>
          </>
        )
      }
    />
  )
}

export const IssueTree = Object.assign(Tree, {
  RootItem: TreeRootItem,
  Item: TreeItem,
})

import {StopIcon} from '@primer/octicons-react'
import {Spinner} from '@primer/react'
import React, {useCallback, useEffect, useState} from 'react'

import type {JobResult} from './content-preview-types'
import styles from './JobResultPreview.module.css'

function beforeFetchRequestHandler(event: Event) {
  // if we let the event propagate, our page-level handlers will think we're doing a page navigation
  // but we're not
  event.stopPropagation()
}

export function JobResultPreview({jobResult}: {jobResult: JobResult}) {
  const ref = React.useRef<HTMLElement>(null)
  const [url, setUrl] = useState<string | null>(null)
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null)

  const beforeFetchResponseHandler = useCallback((e: Event) => {
    const evt = e as CustomEvent
    if (!evt.detail.fetchResponse.succeeded) {
      setError('Failed to load markdown preview')
      // we have to stop propagation to prevent the page-level handler from thinking a page navigation has failed
      evt.preventDefault()
      evt.stopPropagation()
    }
  }, [])

  useEffect(() => {
    if (ref.current) {
      const el = ref.current
