      el.addEventListener('turbo:before-fetch-request', beforeFetchRequestHandler)
      el.addEventListener('turbo:before-fetch-response', beforeFetchResponseHandler)
      // don't set src until after our event listeners are configured so we catch all the events
      // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/set-state-in-effect
      setUrl(buildUrl(jobResult))
      return () => {
        el.removeEventListener('turbo:before-fetch-request', beforeFetchRequestHandler)
        el.removeEventListener('turbo:before-fetch-response', beforeFetchResponseHandler)
      }
    }
  }, [beforeFetchResponseHandler, jobResult])

  return (
    <div className={styles.container}>
      {error ? (
        <div className={styles.error}>
          <span className={styles.errorIcon}>
            <StopIcon size={24} />
          </span>
          <h2 className={styles.errorHeader}>Failed to load job results</h2>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <turbo-frame src={url} ref={ref} id="workflow_run_content" target="_top">
          <div className={styles.spinner}>
            <Spinner srText="Loading Markdown preview…" />
          </div>
        </turbo-frame>
      )}
    </div>
  )
}

function buildUrl(jobResult: JobResult) {
  const components = [
    jobResult.repoOwner,
    jobResult.repoName,
    'actions',
    'runs',
    jobResult.workflowId,
    'job',
    jobResult.jobId,
  ]
  const url = new URL(`/${components.map(String).map(encodeURIComponent).join('/')}`, window.location.origin)
  url.searchParams.set('partial', 'true')
  return url.toString()
}

interface TurboFrameProps extends React.HTMLAttributes<HTMLElement> {
  src: string | null
  target?: string
}

// convince TypeScript that <turbo-frame> does indeed exist
declare module 'react' {
  // eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/no-namespace
  namespace JSX {
    interface IntrinsicElements {
      'turbo-frame': React.DetailedHTMLProps<TurboFrameProps, HTMLElement>
    }
  }
  interface HTMLElementEventMap {
    'turbo:before-fetch-response': CustomEvent
  }
}

import {Spinner} from '@primer/react'

import styles from './LoadingState.module.css'

export function LoadingState() {
  return (
    <div className={styles.container}>
      <Spinner size="large" />
    </div>
  )
}

import CodeMirror from '@github-ui/code-mirror'
import {noop} from '@github-ui/noop'
import {keepPreviousData, useQuery} from '@github-ui/react-query'
import type {SafeHTMLString} from '@github-ui/safe-html'
import {VerifiedHTMLDiv} from '@github-ui/safe-html/VerifiedHTML'
import {verifiedFetchJSON} from '@github-ui/verified-fetch'
import {StopIcon} from '@primer/octicons-react'
import {Spinner} from '@primer/react'
import {SkeletonText, type SkeletonTextProps} from '@primer/react/experimental'
import {clsx} from 'clsx'
import throttle from 'lodash-es/throttle'
import {useEffect, useId, useMemo, useState} from 'react'

import {useGuessCodeMirrorSettings} from '../../hooks/use-code-mirror-config'
import styles from './MarkdownPreview.module.css'

interface MarkdownResponse {
  html: SafeHTMLString
}

export interface MarkdownContent {
  value: string
  name: string
}

interface LoadingSkeletonOptions {
  size: SkeletonTextProps['size']
  lines: number
}

export interface MarkdownPreviewProps {
  content: MarkdownContent
  className?: string
  /** When true, displays raw markdown content using CodeMirror if rendering as HTML fails. Defaults to false. */
  fallbackToRaw?: boolean
  /** When true, hides the anchor links that appear next to headings. Defaults to false. */
  hideHeadingAnchors?: boolean
  /** When defined, use a loading skeleton instead of a spinner with the provided options */
  useLoadingSkeletonWithOptions?: LoadingSkeletonOptions[]
}

export function MarkdownPreview({
  content,
  className,
  fallbackToRaw = false,
  hideHeadingAnchors = false,
  useLoadingSkeletonWithOptions,
}: MarkdownPreviewProps) {
  const throttledValue = useThrottledValue(content.value, content.name, 2000)
  const {status, data} = useQuery({
    queryKey: ['copilot-immersive', 'MarkdownPreview', throttledValue, content.name],
    queryFn: async () => {
      const res = await verifiedFetchJSON('/copilot/immersive/markdown', {
        method: 'POST',
        body: {content: throttledValue, filename: content.name},
      })
      if (!res.ok) {
        throw new Error(`Failed to load rendered markdown (${res.status} on ${res.url})`)
      }
      const value = await res.json()
      return value as MarkdownResponse
    },
    placeholderData: keepPreviousData,
    staleTime: Infinity,
  })

  return (
    <div
      className={clsx(
        'readme html-blob blob',
        styles.markdownPreview,
        hideHeadingAnchors && styles.hideHeadingAnchors,
        className,
      )}
    >
      {status === 'pending' ? (
        useLoadingSkeletonWithOptions ? (
          <div aria-label="Loading Markdown preview…" role="status" aria-live="polite">
            {useLoadingSkeletonWithOptions.map((options, index) => (
              // eslint-disable-next-line @eslint-react/no-array-index-key
              <div key={index} className="tmp-mb-3">
                <SkeletonText size={options.size} lines={options.lines} />
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div className={styles.spinner}>
            <Spinner srText="Loading Markdown preview…" />
          </div>
        )
      ) : status === 'error' ? (
        fallbackToRaw ? (
          <RawMarkdownPreview content={content} />
        ) : (
          <div className={styles.error}>
            <span className={styles.errorIcon}>
              <StopIcon size={24} />
            </span>
            <h2 className={styles.errorHeader}>Markdown rendering failed</h2>
            <p>Refresh the page or check the markdown code</p>
          </div>
        )
      ) : (
        <VerifiedHTMLDiv className="markdown-body" html={data.html} />
      )}
    </div>
  )
}

/**
 * Raw markdown viewer component that displays markdown as plain text using CodeMirror
 */
function RawMarkdownPreview({content}: {content: MarkdownContent}) {
  const labelId = useId()
  const codeMirrorOptions = useGuessCodeMirrorSettings({
    value: content.value,
    language: 'markdown',
  })

  return (
    <>
      <span id={labelId} className="sr-only">
        {content.name} raw markdown contents
      </span>
      <CodeMirror
        ariaLabelledBy={labelId}
        fileName={content.name}
        isReadOnly
        spacing={codeMirrorOptions}
        value={content.value}
        onChange={noop} // Read-only
        hideHelp
      />
    </>
  )
}

/**
 * Returns the value given to it, eventually. The return value changes at most every delay milliseconds.
 * If the resetKey changes, the throttled value is immediately updated and throttling restarts.
 */
function useThrottledValue<T>(value: T, resetKey: string, delay: number) {
  const [throttledValue, setThrottledValue] = useState(value)
  const [lastResetKey, setLastResetKey] = useState(resetKey)
  const throttledSetter = useMemo(() => throttle(setThrottledValue, delay), [delay])

  useEffect(() => {
    // If the reset key changed, immediately update the value and reset throttling
    if (resetKey !== lastResetKey) {
      throttledSetter.cancel() // Cancel any pending throttled calls
      // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/set-state-in-effect
      setThrottledValue(value)
      setLastResetKey(resetKey)
    } else {
      throttledSetter(value)
    }
  }, [throttledSetter, value, resetKey, lastResetKey])

  return throttledValue
}

import {ActionMenu} from '@primer/react'
import {ActionList} from '@primer/styled-react'
import {useMemo} from 'react'

import type {DraftOrExistingIssue} from '../content-preview-types'
import styles from './CreateIssuePreview.module.css'
import {IssueIcon} from './IssueIcon'
import type {TreeNode} from './use-draft-issue-tree-map'

export function ParentSelector({
  node,
  onItemSelected,
}: {
  node: TreeNode<DraftOrExistingIssue>
  onItemSelected: (node: TreeNode<DraftOrExistingIssue>) => void
}) {
  const ancestors = useMemo(() => {
    const stack = []
    let current: TreeNode<DraftOrExistingIssue> | null = node
    while (current) {
      stack.push(current)
      current = current.parent
    }
    return stack.reverse()
  }, [node])

  if (!node.parent) return

  return (
    <ActionMenu>
      <ActionMenu.Button variant="invisible" className={styles.parentSelectorAnchor}>
        <span className="fgColor-muted">Parent:</span> {node?.parent?.item.name}
      </ActionMenu.Button>
      <ActionMenu.Overlay width="large">
        <ActionList>
          {ancestors.map((ancestor, index) => (
            <ActionList.Item
              key={ancestor.item.tag}
              active={ancestor.item.tag === node.item.tag}
              onSelect={() => onItemSelected(ancestor)}
              sx={{paddingLeft: `calc(${index} * var(--base-size-12))`}}
            >
              <ActionList.LeadingVisual>
                <IssueIcon {...ancestor.item} />
              </ActionList.LeadingVisual>
              <div className={styles.truncateText}>{ancestor.item.name}</div>
            </ActionList.Item>
          ))}
        </ActionList>
      </ActionMenu.Overlay>
    </ActionMenu>
  )
}

// eslint-disable-next-line no-restricted-imports
import {useChatState} from '@github-ui/copilot-chat/CopilotChatContext'
import {useChatManager} from '@github-ui/copilot-chat/CopilotChatManagerContext'
import type {CopilotChatReference} from '@github-ui/copilot-chat/utils/copilot-chat-types'
import {getSelectedThread} from '@github-ui/copilot-chat/utils/get-selected-thread'
import InputLabel from '@github-ui/input-label'
import {useIssueCreateDataContext} from '@github-ui/issue-create/IssueCreateDataContext'
import type {RepositoryPickerRepository} from '@github-ui/item-picker/RepositoryPicker'
import {RepositoryPicker as ItemPickerRepositoryPicker} from '@github-ui/item-picker/RepositoryPicker'
import {useCallback, useEffect, useState} from 'react'

import type {TimelineEventTextReference} from '../../timeline-events/timeline-events-types'
import {type DraftIssue, makeReferenceFromVersionedItem} from '../content-preview-types'
import {useContentPreview} from '../ContentPreviewContext'
import styles from './RepositoryPicker.module.css'
import {useDraftIssueRepository} from './use-draft-issue-repository'

type RepositoryPickerProps = {
  draftIssue: DraftIssue
  resolvedDraftIssueRepo: RepositoryPickerRepository | null | undefined
  loadingDraftIssueRepo: boolean
  topRepos: RepositoryPickerRepository[]
}

export function RepositoryPicker({
  draftIssue,
  resolvedDraftIssueRepo,
  loadingDraftIssueRepo,
  topRepos,
}: RepositoryPickerProps) {
  const [key, setKey] = useState<string | undefined>(undefined)
  const {repository: formRepository} = useIssueCreateDataContext()

  const onRepoSelected = useRepoSelectedCallback(draftIssue)

  const onSelect = useCallback(
    (newSelectedRepo: RepositoryPickerRepository | undefined) => {
      // Disallow deselection
      if (newSelectedRepo != null) {
        void onRepoSelected(newSelectedRepo.name, newSelectedRepo.owner.login)
      }
    },
    [onRepoSelected],
  )

  useDraftIssueRepository(draftIssue, resolvedDraftIssueRepo, loadingDraftIssueRepo, topRepos)

  // Side effect to ensure ItemPickerRepositoryPicker to memorize the selected non-top-repositories repo.
  useEffect(() => {
    if (
      !loadingDraftIssueRepo &&
      formRepository != null &&
      topRepos != null &&
      !topRepos.find(node => node.id === formRepository.id)
    ) {
      // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/set-state-in-effect
      setKey(formRepository.id)
    }
  }, [formRepository, loadingDraftIssueRepo, topRepos])

  // A UX only step to force a default repository from the top repositories list instead of letting
  // the ItemPickerRepositoryPicker to do so since it only does this once after the first render.
  const selectedRepo = formRepository ?? topRepos?.[0]
  // Prevents user change when:
  // 1. Waiting for streaming
  // 2. Waiting for resolving draft issue repo
  const readonly = draftIssue.isStreaming || loadingDraftIssueRepo

  return (
    <div className={styles.container}>
      {/* Not using htmlFor because the picker already has its own aria-label ("selected repo: [repo name]") */}
      <InputLabel className={styles.label} required>
        Repository
      </InputLabel>
      <ItemPickerRepositoryPicker
        key={key}
        initialRepository={selectedRepo}
        onSelect={onSelect}
        options={{hasIssuesEnabled: true, readonly}}
        topRepositories={topRepos}
        enforceAtleastOneSelected
      />
    </div>
  )
}

function useRepoSelectedCallback(draftIssue: DraftIssue) {
  const manager = useChatManager()
  const state = useChatState()
  const {updateItem} = useContentPreview()

  return useCallback(
    async (repoName: string, repoOwner: string) => {
      const repoNwo = `${repoOwner}/${repoName}`
      const instructions: TimelineEventTextReference = {
        type: 'text',
        name: `timeline-event: {"type": "switch-issue-repo", "markdownContent": "Changing repository to ${repoNwo}…"}`,
        text: `Update draft issue with tag \`${draftIssue.tag}\` to use the \`${repoNwo}\` repository. DO NOT ask the user for a template. If there is a template in the new repository that makes sense to use, use it. If you cannot find a template, draft a blank issue. Regenerate the title and description. Maintain all conversation context. Update the metadata to follow the new repository and template. DO NOT change the \`tag\` or \`parentTag\` fields.`,
      }

      const references: CopilotChatReference[] = [instructions]
      if (draftIssue.isUserEdited) {
        // Since references of a supported type get "deserialized" back as a versioned item in content preview,
        // we make sure this version contains any user updates that were made.
        // We don't update the template because the draft issue's content is still using the old/blank template
        const updatedDraftIssue = {
          ...draftIssue,
          ...(repoNwo && {repository: repoNwo}),
          // Mark content preview items that correspond to the references as not user-edited so that they don't automatically
          // get closed when the chat reducer clears the currentReferences.
          isUserEdited: false,
        }
        updateItem(updatedDraftIssue)
        references.push(makeReferenceFromVersionedItem(updatedDraftIssue))
      }
      await manager.sendChatMessage({
        thread: getSelectedThread(state),
        content: `Use the ${repoNwo} repository`,
        references: [...references, ...state.currentReferences],
        topic: state.currentTopic,
        context: state.context,
        customInstructions: state.customInstructions,
        model: state.model,
      })
    },
    [draftIssue, manager, state, updateItem],
  )
}

import {sendEvent} from '@github-ui/hydro-analytics'

import type {PreviewableContent} from './content-preview-types'

export function sendContentPreviewEvent(
  eventName: string,
  contentPreview: PreviewableContent,
  additionalData: Record<string, string | number | boolean | undefined | null> = {},
) {
  sendEvent(eventName, {
    contentPreviewType: contentPreview.type,
    mode: 'immersive',
    path: contentPreview.id,
    ...additionalData,
  })
}

// eslint-disable-next-line no-restricted-imports
import {useChatState} from '@github-ui/copilot-chat/CopilotChatContext'
import {useChatManager} from '@github-ui/copilot-chat/CopilotChatManagerContext'
import type {CopilotChatReference} from '@github-ui/copilot-chat/utils/copilot-chat-types'
import {getSelectedThread} from '@github-ui/copilot-chat/utils/get-selected-thread'
import InputLabel from '@github-ui/input-label'
import {useIssueCreateDataContext} from '@github-ui/issue-create/IssueCreateDataContext'
import {ItemPicker} from '@github-ui/item-picker-base/ItemPicker'
import {useQuery} from '@github-ui/react-query'
import {TriangleDownIcon} from '@primer/octicons-react'
import {Button} from '@primer/react'
import {useCallback, useEffect, useId, useMemo, useRef, useState} from 'react'
import {useRelayEnvironment} from 'react-relay'
import {fetchQuery, graphql, readInlineData} from 'relay-runtime'

import type {TimelineEventTextReference} from '../../timeline-events/timeline-events-types'
import {type DraftIssue, makeReferenceFromVersionedItem} from '../content-preview-types'
import {useContentPreview} from '../ContentPreviewContext'
import type {TemplatePickerForm$data, TemplatePickerForm$key} from './__generated__/TemplatePickerForm.graphql'
import type {TemplatePickerQuery} from './__generated__/TemplatePickerQuery.graphql'
import type {
  TemplatePickerTemplate$data,
  TemplatePickerTemplate$key,
} from './__generated__/TemplatePickerTemplate.graphql'
import styles from './RepositoryPicker.module.css'

// eslint-disable-next-line @github-ui/github-monorepo/restrict-relay-queries
const TemplatesGraphqlQuery = graphql`
  query TemplatePickerQuery($owner: String!, $repo: String!) {
    repository(owner: $owner, name: $repo) {
      issueForms {
        __typename
        ...TemplatePickerForm
      }
      issueTemplates {
        __typename
        ...TemplatePickerTemplate
      }
    }
  }
`

// eslint-disable-next-line @github-ui/github-monorepo/restrict-relay-queries
const FormFragment = graphql`
  fragment TemplatePickerForm on IssueForm @inline {
    __typename
    filename
    name
    description
  }
`

// eslint-disable-next-line @github-ui/github-monorepo/restrict-relay-queries
const TemplateFragment = graphql`
  fragment TemplatePickerTemplate on IssueTemplate @inline {
    __typename
    filename
    name
    about
  }
`

type TemplatePickerItem = TemplatePickerTemplate$data | TemplatePickerForm$data

function isTemplate(item: TemplatePickerItem): item is TemplatePickerTemplate$data {
  return item.__typename === 'IssueTemplate'
}

export function useTemplatesQuery({owner, repo}: {owner: string | undefined; repo: string | undefined}) {
  const environment = useRelayEnvironment()

  return useQuery({
    queryKey: ['copilot-immersive-templates', JSON.stringify(environment), owner, repo],
    queryFn: async () => {
      if (!owner || !repo) return []

      const data = await fetchQuery<TemplatePickerQuery>(environment, TemplatesGraphqlQuery, {owner, repo}).toPromise()

      const issueTemplates = (data?.repository?.issueTemplates || []).map(node => {
        // eslint-disable-next-line no-restricted-syntax
        return readInlineData<TemplatePickerTemplate$key>(TemplateFragment, node)
      })

      const issueForms = (data?.repository?.issueForms || []).map(node => {
        // eslint-disable-next-line no-restricted-syntax
        return readInlineData<TemplatePickerForm$key>(FormFragment, node)
      })

      return [...issueTemplates, ...issueForms].sort((a, b) => {
        return a.filename.localeCompare(b.filename)
      })
    },
  })
}

function useTemplateSelectedCallback() {
  const manager = useChatManager()
  const state = useChatState()
  const {updateItem} = useContentPreview()

  return useCallback(
    async (draftIssue: DraftIssue, template: TemplatePickerItem | undefined, repositoryNwo?: string) => {
      const instructions: TimelineEventTextReference = {
        type: 'text',
        name: `timeline-event: {"type": "switch-issue-template", "markdownContent": "${
          template ? `Changing template to '${template.name}'…` : 'Removing template…'
        }"}`,
        text: template
          ? `Update the draft issue with tag \`${draftIssue.tag}\` with the \`${template.filename}\` template${
              repositoryNwo ? ` in the \`${repositoryNwo}\` repository` : ''
            }. Regenerate the title and description following the new template. Maintain all conversation context. Update the metadata to follow the new template. DO NOT change the \`tag\` or \`parentTag\` fields.`
          : `Update draft issue with tag \`${draftIssue.tag}\` to remove the template formatting. Regenerate the title and description to follow a generic format. Maintain all conversation context. DO NOT change the \`tag\` or \`parentTag\` fields.`,
      }

      const references: CopilotChatReference[] = [instructions]
      if (draftIssue.isUserEdited) {
        // Since references of a supported type get "deserialized" back as a versioned item in content preview,
        // we make sure this version contains any user updates that were made.
        // We don't update the template because the draft issue's content is still using the old/blank template
        const updatedDraftIssue = {
          ...draftIssue,
          // there's a race condition where if the repo was changed before selecting the new template,
          // the repo sometimes isn't updated in the draftIssue. Ensure the correct repo is sent:
          ...(repositoryNwo && {repository: repositoryNwo}),
          // Mark content preview items that correspond to the references as not user-edited so that they don't automatically
          // get closed when the chat reducer clears the currentReferences.
          isUserEdited: false,
        }
        updateItem(updatedDraftIssue)
        references.push(makeReferenceFromVersionedItem(updatedDraftIssue))
      }
      await manager.sendChatMessage({
        thread: getSelectedThread(state),
        content: template ? `Use the '${template.name}' template` : 'Remove the template',
        references: [...references, ...state.currentReferences],
        topic: state.currentTopic,
        context: state.context,
        customInstructions: state.customInstructions,
        model: state.model,
      })
    },
    [manager, state, updateItem],
  )
}

export function TemplatePicker({draftIssue}: {draftIssue: DraftIssue}) {
  const {repository} = useIssueCreateDataContext()
  const {data: templates} = useTemplatesQuery({
    owner: repository?.owner.login,
    repo: repository?.name,
  })
  const buttonId = useId()

  if (templates == null || templates.length === 0) {
    return null
  }

  const templateRequired =
    draftIssue.type === 'new-issue' && repository?.isBlankIssuesEnabled != null && !repository.isBlankIssuesEnabled

  return (
    <div className={styles.container}>
      <InputLabel className={styles.label} required={templateRequired} htmlFor={buttonId}>
        Template
      </InputLabel>
      <TemplatePickerInternal
        {...{
          templates,
          draftIssue,
          repositoryNwo: repository?.nameWithOwner,
          buttonId,
        }}
      />
    </div>
  )
}

function TemplatePickerInternal({
  templates,
  draftIssue,
  repositoryNwo,
  buttonId,
}: {
  templates: TemplatePickerItem[]
  draftIssue: DraftIssue
  repositoryNwo?: string
  buttonId: string
}) {
  const [filter, setFilter] = useState<string>('')
  const filteredItems = useMemo(() => {
    if (!filter) {
      return templates
    }
    return templates?.filter(item => {
      return item.name?.toLowerCase().includes(filter.toLowerCase())
    })
  }, [templates, filter])

  const [selectedItem, setSelectedItem] = useState<TemplatePickerItem | undefined>(undefined)
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!draftIssue.template) {
      // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/set-state-in-effect
      setSelectedItem(undefined)
      return
    }

    // Copilot will sometimes return the template with full path, rather than just the filename
    const initTemplate = draftIssue.template.split('/').findLast(Boolean)
    // Sometimes it will return the template name instead of filename as well, so we check both for a match
    const selected = templates?.find(item => item.filename === initTemplate || item.name === initTemplate)
    setSelectedItem(selected)
  }, [templates, draftIssue.template])
  const onTemplateSelected = useTemplateSelectedCallback()

  const templatePickerRef = useRef<HTMLButtonElement>(null)

  return (
    <ItemPicker
      items={filteredItems ?? []}
      initialSelectedItems={selectedItem ? [selectedItem] : []}
      placeholderText={'Search'}
      selectionVariant={'single'}
      filterItems={setFilter}
      title={'Select a template'}
      renderAnchor={(props: React.HTMLAttributes<HTMLElement>): React.ReactElement => {
        return (
          <Button id={buttonId} trailingVisual={TriangleDownIcon} {...props} ref={templatePickerRef}>
            {selectedItem ? <span>{selectedItem.name}</span> : 'Select a template'}
          </Button>
        )
      }}
      getItemKey={item => item.filename}
      convertToItemProps={item => ({
        id: item.filename,
        text: item.name,
        description: (isTemplate(item) ? item.about : item.description) || undefined,
        descriptionVariant: 'block',
        source: item,
      })}
      onSelectionChange={async ([item]) => {
        setSelectedItem(item)
        await onTemplateSelected(draftIssue, item, repositoryNwo)
      }}
      selectPanelRef={templatePickerRef}
      height={'large'}
      width={'medium'}
    />
  )
}

import {assignIssuesToCopilot} from '@github-ui/agent-sessions/utils/copilot-assignment-api'
import {formatAssignmentError} from '@github-ui/agent-sessions/utils/error-handling'
import {isCopilot} from '@github-ui/assignees/copilot-user'
import {commitSetLabelsForLabelableMutation} from '@github-ui/item-picker/commitSetLabelsForLabelableMutation'
import {commitUpdateIssueAssigneesMutation} from '@github-ui/item-picker/commitUpdateIssueAssigneesMutation'
import {commitUpdateIssueIssueTypeMutation} from '@github-ui/item-picker/commitUpdateIssueIssueTypeMutation'
import {commitUpdateIssueMilestoneMutation} from '@github-ui/item-picker/commitUpdateIssueMilestoneMutation'
import {commitUpdateIssueProjectsMutation} from '@github-ui/item-picker/commitUpdateIssueProjectsMutation'
import type {AssigneePickerAssignee} from '@github-ui/item-picker-assignee-tsq'
import type {IssueTypePickerIssueType} from '@github-ui/item-picker-issue-type'
import type {LabelPickerLabel} from '@github-ui/item-picker-label-relay'
import type {MilestonePickerMilestone} from '@github-ui/item-picker-milestone'
import type {ProjectPickerBaseProject as ProjectPickerProject} from '@github-ui/item-picker-project'
import isEqual from 'lodash-es/isEqual'
import {commitMutation, graphql} from 'react-relay'
import type {Environment, IEnvironment, PayloadError} from 'relay-runtime'

import type {DraftIssue} from '../content-preview-types'
import type {Issue} from './use-issue-query'
import type {SavedIssue} from './use-on-save-callback'

export type OnUpdateProps = {
  result: UpdateResult
}

export type ProjectUpdateError = {
  projectId: string
  projectTitle: string
  operation: 'add' | 'remove'
  error: string
}

export type OperationType =
  | 'labels'
  | 'assignees'
  | 'assignCopilot'
  | 'milestone'
  | 'issueType'
  | 'projects'
  | 'content'

export type UpdateResult = {
  successful: OperationType[]
  failed: Array<{
    operation: OperationType
    error: Error
  }>
  issue?: SavedIssue
}

interface IssueComparisonData {
  body?: string | null // Body can be null (treated as empty string for comparison)
  assignees?: AssigneePickerAssignee[]
  labels?: LabelPickerLabel[]
  milestone?: MilestonePickerMilestone | null
  issueType?: IssueTypePickerIssueType | null
  projects?: ProjectPickerProject[]
  hasInaccessibleProject?: boolean
}

export async function commitUpdateIssueMutation({
  environment,
  currentIssue,
  draftIssue,
  proposedChanges,
  canUseCopilotAssignment,
  onCompleted,
}: {
  environment: Environment
  currentIssue: Issue
  draftIssue: DraftIssue
  proposedChanges: IssueComparisonData // Some Draft Issue changes require additional data, so we pass them in seperate to the draft
  canUseCopilotAssignment: boolean
  onCompleted?: (response: OnUpdateProps, errors?: readonly PayloadError[]) => void
}): Promise<void> {
  const currentLabelNames =
    currentIssue.labels?.nodes
      ?.filter((label): label is NonNullable<typeof label> => label != null)
      .map(label => label.name) || []

  const proposedLabelNames = proposedChanges.labels?.map(label => label.name) || []

  const labelsChanged =
    proposedChanges.labels !== undefined && !isEqual(currentLabelNames.sort(), proposedLabelNames.sort())

  const milestoneChanged =
    proposedChanges.milestone !== undefined &&
    (proposedChanges.milestone?.title || null) !== (currentIssue.milestone?.title || null)

  const issueTypeChanged =
    proposedChanges.issueType !== undefined && proposedChanges.issueType?.name !== currentIssue.issueType?.name

  // For projects comparison, compare project IDs
  const currentProjectIds =
    currentIssue.projectItems?.nodes
      ?.filter((item): item is NonNullable<typeof item> => item != null)
      .map(item => item.project)
      .filter((project): project is NonNullable<typeof project> => project != null)
      .map(project => project.id)
      .filter(Boolean) || []

  const proposedProjectIds = proposedChanges.projects?.map(project => project.id) || []

  const projectsChanged =
    proposedChanges.projects !== undefined && !isEqual(currentProjectIds.sort(), proposedProjectIds.sort())

  const titleChanged = draftIssue.name !== currentIssue.title
  const bodyChanged = proposedChanges.body !== undefined && proposedChanges.body !== (currentIssue.body || '')

  const metadataPromises: Array<{operation: OperationType; promise: Promise<void>}> = []

  const handleAllMutationsComplete = (successfulOperations: OperationType[]): SavedIssue => {
    // Start with currentIssue data and only apply fields that were successfully mutated

    // Only use proposed title if content mutation succeeded, otherwise keep current
    const finalTitle = successfulOperations.includes('content') ? draftIssue.name : currentIssue.title

    // Only use proposed assignees if assignees mutation succeeded, otherwise keep current
    const finalAssignees =
      successfulOperations.includes('assignees') && proposedChanges.assignees !== undefined
        ? proposedChanges.assignees.map(a => a.login)
        : currentIssue.assignees?.nodes?.map(a => a?.login).filter((login): login is string => Boolean(login)) || []

    return {
      draftType: 'update-issue',
      tag: draftIssue?.tag || '',
      parentTag: draftIssue?.parentTag,
      blockedByTags: draftIssue?.blockedByTags,
      isBlockingTags: draftIssue?.isBlockingTags,
      title: finalTitle,
      number: draftIssue?.issueNumber || currentIssue.number || 0,
      url: currentIssue.url || '',
      repository: {
        databaseId: currentIssue.repository?.databaseId || 0,
        name: currentIssue.repository?.name || '',
        owner: {
          login: currentIssue.repository?.owner.login || '',
        },
      },
      template: draftIssue?.template,
      assignees: finalAssignees,
    }
  }

  // Run specialized mutations for each changed metadata field
  if (labelsChanged) {
    const promise = new Promise<void>((resolve, reject) => {
      commitSetLabelsForLabelableMutation({
        environment,
        input: {
          labelableId: currentIssue.id,
          labels: proposedChanges.labels || [], // Use empty array when removing all labels
          labelableTypeName: 'Issue' as const,
        },
        clientMutationId: draftIssue.messageId,
        onCompleted: () => {
          resolve()
        },
        onError: error => {
          reject(error)
        },
      })
    })
    metadataPromises.push({operation: 'labels', promise})
  }

  const currentAssignees = currentIssue.assignees?.nodes?.filter(
    (assignee): assignee is NonNullable<typeof assignee> => assignee != null,
  )
  const assigneePromise = handleAssigneeUpdates({
    currentIssue,
    currentAssignees,
    selectedAssignees: proposedChanges.assignees,
    canUseCopilotAssignment,
    environment,
    messageId: draftIssue.messageId,
  })
  if (assigneePromise) {
    metadataPromises.push({operation: 'assignees', promise: assigneePromise})
  }

  if (milestoneChanged) {
    const promise = new Promise<void>((resolve, reject) => {
      commitUpdateIssueMilestoneMutation({
        environment,
        input: {
          issueId: currentIssue.id,
          milestone: proposedChanges.milestone ?? null,
          clientMutationId: draftIssue.messageId,
        },
        onCompleted: () => {
          resolve()
        },
        onError: error => {
          reject(error)
        },
      })
    })
    metadataPromises.push({operation: 'milestone', promise})
  }

  if (issueTypeChanged) {
    const promise = new Promise<void>((resolve, reject) => {
      commitUpdateIssueIssueTypeMutation({
        environment,
        input: {
          issueId: currentIssue.id,
          issueTypeId: proposedChanges.issueType?.id || null,
          clientMutationId: draftIssue.messageId,
        },
        onCompleted: () => {
          resolve()
        },
        onError: error => {
          reject(error)
        },
      })
    })
    metadataPromises.push({operation: 'issueType', promise})
  }

  if (projectsChanged) {
    const promise = new Promise<void>((resolve, reject) => {
      // Use helper function to convert issue project data
      const currentProjects = currentIssue.projectItems?.nodes || []

      void (async () => {
        try {
          await updateIssueProjects(
            currentIssue.id,
            proposedChanges.projects || [],
            currentProjects,
            environment,
            draftIssue.messageId,
            proposedChanges.hasInaccessibleProject,
          )
          resolve()
        } catch (projectError: unknown) {
          // Project updates failed
          const errorMessage = projectError instanceof Error ? projectError.message : 'Project update failed'
          const error = new Error(`Project update failed: ${errorMessage}`)
          reject(error)
        }
      })()
    })
    metadataPromises.push({operation: 'projects', promise})
  }

  if (titleChanged || bodyChanged) {
    const promise = new Promise<void>((resolve, reject) => {
      commitMutation(environment, {
        // eslint-disable-next-line @github-ui/github-monorepo/restrict-relay-queries
        mutation: graphql`
          mutation updateIssueMutationContentOnlyMutation($input: UpdateIssueInput!) @raw_response_type {
            updateIssue(input: $input) {
              issue {
                id
                title
                body
                bodyVersion
              }
            }
          }
        `,
        variables: {
          input: {
            id: currentIssue.id,
            clientMutationId: draftIssue.messageId,
            ...(titleChanged && {title: draftIssue.name}),
            ...(bodyChanged && {
              body: proposedChanges.body,
              bodyVersion: draftIssue?.bodyVersion,
            }),
          },
        },
        onCompleted: () => {
          resolve()
        },
        onError: error => {
          reject(error)
        },
      })
    })
    metadataPromises.push({operation: 'content', promise})
  }

  const updateResult: UpdateResult = {
    successful: [],
    failed: [],
  }

  if (metadataPromises.length > 0) {
    // Wait for all mutations to complete (they run in parallel)
    const results = await Promise.allSettled(metadataPromises.map(mp => mp.promise))

    for (const [index, result] of results.entries()) {
      const operation = metadataPromises[index]?.operation
      if (!operation) continue

      if (result.status === 'fulfilled') {
        updateResult.successful.push(operation)
      } else {
        updateResult.failed.push({
          operation,
          error: result.reason instanceof Error ? result.reason : new Error('Unknown error'),
        })
      }
    }
  }

  // Handle Copilot assignment if needed and if all other mutations all succeeded
  const assignCopilotOperation = 'assignCopilot'
  try {
    await handleCopilotAssigneeUpdates({
      currentIssue,
      selectedAssignees: proposedChanges.assignees,
      canUseCopilotAssignment,
      hasOtherUpdateErrors: updateResult.failed.length > 0,
      onCompleted: () => updateResult.successful.push(assignCopilotOperation),
    })
  } catch (error) {
    updateResult.failed.push({
      operation: assignCopilotOperation,
      error: error instanceof Error ? error : new Error('Unknown error'),
    })
  }

  if (updateResult.successful.length === 0 && updateResult.failed.length === 0) {
    // No changes were needed, but this is still a successful operation
    const noChangeIssue: SavedIssue = {
      draftType: 'update-issue',
      tag: draftIssue?.tag || '',
      parentTag: draftIssue?.parentTag,
      blockedByTags: draftIssue?.blockedByTags,
      isBlockingTags: draftIssue?.isBlockingTags,
      title: currentIssue.title,
      number: draftIssue?.issueNumber || currentIssue.number || 0,
      url: currentIssue.url || '',
      repository: {
        databaseId: currentIssue.repository?.databaseId || 0,
        name: currentIssue.repository?.name || '',
        owner: {
          login: currentIssue.repository?.owner.login || '',
        },
      },
      template: draftIssue?.template,
      assignees:
        currentIssue.assignees?.nodes?.map(a => a?.login).filter((login): login is string => Boolean(login)) || [],
    }

    if (onCompleted) {
      onCompleted({
        result: {
          successful: [],
          failed: [],
          issue: noChangeIssue,
        },
      })
    }

    return
  }

  const updatedIssue = handleAllMutationsComplete(updateResult.successful)

  if (onCompleted) {
    onCompleted({
      result: {
        successful: updateResult.successful,
        failed: updateResult.failed,
        issue: updatedIssue,
      },
    })
  }
}

const assigneesEqual = (proposed: AssigneePickerAssignee[], current: Array<{readonly login: string}>): boolean => {
  if (proposed.length !== current.length) {
    return false
  }
  const proposedLogins = new Set(proposed.map(assignee => assignee.login))
  return current.every(assignee => proposedLogins.has(assignee.login))
}

function handleAssigneeUpdates({
  currentIssue,
  currentAssignees,
  selectedAssignees,
  canUseCopilotAssignment,
  environment,
  messageId,
}: {
  currentIssue: Issue
  currentAssignees?: Array<{readonly login: string}>
  selectedAssignees?: AssigneePickerAssignee[]
  canUseCopilotAssignment: boolean
  environment: Environment
  messageId: string
}): Promise<void> | void {
  if (selectedAssignees === undefined) {
    return // No permission
  }

  const proposedNonCopilotAssignees = selectedAssignees.filter(assignee => !isCopilot(assignee.login)) ?? []
  const assigneesToSave = canUseCopilotAssignment ? proposedNonCopilotAssignees : selectedAssignees
  const regularAssigneesChanged = !assigneesEqual(assigneesToSave, currentAssignees ?? [])

  if (regularAssigneesChanged) {
    return new Promise<void>((resolve, reject) => {
      commitUpdateIssueAssigneesMutation({
        environment,
        input: {
          issueId: currentIssue.id,
          assignedActors: assigneesToSave, // Use empty array when removing all assignees
          participants: [], // Keep existing participants unchanged
        },
        clientMutationId: messageId,
        onCompleted: () => {
          resolve()
        },
        onError: error => {
          reject(error)
        },
      })
    })
  }
}

async function handleCopilotAssigneeUpdates({
  currentIssue,
  selectedAssignees,
  canUseCopilotAssignment,
  hasOtherUpdateErrors,
  onCompleted,
}: {
  currentIssue: Issue
  selectedAssignees?: AssigneePickerAssignee[]
  canUseCopilotAssignment: boolean
  hasOtherUpdateErrors: boolean
  onCompleted?: () => void
}): Promise<void> {
  if (selectedAssignees === undefined || !canUseCopilotAssignment) {
    // No permission or Copilot assignment not allowed
    return
  }

  const proposedAssigningToCopilot = selectedAssignees?.some(assignee => isCopilot(assignee.login)) ?? false
  if (!proposedAssigningToCopilot) {
    // No Copilot assignment requested
    return
  }

  return (async () => {
    if (hasOtherUpdateErrors) {
      throw new Error('Copilot assignment skipped due to other update error(s)')
    }

    const targetBranch = currentIssue.repository.defaultBranchRef?.name ?? ''
    if (!targetBranch) {
      throw new Error('Cannot assign to Copilot: default branch not found')
    }

    try {
      const response = await assignIssuesToCopilot(
        currentIssue.repository.owner.login,
        currentIssue.repository.name,
        [currentIssue.number],
        {
          instructions: '', // Empty instructions as requested
          targetRepo: `${currentIssue.repository.owner.login}/${currentIssue.repository.name}`,
          targetBranch,
        },
      )
      if (!response.ok) {
        const message = formatAssignmentError(response, 'Assignment failed')
        throw new Error(message)
      }
      onCompleted?.()
    } catch (error) {
      throw error instanceof Error ? error : new Error('Unknown error')
    }
  })()
}

async function updateIssueProjects(
  issueId: string,
  desiredProjects: ProjectPickerProject[],
  currentProjectNodes: NonNullable<NonNullable<Issue['projectItems']>['nodes']>,
  environment: IEnvironment,
  messageId: string,
  hasInaccessibleProject?: boolean,
) {
  const currentProjects = currentProjectNodes.filter(current => current != null && current.project != null)

  const projectsToAdd = desiredProjects.filter(
    desired => !currentProjects.some(current => current?.project.id === desired.id),
  )

  // TODO: Workaround for https://github.com/github/copilot-voyager/issues/786
  // Here we will ignore project removals if there are inaccessible projects
  const projectsToRemove = hasInaccessibleProject
    ? []
    : currentProjects.filter(current => !desiredProjects.some(desired => desired.id === current?.project.id))

  // If no changes in projects, do nothing
  if (projectsToAdd.length === 0 && projectsToRemove.length === 0) {
    return
  }

  const projectErrors: ProjectUpdateError[] = []
  const projectPromises: Array<Promise<void>> = []

  // Process additions individually using commitUpdateIssueProjectsMutation
  for (const project of projectsToAdd) {
    const promise = new Promise<void>(resolve => {
      commitUpdateIssueProjectsMutation({
        environment: environment as Environment,
        projectId: project.id,
        issueId,
        clientMutationId: messageId,
        onCompleted: () => {
          resolve()
        },
        onError: (error: Error) => {
          projectErrors.push({
            projectId: project.id,
            projectTitle: project.title,
            operation: 'add',
            error: error.message,
          })
          resolve() // Resolve anyway to continue with other projects
        },
      })
    })
    projectPromises.push(promise)
  }

  // Process removals individually - we need to find the item IDs from currentProjectItems,
  for (const associatedProject of projectsToRemove) {
    // For removal, we need a different approach since commitDeleteIssueProjectsMutation
    // requires connectionId and is designed for UI contexts.
    // Instead, we'll use a simple GraphQL mutation to remove the item.
    const promise = new Promise<void>(resolve => {
      commitMutation(environment, {
        // eslint-disable-next-line @github-ui/github-monorepo/restrict-relay-queries
        mutation: graphql`
          mutation updateIssueMutationDeleteProjectMutation($input: DeleteProjectV2ItemInput!) {
            deleteProjectV2Item(input: $input) {
              deletedItemId
            }
          }
        `,
        variables: {
          input: {
            clientMutationId: messageId,
            itemId: associatedProject?.id, // This should be the project item ID, not project ID
            projectId: associatedProject?.project.id, // The actual project ID
          },
        },
        onCompleted: () => {
          resolve()
        },
        onError: (error: Error) => {
          projectErrors.push({
            projectId: associatedProject?.project.id || 'unknown',
            projectTitle: associatedProject?.project.title || 'unknown',
            operation: 'remove',
            error: error.message,
          })
          resolve() // Resolve anyway to continue with other projects
        },
      })
    })
    projectPromises.push(promise)
  }

  // Wait for all project operations to complete
  await Promise.all(projectPromises)

  // If there were any errors, throw an error with details
  if (projectErrors.length > 0) {
    const errorMessage = `Failed to update ${projectErrors.length} project(s): ${projectErrors
      .map(err => `${err.operation} "${err.projectTitle}" (${err.error})`)
      .join(', ')}`

    interface ProjectUpdateErrorWithDetails extends Error {
      projectErrors: ProjectUpdateError[]
      detailedMessage: string
    }

    const error = new Error('Unable to update issue projects') as ProjectUpdateErrorWithDetails
    error.projectErrors = projectErrors
    error.detailedMessage = errorMessage
    throw error
  }
}

import {isCopilot} from '@github-ui/assignees/copilot-user'
import {validateIssueTitle} from '@github-ui/entity-validators'
import {useIssueCreateDataContext} from '@github-ui/issue-create/IssueCreateDataContext'
import {instanceOfIssueFormData, type IssueCreatePayload} from '@github-ui/issue-create/Model'
import type {IssueFormRef} from '@github-ui/issue-form/Types'
import type {RepositoryPickerRepository} from '@github-ui/item-picker/RepositoryPicker'
import {Button} from '@primer/react'
import React, {type RefObject, useCallback, useState} from 'react'
import {useRelayEnvironment} from 'react-relay'

import type {DraftIssue} from '../content-preview-types'
import {commitUpdateIssueMutation, type OnUpdateProps} from './update-issue-mutation'
import {fetchIssue} from './use-issue-query'
import type {SavedIssue} from './use-on-save-callback'
import {useTransformCopilotImageURLsFunction} from './use-transform-copilot-image-urls-function'

export type UpdateIssueButtonProps = {
  issueRepo: RepositoryPickerRepository
  draftIssue: DraftIssue
  template?: IssueCreatePayload
  issueFormRef: RefObject<IssueFormRef | null>
  onUpdate?: (issue: SavedIssue) => void
  onUpdateError?: (error: Error | OnUpdateProps) => void
  onClose: () => void
}

export function UpdateIssueButton({
  issueRepo,
  draftIssue,
  template,
  issueFormRef,
  onUpdate,
  onUpdateError,
  onClose,
}: UpdateIssueButtonProps) {
  const environment = useRelayEnvironment()
  const [isUpdating, setIsUpdating] = useState(false)

  const {clearSessionData, assignees, labels, milestone, issueType, projects} = useIssueCreateDataContext()

  // Apply permission filtering to the metadata before sending to mutation
  const getPermissionFilteredMetadata = useCallback(() => {
    const permissions = issueRepo.viewerIssueCreationPermissions

    return {
      // Null values mean the value is being unset
      // Undefined values mean the value is not being changed
      assignees: permissions?.assignable ? assignees : undefined,
      labels: permissions?.labelable ? labels : undefined,
      milestone: permissions?.milestoneable ? milestone : undefined,
      issueType: permissions?.typeable ? issueType || null : undefined,
      projects: permissions?.triageable ? projects : undefined,
      hasInaccessibleProject: permissions?.triageable ? draftIssue.rawProjectsInput?.includes('') : undefined,
    }
  }, [
    assignees,
    draftIssue.rawProjectsInput,
    issueRepo.viewerIssueCreationPermissions,
    issueType,
    labels,
    milestone,
    projects,
  ])

  const onClearSession = useCallback(() => {
    clearSessionData()
    issueFormRef.current?.clearSessionStorage()
  }, [clearSessionData, issueFormRef])

  const clearOnSave = useCallback(() => {
    onClearSession()
    onClose?.()
  }, [onClearSession, onClose])

  const transformCopilotImageUrls = useTransformCopilotImageURLsFunction()

  const handleUpdate = useCallback(
    async (e: React.MouseEvent<HTMLButtonElement>) => {
      e.preventDefault()

      setIsUpdating(true)

      try {
        // Instead of using the existingIssue from the Preview, we opt to fetch
        // the current issue state right before the mutation. This ensures we are not
        // operating against stale data, and apply the state the user expects.
        const [owner, repo] = draftIssue.repository?.split('/') ?? []
        if (!owner || !repo || !draftIssue.issueNumber) {
          const error = new Error('Invalid repository information')
          error.name = 'OTHER'
          onUpdateError?.(error)
          setIsUpdating(false)
          return
        }

        // Fetch the current issue state from the network to ensure we have the latest data
        const currentIssue = await fetchIssue(
          environment,
          owner,
          repo,
          draftIssue.issueNumber,
          true, // forceNetwork = true to bypass cache
        )

        if (!currentIssue) {
          const error = new Error('Issue not found or no longer accessible')
          error.name = 'ISSUE_NOT_FOUND'
          onUpdateError?.(error)
          setIsUpdating(false)
          return
        }

        // Since we can't submit directly through CreateIssueForm, we need to validate the inputs manually
        // TODO: we need a better way to handle validation
        const inputValidationResult = validateIssueTitle(draftIssue.name)
        if (!inputValidationResult.isValid) {
          const error = new Error(inputValidationResult.errorMessage || 'Invalid title')
          error.name = 'INVALID_TITLE'
          onUpdateError?.(error)
          return
        }

        let issueBodyToSave = draftIssue.body ?? ''

        const isIssueForm = template && instanceOfIssueFormData(template.data)
        if (isIssueForm && issueFormRef.current) {
          const invalidInputs = issueFormRef.current.getInvalidInputs()
          if (invalidInputs.length > 0) {
            const error = new Error('Please fix the highlighted errors in the form.')
            error.name = 'INVALID_FORM_INPUTS'
            onUpdateError?.(error)
            invalidInputs[0]?.focus()
            return
          } else {
            issueBodyToSave = issueFormRef.current.markdown() ?? ''
          }
        }

        issueBodyToSave = await transformCopilotImageUrls(issueRepo, issueBodyToSave)

        const currentAssignees =
          currentIssue.assignees?.nodes?.filter(
            (assignee): assignee is NonNullable<typeof assignee> => assignee != null,
          ) ?? []
        const isCopilotCurrentlyAssigned = currentAssignees.some(assignee => isCopilot(assignee.login))
        const viewerCanAssign = issueRepo.viewerIssueCreationPermissions?.assignable ?? false
        const canUseCopilotAssignment =
          viewerCanAssign &&
          !currentIssue.repository?.isArchived &&
          !!currentIssue.repository?.isCopilotAgentEnabled &&
          !isCopilotCurrentlyAssigned

        // Get permission-filtered metadata to respect user permissions
        const filteredMetadata = getPermissionFilteredMetadata()
        // Prepare fields that need special processing in the button
        const proposedChanges = {
          body: issueBodyToSave,
          assignees: filteredMetadata.assignees,
          labels: filteredMetadata.labels,
          milestone: filteredMetadata.milestone,
          issueType: filteredMetadata.issueType,
          projects: filteredMetadata.projects,
          hasInaccessibleProject: filteredMetadata.hasInaccessibleProject,
        }

        // Use the new reworked mutation that compares current vs draft
        await commitUpdateIssueMutation({
          environment,
          currentIssue, // Use the fresh issue data
          draftIssue,
          proposedChanges,
          canUseCopilotAssignment,
          onCompleted: (onUpdateProps: OnUpdateProps) => {
            if (onUpdateProps.result.failed.length > 0) {
              // Any errors: pass OnUpdateProps to onUpdateError for handling
              onUpdateError?.(onUpdateProps)
            } else if (onUpdateProps.result.successful.length > 0 && onUpdateProps.result.issue) {
              // No errors: use onUpdate with converted issue
              clearOnSave()
              onClose()

              // Pass SavedIssue directly to the callback
              onUpdate?.(onUpdateProps.result.issue)
            } else {
              // No operations performed (likely no changes detected)
              clearOnSave()
              onClose()
              // Don't call onUpdate since nothing changed
            }
          },
        })
      } catch {
        // Mutation errors are handled by the onError callback
        const error = new Error('Update failed')
        error.name = 'OTHER'
        onUpdateError?.(error)
      } finally {
        setIsUpdating(false)
      }
    },
    [
      clearOnSave,
      draftIssue,
      environment,
      getPermissionFilteredMetadata,
      issueFormRef,
      issueRepo,
      onClose,
      onUpdate,
      onUpdateError,
      template,
      transformCopilotImageUrls,
    ],
  )

  return (
    <>
      <Button
        variant="primary"
        data-testid="update-issue-button"
        onClick={handleUpdate}
        disabled={isUpdating || !draftIssue.bodyVersion}
      >
        {isUpdating ? 'Updating...' : 'Update'}
      </Button>
    </>
  )
}

// eslint-disable-next-line no-restricted-imports
import {useChatState} from '@github-ui/copilot-chat/CopilotChatContext'
import {sendEvent} from '@github-ui/hydro-analytics'
import {getSafeConfig} from '@github-ui/issue-create/getSafeConfig'
import {IssueCreateContextProvider} from '@github-ui/issue-create/IssueCreateContext'
import type {PreselectedDataTypes} from '@github-ui/issue-create/IssueCreateDataContext'
import type {IssueFormRef} from '@github-ui/issue-form/Types'
import {IssueViewerLoading} from '@github-ui/issue-viewer/IssueViewerLoading'
import type {RepositoryPickerRepository} from '@github-ui/item-picker/RepositoryPicker'
import {testIdProps} from '@github-ui/test-id-props'
import {IssueDraftIcon, RepoIcon} from '@primer/octicons-react'
import {Banner, Stack} from '@primer/react'
import {clsx} from 'clsx'
import {useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState, useSyncExternalStore} from 'react'

import {BROWSER_ANIMATION_DURATION} from '../../../utils/constants'
import type {DraftIssue} from '../content-preview-types'
import {FailureState} from '../FailureState'
import filePreviewStyles from '../FilePreview.module.css'
import issuePreviewStyles from '../IssuePreview.module.css'
import {VersionSelector} from '../VersionSelector'
import {CreateIssueForm} from './CreateIssueForm'
import styles from './CreateIssuePreview.module.css'
import {TemplatePicker} from './TemplatePicker'
import type {OnUpdateProps, ProjectUpdateError} from './update-issue-mutation'
import {UpdateIssueButton} from './UpdateIssueButton'
import {useOnChangeCallback} from './use-on-change-callback'
import {useOnFieldChangeCallback} from './use-on-field-change-callback'
import type {ContinuableError, SavedIssue} from './use-on-save-callback'
import {useOnSaveCallback} from './use-on-save-callback'
import {useRepoQuery} from './use-repo-query'
import {useDraftIssueFormTemplateQuery} from './use-template-query'
import {useTransformCopilotImageURLsFunction} from './use-transform-copilot-image-urls-function'

export const METADATA_FOOTER_BREAKPOINT = 1024

export type UpdateIssuePreviewProps = {
  issue: DraftIssue
  isPreviewOpening: boolean
  onClose: () => void
}

export function UpdateIssuePreview({isPreviewOpening, issue: updateIssue, onClose}: UpdateIssuePreviewProps) {
  // There are rendering issues if we draw the form while animating the workbench opening, so we show loading until the animation is done.
  const [isAnimating, setIsAnimating] = useState(isPreviewOpening)
  const [updateErrors, setUpdateErrors] = useState<Error[] | null>(null)
  const [prevIssueId, setPrevIssueId] = useState(updateIssue.id)

  useEffect(() => {
    if (isAnimating) {
      const timer = setTimeout(() => setIsAnimating(false), BROWSER_ANIMATION_DURATION)
      return () => clearTimeout(timer)
    }
  }, [isAnimating])

  // Reset updateErrors when switching to a different issue
  if (prevIssueId !== updateIssue.id) {
    setPrevIssueId(updateIssue.id)
    setUpdateErrors(null)
  }

  const {selectedThreadID} = useChatState()

  const isRepositoryStreamed = updateIssue.repository != null || !updateIssue.isStreaming
  const isIssueBodyVersionStreamed = updateIssue.bodyVersion != null || !updateIssue.isStreaming
  const isMalformedDraft = isIssueBodyVersionStreamed && !updateIssue.bodyVersion

  // Load repository data
  const [owner, repo] = updateIssue.repository?.split('/') ?? []
  const {isLoading: loadingIssueRepo, data: issueRepo} = useRepoQuery({owner, repo})
  const {isLoading: loadingTemplate, data: templateData} = useDraftIssueFormTemplateQuery({
    repoId: issueRepo?.id ?? '',
    filename: updateIssue.template || '',
  })

  const noRepoAccess = useMemo(() => {
    return (
      issueRepo != null &&
      (!issueRepo.hasIssuesEnabled ||
        (issueRepo.viewerInteractionLimitReasonHTML != null && issueRepo.viewerInteractionLimitReasonHTML?.length > 0))
    )
  }, [issueRepo])

  const transformCopilotImageUrls = useTransformCopilotImageURLsFunction()

  const onSaveCallback = useOnSaveCallback()
  const onUpdateCallback = useCallback(
    async (issue: SavedIssue) => {
      await onSaveCallback([issue])
    },
    [onSaveCallback],
  )

  const onUpdateError = useCallback(
    async (error: Error | OnUpdateProps) => {
      let errorType = 'OTHER'

      // Handle OnUpdateProps from button (mutations with failures)
      if (error && typeof error === 'object' && 'result' in error) {
        const {result} = error
        if (result.issue === undefined) {
          // Handle case where issue is not defined
          const unknownError = new Error('Issue data is unavailable')
          unknownError.name = 'UNKNOWN_ERROR'
          setUpdateErrors([unknownError])
          return
        }

        // Extract databaseId from result.issue when available
        const sourceIssueId = result.issue?.repository?.databaseId

        // Check if all mutations failed
        if (result.failed.length > 0 && result.successful.length === 0) {
          // All mutations failed - show error banner for each failure
          errorType = 'MULTIPLE_UPDATE_FAILURES'

          sendEvent('dotcom_chat.activate.error', {
            target: 'ERROR_ISSUE_UPDATE_FAILED',
            mode: 'immersive',
            message: `Issue update failed: ${result.failed.length} operations failed`,
            draftIssueId: updateIssue.id,
            sourceIssueId,
            repository: updateIssue.repository,
            errorType,
            failedOperationCount: result.failed.length,
            failedOperations: JSON.stringify(
              result.failed.map(f => ({operation: f.operation, error: f.error.message})),
            ),
          })

          // Create an error for each failed operation
          const errors = result.failed.map(failedOp => {
            const failureError = new Error(failedOp.error.message)
            failureError.name = `${failedOp.operation.toUpperCase()}_UPDATE_FAILED`
            ;(failureError as Error & {operation: string}).operation = failedOp.operation

            // For project operations, extract projectErrors from the original error
            if (failedOp.operation === 'projects') {
              const originalError = failedOp.error as Error & {projectErrors?: ProjectUpdateError[]}
              if (originalError.projectErrors) {
                ;(failureError as Error & {projectErrors: ProjectUpdateError[]}).projectErrors =
                  originalError.projectErrors
              }
            }

            return failureError
          })

          setUpdateErrors(errors)
        } else if (result.failed.length > 0 && result.successful.length > 0) {
          // Partial failure - continue with onSaveCallback logic with additional message
          const failedOperations = result.failed.map(f => f.operation).join(', ')
          result.issue.errors = result.failed.map(failedOp => {
            const continuableError: ContinuableError = {
              message: failedOp.error.message,
              type: 'update',
            }
            return continuableError
          })
          const successfulOperations = result.successful.join(', ')

          errorType = 'PARTIAL_UPDATE_FAILURE'
          sendEvent('dotcom_chat.activate.error', {
            target: 'ERROR_ISSUE_UPDATE_PARTIAL_FAILURE',
            mode: 'immersive',
            message: `Issue update partial failure. Failed: ${failedOperations}. Succeeded: ${successfulOperations}`,
            draftIssueId: updateIssue.id,
            sourceIssueId,
            repository: updateIssue.repository,
            errorType,
            failedOperationCount: result.failed.length,
            failedOperations: JSON.stringify(
              result.failed.map(f => ({operation: f.operation, error: f.error.message})),
            ),
            successfulOperations: JSON.stringify(result.successful),
          })

          // Continue with success flow but with warning message
          try {
            await onSaveCallback([result.issue])
          } catch {
            // Fall back to error display if we can't continue with success flow
            const partialError = new Error(`Partial failure: ${failedOperations} failed`)
            partialError.name = errorType
            setUpdateErrors([partialError])
          }
        }

        return // OnUpdateProps handling complete
      }

      // Handle standard error
      if (error instanceof Error) {
        const standardError = new Error(error.message)
        if ('name' in error) {
          standardError.name = error.name
        } else {
          standardError.name = errorType
        }

        sendEvent('dotcom_chat.activate.error', {
          target: 'ERROR_ISSUE_UPDATE_FAILED',
          mode: 'immersive',
          message: error.message,
          issueId: updateIssue.id,
          errorType,
        })

        setUpdateErrors([standardError])
      }
    },
    [updateIssue, onSaveCallback],
  )

  const onChange = useOnChangeCallback(updateIssue)
  const onFieldChange = useOnFieldChangeCallback(updateIssue, onChange)

  const containerRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null)
  const renderNarrowView = useRenderNarrowView(containerRef)

  const issueFormRef = useRef<IssueFormRef>(null)

  const errorBanners = useMemo(() => {
    if (!updateErrors || updateErrors.length === 0) return null

    return updateErrors.map(error => {
      let title: string
      let description: string

      switch (error.name) {
        case 'LABELS_UPDATE_FAILED':
          title = 'Failed to update labels'
          description = 'Could not update issue labels. Please try again.'
          break
        case 'ASSIGNEES_UPDATE_FAILED':
          title = 'Failed to update assignees'
          description = 'Could not update issue assignees. Please try again.'
          break
        case 'ASSIGNCOPILOT_UPDATE_FAILED':
          title = 'Failed to update assignees'
          description = 'Could not assign Copilot to the issue. Please try again.'
          break
        case 'MILESTONE_UPDATE_FAILED':
          title = 'Failed to update milestone'
          description = 'Could not update issue milestone. Please try again.'
          break
        case 'ISSUETYPE_UPDATE_FAILED':
          title = 'Failed to update issue type'
          description = 'Could not update issue type. Please try again.'
          break
        case 'PROJECTS_UPDATE_FAILED':
          title = 'Failed to update projects'
          description = 'Could not update project assignments. Please try again.'
          break
        case 'CONTENT_UPDATE_FAILED':
          title = 'Failed to update content'
          description = 'Could not update issue title or body. Please try again.'
          break
        case 'FORBIDDEN':
          title = 'Permission Denied'
          description = "You don't have permission to update this issue. Please contact the repo owner."
          break
        case 'NOT_FOUND':
          title = 'Issue Not Found'
          description = 'The issue you are trying to update could not be found or no longer exists.'
          break
        case 'INVALID_TITLE':
          title = 'Invalid issue title'
          description = error.message!
          break
        case 'INVALID_FORM_INPUTS':
          title = 'Invalid form inputs'
          description = error.message!
          break
        case 'STALE_DATA':
          title = 'Issue Outdated'
          description =
            'The issue you are editing has changed. Please copy your edits and have Copilot draft a new update.'
          break
        case 'PROJECT_UPDATE_FAILED': {
          title = 'Failed to update project assignment'
          const projectErrors = (error as Error & {projectErrors?: ProjectUpdateError[]}).projectErrors ?? []
          if (projectErrors.length > 0) {
            const addErrors = projectErrors.filter((err: ProjectUpdateError) => err.operation === 'add')
            const removeErrors = projectErrors.filter((err: ProjectUpdateError) => err.operation === 'remove')

            const errorParts: string[] = []
            if (addErrors.length > 0) {
              errorParts.push(
                `Failed to add to ${addErrors.length} project(s): ${addErrors
                  .map((err: ProjectUpdateError) => `"${err.projectTitle}"`)
                  .join(', ')}`,
              )
            }

            if (removeErrors.length > 0) {
              errorParts.push(
                `Failed to remove ${removeErrors.length} project(s): ${removeErrors
                  .map((err: ProjectUpdateError) => `"${err.projectTitle}"`)
                  .join(', ')}`,
              )
            }

            description = `The issue was updated successfully, but there were problems with project assignments. ${errorParts.join(
              '. ',
            )}.`
          } else {
            description = 'The issue was updated successfully, but there were problems with project assignments.'
          }
          break
        }
        default:
          title = 'An error has occurred'
          description = 'Please try again later.'
          break
      }

      const variant = error.name === 'PROJECT_UPDATE_FAILED' ? 'warning' : 'critical'
      return <Banner key={`error-${error.name}`} variant={variant} title={title} description={description} />
    })
  }, [updateErrors])

  const preselectedData = useMemo<PreselectedDataTypes>(() => ({repository: issueRepo ?? undefined}), [issueRepo])

  if (isAnimating || !isRepositoryStreamed || loadingIssueRepo || loadingTemplate) {
    return <IssueViewerLoading optionConfig={{useViewportQueries: false}} />
  }

  if (!issueRepo) {
    return renderRepositoryNotFoundBlankslate(updateIssue.repository)
  }

  if (noRepoAccess) {
    return renderNoRepoAccessBlankslate(issueRepo)
  }

  if (isMalformedDraft) {
    return renderMalformedDraftBlankslate()
  }

  return (
    <IssueCreateContextProvider
      optionConfig={getSafeConfig({
        storageKeyPrefix: `${updateIssue.id}@${selectedThreadID!}`,
        insidePortal: renderNarrowView,
        disableCopilotAssignmentModal: true,
        isCopilotImmersive: true,
      })}
      preselectedData={preselectedData}
      {...testIdProps('update-issue-preview')}
    >
      <div className={clsx(issuePreviewStyles.container, styles.container)} ref={containerRef}>
        <div className={filePreviewStyles.toolbar}>
          <div className={clsx(filePreviewStyles.toolbarLeft, styles.toolbarLeft)}>
            <VersionSelector item={updateIssue} />
          </div>
          <div className={filePreviewStyles.toolbarActions}>
            <UpdateIssueButton
              issueRepo={issueRepo}
              draftIssue={updateIssue}
              template={templateData ?? undefined}
              issueFormRef={issueFormRef}
              onUpdate={onUpdateCallback}
              onUpdateError={onUpdateError}
              onClose={onClose}
            />
          </div>
        </div>
        <div className={styles.form}>
          {updateErrors && errorBanners}
          <Stack direction={'horizontal'} align={'center'} gap={'condensed'} className="py-2">
            <RepoIcon />
            <b>{`${issueRepo.owner.login}/${issueRepo.name}`}</b>
            <TemplatePicker draftIssue={updateIssue} />
          </Stack>
          <CreateIssueForm
            draftIssue={updateIssue}
            template={templateData ?? undefined}
            issueFormRef={issueFormRef}
            onChange={onChange}
            onClose={onClose}
            onBeforeCreate={issueBody => transformCopilotImageUrls(issueRepo, issueBody)}
            onFieldChange={onFieldChange}
          />
        </div>
      </div>
    </IssueCreateContextProvider>
  )
}

function renderMalformedDraftBlankslate() {
  return (
    <FailureState
      title="Malformed draft issue"
      description="The drafted issue contains malformed data and the preview is not available. Please retry."
      icon={IssueDraftIcon}
    />
  )
}

function renderNoRepoAccessBlankslate(repository: RepositoryPickerRepository) {
  if (!repository.hasIssuesEnabled) {
    return (
      <FailureState
        title="Issues are disabled"
        description="Issues are disabled on this repository. Please select a different repository above to continue updating your issue."
        icon={IssueDraftIcon}
      />
    )
  }

  if (repository.viewerInteractionLimitReasonHTML) {
    return (
      <FailureState
        title="You cannot update issues in this repository"
        description={repository.viewerInteractionLimitReasonHTML}
        icon={IssueDraftIcon}
      />
    )
  }
}

// TODO: better blankslate?
function renderRepositoryNotFoundBlankslate(repository?: string) {
  if (!repository) {
    return (
      <FailureState
        title="Malformed draft issue"
        description="The drafted issue contains malformed data and the preview is not available. Please retry."
        icon={IssueDraftIcon}
      />
    )
  }

  return (
    <FailureState
      title={`${repository} is not found`}
      description="Please verify if the repository exists and is accessible before trying again."
      icon={IssueDraftIcon}
    />
  )
}

function useRenderNarrowView<T extends HTMLElement>(elementRef: React.RefObject<T | null>) {
  const subscribe = (notify: () => void) => {
    const resizeObserver = new ResizeObserver(notify)
    resizeObserver.observe(document.documentElement)
    return () => {
      resizeObserver.unobserve(document.documentElement)
      resizeObserver.disconnect()
    }
  }

  const onChange = () => {
    if (!elementRef.current) return true

    return elementRef.current.clientWidth < METADATA_FOOTER_BREAKPOINT
  }

  return useSyncExternalStore(subscribe, onChange)
}

import {fetchAssignees} from '@github-ui/item-picker-assignee-tsq'
import {getActorCapabilities} from '@github-ui/item-picker-assignee-tsq/utils/assignee-picker'
import {useQuery, useQueryClient} from '@github-ui/react-query'

export function useAssigneesQuery({
  owner,
  repo,
  assignees,
}: {
  owner: string | undefined
  repo: string | undefined
  assignees: string[] | undefined
}) {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient()
  const capabilities = getActorCapabilities({includeAuthorableBots: false, includeAssignableBots: true})
  const queryKey = ['copilot-immersive-assignees', JSON.stringify(queryClient), capabilities, owner, repo, assignees]

  const queryFn = async () => {
    if (!owner || !repo || !assignees) return []

    // TSQ implementation uses loginNames parameter to fetch specific assignees
    const response = await fetchAssignees(queryClient, {
      owner,
      repo,
      query: '',
      loginNames: assignees,
      capabilities,
    })
    return response.assignees
  }

  return useQuery({
    queryKey,
    queryFn,
  })
}

import {isCopilot} from '@github-ui/assignees/copilot-user'
import type {DraftIssueProjectReference} from '@github-ui/copilot-chat/utils/copilot-chat-types'
import {serializeDraftIssueProjects} from '@github-ui/copilot-chat/utils/draft-issue-serialization'
import {isFeatureEnabled} from '@github-ui/feature-flags'
import type {IssueCreatePayload} from '@github-ui/issue-create/Model'
import {instanceOfIssueFormData} from '@github-ui/issue-create/Model'
import type {IssueFormRef} from '@github-ui/issue-form/Types'
import {fetchRepository, type RepositoryPickerRepository} from '@github-ui/item-picker/RepositoryPicker'
import {useMutation, useQuery} from '@github-ui/react-query'
import {reactFetchJSON} from '@github-ui/verified-fetch'
import type {RefObject} from 'react'
import {useRelayEnvironment} from 'react-relay'
import {Environment} from 'relay-runtime'

import type {DraftIssue, DraftOrExistingIssue} from '../content-preview-types'
import {fetchRepositoryDefaultBranch} from '../utils/use-repository-default-branch'
import {type TreeNode, visitDependenciesAsync} from './use-draft-issue-tree-map'
import type {ContinuableError} from './use-on-save-callback'
import {useTransformCopilotImageURLsFunction} from './use-transform-copilot-image-urls-function'

type DraftIssueMetadata = {
  title: string
  tag: string
  repository_nwo: string
  body?: string
  parent_tag?: string
  labels?: string[]
  assignees?: string[]
  milestone?: string
  template?: string
  issue_type?: string
  projects?: Array<DraftIssueProjectReference | string>
  blocked_by_tags?: string[]
  agent_assignment?: AgentAssignmentAttributes
}
type ExistingIssueMetadata = {
  tag: string
  parent_tag?: string
  repository_nwo: string
  number: number
  blocked_by_tags?: string[]
  agent_assignment?: AgentAssignmentAttributes
}

type AgentAssignmentAttributes = {
  target_repo?: string
  base_ref?: string
  custom_instructions?: string
  custom_agent?: string
  model?: string
}

type IssueMetadata = DraftIssueMetadata | ExistingIssueMetadata

type BulkCreateResponse = {
  jobStatusUrl: string
  jobId: number
}

type IssueIdentifiers = {
  tag: string
  id: number
  number: number
  href: string
  repository_id: number
  errors?: ContinuableError[]
}

export type BulkCreateStatusResponse = {
  job: {
    state: 'pending' | 'queued' | 'started' | 'success' | 'error'
    error_message: string | null
    percentage: number
    completed_issues: IssueIdentifiers[]
  }
}

export function useBulkCreateMutationWithStatus({
  issueNode,
  issueFormRef,
  issueTemplate,
}: {
  issueNode: TreeNode<DraftOrExistingIssue> | undefined
  issueFormRef?: RefObject<IssueFormRef | null>
  issueTemplate?: IssueCreatePayload | null
}) {
  const mutation = useBulkCreateMutation({issueNode, issueFormRef, issueTemplate})
  const query = useBulkCreateStatusQuery(mutation.data?.jobStatusUrl)

  return {
    // Caller will invoke the mutation
    mutate: mutation.mutate,
    // But get results from the job status endpoint
    data: query.data,
    // We coalesce the errors
    error: mutation.error || query.error,
    // As well as the status checks
    isError: mutation.isError || query.isError,
    // If either `fetch` is running, _or_ the job is still running
    isLoading:
      mutation.isPending || query.isLoading || ['pending', 'queued', 'started'].includes(query.data?.job.state || ''),
    // If the job completed successfully
    isComplete: query.data?.job.state === 'success',
  }
}

function useBulkCreateMutation({
  issueNode,
  issueFormRef,
  issueTemplate,
}: {
  issueNode: TreeNode<DraftOrExistingIssue> | undefined
  issueFormRef?: RefObject<IssueFormRef | null>
  issueTemplate?: IssueCreatePayload | null
}) {
  const environment = useRelayEnvironment()
  const transformImageUrls = useTransformCopilotImageURLsFunction()
  const isSingleIssue = !!issueNode && !issueNode.parent && issueNode.children.length === 0

  return useMutation({
    mutationKey: ['copilot-issues-bulk-create', issueNode?.item.tag],
    mutationFn: async () => {
      if (!issueNode) {
        throw new Error('Cannot create issue: issue node is not yet available')
      }

      // Flatten the tree into the list expected by the API.
      const issues: IssueMetadata[] = []
      const repositories = new Map<string, RepositoryPickerRepository | null>()
      await visitDependenciesAsync(issueNode, async node => {
        const draft = node.item
        if (draft.type === 'existing-issue') {
          // Existing issues don't need any additional transformation,
          // as we're not editing for content
          issues.push({
            tag: draft.tag,
            // eslint-disable-next-line camelcase
            parent_tag: draft.parentTag,
            // eslint-disable-next-line camelcase
            repository_nwo: draft.repository,
            number: draft.number,
            // Rather than referencing the `blockedByTags` directly from the draft issue, we use the node references
            // to ensure we're covering relationships that Copilot may have returned in either direction.
            // eslint-disable-next-line camelcase
            blocked_by_tags: node.blockedBy.length ? node.blockedBy.map(dependency => dependency.item.tag) : undefined,
          })
          return
        }

        let issueBodyToSave = draft.body

        // For single issues with issue forms, use form markdown instead of draft body.
        // The validation has already performed in CreateIssueButton, so if we get here,
        // we know that the form content is valid.
        if (isSingleIssue && issueTemplate && instanceOfIssueFormData(issueTemplate.data) && issueFormRef?.current) {
          issueBodyToSave = issueFormRef.current.markdown() ?? draft.body
        }

        if (issueBodyToSave && draft.repository) {
          let repository = repositories.get(draft.repository)
          if (!repository) {
            const [repoOwner, repoName] = draft.repository.split('/')
            if (repoOwner != null && repoName != null) {
              repository = await fetchRepository(environment, repoOwner, repoName)
              repositories.set(draft.repository, repository)
            }
          }
          issueBodyToSave = await transformImageUrls(repository, issueBodyToSave)
        }

        issues.push({
          title: draft.name,
          tag: draft.tag,
          // eslint-disable-next-line camelcase
          repository_nwo: draft.repository!,
          body: issueBodyToSave,
          // eslint-disable-next-line camelcase
          parent_tag: draft.parentTag,
          labels: draft.labels,
          assignees: draft.assignees,
          milestone: draft.milestone,
          // eslint-disable-next-line camelcase
          issue_type: draft.issueType,
          template: issueTemplate?.fileName,
          // Send project objects with both id and title when feature flag is enabled
          // Otherwise send just titles for backward compatibility
          projects: serializeDraftIssueProjects(draft.projects),
          // Rather than referencing the `blockedByTags` directly from the draft issue, we use the node references
          // to ensure we're covering relationships that Copilot may have returned in either direction.
          // eslint-disable-next-line camelcase
