          blocked_by_tags: node.blockedBy.length ? node.blockedBy.map(dependency => dependency.item.tag) : undefined,
          // eslint-disable-next-line camelcase
          agent_assignment: await createAgentAssignment(draft, environment),
        })
      })

      const bulkCreateResponse = await reactFetchJSON('/copilot_issues/bulk_create', {
        method: 'POST',
        body: {
          data: {
            issues,
          },
        },
      })

      if (bulkCreateResponse.status === 429) {
        const rateLimitData = (await bulkCreateResponse.json()) as {error: string}
        throw new Error(rateLimitData.error || 'Rate limit exceeded. Please try again later.')
      }

      if (!bulkCreateResponse.ok) {
        const errorData = (await bulkCreateResponse.json()) as {error: {issue_tag: string; message: string}}
        throw new Error(errorData.error.message || `Failed to create issues.`)
      }

      return (await bulkCreateResponse.json()) as BulkCreateResponse
    },
  })
}

export async function createAgentAssignment(
  draft: DraftIssue,
  environment: Environment,
): Promise<AgentAssignmentAttributes | undefined> {
  if (!isFeatureEnabled('copilot_api_bulk_create_agent_assignments')) return undefined
  if (!(draft.assignees?.some(assignee => isCopilot(assignee)) ?? false)) return undefined

  const [owner, repo] = draft.repository!.split('/')
  // By the time we've reached this code, the repo must've been validated already, so owner and repo must be defined
  const defaultBranch = await fetchRepositoryDefaultBranch(environment, owner!, repo!)
  if (!defaultBranch) return undefined
  // TODO - we can only get the additional attributes from user's selections in the "Assign to Copilot" modal,
  // and we don't have design to hook this up into our flow yet. So we'll only support Copilot assignment on default
  // branch for now, and leave other attributes as undefined to fallback to their default values.
  // https://github.com/github/copilot-voyager/issues/1036
  return {
    // eslint-disable-next-line camelcase
    target_repo: draft.repository,
    // eslint-disable-next-line camelcase
    base_ref: defaultBranch,
  }
}

function useBulkCreateStatusQuery(jobStatusUrl: string | undefined) {
  return useQuery({
    enabled: !!jobStatusUrl,
    queryKey: ['copilot-issues-bulk-create-status', jobStatusUrl],
    queryFn: async ctx => {
      const response = await reactFetchJSON(jobStatusUrl!) // query is only enabled if jobStatusUrl exists
      if (!response.ok) {
        const errorData = (await response.json()) as BulkCreateStatusResponse
        // Throwing an error will put the query into an error state.
        // However we still want to update any existing data in the cache,
        // as the job status may have succeeded on a previous iteration.
        ctx.client.setQueryData(ctx.queryKey, errorData)
        throw new Error(errorData?.job.error_message || 'Failed to fetch job status')
      }
      return (await response.json()) as BulkCreateStatusResponse
    },
    refetchInterval: query => {
      // If we threw an error, stop polling
      if (query.state.error) return false

      // If `JobStatus#finished?`, stop polling
      if (query.state.data?.job.state === 'success') return false
      if (query.state.data?.job.state === 'error') return false

      return 2000 // Poll every 2 seconds
    },
  })
}

import {validateIssueBody, validateIssueTitle} from '@github-ui/entity-validators'
import {instanceOfIssueFormData, type IssueCreatePayload} from '@github-ui/issue-create/Model'
import {IssueFormElements} from '@github-ui/issue-form/IssueFormElements'
import type {IssueFormRef} from '@github-ui/issue-form/Types'
import {fetchRepository, type RepositoryPickerRepository} from '@github-ui/item-picker/RepositoryPicker'
import {PartialEntry} from '@github-ui/react-core/partial-entry'
import {createReactRootErrorHandlers} from '@github-ui/react-core/react-root-error-handlers'
import {RelayEnvironmentProvider} from '@github-ui/relay-environment'
import {type MutableRefObject, type RefObject, useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState} from 'react'
import {flushSync} from 'react-dom'
import {createRoot} from 'react-dom/client'
import {useRelayEnvironment} from 'react-relay'
import type {Environment} from 'relay-runtime'

import type {DraftIssue, DraftOrExistingIssue} from '../content-preview-types'
import {type TreeNode, visit} from './use-draft-issue-tree-map'
import {fetchIssue} from './use-issue-query'
import {fetchTemplate} from './use-template-query'

export type ValidationResult = {
  [tag: string]: {
    errorMessages: string[]
  }
}

const MAX_ISSUE_WRITES = 60 // Must match MAX_ISSUE_WRITES in copilot_issues/bulk_create_job_status.rb
const MAX_HEIGHT = 7 // Height of the tree *not* including the parent. Must match sub-issues/utils/use-alert.ts and hierarchy/app/models/sub_issue.rb.
const MAX_SUBISSUES_BREADTH = 100 // Maximum number of sub-issues an issue can have. Must match MAXIMUM_BREADTH in hierarchy/app/models/sub_issue.rb.

// Use TREE_ERROR_KEY inside a function or hook to avoid accessing 'crypto' at module scope
export const TREE_ERROR_KEY = getTreeErrorKey()
export const MAX_ISSUE_COUNTS_ERROR = `You cannot create more than ${MAX_ISSUE_WRITES} new issues at a time. Please remove some draft issues from the tree and try again.`
export const MAX_HEIGHT_ERROR = `You cannot add more than ${MAX_HEIGHT} levels of sub-issues. Please remove some issues from the tree and try again.`

export function useBulkIssueValidation(issueNode: TreeNode<DraftOrExistingIssue>) {
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false)
  const [result, setResult] = useState<ValidationResult>({})

  const environment = useRelayEnvironment()
  const issueFormRef = useRef<IssueFormRef | null>(null)

  useEffect(() => {
    async function doValidation() {
      setLoading(true)
      setResult(await validateIssues(environment, issueFormRef, issueNode))
      setLoading(false)
    }

    void doValidation()
  }, [environment, issueNode])

  return {
    loading,
    result,
  }
}

// Returns true if the tree height exceeds MAX_HEIGHT (not including the root node)
// e.g. a tree with only a root node has height 0, a tree with root and children has height 1, etc.
// Optimized to return early when any branch exceeds MAX_HEIGHT
function isTreeHeightExceeded(node: TreeNode<DraftOrExistingIssue>, currentDepth = 0): boolean {
  // If we've already exceeded the maximum height, return true immediately
  if (currentDepth > MAX_HEIGHT) {
    return true
  }

  if (!node.children || node.children.length === 0) {
    return false
  }

  // Check each child branch - return true immediately if any exceeds the limit
  for (const child of node.children) {
    if (isTreeHeightExceeded(child, currentDepth + 1)) {
      return true
    }
  }

  return false
}

function getTreeErrorKey() {
  return crypto.randomUUID()
}

async function validateIssues(
  environment: Environment,
  issueFormRef: MutableRefObject<IssueFormRef | null>,
  issueNode: TreeNode<DraftOrExistingIssue>,
): Promise<ValidationResult> {
  const result: ValidationResult = {}

  const drafts = new Map<string, TreeNode<DraftOrExistingIssue>>()
  visit(issueNode, node => drafts.set(node.item.tag, node))

  // Check for total number of new issues and tree height
  if (drafts.size > MAX_ISSUE_WRITES) {
    result[TREE_ERROR_KEY] = {
      errorMessages: [MAX_ISSUE_COUNTS_ERROR],
    }
    return result
  }

  if (isTreeHeightExceeded(issueNode)) {
    result[TREE_ERROR_KEY] = {
      errorMessages: [MAX_HEIGHT_ERROR],
    }
    return result
  }

  for (const [tag, node] of drafts) {
    const draft = node.item
    result[tag] = {errorMessages: []}

    if (draft.type === 'new-issue') {
      // Sometimes Copilot returns issues without a repository.
      // The UX can correct for this on render, but the user may not be looking at every sub-issue.
      // If this issue doesn't have a repository, default to the same repository as its parent issue.
      if (!draft.repository && draft.parentTag) {
        draft.repository = drafts.get(draft.parentTag)?.item?.repository
      }

      // If it still doesn't have a repository, fail validation
      if (!draft.repository) {
        result[tag].errorMessages.push('Repository is required')
        continue
      }
    }

    const [owner, repo] = draft.repository?.split('/') || []
    if (!owner || !repo) {
      result[tag].errorMessages.push('Repository must be in the format "owner/repo"')
      continue
    }

    const repository = await fetchRepository(environment, owner, repo)
    if (!repository) {
      result[tag].errorMessages.push(`Repository not found`)
      continue
    }

    if (repository.isArchived) {
      result[tag].errorMessages.push(`Repository is archived`)
      continue
    }

    if (!repository.hasIssuesEnabled) {
      result[tag].errorMessages.push(`Repository does not have issues enabled`)
      continue
    }

    if (repository.viewerInteractionLimitReasonHTML) {
      result[tag].errorMessages.push(repository.viewerInteractionLimitReasonHTML)
      continue
    }

    // If this issue has sub-issues, the user must have permission to create subissues in this repository
    if (
      node.children.length > 0 &&
      (repository.isLocked ||
        (repository.viewerCanPush === false && repository.viewerIssueCreationPermissions?.triageable === false))
    ) {
      result[tag].errorMessages.push('You do not have permission to create subissues in this repository')
      continue
    }

    if (draft.type === 'existing-issue') {
      if (!draft.number) {
        result[tag].errorMessages.push('Issue number is required')
        continue
      }

      // Validate that existing issues actually... exist
      const existingIssue = await fetchIssue(environment, owner, repo, draft.number)
      if (!existingIssue) {
        result[tag].errorMessages.push(`Issue not found`)
        continue
      }

      // Check if adding new sub-issues would exceed the maximum breadth
      if (node.children.length > 0) {
        const existingSubIssueCount = existingIssue.subIssues?.totalCount ?? 0
        const newSubIssueCount = node.children.length
        if (existingSubIssueCount + newSubIssueCount > MAX_SUBISSUES_BREADTH) {
          result[tag].errorMessages.push(
            `This issue already has ${existingSubIssueCount} sub-issue${
              existingSubIssueCount === 1 ? '' : 's'
            }. Adding ${newSubIssueCount} more would exceed the maximum of ${MAX_SUBISSUES_BREADTH} sub-issues.`,
          )
          continue
        }
      }

      // Everything past this point only pertains to creating new issues
      continue
    }

    // Copilot will sometimes return the template with full path, rather than just the filename
    const templateFilename = draft.template?.split('/').findLast(Boolean)
    const template = templateFilename ? await fetchTemplate(environment, repository.id, templateFilename) : undefined
    if (draft.template && !template) {
      result[tag].errorMessages.push(`Issue template not found`)
      continue
    }

    if (!repository.isBlankIssuesEnabled && !template) {
      result[tag].errorMessages.push('Issue template is required')
      continue
    }

    const titleValidation = validateIssueTitle(draft.name)
    if (!titleValidation.isValid) {
      result[tag].errorMessages.push(titleValidation.errorMessage || 'Issue title is invalid')
    }

    if (template && instanceOfIssueFormData(template.data)) {
      // TODO do something about incomplete template data?

      // Clear the ref before rendering the form
      issueFormRef.current = null

      const container = document.createElement('div')
      const errorHandlers = createReactRootErrorHandlers({appName: 'copilot-bulk-issue-validation'})
      const root = createRoot(container, errorHandlers)
      // Root#render is async by nature. We need to force the render in order to process and validate form fields.
      // eslint-disable-next-line @eslint-react/dom/no-flush-sync
      flushSync(() => {
        root.render(
          <PartialEntry partialName={'copilot-creates-issues-bulk-validation'} wasServerRendered={false}>
            <RelayEnvironmentProvider environment={environment}>
              <IssueFormWrapper
                repository={repository}
                draftIssue={draft}
                template={template}
                outputRef={issueFormRef}
              />
            </RelayEnvironmentProvider>
          </PartialEntry>,
        )
      })

      if (!issueFormRef.current) {
        throw new Error('IssueFormRef is not set')
      }

      // Compiler doesn't recognize that the ref.current is set after rendering
      // We just checked for null/undefined above, so this cast is safe
      const issueForm = issueFormRef.current as IssueFormRef

      try {
        const invalidInputs = issueForm.getInvalidInputs()
        if (invalidInputs.length > 0) {
          // Invalid inputs do not have any label to identify the field in error
          result[tag].errorMessages.push(`Invalid form input`)
          continue
        }

        // If there were no invalid inputs, we can set the body
        draft.body = issueForm.markdown()
      } finally {
        issueForm.clearSessionStorage()
        root.unmount()
        container.remove()
      }
    }

    const bodyValidation = validateIssueBody(draft.body ?? '')
    if (!bodyValidation.isValid) {
      result[tag].errorMessages.push(bodyValidation.errorMessage || 'Issue body is invalid')
    }
  }

  return result
}

function IssueFormWrapper({
  repository,
  draftIssue,
  template,
  outputRef,
}: {
  repository: RepositoryPickerRepository
  draftIssue: DraftIssue
  template: IssueCreatePayload
  outputRef: RefObject<IssueFormRef | null>
}) {
  const subject = useMemo(() => {
    return {
      type: 'issue' as const,
      repository: {
        databaseId: repository.databaseId!,
        nwo: `${repository.owner.login}/${repository.name}`,
        slashCommandsEnabled: repository.slashCommandsEnabled,
      },
    }
  }, [repository])

  if (!instanceOfIssueFormData(template.data)) {
    return
  }

  return (
    <IssueFormElements
      outputRef={outputRef}
      issueFormRef={template.data}
      subject={subject}
      sessionStorageKey={`copilot-creates-issues:${draftIssue.tag}`}
      defaultValuesById={draftIssue.defaultValuesById}
    />
  )
}

import {useIssueCreateDataContext} from '@github-ui/issue-create/IssueCreateDataContext'
import {formatActorLogin} from '@github-ui/item-picker-assignee-tsq/utils/assignee-picker'
import isEqual from 'lodash-es/isEqual'

import type {DraftIssue} from '../content-preview-types'
import {useAssigneesQuery} from './use-assignees-query'
import {useDraftIssueFormSync} from './use-draft-issue-form-sync'
import {useOnChangeCallback} from './use-on-change-callback'

export function useDraftIssueAssignees(draftIssue: DraftIssue) {
  const {
    repository: formRepository,
    assignees: formAssignees,
    setAssignees: setFormAssignees,
  } = useIssueCreateDataContext()
  const onChange = useOnChangeCallback(draftIssue)

  return useDraftIssueFormSync(draftIssue, {
    draftValue: draftIssue.assignees,
    formValue: formAssignees,
    setFormValue: setFormAssignees,
    onChange: (newValue, skipVersionBump) =>
      onChange({assignees: newValue.map(a => formatActorLogin(a))}, skipVersionBump),
    resolverResult: useAssigneesQuery({
      owner: formRepository?.owner.login,
      repo: formRepository?.name,
      assignees: draftIssue.assignees ?? [],
    }),
    hasPermission: repo => repo?.viewerIssueCreationPermissions?.assignable,
    isEqual,
    defaultValue: [],
  })
}

import {useIssueCreateDataContext} from '@github-ui/issue-create/IssueCreateDataContext'
import type {RepositoryPickerRepository} from '@github-ui/item-picker/RepositoryPicker'
import type {UseQueryResult} from '@github-ui/react-query'
import {useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useRef} from 'react'

import type {DraftIssue} from '../content-preview-types'

/**
 * Configuration options for synchronizing draft issue data with form state.
 *
 * @template TFormValue - The type of the form value from `IssueCreateDataContext`
 * @template TDraftValue - The type of the draft value from `DraftIssue`
 */
interface UseDraftIssueFormSyncOptions<TFormValue, TDraftValue> {
  /** The current draft value extracted from the draft issue preview object */
  draftValue: TDraftValue
  /** The current form value in the UI context */
  formValue: TFormValue
  /** Setter to update the form value in the UI context */
  setFormValue: (value: TFormValue) => void
  /**
   * Callback fired when the form value changes. This should call `useOnChangeCallback`.
   * @param newValue - The new form value
   * @param skipVersionBump - Whether to skip incrementing the draft version
   */
  onChange: (newValue: TFormValue, skipVersionBump: boolean) => void
  /**
   * Partial result of `useQuery` hook which resolves Copilot's text values into form values.
   */
  resolverResult: Pick<UseQueryResult<TFormValue>, 'isLoading' | 'data'>
  /**
   * Function to check if the user has permission to modify the specific metadata field.
   */
  hasPermission: (repository: RepositoryPickerRepository | undefined) => boolean | undefined
  /**
   * Function to determine if two form values are equal.
   */
  isEqual: (a: TFormValue | undefined, b: TFormValue | undefined) => boolean
  /**
   * The default/initial value to use as a fallback.
   */
  defaultValue: TFormValue
}

/**
 * A React hook that synchronizes draft issue data with form state, handling the
 * coordination between streaming draft data and form values.
 *
 * This hook manages the synchronization lifecycle when:
 * - Opening a preview from streaming draft data
 * - Opening a preview from completed/streamed blocks
 * - Switching between different drafts
 * - Modifying form values by the user
 *
 * The hook ensures that form values are properly resolved from draft data while respecting
 * user permissions and preventing race conditions that could lead to infinite update loops.
 */
export function useDraftIssueFormSync<TFormValue, TDraftValue>(
  draftIssue: DraftIssue,
  options: UseDraftIssueFormSyncOptions<TFormValue, TDraftValue>,
) {
  const {draftValue, formValue, setFormValue, onChange, resolverResult, hasPermission, isEqual, defaultValue} = options
  const {repository} = useIssueCreateDataContext()

  const isResolvingFormValue = useRef(true)
  const lastKnownFormValue = useRef<TFormValue | undefined>(undefined)
  const lastKnownDraft = useRef<`${DraftIssue['id']}`>(draftIssue.id)

  // Reset on switching a draft
  useEffect(() => {
    if (lastKnownDraft.current !== draftIssue.id) {
      isResolvingFormValue.current = true
      lastKnownFormValue.current = undefined
      lastKnownDraft.current = draftIssue.id
    }
  }, [draftIssue.id, formValue])

  // When switching drafts with different repositories,
  // we need to make sure the repository from context matches the draft's data
  const hasMatchingRepo = useMemo(() => {
    return draftIssue.repository && repository?.nameWithOwner === draftIssue.repository
  }, [draftIssue.repository, repository?.nameWithOwner])

  const {isLoading: loadingResolvedValue, data: resolvedValue} = resolverResult

  const isValueResolved = useMemo(() => {
    // Only process changes if data is streamed,
    // this is to prevent racing condition that leads to infinite loop
    const isValueStreamed = draftValue != null || !draftIssue.isStreaming
    return isValueStreamed && !loadingResolvedValue
  }, [draftValue, draftIssue.isStreaming, loadingResolvedValue])

  // Update resolving state
  useEffect(() => {
    if (hasMatchingRepo && !isValueResolved) {
      isResolvingFormValue.current = true
    }
  }, [hasMatchingRepo, isValueResolved])

  const onValueChange = useCallback(
    (newValue: TFormValue, skipVersionBump: boolean) => {
      if (lastKnownFormValue.current === undefined || !isEqual(lastKnownFormValue.current, newValue)) {
        onChange(newValue, skipVersionBump)
        lastKnownFormValue.current = newValue
      }
    },
    [isEqual, onChange],
  )

  // Updates from resolving draftIssue.[draftFieldName]. It only runs after:
  // - Opening a preview from streaming
  // - Opening a preview from streamed block
  // - Switching to a different draft
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!hasMatchingRepo || !isValueResolved) {
      return
    }

    if (hasPermission(repository)) {
      // Since we checked `isValueResolved`, we know that `resolvedValue` is defined
      setFormValue(resolvedValue as TFormValue)
      onValueChange(resolvedValue as TFormValue, true)
    } else if (repository != null) {
      setFormValue(defaultValue)
      onValueChange(defaultValue, true)
    }
  }, [
    hasMatchingRepo,
    isValueResolved,
    onValueChange,
    repository,
    resolvedValue,
    setFormValue,
    hasPermission,
    defaultValue,
  ])

  // Update resolving state
  useEffect(() => {
    if (
      !isResolvingFormValue.current ||
      lastKnownFormValue.current === undefined ||
      !hasMatchingRepo ||
      !isValueResolved ||
      !repository
    ) {
      return
    }

    const viewerHasPermission = hasPermission(repository)
    if (viewerHasPermission && formValue === resolvedValue) {
      isResolvingFormValue.current = false
    } else if (!viewerHasPermission && isEqual(formValue, defaultValue)) {
      isResolvingFormValue.current = false
    }
  }, [
    draftIssue.id,
    hasMatchingRepo,
    isValueResolved,
    resolvedValue,
    repository,
    formValue,
    hasPermission,
    defaultValue,
    isEqual,
  ])

  // Process form changes if not resolving from draftIssue data
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!isResolvingFormValue.current) {
      onValueChange(formValue, false)
    }
  }, [formValue, onValueChange])
}

import {useIssueCreateDataContext} from '@github-ui/issue-create/IssueCreateDataContext'
import isEqual from 'lodash-es/isEqual'

import type {DraftIssue} from '../content-preview-types'
import {useDraftIssueFormSync} from './use-draft-issue-form-sync'
import {useLabelsQuery} from './use-labels-query'
import {useOnChangeCallback} from './use-on-change-callback'

export function useDraftIssueLabels(draftIssue: DraftIssue) {
  const {repository: formRepository, labels: formLabels, setLabels: setFormLabels} = useIssueCreateDataContext()
  const onChange = useOnChangeCallback(draftIssue)

  return useDraftIssueFormSync(draftIssue, {
    draftValue: draftIssue.labels,
    formValue: formLabels,
    setFormValue: setFormLabels,
    onChange: (newValue, skipVersionBump) => onChange({labels: newValue.map(l => l.name)}, skipVersionBump),
    resolverResult: useLabelsQuery({
      owner: formRepository?.owner.login,
      repo: formRepository?.name,
      labels: draftIssue.labels ?? [],
    }),
    hasPermission: repo => repo?.viewerIssueCreationPermissions?.labelable,
    isEqual,
    defaultValue: [],
  })
}

import {useIssueCreateDataContext} from '@github-ui/issue-create/IssueCreateDataContext'
import {useMemo} from 'react'

import type {DraftIssue} from '../content-preview-types'
import {useDraftIssueFormSync} from './use-draft-issue-form-sync'
import {useMilestoneQuery} from './use-milestone-query'
import {useOnChangeCallback} from './use-on-change-callback'

function isEqual(left: string | undefined, right: string | undefined) {
  return left?.toLowerCase() === right?.toLowerCase()
}

export function useDraftIssueMilestone(draftIssue: DraftIssue) {
  const {
    repository: formRepository,
    milestone: formMilestone,
    setMilestone: setFormMilestone,
  } = useIssueCreateDataContext()
  const onChange = useOnChangeCallback(draftIssue)

  const resolverQuery = useMilestoneQuery({
    owner: formRepository?.owner.login,
    repo: formRepository?.name,
    milestone: draftIssue.milestone,
  })
  const resolvedMilestone = useMemo(() => resolverQuery.data?.[0] ?? null, [resolverQuery.data])

  return useDraftIssueFormSync(draftIssue, {
    draftValue: draftIssue.milestone,
    formValue: formMilestone,
    setFormValue: setFormMilestone,
    onChange: (newValue, skipVersionBump) => onChange({milestone: newValue?.title}, skipVersionBump),
    resolverResult: {
      isLoading: resolverQuery.isLoading,
      data: resolvedMilestone,
    },
    hasPermission: repo => repo?.viewerIssueCreationPermissions?.milestoneable,
    isEqual: (a, b) => isEqual(a?.title, b?.title),
    defaultValue: null,
  })
}

import {useIssueCreateDataContext} from '@github-ui/issue-create/IssueCreateDataContext'
import isEqual from 'lodash-es/isEqual'

import type {DraftIssue} from '../content-preview-types'
import {useDraftIssueFormSync} from './use-draft-issue-form-sync'
import {useOnChangeCallback} from './use-on-change-callback'
import {useProjectsQuery} from './use-projects-query'

export function useDraftIssueProjects(draftIssue: DraftIssue) {
  const {repository: formRepository, projects: formProjects, setProjects: setFormProjects} = useIssueCreateDataContext()
  const onChange = useOnChangeCallback(draftIssue)

  return useDraftIssueFormSync(draftIssue, {
    draftValue: draftIssue.projects,
    formValue: formProjects,
    setFormValue: setFormProjects,
    onChange: (newValue, skipVersionBump) =>
      onChange({projects: newValue.map(p => ({id: p.id, title: p.title}))}, skipVersionBump),
    resolverResult: useProjectsQuery({
      owner: formRepository?.owner.login,
      repo: formRepository?.name,
      projects: draftIssue.projects ?? [],
    }),
    hasPermission: repo => repo?.viewerIssueCreationPermissions?.triageable,
    isEqual,
    defaultValue: [],
  })
}

import {useIssueCreateDataContext} from '@github-ui/issue-create/IssueCreateDataContext'
import type {RepositoryPickerRepository} from '@github-ui/item-picker/RepositoryPicker'
import {useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useRef} from 'react'

import type {DraftIssue} from '../content-preview-types'
import {useOnChangeCallback} from './use-on-change-callback'

function isEqual(left: string | undefined, right: string | undefined) {
  return left?.toLowerCase() === right?.toLowerCase()
}

export function useDraftIssueRepository(
  draftIssue: DraftIssue,
  resolvedDraftIssueRepo: RepositoryPickerRepository | null | undefined,
  loadingDraftIssueRepo: boolean,
  topRepos: RepositoryPickerRepository[],
) {
  const {repository: formRepository, setRepository: setFormRepository} = useIssueCreateDataContext()
  const isResolvingFormRepository = useRef(true)
  const lastKnownDraft = useRef<`${DraftIssue['id']}` | null>(null)
  const lastKnownFormRepository = useRef<RepositoryPickerRepository | null | undefined>(undefined)
  const onChange = useOnChangeCallback(draftIssue)

  // Reset on switching a draft
  useEffect(() => {
    if (lastKnownDraft.current !== draftIssue.id) {
      isResolvingFormRepository.current = true
      lastKnownFormRepository.current = undefined
      lastKnownDraft.current = draftIssue.id
    }
  }, [draftIssue.id, formRepository])

  const isRepositoryResolved = useMemo(() => {
    // Only process changes if repository data is streamed,
    // this is to prevent racing condition that leads to infinite loop
    const isRepositoryStreamed = draftIssue.repository != null || !draftIssue.isStreaming
    return isRepositoryStreamed && !loadingDraftIssueRepo
  }, [draftIssue.isStreaming, draftIssue.repository, loadingDraftIssueRepo])

  // Update resolving state
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!isRepositoryResolved) {
      isResolvingFormRepository.current = true
    }
  }, [isRepositoryResolved])

  const onRepositoryChange = useCallback(
    (newRepository: RepositoryPickerRepository | undefined, skipVersionBump: boolean) => {
      if (
        lastKnownFormRepository.current === undefined ||
        !isEqual(lastKnownFormRepository.current?.nameWithOwner, newRepository?.nameWithOwner)
      ) {
        onChange({repository: newRepository?.nameWithOwner}, skipVersionBump)
        lastKnownFormRepository.current = newRepository ?? null
      }
    },
    [onChange],
  )

  // Updates from resolving draftIssue.repository. It only runs after:
  // - Opening a preview from streaming
  // - Opening a preview from streamed block
  // - Switching to a different draft
  useEffect(() => {
    // Ignore when repository are not loaded and is not resolved for initial load
    if (!isRepositoryResolved) {
      return
    }

    if (resolvedDraftIssueRepo != null) {
      setFormRepository(resolvedDraftIssueRepo)
      onRepositoryChange(resolvedDraftIssueRepo, true)
    } else if (topRepos.length > 0) {
      setFormRepository(topRepos[0])
      onRepositoryChange(topRepos[0], true)
    } else {
      setFormRepository(undefined)
      onRepositoryChange(undefined, true)
    }
  }, [isRepositoryResolved, onRepositoryChange, resolvedDraftIssueRepo, setFormRepository, topRepos])

  // Update resolving state
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!isResolvingFormRepository.current || lastKnownFormRepository.current === undefined || !isRepositoryResolved) {
      return
    }

    if (resolvedDraftIssueRepo != null && resolvedDraftIssueRepo === formRepository) {
      isResolvingFormRepository.current = false
    } else if (resolvedDraftIssueRepo == null && topRepos.length > 0 && formRepository === topRepos[0]) {
      isResolvingFormRepository.current = false
    } else if (resolvedDraftIssueRepo == null && topRepos.length <= 0 && formRepository === undefined) {
      isResolvingFormRepository.current = false
    }
  }, [
    draftIssue.id, // Make sure to re-evaluate when switching drafts
    isRepositoryResolved,
    resolvedDraftIssueRepo,
    topRepos,
    formRepository,
  ])

  // Process form changes if not resolving from draftIssue data
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!isResolvingFormRepository.current) {
      onRepositoryChange(formRepository, false)
    }
  }, [formRepository, onRepositoryChange])
}

import {useMemo} from 'react'

import type {DraftOrExistingIssue} from '../content-preview-types'
import {useContentPreview} from '../ContentPreviewContext'

export type TreeNode<T> = {
  item: T
  parent: TreeNode<T> | null
  children: Array<TreeNode<T>>
  blockedBy: Array<TreeNode<T>>
  isBlocking: Array<TreeNode<T>>
}

export function visit<T>(node: TreeNode<T>, callback: (node: TreeNode<T>) => void): void {
  callback(node)
  for (const child of node.children) {
    visit(child, callback)
  }
}

export function visitDependencies<T>(
  node: TreeNode<T>,
  callback: (node: TreeNode<T>) => void,
  visited: Set<TreeNode<T>> = new Set(),
): void {
  if (visited.has(node)) return
  visited.add(node)
  callback(node)

  for (const child of node.children) {
    visitDependencies(child, callback, visited)
  }
  for (const blockedBy of node.blockedBy) {
    visitDependencies(blockedBy, callback, visited)
  }
  for (const isBlocking of node.isBlocking) {
    visitDependencies(isBlocking, callback, visited)
  }
}

export async function visitDependenciesAsync<T>(
  node: TreeNode<T>,
  callback: (node: TreeNode<T>) => Promise<void>,
  visited: Set<TreeNode<T>> = new Set(),
): Promise<void> {
  if (visited.has(node)) return
  visited.add(node)
  await callback(node)

  for (const child of node.children) {
    await visitDependenciesAsync(child, callback, visited)
  }
  for (const blockedBy of node.blockedBy) {
    await visitDependenciesAsync(blockedBy, callback, visited)
  }
  for (const isBlocking of node.isBlocking) {
    await visitDependenciesAsync(isBlocking, callback, visited)
  }
}

export function toTreeMap<T, TKey = keyof T>(
  items: T[],
  keySelector: (item: T) => TKey,
  parentKeySelector: (item: T) => TKey | undefined,
  blockedByKeySelector?: (item: T) => TKey[] | undefined,
  isBlockingKeySelector?: (item: T) => TKey[] | undefined,
): Map<TKey, TreeNode<T>> {
  // Seed a Map of all items by key
  const itemMap = items.reduce((map, item) => {
    map.set(keySelector(item), {item, parent: null, children: [], blockedBy: [], isBlocking: []})
    return map
  }, new Map<TKey, TreeNode<T>>())

  for (const item of items) {
    const currentNode = itemMap.get(keySelector(item))
    const parentKey = parentKeySelector(item)

    // Link parents by parentKey
    if (parentKey != null) {
      const parentNode = itemMap.get(parentKey)
      if (parentNode && currentNode) {
        currentNode.parent = parentNode
        parentNode.children.push(currentNode)
      }
    }

    // Add dependencies
    const blockedByKeys = blockedByKeySelector?.(item) ?? []
    for (const blockedByKey of blockedByKeys) {
      const dependencyNode = itemMap.get(blockedByKey)
      // Prevent circular dependency. If A is blocked by B, B cannot also be blocked by A.
      if (dependencyNode && currentNode && !dependencyNode.blockedBy.includes(currentNode)) {
        if (!currentNode.blockedBy.includes(dependencyNode)) {
          currentNode.blockedBy.push(dependencyNode)
        }
        if (!dependencyNode.isBlocking.includes(currentNode)) {
          dependencyNode.isBlocking.push(currentNode)
        }
      }
    }

    const isBlockingKeys = isBlockingKeySelector?.(item) ?? []
    for (const isBlockingKey of isBlockingKeys) {
      const dependencyNode = itemMap.get(isBlockingKey)
      // Prevent circular dependency. If A is blocking B, B cannot also be blocking A.
      if (dependencyNode && currentNode && !dependencyNode.isBlocking.includes(currentNode)) {
        if (!currentNode.isBlocking.includes(dependencyNode)) {
          currentNode.isBlocking.push(dependencyNode)
        }
        if (!dependencyNode.blockedBy.includes(currentNode)) {
          dependencyNode.blockedBy.push(currentNode)
        }
      }
    }
  }

  // Return the complete Map; callers can decide what subtree they need
  return itemMap
}

/**
 * Convert content preview draft issue items into a tree structure.
 *
 * @returns a `Map` of all draft issues, where
 *   - `key` is the issue `tag`
 *   - `value` is a `TreeNode`, containing the draft issue, and pointers to its parent and children
 */
export function useDraftIssueTreeMap() {
  const {items, allOpenItems, versionedItems} = useContentPreview()

  return useMemo(() => {
    const openItemsSet = new Set(allOpenItems)
    const latestDraftIssues = [...versionedItems.entries()]
      .filter(([id]) => id.startsWith('new-issue:') || id.startsWith('existing-issue:'))
      // eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/no-unused-vars
      .map(([_id, versionIds]) => {
        // For open tabs, we want to honour the selected version.
        const openedVersionId = versionIds.find(id => openItemsSet.has(id))
        if (openedVersionId) {
          return items.get(openedVersionId)
        }
        // For items that aren't open in preview, default to the latest version.
        const latestVersionId = versionIds.at(-1)
        return latestVersionId ? items.get(latestVersionId) : undefined
      })
      .filter((x): x is DraftOrExistingIssue => !!x)

    return toTreeMap(
      latestDraftIssues,
      item => item.tag,
      item => item.parentTag,
      item => item.blockedByTags,
      item => item.isBlockingTags,
    )
  }, [items, allOpenItems, versionedItems])
}

import {useIssueCreateDataContext} from '@github-ui/issue-create/IssueCreateDataContext'
import {useMemo} from 'react'

import type {DraftIssue} from '../content-preview-types'
import {useDraftIssueFormSync} from './use-draft-issue-form-sync'
import {useIssueTypesQuery} from './use-issue-types-query'
import {useOnChangeCallback} from './use-on-change-callback'

function isEqual(left: string | undefined, right: string | undefined) {
  return left?.toLowerCase() === right?.toLowerCase()
}

export function useDraftIssueType(draftIssue: DraftIssue) {
  const {
    repository: formRepository,
    issueType: formIssueType,
    setIssueType: setFormIssueType,
  } = useIssueCreateDataContext()
  const onChange = useOnChangeCallback(draftIssue)

  const resolverQuery = useIssueTypesQuery({
    owner: formRepository?.owner.login,
    repo: formRepository?.name,
  })
  const resolvedIssueType = useMemo(() => {
    if (resolverQuery.isLoading) return null
    if (!draftIssue.issueType) return null
    return resolverQuery.data?.find(t => isEqual(t.name, draftIssue.issueType)) || null
  }, [draftIssue.issueType, resolverQuery.data, resolverQuery.isLoading])

  return useDraftIssueFormSync(draftIssue, {
    draftValue: draftIssue.issueType,
    formValue: formIssueType,
    setFormValue: setFormIssueType,
    onChange: (newValue, skipVersionBump) => onChange({issueType: newValue?.name}, skipVersionBump),
    resolverResult: {
      isLoading: resolverQuery.isLoading,
      data: resolvedIssueType,
    },
    hasPermission: repo => repo?.viewerIssueCreationPermissions?.typeable,
    isEqual: (a, b) => isEqual(a?.name, b?.name),
    defaultValue: null,
  })
}

import {validateIssueBody, validateIssueTitle} from '@github-ui/entity-validators'
import {sendEvent} from '@github-ui/hydro-analytics'
import {instanceOfIssueFormData, type IssueCreatePayload} from '@github-ui/issue-create/Model'
import type {IssueFormElementRef, IssueFormRef} from '@github-ui/issue-form/Types'
import type {RepositoryPickerRepository} from '@github-ui/item-picker/RepositoryPicker'
import type {BannerProps} from '@primer/react'
import type {RefObject} from 'react'
import {useCallback, useEffect} from 'react'

import type {DraftOrExistingIssue} from '../content-preview-types'
import {type BulkCreateStatusResponse, useBulkCreateMutationWithStatus} from './use-bulk-create-mutation'
import {type TreeNode, visitDependencies} from './use-draft-issue-tree-map'
import {useOnSaveCallback} from './use-on-save-callback'

export class CreateError extends Error {
  readonly type: string
  readonly bannerTitle: string
  readonly bannerVariant: BannerProps['variant']

  constructor(message: string) {
    super(message)
    this.name = 'CreateError'
    this.type = 'creation'
    this.bannerTitle = 'Errors in issue creation'
    this.bannerVariant = 'critical'
  }
}

export class ValidationError extends Error {
  readonly type: string
  readonly bannerTitle: string
  readonly bannerVariant: BannerProps['variant']

  constructor(message: string) {
    super(message)
    this.name = 'ValidationError'
    this.type = 'validation'
    this.bannerTitle = 'Validation errors in issue content'
    this.bannerVariant = 'warning'
  }
}

export type SingleIssueCreateError = ValidationError | CreateError

export function useIssueCreationHandler({
  issueNode,
  onComplete,
  onError,
  issueFormRef,
  issueTemplate,
  formRepository,
}: {
  issueNode: TreeNode<DraftOrExistingIssue> | undefined
  onComplete: () => void
  onError?: (error: SingleIssueCreateError) => void
  issueFormRef?: RefObject<IssueFormRef | null>
  issueTemplate?: IssueCreatePayload | null
  formRepository?: RepositoryPickerRepository | undefined
}) {
  const {mutate, isLoading, data, isComplete, isError, error} = useBulkCreateMutationWithStatus({
    issueNode,
    issueFormRef,
    issueTemplate,
  })
  const onSaveSuccess = useOnSaveCallback()

  // Validation logic for single issue creation with focus behavior
  const validateSingleIssueCreation = useCallback(() => {
    if (!issueNode) return false

    const issue = issueNode.item

    // Only validate draft issues (existing issues don't need validation for creation)
    if (issue.type !== 'new-issue') {
      return true
    }

    const title = issue.name || ''
    let refToFocusOnError: HTMLInputElement | IssueFormElementRef | undefined = undefined

    if (!formRepository || !issue.repository) {
      onError?.(new ValidationError('Please select a repository for the issue before creating it.'))
      sendEvent('dotcom_chat.activate', {
        target: 'BROWSER_ISSUE_NO_REPO_SELECTED_ERROR',
        mode: 'immersive',
        issueTag: issue.tag,
        selectedRepository: formRepository?.nameWithOwner || 'none',
        suggestedRepository: issue.repository || 'none',
      })
      return false
    }

    // Validate repository mismatch between draft issue and form repository
    // This technically shouldn't happen because it should've triggered an onChange event that would update
    // both the draft issue and the form repository to be in sync, but just in case
    const issueRepoLower = issue.repository.toLowerCase()
    const formRepoLower = formRepository.nameWithOwner.toLowerCase()

    if (issueRepoLower !== formRepoLower) {
      onError?.(
        new ValidationError(
          `The selected repository (${formRepository.nameWithOwner}) doesn't match the repository Copilot suggested (${issue.repository}). Please select a repository you have access to.`,
        ),
      )
      // Log analytics event for repository mismatch
      sendEvent('dotcom_chat.activate', {
        target: 'BROWSER_ISSUE_REPO_MISMATCH_ERROR',
        mode: 'immersive',
        issueTag: issue.tag,
        selectedRepository: formRepository.nameWithOwner,
        suggestedRepository: issue.repository,
      })
      return false
    }

    // Validate issue title
    const titleValidationResult = validateIssueTitle(title)
    if (!titleValidationResult.isValid) {
      onError?.(new ValidationError(titleValidationResult.errorMessage || 'Issue title is invalid'))
      return false
    }

    // Validate issue body
    const bodyValidationResult = validateIssueBody(issue.body || '')
    if (!bodyValidationResult.isValid) {
      onError?.(new ValidationError(bodyValidationResult.errorMessage || 'Issue body is invalid'))
      return false
    }

    // If template is an issue form, validate form elements
    const isIssueForm = issueTemplate && instanceOfIssueFormData(issueTemplate.data)
    if (isIssueForm && issueFormRef!.current) {
      const invalidInputs = issueFormRef!.current.getInvalidInputs()
      if (invalidInputs.length > 0) {
        // Use first invalid input if there's no title ref yet
        refToFocusOnError = refToFocusOnError || invalidInputs[0]
        onError?.(new ValidationError('Please fix the validation errors in the form'))
        refToFocusOnError?.focus()
        return false
      }
    }

    return true
  }, [issueNode, issueTemplate, issueFormRef, onError, formRepository])

  const handleCreate = useCallback(() => {
    if (!issueNode || isLoading || isComplete) {
      return
    }

    // Perform validation if this is a single issue
    if (!issueNode.parent && issueNode.children.length === 0) {
      const isValid = validateSingleIssueCreation()
      if (!isValid) return
    }

    mutate()
  }, [isLoading, isComplete, mutate, issueNode, validateSingleIssueCreation])

  // Transform response data into the format expected by onSaveSuccess
  const transformResponseData = useCallback(
    (responseData: BulkCreateStatusResponse | undefined, rootNode: TreeNode<DraftOrExistingIssue> | undefined) => {
      if (!responseData || !rootNode) return []
      if (responseData.job.completed_issues.length === 0) return []

      const drafts = new Map<string, DraftOrExistingIssue>()
      visitDependencies(rootNode, (node: TreeNode<DraftOrExistingIssue>) => {
        drafts.set(node.item.tag, node.item)
      })

      return responseData.job.completed_issues.map(issue => {
        const draft = drafts.get(issue.tag)!
        const [owner, repo] = draft.repository!.split('/')

        return {
          draftType: draft.type,
          tag: issue.tag,
          parentTag: draft.parentTag,
          blockedByTags: draft.blockedByTags,
          isBlockingTags: draft.isBlockingTags,
          template: draft.type === 'new-issue' ? draft.template : undefined,
          repository: {
            databaseId: issue.repository_id,
            name: repo!,
            owner: {
              login: owner!,
            },
          },
          number: issue.number,
          title: draft.name,
          url: issue.href,
          assignees: 'assignees' in draft ? (draft.assignees ?? []) : [],
          errors: issue.errors || [],
        }
      })
    },
    [],
  )

  // Handle errors
  useEffect(() => {
    if (isError && error) {
      onError?.(new CreateError(error.message))
    }
  }, [isError, error, onError])

  // Handle successful creation
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!data || !isComplete) return

    const createdIssues = transformResponseData(data, issueNode)

    onComplete()
    void onSaveSuccess(createdIssues)
  }, [data, isComplete, issueNode, onComplete, onSaveSuccess, transformResponseData])

  return {
    handleCreate,
    isLoading,
    isComplete,
    isError,
    error,
    data,
  }
}

/* eslint eslint-comments/no-use: off */
/* eslint-disable relay/unused-fields */

import {useQuery} from '@github-ui/react-query'
import {fetchQuery, graphql, readInlineData, useRelayEnvironment} from 'react-relay'
import type {IEnvironment} from 'relay-runtime'

import type {
  useIssueQueryIssueContent$data,
  useIssueQueryIssueContent$key,
} from './__generated__/useIssueQueryIssueContent.graphql'
import type {useIssueQueryQuery} from './__generated__/useIssueQueryQuery.graphql'

// eslint-disable-next-line @github-ui/github-monorepo/restrict-relay-queries
const IssueContentFragment = graphql`
  fragment useIssueQueryIssueContent on Issue @inline {
    id
    databaseId
    number
    title
    body
    url
    repository {
      id
      databaseId
      name
      owner {
        login
      }
      isArchived
      isCopilotAgentEnabled
      defaultBranchRef {
        name
      }
    }
    assignees(first: 20) {
      nodes {
        login
      }
    }
    labels(first: 20) {
      nodes {
        name
      }
    }
    milestone {
      title
    }
    issueType {
      name
    }
    projectItems(first: 20) {
      nodes {
        id
        project {
          id
          title
        }
      }
    }
    subIssues(first: 1) {
      totalCount
    }
  }
`

export type Issue = Omit<useIssueQueryIssueContent$data, ' $fragmentType'>

export function readIssueFromFragment(key: useIssueQueryIssueContent$key): Issue {
  // eslint-disable-next-line no-restricted-syntax
  return readInlineData(IssueContentFragment, key) as Issue
}

// eslint-disable-next-line @github-ui/github-monorepo/restrict-relay-queries
export const IssueQuery = graphql`
  query useIssueQueryQuery($owner: String!, $name: String!, $number: Int!) {
    repository(owner: $owner, name: $name) {
      issue(number: $number) {
        ...useIssueQueryIssueContent
      }
    }
  }
`

export function useIssueQuery({owner, repo, issueNumber}: {owner?: string; repo?: string; issueNumber?: number}) {
  const environment = useRelayEnvironment()

  const query = useQuery({
    queryKey: ['copilot-immersive-issue', JSON.stringify(environment), owner, repo, issueNumber],
    queryFn: async (): Promise<Issue | null> => {
      if (!owner || !repo || !issueNumber) {
        return null
      }

      return await fetchIssue(environment, owner, repo, issueNumber)
    },
  })

  const forceFetchFromNetwork = async () => {
    if (!owner || !repo || !issueNumber) {
      return null
    }

    // Bypass cache entirely and fetch directly from network
    return await fetchIssue(environment, owner, repo, issueNumber, true)
  }

  return {
    ...query,
    forceFetchFromNetwork,
  }
}

export async function fetchIssue(
  environment: IEnvironment,
  owner: string,
  repo: string,
  issueNumber: number,
  forceNetwork = false,
) {
  try {
    const data = await fetchQuery<useIssueQueryQuery>(
      environment,
      IssueQuery,
      {
        owner,
        name: repo,
        number: issueNumber,
      },
      {fetchPolicy: forceNetwork ? 'network-only' : 'store-or-network'},
    ).toPromise()

    if (data?.repository?.issue == null) {
      return null
    }

    return readIssueFromFragment(data.repository.issue)
  } catch (e) {
    // Because this query fetches a nested resource (repository -> issue),
    // relay will actually throw if the issue is not found, rather than return null.
    // Check for that specific condition here and return null, otherwise re-throw.
    if (
      typeof e === 'object' &&
      e != null &&
      'name' in e &&
      e.name === 'ValidationError' &&
      'message' in e &&
      typeof e.message === 'string' &&
      e.message.includes('NOT_FOUND')
    ) {
      return null
    }

    throw e
  }
}

import {fetchIssueTypes} from '@github-ui/item-picker-issue-type-tsq'
import {useQuery, useQueryClient} from '@github-ui/react-query'

const ISSUE_TYPES_LIMIT = 25

export function useIssueTypesQuery({owner, repo}: {owner: string | undefined; repo: string | undefined}) {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient()
  const queryKey = ['copilot-immersive-issue-types', JSON.stringify(queryClient), owner, repo]

  const queryFn = async () => {
    if (!owner || !repo) return []

    // TSQ implementation uses an empty query to fetch all issue types for the repository
    const response = await fetchIssueTypes(queryClient, {
      owner,
      repo,
      query: '',
      count: ISSUE_TYPES_LIMIT,
    })
    return response.issueTypes
  }

  return useQuery({
    queryKey,
    queryFn,
  })
}

/* eslint relay/unused-fields: off */
import {useQuery} from '@github-ui/react-query'
import {clientSideRelayFetchQueryRetained} from '@github-ui/relay-environment'
import {graphql, useRelayEnvironment} from 'react-relay'

import type {useLabelsQueryQuery} from './__generated__/useLabelsQueryQuery.graphql'

// eslint-disable-next-line @github-ui/github-monorepo/restrict-relay-queries
const LabelPickerGraphqlQuery = graphql`
  query useLabelsQueryQuery($owner: String!, $repo: String!, $labelNames: String = "", $count: Int = 100) {
    repository(owner: $owner, name: $repo) {
      labels(names: $labelNames, first: $count, orderBy: {field: NAME, direction: ASC}) {
        nodes {
          id
          color
          name
          nameHTML
          description
          url
        }
      }
    }
  }
`

export function useLabelsQuery({owner, repo, labels}: {owner?: string; repo?: string; labels?: string[]}) {
  const environment = useRelayEnvironment()

  return useQuery({
    queryKey: ['copilot-immersive-labels', JSON.stringify(environment), owner, repo, labels],
    queryFn: async () => {
      if (!owner || !repo || !labels) return []

      const data = await clientSideRelayFetchQueryRetained<useLabelsQueryQuery>({
        environment,
        query: LabelPickerGraphqlQuery,
        variables: {
          owner,
          repo,
          labelNames: labels.join(','),
          count: labels.length,
        },
      }).toPromise()

      return (data?.repository?.labels?.nodes ?? [])
        .filter(node => !!node)
        .map(node => {
          return node
        })
    },
  })
}

import {fetchMilestones} from '@github-ui/item-picker-milestone-tsq'
import {useQuery, useQueryClient} from '@github-ui/react-query'

export function useMilestoneQuery({
  owner,
  repo,
  milestone,
}: {
  owner: string | undefined
  repo: string | undefined
  milestone: string | undefined
}) {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient()

  return useQuery({
    queryKey: ['copilot-immersive-milestones', JSON.stringify(queryClient), owner, repo, milestone],
    queryFn: async () => {
      if (!owner || !repo || !milestone) return []

      const response = await fetchMilestones(queryClient, {
        owner,
        repo,
        query: milestone,
        count: 1,
      })
      return response.milestones
    },
  })
}

// eslint-disable-next-line no-restricted-imports
import {useChatState} from '@github-ui/copilot-chat/CopilotChatContext'
import {sendEvent} from '@github-ui/hydro-analytics'
import {useSessionStorage} from '@github-ui/use-safe-storage/session-storage'
import isEqual from 'lodash-es/isEqual'
import {useCallback} from 'react'

import type {DraftIssue} from '../content-preview-types'
import {useContentPreview} from '../ContentPreviewContext'
import {useUserEditedNewIssueId} from './use-user-edited-new-issue-id'

export function useOnChangeCallback(draftIssue: DraftIssue) {
  const {openItem, updateItem} = useContentPreview()
  const {selectedThreadID} = useChatState()
  const userEditedNewIssueId = useUserEditedNewIssueId(draftIssue)
  const storageKey = `edited-${selectedThreadID}-${userEditedNewIssueId}`
  // eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/no-unused-vars
  const [_, writeEditedIssueToStorage] = useSessionStorage<DraftIssue | undefined>(storageKey, undefined)

  return useCallback(
    (editedIssue: Partial<DraftIssue>, skipVersionBump: boolean = false) => {
      let hasChanges = false
      let property: keyof typeof editedIssue
      for (property in editedIssue) {
        if (!isEqual(editedIssue[property], draftIssue[property])) {
          hasChanges = true

          // For metadata fields only, report changes
          if (property !== 'name' && property !== 'body' && property !== 'defaultValuesById') {
            sendEvent('dotcom_chat.activate', {
              target: 'BROWSER_ISSUE_USER_CHANGED_FIELD',
              mode: 'immersive',
              threadId: selectedThreadID,
              repository: draftIssue.repository,
              issueTag: draftIssue.tag,
              userChangedField: property,
              wasEmpty: wasEmpty(draftIssue[property]),
            })
          }
        }
      }

      if (!hasChanges) {
        return
      }

      const updatedItem: DraftIssue = {
        ...draftIssue,
        ...editedIssue,
      }

      if (!skipVersionBump) {
        updatedItem.id = userEditedNewIssueId
        updatedItem.isUserEdited = true
        writeEditedIssueToStorage(updatedItem)
      }

      updateItem(updatedItem)
      openItem(updatedItem.id)
    },
    [draftIssue, updateItem, openItem, selectedThreadID, userEditedNewIssueId, writeEditedIssueToStorage],
  )
}

export function wasEmpty(value: unknown): boolean {
  switch (typeof value) {
    case 'string':
      return !value
    case 'object':
      if (Array.isArray(value)) {
        return !value || (value as unknown[]).length === 0
      } else {
        return value == null
      }
    default:
      return value == null
  }
}

import {GENERATED_FIELD_PREFIX} from '@github-ui/issue-form/utils/default-value'
import {useCallback} from 'react'

import type {DraftIssue} from '../content-preview-types'

export function useOnFieldChangeCallback(
  draftIssue: DraftIssue,
  onChange: (editedIssue: Partial<DraftIssue>, skipVersionBump?: boolean) => void,
) {
  return useCallback(
    (id: string | null | undefined, indexOfFormElement: number | null | undefined, value: string) => {
      const fieldId = id || `${GENERATED_FIELD_PREFIX}-${indexOfFormElement}`

      if (draftIssue.defaultValuesById && draftIssue.defaultValuesById[fieldId] === value) {
        return
      }

      const updatedIssue = {
        ...draftIssue,
        defaultValuesById: {...draftIssue.defaultValuesById},
      }

      updatedIssue.defaultValuesById[fieldId] = value

      onChange(updatedIssue, false)
    },
    [draftIssue, onChange],
  )
}

import {isCopilot} from '@github-ui/assignees/copilot-user'
// eslint-disable-next-line no-restricted-imports
import {useChatState} from '@github-ui/copilot-chat/CopilotChatContext'
import {useChatManager} from '@github-ui/copilot-chat/CopilotChatManagerContext'
import {NullMessageId, type TextReference} from '@github-ui/copilot-chat/utils/copilot-chat-types'
import {getSelectedThread} from '@github-ui/copilot-chat/utils/get-selected-thread'
import {sendEvent} from '@github-ui/hydro-analytics'
import {dump} from 'js-yaml'
import {useCallback} from 'react'

import type {IssueTreeItem} from '../../../markdown-extensions/issue-blocks/issue-tree/IssueTreeBlock'
import type {TimelineEventTextReference} from '../../timeline-events/timeline-events-types'
import {useContentPreview} from '../ContentPreviewContext'

export type ContinuableError = {
  message: string
  type: 'project' | 'dependency' | 'update' | 'other'
}

const ERROR_TYPE_DESCRIPTION: Record<ContinuableError['type'], string> = {
  project: 'Project assignment errors',
  dependency: 'Dependency errors',
  update: 'Partial update errors',
  other: 'Other errors',
}

export type SavedIssue = {
  draftType: 'new-issue' | 'update-issue' | 'existing-issue'
  tag: string
  parentTag: string | undefined
  blockedByTags: string[] | undefined
  isBlockingTags: string[] | undefined
  template: string | undefined
  repository: {
    databaseId: number
    name: string
    owner: {
      login: string
    }
  }
  number: number
  title: string
  url: string
  assignees: string[]
  errors?: ContinuableError[]
}

export function useOnSaveCallback() {
  const manager = useChatManager()
  const state = useChatState()
  const {closeItems, openItem, openPreviewPane, versionedItems, updateItem} = useContentPreview()

  return useCallback(
    async (savedIssues: SavedIssue[]) => {
      if (savedIssues.length === 0) {
        return
      }

      const primarySavedIssue = savedIssues.find(issue => !issue.parentTag) ?? savedIssues[0]!

      sendEvent('dotcom_chat.activate', {
        target: 'BROWSER_ISSUES_SAVED',
        mode: 'immersive',
        threadId: getSelectedThread(state)?.id,
        chatReferrer: document.referrer,
        repositoryId: primarySavedIssue.repository.databaseId,
        draftType: primarySavedIssue.draftType,
        issueTag: primarySavedIssue.tag,
        issueNumber: primarySavedIssue.number,
        templateType: primarySavedIssue.template
          ? primarySavedIssue.template.endsWith('.md')
            ? 'markdown'
            : 'yaml'
          : 'none',
        issuesWithCopilotAssigned: savedIssues.filter(issue => issue.assignees.some(s => isCopilot(s))).length,
        savedIssuesCount: savedIssues.length,
      })

      const savedIssuesUrls: string[] = []
      const savedIssuesForYaml: IssueTreeItem[] = []
      const obsoleteDraftTags: string[] = []

      for (const savedIssue of savedIssues) {
        const owner = savedIssue.repository.owner.login
        const repo = savedIssue.repository.name
        const number = savedIssue.number

        // Add saved issue in the content preview context
        // This happens first so that we don't have to wait for the API call to complete
        // to show the issue in the preview pane
        updateItem({
          type: 'issue',
          id: getIssuePreviewId(savedIssue),
          messageId: NullMessageId,
          name: savedIssue.title,
          owner,
          repo,
          number,
          href: savedIssue.url,
        })

        const parentIssue = savedIssues.find(issue => issue.tag === savedIssue.parentTag)
        const blockedByIssues = savedIssue.blockedByTags
          ?.map(tag => savedIssues.find(issue => issue.tag === tag))
          .filter(issue => issue != null)
        const isBlockingIssues = savedIssue.isBlockingTags
          ?.map(tag => savedIssues.find(issue => issue.tag === tag))
          .filter(issue => issue != null)

        const blockedByTags = blockedByIssues?.length ? blockedByIssues.map(issue => getIssueTag(issue)) : undefined
        const isBlockingTags = isBlockingIssues?.length ? isBlockingIssues.map(issue => getIssueTag(issue)) : undefined

        savedIssuesForYaml.push({
          tag: `${owner}/${repo}#${number}`,
          parentTag: parentIssue ? getIssueTag(parentIssue) : undefined,
          blockedByTags,
          isBlockingTags,
          title: savedIssue.title,
          repository: `${savedIssue.repository.owner.login}/${savedIssue.repository.name}`,
          number: savedIssue.number,
          state: 'open',
          url: savedIssue.url,
        })
        savedIssuesUrls.push(getIssueUrl(savedIssue))
        obsoleteDraftTags.push(savedIssue.tag)
      }

      // Since the issues have been saved, we no longer need the draft open.
      // We don't know which draft versions may be open, so we close them all.
      // This updates context state, so needs to be done in one call outside the loop above.
      closeItems(
        savedIssues
          .map(issue => `${issue.draftType}:${issue.tag}` as const)
          .flatMap(unversionedDraftId => versionedItems.get(unversionedDraftId) ?? []),
      )

      // Open only the root/primary issue in the preview pane
      openItem(getIssuePreviewId(primarySavedIssue))
      openPreviewPane()

      const instructions: TimelineEventTextReference = {
        type: 'text',
        name: `timeline-event: {"type": "issue-saved", "markdownContent": "${instructionsMarkdownContent(
          savedIssues,
        )}"}`,
        text:
          `${issueFetchInstructions(savedIssues)}\n` +
          '````yaml type="issue-tree"\n' +
          `${dump(
            {data: savedIssuesForYaml},
            {
              forceQuotes: true,
              noArrayIndent: true,
              lineWidth: -1,
            },
          )}` +
          '````',
      }

      const ignoreObsoleteDraftsInstruction: TextReference = {
        type: 'text',
        name: 'ignore-obsolete-drafts',
        text: `From now on, you should only interact with these newly saved issues and not their corresponding drafts. You MUST exclude the draft issues with the following tags from any future tree creation: ${obsoleteDraftTags.join(
          ', ',
        )}`,
      }

      const references = [
        // Instructions on how to return the newly-saved issues
        instructions,
        // Instructions on how to ignore obsolete drafts
        ignoreObsoleteDraftsInstruction,
        // Any unsent draft edits, so Copilot keeps an updated context of the user's actions
        ...state.currentReferences,
      ]

      const continuableErrors: ContinuableError[] = savedIssues.flatMap(issue => issue.errors || [])

      if (continuableErrors.length) {
        references.push({
          type: 'text',
          name: 'summarize-continuable-errors',
          text: `These issues were saved with some errors. You MUST give a high-level summary of these errors at the end of your message in the language used in the conversation. Always prefix it with the word "Note:" in bold for visibility. Be clear but succinct. Here are the errors, grouped by type:\n${groupErrorsByType(
            continuableErrors,
          )}`,
        })
      }

      const content =
        savedIssuesUrls.length > 1
          ? `Saved the issues: ${savedIssuesUrls.join(', ')}`
          : `Saved the issue: ${savedIssuesUrls[0]}`

      await manager.sendChatMessage({
        thread: getSelectedThread(state),
        content,
        references,
        topic: state.currentTopic,
        context: state.context,
        customInstructions: state.customInstructions,
        model: state.model,
      })
    },
    [closeItems, manager, openItem, openPreviewPane, state, updateItem, versionedItems],
  )
}

function groupErrorsByType(errors: ContinuableError[]): string {
  const grouped: Record<string, string[]> = {}
  for (const error of errors) {
    const description = ERROR_TYPE_DESCRIPTION[error.type] || 'Other errors'
    if (!grouped[description]) {
      grouped[description] = []
    }
    grouped[description].push(error.message)
  }

  return Object.entries(grouped)
    .map(([type, messages]) => `${type}:\n${messages.map(m => `- ${m}`).join('\n')}\n`)
    .join('\n')
}

function getIssueUrl(savedIssue: SavedIssue): string {
  return `[${getIssueTag(savedIssue)}](${savedIssue.url})`
}

function getIssueTag(savedIssue: SavedIssue): string {
  const owner = savedIssue.repository.owner.login
  const repo = savedIssue.repository.name
  const number = savedIssue.number
  return `${owner}/${repo}#${number}`
}

function instructionsMarkdownContent(savedIssues: SavedIssue[]) {
  if (savedIssues.length > 1) {
    return `Saved ${savedIssues.length} issues`
  } else {
    const savedIssue = savedIssues[0]!
    const url = getIssueUrl(savedIssue)
    const action = savedIssue.draftType === 'new-issue' ? 'Created' : 'Updated'
    return `${action} issue ${url}`
  }
}

function issueFetchInstructions(savedIssues: SavedIssue[]) {
  if (savedIssues.length > 1) {
    return 'Fetch the details for all saved issues and return them in a code block with YAML following this example format:'
  } else {
    return 'Fetch the issue details and return them in a code block with YAML following this example format:'
  }
}

function getIssuePreviewId(issue: SavedIssue): `issue:${string}/${string}/${number}` {
  const owner = issue.repository.owner.login
  const repo = issue.repository.name
  const number = issue.number
  return `issue:${owner}/${repo}/${number}` as const
}

// eslint-disable-next-line no-restricted-imports
import {useChatState} from '@github-ui/copilot-chat/CopilotChatContext'
import {useChatManager} from '@github-ui/copilot-chat/CopilotChatManagerContext'
import type {CopilotChatMessage} from '@github-ui/copilot-chat/utils/copilot-chat-types'
import {getSelectedThread} from '@github-ui/copilot-chat/utils/get-selected-thread'
import {sendEvent} from '@github-ui/hydro-analytics'
import {XIcon} from '@primer/octicons-react'
import {useCallback} from 'react'

import type {TimelineEventTextReference} from '../../timeline-events/timeline-events-types'

export function useParentMessageRetryCallback() {
  const manager = useChatManager()
  const state = useChatState()

  return useCallback(
    async (message: CopilotChatMessage, customMessage?: string, customInstructions?: string) => {
      let parentMessage: CopilotChatMessage | undefined = undefined
      if (message.parentMessageID) {
        parentMessage = state.messages.findLast(m => m.id === message.parentMessageID && m.id !== 'root')
      }
      if (!parentMessage || !parentMessage.content) return

      sendEvent('dotcom_chat.activate', {
        target: 'BROWSER_ISSUE_RETRY_MESSAGE',
        mode: 'immersive',
        threadId: getSelectedThread(state)?.id,
        customMessage,
      })

      const instructions: TimelineEventTextReference = {
        type: 'text',
        name: `timeline-event: {"type": "automatic-retry", "markdownContent": "${
          customMessage ?? `Copilot failed to generate a response, retrying…`
        }"}`,
        text: customInstructions,
        icon: XIcon,
      }

      await manager.sendChatMessage({
        thread: getSelectedThread(state),
        content: parentMessage.content,
        references: [...(parentMessage.references ?? []), instructions],
        topic: state.currentTopic,
        context: state.context,
        customInstructions: state.customInstructions,
        model: state.model,
        parentMessageId: message.id,
      })
    },
    [manager, state],
  )
}

// eslint-disable-next-line no-restricted-imports
import {useChatState} from '@github-ui/copilot-chat/CopilotChatContext'
import {getActiveMessages} from '@github-ui/copilot-chat/utils/copilot-chat-subthreading-helpers'
import type {MediaContentItem} from '@github-ui/copilot-chat/utils/copilot-chat-types'
import {useMemo} from 'react'

// Hook to get media content from active messages
function useMediaContent(): MediaContentItem[] {
  const {messages} = useChatState()
  const activeMessages = getActiveMessages(messages)
  return useMemo(() => activeMessages.flatMap(message => message.mediaContent || []), [activeMessages])
}

// We replace the image placeholders in the issue body before sending it to the issue form to avoid
// unnecessary versioning when comparing the raw body to the processed one.
export function usePreProcessedDraftIssueContent(value: string | undefined): string {
  const media = useMediaContent()

  if (!value) {
    return ''
  }

  // The below works for local development, to emulate real images being uploaded
  // It can be triggered by `Link to ![image1](image1)` in the chat
  // const media: Array<{chatAttachmentUrl: string}> = [
  //   {chatAttachmentUrl: 'https://github.com/github-copilot/chat/attachments/215'},
  //   {chatAttachmentUrl: 'https://github.com/github-copilot/chat/attachments/216'},
  //   {chatAttachmentUrl: 'https://github.com/github-copilot/chat/attachments/217'},
  // ]

  return replaceImagePlaceholders(value, media)
}

// This is where we want to finalize the draft content received from Copilot message
// before we send it to the preview form for user to review or edit
export function replaceImagePlaceholders(issueBody: string | undefined, media: MediaContentItem[]) {
  if (!issueBody) {
    return ''
  }

  // Find all markdown image links in form of ![anytext](image1)
  const matches = issueBody.match(/(!\[.*?\]\(image\d+\))/g)
  if (matches == null) {
    return issueBody
  }

  for (const match of matches) {
    const urlPlaceholderRegex = /\(image(\d+)\)/

    // The regex matches the above regex, so must always return a match
    const imageIdString = match.match(urlPlaceholderRegex)![1]!

    // Image numbers are 1-indexed
    const imageId = parseInt(imageIdString, 10)

    // Images are reverse numbered in model output, so that if there are multiple issues drafted
    // in the same thread, this logic only picks up the images above the issue being created
    // This also supports scenario where model can not accurately number the images due to long thread
    const imageIndex = media.length - imageId

    // chatAttachmentUrl is sometimes populated (e.g. for shared threads), but if it's not, we use the url
    const url = media[imageIndex]?.chatAttachmentUrl || media[imageIndex]?.url
    if (url) {
      // Keep the alt text, but replace the image1 with the chat attachment URL
      const updatedMarkdown = match.replace(urlPlaceholderRegex, `(${url})`)
      issueBody = issueBody.replace(match, updatedMarkdown)
    }
  }
  return issueBody
}

import type {DraftIssueProjectReference} from '@github-ui/copilot-chat/utils/copilot-chat-types'
import type {ProjectPickerBaseProject as ProjectPickerProject} from '@github-ui/item-picker-project'
import {searchProjectsQueryOptions} from '@github-ui/item-picker-project-tsq'
import {useQuery, useQueryClient} from '@github-ui/react-query'

export function useProjectsQuery({
  owner,
  repo,
  projects,
}: {
  owner?: string
  repo?: string
  projects?: DraftIssueProjectReference[]
}) {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient()

  return useQuery({
    queryKey: ['copilot-immersive-projects', owner, repo, projects],
    queryFn: async () => {
      if (!owner || !repo || !projects || !projects.length) return []

      const desiredIds = new Set(projects.filter(project => Boolean(project.id)).map(project => project.id!))
      const desiredTitles = projects.map(project => project.title).filter((title): title is string => Boolean(title))
      const desiredTitlesLower = new Set(desiredTitles.map(title => title.toLowerCase()))

      // Check if all projects have IDs - if so, use ID-only matching for precision
      const allProjectsHaveIds = projects.length > 0 && projects.every(project => Boolean(project.id))

      const queryTerms = desiredTitles.length > 0 ? Array.from(new Set(desiredTitles)).join(',') : ''

      const {repositoryProjects, recentRepositoryProjects, ownerProjects, recentOwnerProjects} =
        await queryClient.fetchQuery(searchProjectsQueryOptions({owner, repo, query: queryTerms}))

      const uniqProjects = [
        ...[...repositoryProjects, ...recentRepositoryProjects, ...ownerProjects, ...recentOwnerProjects]
          .reduce((acc, item) => {
            acc.set(item.id, item)
            return acc
          }, new Map<string, ProjectPickerProject>())
          .values(),
      ]

      const filteredProjects = uniqProjects
        .filter(item => item.closed === false)
        .filter(item => item.hasReachedItemsLimit === false)
        // Despite querying by specific names, the query still returns several irrelevant projects
        // This adds an id and case-insensitive comparison on title, which should be what the user is asking for.
        .filter(item => {
          // If all projects have IDs, use ID-only matching for precision
          if (allProjectsHaveIds) {
            return item.id && desiredIds.has(item.id)
          }

          // Otherwise, use the existing mixed logic
          // Match by ID if this item has a desired ID
          if (item.id && desiredIds.has(item.id)) {
            return true
          }

          // Otherwise, match by title if this item's title is desired
          return desiredTitlesLower.has(item.title.toLowerCase())
        })

      // Remove duplicates - use different strategies based on matching approach
      if (allProjectsHaveIds) {
        // When using ID-only matching, no deduplication needed since IDs are unique
        return filteredProjects
      } else {
        // When using mixed matching, deduplicate by title and prioritize projects with desired IDs
        const seenTitles = new Set<string>()
        const sortedProjects = filteredProjects.sort((a, b) => {
          // Prioritize projects with desired IDs
          const aHasDesiredId = desiredIds.has(a.id)
          const bHasDesiredId = desiredIds.has(b.id)

          if (aHasDesiredId && !bHasDesiredId) return -1
          if (!aHasDesiredId && bHasDesiredId) return 1
          return 0
        })

        return sortedProjects.filter(item => {
          const lowerTitle = item.title.toLowerCase()
          if (seenTitles.has(lowerTitle)) {
            return false
          }
          seenTitles.add(lowerTitle)
          return true
        })
      }
    },
  })
}

import {fetchRepository} from '@github-ui/item-picker/RepositoryPicker'
import {useQuery} from '@github-ui/react-query'
import {useRelayEnvironment} from 'react-relay'

export function useRepoQuery({owner, repo}: {owner?: string; repo?: string}) {
  const environment = useRelayEnvironment()

  return useQuery({
    queryKey: ['copilot-immersive-current-repo', JSON.stringify(environment), owner, repo],
    queryFn: async () => {
      if (!owner || !repo) {
        return null
      }

      return await fetchRepository(environment, owner, repo)
    },
  })
}

import {useQuery} from '@github-ui/react-query'
import {useRelayEnvironment} from 'react-relay'
import {Environment, fetchQuery, graphql} from 'relay-runtime'

import type {useRepositoryDefaultBranchQuery} from './__generated__/useRepositoryDefaultBranchQuery.graphql'

// eslint-disable-next-line @github-ui/github-monorepo/restrict-relay-queries
const repositoryDefaultBranchGraphQLQuery = graphql`
  query useRepositoryDefaultBranchQuery($owner: String!, $name: String!) {
    repository(owner: $owner, name: $name) {
      defaultBranchRef {
        name
      }
    }
  }
`

/**
 * Hook to fetch the default branch name for a repository.
 * @param owner - The repository owner login
 * @param name - The repository name
 * @returns Query result containing the default branch name or null
 */
export function useRepositoryDefaultBranch(owner?: string, name?: string) {
  const environment = useRelayEnvironment()

  return useQuery({
    queryKey: ['repository-default-branch', JSON.stringify(environment), owner, name],
    queryFn: async () => {
      if (!owner || !name) {
        return null
      }

      return fetchRepositoryDefaultBranch(environment, owner, name)
    },
  })
}

/**
 * Fetches the default branch name for a repository using GraphQL.
 * @param environment - The Relay environment for making GraphQL queries
 * @param owner - The repository owner login
 * @param name - The repository name
 * @returns Promise resolving to the default branch name or null if not found
 */
export const fetchRepositoryDefaultBranch = async (
  environment: Environment,
  owner: string,
  name: string,
): Promise<string | null> => {
  const data = await fetchQuery<useRepositoryDefaultBranchQuery>(
    environment,
    repositoryDefaultBranchGraphQLQuery,
    {
      owner,
      name,
    },
    {fetchPolicy: 'store-or-network'},
  ).toPromise()

  const branchName: string | null | undefined = data?.repository?.defaultBranchRef?.name
  if (typeof branchName === 'string') {
    return branchName
  }
  return null
}

// eslint-disable-next-line no-restricted-imports
import {useChatState} from '@github-ui/copilot-chat/CopilotChatContext'
import {useChatManager} from '@github-ui/copilot-chat/CopilotChatManagerContext'
import {referenceID} from '@github-ui/copilot-chat/utils/copilot-chat-helpers'
import type {CopilotChatReference} from '@github-ui/copilot-chat/utils/copilot-chat-types'
import safeStorage from '@github-ui/safe-storage'
import isEqual from 'lodash-es/isEqual'
import {useEffect, useRef} from 'react'

import {
  type ContentPreviewContext,
  type ItemsMap,
  makeReferenceFromVersionedItem,
  makeVersionedItemFromReference,
  type PreviewableContent,
  type PreviewableContentIdentifier,
  type VersionedPreviewableContent,
} from './content-preview-types'

const editedItemReferenceTypes: Array<CopilotChatReference['type']> = [
  'thread-scoped-file',
  'draft-issue',
  'draft-update-issue',
]

const isEditedItem = (item: PreviewableContent): item is VersionedPreviewableContent =>
  !!(item as {isUserEdited: boolean | undefined}).isUserEdited

const mapsEqual = (a: Map<unknown, unknown>, b: Map<unknown, unknown>) => {
  return a.size === b.size && Array.from(a).every(([k, v]) => isEqual(b.get(k), v))
}

const haveEditedItemReferencesChanged = (a: CopilotChatReference[], b: CopilotChatReference[]) => {
  const aContentsById = new Map(a.filter(r => editedItemReferenceTypes.includes(r.type)).map(r => [referenceID(r), r]))
  const bContentsById = new Map(b.filter(r => editedItemReferenceTypes.includes(r.type)).map(r => [referenceID(r), r]))
  return !mapsEqual(aContentsById, bContentsById)
}

const haveEditedItemsChanged = (a: ItemsMap, b: ItemsMap) => {
  const userEditedA = new Map(Array.from(a).filter(([, f]) => isEditedItem(f)))
  const userEditedB = new Map(Array.from(b).filter(([, f]) => isEditedItem(f)))
  return !mapsEqual(userEditedA, userEditedB)
}

/**
 * Keeps references in `CopilotChatState.currentReferences` synchronized with the user-edited
 * items in `ContentPreviewContext.items`. Either one might be updated independently, so this hook ensures the
 * two should always remain aligned.
 *
 * This logic is pretty messy, and doing it like this isn't really idiomatic React, but making this effect-based ensures
 * that we can never forget to update one state or the other across the dozens of callsites where we might make changes.
 *
 * Long-term, it would probably be better to resolve this somehow so that we only have one source of truth rather than
 * two. But that probably means rewriting all of `ContentPreviewContext`.
 */
export function useSyncEditedItemsWithCurrentReferences({items, removeItems, updateItem}: ContentPreviewContext) {
  const {currentReferences, messages, selectedThreadID} = useChatState()
  const manager = useChatManager()

  const previousItemsRef = useRef(items)
  const previousReferencesRef = useRef(currentReferences)
  const sessionStorage = safeStorage('sessionStorage')

  useEffect(() => {
    const itemsDidChange = haveEditedItemsChanged(previousItemsRef.current, items)
    previousItemsRef.current = items

    const referencesDidChange = haveEditedItemReferencesChanged(previousReferencesRef.current, currentReferences)
    previousReferencesRef.current = currentReferences

    // If both change at the same time (shouldn't happen) we will use ContentPreviewContext as the source of truth
    if (itemsDidChange) {
      // Update CopilotChatState.currentReferences to match ContentPreviewContext.items
      const itemReferences = Array.from(items.values())
