        .filter(isEditedItem)
        .map(item => makeReferenceFromVersionedItem(item))
        .filter(item => item != null)

      const itemReferencesById = new Map(itemReferences.map(ref => [referenceID(ref), ref]))

      // Remove or update existing references
      const encounteredIds = new Set<string>()
      for (const currentReference of currentReferences) {
        if (editedItemReferenceTypes.includes(currentReference.type)) {
          const id = referenceID(currentReference)
          encounteredIds.add(id)

          const itemReference = itemReferencesById.get(id)

          if (!itemReference) {
            manager.removeReference(currentReference)
          } else if (haveEditedItemReferencesChanged([itemReference], [currentReference])) {
            manager.addReference(itemReference, 'content-preview')
          }
        }
      }

      // Add new references
      for (const [id, itemReference] of itemReferencesById)
        if (!encounteredIds.has(id)) manager.addReference(itemReference, 'content-preview')
    } else if (referencesDidChange) {
      // Update ContentPreviewContext.items to match CopilotChatState.currentReferences

      // Note this is essentially the exact same logic as the other case, but everything is reversed
      const referenceItems = currentReferences
        .map(ref => makeVersionedItemFromReference({ref, messageIndex: messages.length}))
        .filter(item => item != null)

      const referenceItemsById = new Map(referenceItems.map(item => [item.id, item]))

      // Remove or update existing items
      const encounteredIds = new Set<PreviewableContentIdentifier>()
      for (const [id, currentItem] of items) {
        if (isEditedItem(currentItem)) {
          encounteredIds.add(id)

          const referenceItem = referenceItemsById.get(id as (typeof currentItem)['id'])
          if (referenceItem != null) {
            const hasChanged = haveEditedItemsChanged(
              new Map([[referenceItem.id, referenceItem]]),
              new Map([[currentItem.id, currentItem]]),
            )
            if (hasChanged) updateItem(referenceItem)
          } else {
            removeItems([id])
            sessionStorage.removeItem(`edited-${selectedThreadID}-${id}`)
          }
        }
      }

      // Add new items
      for (const [id, referenceItem] of referenceItemsById) if (!encounteredIds.has(id)) updateItem(referenceItem)
    }
  }, [items, manager, currentReferences, messages.length, removeItems, updateItem, sessionStorage, selectedThreadID])
}

import {TemplateQuery} from '@github-ui/issue-create/CreateIssue'
import type {CreateIssueQuery} from '@github-ui/issue-create/CreateIssueQuery'
import {getSelectedTemplate} from '@github-ui/issue-create/use-handle-template-change'
import {useQuery} from '@github-ui/react-query'
import {type Environment, fetchQuery, useRelayEnvironment} from 'react-relay'

export function useDraftIssueFormTemplateQuery({repoId, filename}: {repoId: string; filename: string}) {
  const environment = useRelayEnvironment()

  return useQuery({
    queryKey: ['copilot-immersive-template-payload', JSON.stringify(environment), repoId, filename],
    queryFn: async () => {
      if (!repoId || !filename) {
        return null
      }

      return await fetchTemplate(environment, repoId, filename)
    },
  })
}

// TODO this should live with the template picker
export async function fetchTemplate(environment: Environment, repoId: string, filename: string) {
  const response = await fetchQuery<CreateIssueQuery>(environment, TemplateQuery, {
    repoId,
    filename,
  }).toPromise()

  if (!response || !response.node) {
    return null
  }

  return getSelectedTemplate(response.node) ?? null
}

import {fetchTopRepositories} from '@github-ui/item-picker/RepositoryPicker'
import {useQuery} from '@github-ui/react-query'
import {useRelayEnvironment} from 'react-relay'

export function useTopReposQuery() {
  const environment = useRelayEnvironment()

  return useQuery({
    queryKey: ['copilot-immersive-top-repos', JSON.stringify(environment)],
    queryFn: async () => {
      const topRepositoriesFirst = 10
      const hasIssuesEnabled = true
      return await fetchTopRepositories(environment, {first: topRepositoriesFirst, hasIssuesEnabled})
    },
  })
}

import {uploadFile} from '@github-ui/comment-box/fileUpload'
// eslint-disable-next-line no-restricted-imports
import {useChatState} from '@github-ui/copilot-chat/CopilotChatContext'
import {getActiveMessages} from '@github-ui/copilot-chat/utils/copilot-chat-subthreading-helpers'
import {sendEvent} from '@github-ui/hydro-analytics'
import type {RepositoryPickerRepository} from '@github-ui/item-picker/RepositoryPicker'
import {useCallback} from 'react'

export function useTransformCopilotImageURLsFunction() {
  const {messages} = useChatState()
  const activeMessages = getActiveMessages(messages)

  return useCallback(
    (draftIssueRepo: RepositoryPickerRepository | null | undefined, issueBody: string) => {
      if (draftIssueRepo == null) {
        return Promise.resolve(issueBody)
      }

      return transformCopilotImageUrls(issueBody, draftIssueRepo.databaseId, activeMessages[0]?.threadID)
    },
    [activeMessages],
  )
}

// Download chat attachment URL images, and re-upload them into the issue
// and replace the chat image URLs with the new ones
async function transformCopilotImageUrls(
  issueBody: string,
  repositoryId: number | null | undefined,
  threadId: string | null | undefined,
): Promise<string> {
  // The image alt tag might have been edited by user, but as long as they keep the
  // image URL pointing at chat attachment, we re-upload these
  // Supports environments inside github.com domain, e.g. review-lab
  // This supports current style (numbered) and future style (GUID) image links
  const imageRx = /(!\[.*\]\(https:\/\/(?:.+\.)?github\.com\/github-copilot\/chat\/attachments\/[0-9a-fA-F-]+\))/g

  // Find all unique URLs in the issue body and make a set
  const matches = issueBody.match(imageRx)
  const chatAttachmentURLs = new Set<string>()
  for (const match of matches || []) {
    // This regex must match the above one
    const url = match.match(/https:\/\/(?:.+\.)?github\.com\/github-copilot\/chat\/attachments\/[0-9a-fA-F-]+/)?.[0]
    if (url) {
      chatAttachmentURLs.add(url)
    }
  }

  if (chatAttachmentURLs.size > 0) {
    // Download all images into memory
    const getFilesPromises = Array.from(chatAttachmentURLs).map(async url => {
      // Note that our CSP only allows downloading from a few allowed domains, protecting us from
      // fetching outside of github
      const response = await fetch(url)
      // The below works for local development, to test that image can be read and uploaded
      // const response = await fetch('http://alambic.github.localhost/avatars/u/2?s=40')
      const blob = await response.blob()
      const extension = blob.type.split('/')[1]
      const filename = `copilot-image.${extension}`
      const imageBitmap = await createImageBitmap(blob)
      const width = imageBitmap.width
      imageBitmap.close()
      return {file: new File([blob], filename, {type: blob.type}), url, width}
    })
    const files = await Promise.all(getFilesPromises)

    // Reupload the images to the issue
    const uploadedFiles = await Promise.all(
      files.map(async file => {
        if (file) {
          return {
            uploadedFile: await uploadFile(file.file, repositoryId?.toString()),
            originalUrl: file.url,
            width: file.width,
          }
        }
      }),
    )

    // Replace the original ![anytext](image1) with <img alt="anytext" width="123" src="newURL" />
    for (const file of uploadedFiles) {
      if (file) {
        const {uploadedFile, originalUrl, width} = file
        const newURL = uploadedFile.url
        if (newURL) {
          const regex = new RegExp(`!\\[(.*?)\\]\\(${originalUrl}\\)`, 'g')
          // eslint-disable-next-line github/unescaped-html-literal
          issueBody = issueBody.replace(regex, `<img alt="$1" width="${width}" src="${newURL}" />`)
        }
      }
    }

    sendEvent('dotcom_chat.activate', {
      target: 'BROWSER_ISSUE_CREATED_IMAGES',
      mode: 'immersive',
      imageCount: chatAttachmentURLs.size,
      threadId,
    })
  }

  return issueBody
}

// eslint-disable-next-line no-restricted-imports
import {useChatState} from '@github-ui/copilot-chat/CopilotChatContext'

import type {DraftIssue} from '../content-preview-types'

export function useUserEditedNewIssueId(draftIssue: DraftIssue): DraftIssue['id'] {
  const {messages} = useChatState()
  const {tag, issueNumber} = draftIssue
  const type = issueNumber ? 'update-issue' : 'new-issue'
  return `${type}:${tag}#${messages.length}` // messages.length represents the next user message index
}
import type {CommentBoxHandle} from '@github-ui/comment-box/CommentBox'
import {CommentBoxButton} from '@github-ui/comment-box/CommentBoxButton'
import {ssrSafeDocument, ssrSafeWindow} from '@github-ui/ssr-utils'
import {ScopedCommands} from '@github-ui/ui-commands'
import {clsx} from 'clsx'
import {useCallback, useEffect, useRef, useState} from 'react'

import {usePersistedDiffCommentData} from '../../hooks/use-persisted-comment-data'
import type {DiffSide, SuggestedChangesConfiguration} from '../../conversation-types'
import {validateSuggestedChange} from '../../util/validate-suggested-change'
import styles from './AddCommentEditor.module.css'
import {ConversationCommentBox} from './ConversationCommentBox'
import {useIsCommentBatchPending, usePullRequestBasePath} from '@github-ui/pull-requests/pull-request-store-selectors'
import {useDiffLinesActions, useGetUpdatePersistedComment} from '../../stores/selectors'
import type {DiffLineKey} from '../../types'
import {handleLineSelectionEffects} from '../../helpers/line-selection'
import {extractSuggestionFromMarkdown} from '../../helpers/extract-suggestion-content'
import {forceAnnouncementToScreenReaders} from '@github-ui/pull-requests/utils/force-announcement-to-screen-reader'
import {getDiffLineMarkersKey} from '../../helpers/line-helpers'

export interface AddCommentEditorProps {
  batchingEnabled?: boolean
  condensed: boolean
  fileLevelComment?: boolean
  filePath: string
  focusOnMount?: boolean
  isReplying?: boolean
  lineNumber?: number
  onAddComment?: (args: {
    commentText: string
    onCompleted?: () => void
    onError: (error: Error) => void
    submitBatch?: boolean
  }) => void
  onCancelComment?: (shouldFocusCell?: boolean) => void
  onPersistedCommentExists?: () => void
  quotedText?: string
  side?: DiffSide
  startLineNumber?: number
  threadId?: string
  suggestedChangesConfig?: SuggestedChangesConfiguration
  commentLineChangeConfig?: CommentLineChangeConfig
  setStartLineNumber?: (lineNumber: number | undefined) => void
}

export interface CommentLineChangeConfig {
  diffSide: DiffSide
  endLineNumber: number
  endDiffLineKey: DiffLineKey
  isFileLevelComment: boolean
  startConversationOnNewLine: () => void
  closeLineMovingDialog: () => void
  setErrorMessage: (message: string) => void
  startDiffLineKey?: DiffLineKey
  startLineNumber?: number
}

// Define explicit action types
const SUBMISSION_ACTION = {
  COMMENT: 'comment',
  REVIEW: 'review',
} as const

type SubmissionAction = (typeof SUBMISSION_ACTION)[keyof typeof SUBMISSION_ACTION]

export function AddCommentEditor({
  batchingEnabled,
  condensed,
  fileLevelComment,
  filePath,
  focusOnMount,
  isReplying,
  lineNumber,
  onAddComment,
  onCancelComment,
  onPersistedCommentExists,
  quotedText = '',
  side,
  startLineNumber,
  threadId,
  suggestedChangesConfig,
  commentLineChangeConfig,
}: AddCommentEditorProps) {
  const [commentText, setCommentText] = useState(quotedText)
  const [submittingAction, setSubmittingAction] = useState<SubmissionAction | null>(null)
  const quotedTextRef = useRef(quotedText)
  const [errorMessage, setErrorMessage] = useState<string | undefined>()
  const editorRef = useRef<CommentBoxHandle>(null)
  const commentButtonRef = useRef<HTMLButtonElement>(null)
  const commentBatchPending = useIsCommentBatchPending()
  const updatePersistedComment = useGetUpdatePersistedComment()
  const previousCommentLineConfigRef = useRef<CommentLineChangeConfig | undefined>(undefined)
  const {selectMultipleLines, clearSelectedLines} = useDiffLinesActions()
  const setErrorMessageWithScreenReaderAnnouncement = useCallback(
    (message: string | undefined) => {
      forceAnnouncementToScreenReaders(message ?? '', 100)
      setErrorMessage(message)
    },
    [setErrorMessage],
  )

  const isSubmitting = submittingAction !== null
  const basePath = usePullRequestBasePath()

  const {persistCommentToStorage, removePersistedCommentFromStorage, persistCommentOnGivenLineToStorage} =
    usePersistedDiffCommentData({
      diffSide: side,
      filePath,
      line: lineNumber,
      subjectId: basePath,
      threadId,
      handlePersistedCommentExists: ({text, shouldFocus, startLine, startSide, startLineKey, endLineKey}) => {
        if (!text) return
        setCommentText(text)
        if (shouldFocus && editorRef.current) {
          editorRef.current.focus()
          editorRef.current.moveCursorToEnd()
          persistCommentToStorage({text, startLine, startSide, startLineKey, endLineKey}) // update to ensure shouldFocus is removed
          if (startLine) {
            //if we should focus and there is a start line, it means we moved a multiline comment
            const lineOrientation = side === 'LEFT' ? 'left' : 'right'
            selectMultipleLines(
              {
                endLineKey,
                endOrientation: lineOrientation,
                startLineKey,
                startOrientation: lineOrientation,
                firstLineSelectionOnDrag: {
                  lineKey: startLineKey ?? 'b:0-l:0-r:0',
                  lineNumber: lineNumber ?? 0,
                  orientation: lineOrientation,
                  shouldStartConversation: false,
                },
              },
              handleLineSelectionEffects,
            )
          }
        }

        onPersistedCommentExists?.()
      },
      fileLevelComment: !!fileLevelComment,
    })

  useEffect(() => {
    if (quotedText !== quotedTextRef.current) {
      quotedTextRef.current = quotedText

      // The effect is only triggered when quotedText changes which only happens when "quote reply" is clicked
      // We maintain a ref to the previous quotedText to avoid unnecessary state updates mitigating performance concerns
      // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/set-state-in-effect
      setCommentText(quotedText)
    }
  }, [quotedText])

  useEffect(() => {
    if (commentLineChangeConfig) {
      if (extractSuggestionFromMarkdown(commentText) !== null) {
        commentLineChangeConfig.setErrorMessage(
          'Changing the line number of a comment with a suggested change is not supported.',
        )
        return
      }
      commentLineChangeConfig.closeLineMovingDialog()
      persistCommentOnGivenLineToStorage({
        text: commentText,
        givenLineDiffSide: commentLineChangeConfig.diffSide,
        givenFilePath: filePath,
        givenLine: commentLineChangeConfig.endLineNumber,
        shouldBeFileLevelComment: commentLineChangeConfig.isFileLevelComment,
        startLine: commentLineChangeConfig.startLineNumber,
        startLineKey: commentLineChangeConfig.startDiffLineKey,
        endLineKey: commentLineChangeConfig.endDiffLineKey,
      })

      removePersistedCommentFromStorage()
      if (lineNumber !== commentLineChangeConfig.endLineNumber || commentLineChangeConfig.isFileLevelComment) {
        // we don't want to cancel the current comment if the end line is the same
        commentLineChangeConfig.startConversationOnNewLine()
        onCancelComment?.(false)
      } else if (
        commentLineChangeConfig.endDiffLineKey &&
        commentLineChangeConfig.startDiffLineKey &&
        previousCommentLineConfigRef.current !== commentLineChangeConfig
      ) {
        // if the line number is the same and there is a different start line number, we need to modify the selection
        // in order to prevent infinite loops we need to track the previous comment line config. I don't love this
        // solution, but given how we have to manage the line selection in order to move multi-line comments, this was
        // necessary for changing the start line only of a multi-line comment, or moving a multi-ine comment back to
        // a single line comment.
        previousCommentLineConfigRef.current = commentLineChangeConfig
        const lineOrientation = side === 'LEFT' ? 'left' : 'right'
        selectMultipleLines(
          {
            endLineKey: commentLineChangeConfig.endDiffLineKey,
            endOrientation: lineOrientation,
            startLineKey: commentLineChangeConfig.startDiffLineKey,
            startOrientation: lineOrientation,
            firstLineSelectionOnDrag: {
              lineKey: commentLineChangeConfig.startDiffLineKey ?? 'b:0-l:0-r:0',
              lineNumber: lineNumber ?? 0,
              orientation: lineOrientation,
              shouldStartConversation: false,
            },
          },
          handleLineSelectionEffects,
        )
      } else if (!commentLineChangeConfig.startDiffLineKey) {
        clearSelectedLines()
      }
    }
  }, [
    clearSelectedLines,
    commentLineChangeConfig,
    commentText,
    filePath,
    lineNumber,
    onCancelComment,
    persistCommentOnGivenLineToStorage,
    removePersistedCommentFromStorage,
    selectMultipleLines,
    side,
  ])

  useEffect(() => {
    if (focusOnMount) {
      const timeout = setTimeout(() => {
        if (!ssrSafeDocument || !ssrSafeWindow) return

        // If the comment button is not in view, scroll it into view, so the user can see the comment box
        if (commentButtonRef.current) {
          const commentButton = commentButtonRef.current
          const commentButtonRect = commentButton.getBoundingClientRect()
          const documentClientHeight = ssrSafeDocument?.documentElement.clientHeight || 0
          const commentButtonIsVisible =
            commentButtonRect.top >= 0 &&
            commentButtonRect.bottom <= (ssrSafeWindow?.innerHeight || documentClientHeight)

          if (!commentButtonIsVisible) {
            // this element will always be rendered below the fold if not visible, so we need to scroll it into view
            // we want this to sit comfortably above the fold, so we scroll it to 2/3 of the way down the viewport
            const targetTop = commentButtonRect.top
            const documentTop = ssrSafeDocument?.documentElement.getBoundingClientRect().top || 0
            const twoThirdsOfDocumentHeight = documentClientHeight * (2 / 3)
            const twoThirdsOfClientHeight = commentButton.clientHeight * (2 / 3)
            const targetScroll = targetTop - documentTop - twoThirdsOfDocumentHeight + twoThirdsOfClientHeight

            ssrSafeWindow?.scrollTo({top: targetScroll, behavior: 'smooth'})
          }
        }

        if (editorRef.current) {
          // Focus the editor after scrolling to the comment button
          editorRef.current.focus()

          // Move the cursor to the end of the comment text
          editorRef.current.moveCursorToEnd()
        }
      }, 100)

      return () => {
        window.clearTimeout(timeout)
      }
    }
  }, [focusOnMount])

  const handleAddComment = ({submitBatch, action}: {submitBatch: boolean; action: SubmissionAction}) => {
    let msg = 'Failed to save comment'

    if (isSubmitting) return

    if (!commentText.trim()) {
      setErrorMessageWithScreenReaderAnnouncement(`${msg}: Body can't be blank`)
      return
    }

    setSubmittingAction(action)
    setErrorMessage(undefined)
    setErrorMessageWithScreenReaderAnnouncement(undefined)

    const suggestedChangedEvaluation = validateSuggestedChange(
      commentText,
      suggestedChangesConfig?.sourceContentFromDiffLines ?? '',
    )

    if (!suggestedChangedEvaluation.isValid) {
      setErrorMessageWithScreenReaderAnnouncement(suggestedChangedEvaluation.errorMessage)
      setErrorMessage(suggestedChangedEvaluation.errorMessage)
      setSubmittingAction(null)

      return
    }
    if (lineNumber !== undefined && !isReplying) {
      const lineKey = getDiffLineMarkersKey(lineNumber, side ?? 'RIGHT')
      updatePersistedComment(lineKey, false)
    }

    onAddComment?.({
      commentText,
      onCompleted() {
        setSubmittingAction(null)
        closeCommentBox()
      },
      onError(error) {
        setSubmittingAction(null)

        //if we failed to save, we want to re-persist the comment state
        if (lineNumber !== undefined && !isReplying) {
          const lineKey = getDiffLineMarkersKey(lineNumber, side ?? 'RIGHT')
          updatePersistedComment(lineKey, true)
        }
        if (error.message) {
          msg = `${msg}: ${error.message}`
        }
        setErrorMessageWithScreenReaderAnnouncement(msg)
      },
      submitBatch,
    })
  }

  const handleAddReviewCommentAsPending = () => {
    handleAddComment({submitBatch: false, action: SUBMISSION_ACTION.REVIEW})
  }
  const handleSubmitPendingComments = () => {
    handleAddComment({submitBatch: true, action: SUBMISSION_ACTION.COMMENT})
  }

  const closeCommentBox = useCallback(() => {
    if (lineNumber !== undefined && !isReplying) {
      const lineKey = getDiffLineMarkersKey(lineNumber, side ?? 'RIGHT')
      updatePersistedComment(lineKey, false)
    }
    removePersistedCommentFromStorage()
    setCommentText('')
    onCancelComment?.()
  }, [lineNumber, isReplying, removePersistedCommentFromStorage, onCancelComment, side, updatePersistedComment])
  const handleCommentTextChange = (text: string) => {
    setErrorMessageWithScreenReaderAnnouncement(undefined)
    if (lineNumber !== undefined && !isReplying) {
      const lineKey = getDiffLineMarkersKey(lineNumber, side ?? 'RIGHT')
      const hasText = text.trim().length > 0
      updatePersistedComment(lineKey, hasText)
    }
    persistCommentToStorage({text, startLine: startLineNumber, startSide: startLineNumber ? side : undefined})
    setCommentText(text)
  }

  return (
    <ScopedCommands
      commands={{
        'pull-requests-conversations:submit-comment-and-review': handleSubmitPendingComments,
      }}
    >
      {/* eslint-disable-next-line jsx-a11y/no-static-element-interactions */}
      <div
        className={styles.AddCommentEditor}
        onKeyDown={event => {
          // eslint-disable-next-line @github-ui/ui-commands/no-manual-shortcut-logic
          if (event.key === 'Escape' && !commentText.trim()) {
            event.preventDefault()
            closeCommentBox()
          } else {
            event.stopPropagation()
          }
        }}
      >
        <ConversationCommentBox
          ref={editorRef}
          label="Add diff comment"
          value={commentText}
          onChange={handleCommentTextChange}
          onPrimaryAction={batchingEnabled ? handleAddReviewCommentAsPending : handleSubmitPendingComments}
          suggestedChangesConfig={suggestedChangesConfig}
          markdownErrorMessage={errorMessage}
          lineNumber={lineNumber}
          filePath={filePath}
          startLineNumber={startLineNumber}
          disabled={isSubmitting}
          className={clsx(
            styles.ConversationCommentBox,
            condensed || isReplying ? styles.hideMarkdownFooter : styles.showMarkdownFooter,
            isReplying && styles.isReplying,
            condensed && styles.condensed,
          )}
        >
          {onCancelComment && (
            <CommentBoxButton className="py-1 px-2" variant="default" onClick={closeCommentBox}>
              Cancel
            </CommentBoxButton>
          )}
          {!commentBatchPending && (
            <CommentBoxButton
              className="py-1 px-2"
              variant={batchingEnabled ? 'default' : 'primary'}
              onClick={handleSubmitPendingComments}
              ref={commentButtonRef}
              loading={submittingAction === SUBMISSION_ACTION.COMMENT}
              inactive={isSubmitting || commentText.trim().length === 0}
            >
              {isReplying ? 'Reply' : 'Comment'}
            </CommentBoxButton>
          )}
          {batchingEnabled && (
            <CommentBoxButton
              className="py-1 px-2"
              variant="primary"
              onClick={handleAddReviewCommentAsPending}
              loading={submittingAction === SUBMISSION_ACTION.REVIEW}
              inactive={isSubmitting || commentText.trim().length === 0}
            >
              {commentBatchPending ? 'Add review comment' : 'Start a review'}
            </CommentBoxButton>
          )}
        </ConversationCommentBox>
      </div>
    </ScopedCommands>
  )
}

import {GitHubAvatar} from '@github-ui/github-avatar'
import type {SafeHTMLString} from '@github-ui/safe-html'
import {VerifiedHTMLDiv} from '@github-ui/safe-html/VerifiedHTML'
import {AlertIcon, ChevronDownIcon, ChevronRightIcon, InfoIcon, StopIcon} from '@primer/octicons-react'
import {Button, IconButton, Text, Truncate} from '@primer/react'
import {clsx} from 'clsx'

import type {DiffAnnotation} from '../../conversation-types'
import {DiffAnnotationLevels} from '../../conversation-types'
import styles from './Annotation.module.css'
import {AnnotationPresentationMap} from './AnnotationIcon'
import {useEffect, useMemo, useState, type PropsWithChildren} from 'react'
import safeStorage from '@github-ui/safe-storage'
import {ssrSafeWindow} from '@github-ui/ssr-utils'

export const IconMap = {
  [DiffAnnotationLevels.Failure]: <StopIcon className="fgColor-danger mr-2" />,
  [DiffAnnotationLevels.Warning]: <AlertIcon className="fgColor-attention mr-2" />,
  [DiffAnnotationLevels.Notice]: <InfoIcon className="fgColor-muted mr-2" />,
}

/**
 * A check annotation
 * Preferred method is to pass in ghostUser from the app payload
 * Because this is a shared package, ghostUser is optional and will default to constants
 */
export function Annotation({annotation, inSidePanel}: {annotation: DiffAnnotation; inSidePanel?: boolean}) {
  const AnnotationHeadingTag = useMemo(() => (inSidePanel ? 'h3' : 'h2'), [inSidePanel])
  const {isCollapsed, handleToggleCollapsed} = useAnnotationCollapseState(annotation)
  return (
    <div
      className={clsx(
        'p-1 bgColor-default',
        AnnotationPresentationMap[annotation.annotationLevel].borderColor,
        styles.annotation,
      )}
    >
      <div className="d-flex flex-items-center">
        {!inSidePanel && (
          <IconButton
            aria-label={isCollapsed ? 'Expand annotation' : 'Collapse annotation'}
            icon={isCollapsed ? ChevronRightIcon : ChevronDownIcon}
            size="small"
            variant="invisible"
            onClick={handleToggleCollapsed}
          />
        )}
        {!inSidePanel && <AnnotationBanner annotation={annotation} />}
        {inSidePanel && <SidePanelAnnotationBanner annotation={annotation} />}
      </div>
      {(!isCollapsed || inSidePanel) && (
        <>
          <div className="tmp-ml-4 mt-2">
            {annotation.title && (
              <Text
                weight="semibold"
                size="medium"
                className={AnnotationPresentationMap[annotation.annotationLevel].primaryColorClass}
              >
                {annotation.title}
              </Text>
            )}
          </div>
          <AnnotationHeadingTag className="sr-only">
            {AnnotationPresentationMap[annotation.annotationLevel].ariaLabel}: {annotation.title}
          </AnnotationHeadingTag>
          <div className="tmp-mb-3 mt-1 tmp-ml-4">
            {/* Message is sanitized from the server, see PullRequests::PageData::Annotations::Loader */}
            {annotation.message && (
              /* eslint-disable-next-line @github-ui/github-monorepo/no-cast-as-safe-html-string -- HTML is sanitized server-side. TODO: Move type assertion to data ingress point. */
              <VerifiedHTMLDiv html={annotation.message as SafeHTMLString} className={styles.annotationMessage} />
            )}
          </div>
          <div className="d-flex flex-items-center flex-justify-between tmp-ml-4 pr-2 pb-2">
            <div className={styles.annotationSource}>
              <GitHubAvatar src={annotation.appAvatarUrl} alt={annotation.appAvatarAltText} />
              <Truncate
                className="ml-2"
                maxWidth="300px"
                inline
                title={`${annotation.checkSuiteName} / ${annotation.checkRun.name}`}
              >
                <Text weight="semibold" size="small">
                  {annotation.checkSuiteName}
                </Text>{' '}
                / <Text size="small">{annotation.checkRun.name}</Text>
              </Truncate>
            </div>
            {annotation.checkRun.detailsUrl && (
              <Button as="a" href={annotation.checkRun.detailsUrl} size="small">
                View details
              </Button>
            )}
          </div>
        </>
      )}
    </div>
  )
}

function Emphasis({children}: PropsWithChildren) {
  return <span className="text-semibold fgColor-default">{children}</span>
}

function AnnotationBanner({annotation}: {annotation: DiffAnnotation}) {
  const diffSideLetter = 'R'
  return (
    <h2 className={styles.inlineReviewThreadHeading}>
      {IconMap[annotation.annotationLevel]}
      {AnnotationPresentationMap[annotation.annotationLevel].ariaLabel} on line{' '}
      <Emphasis>
        {diffSideLetter}
        {annotation.endLine}
      </Emphasis>
    </h2>
  )
}

function SidePanelAnnotationBanner({annotation}: {annotation: DiffAnnotation}) {
  return (
    <div className={clsx(styles.inlineReviewThreadHeading, 'tmp-ml-4', 'pt-1', 'pl-0')}>
      <Emphasis>{AnnotationPresentationMap[annotation.annotationLevel].ariaLabel}</Emphasis>
    </div>
  )
}

export function createAnnotationLocalStorageKey(annotation: DiffAnnotation) {
  return `AnnotationIsCollapsed_${annotation.path}_${annotation.endLine}_${annotation.annotationLevel}_${annotation.title}`
}

export function useAnnotationCollapseState(annotation: DiffAnnotation) {
  const safeLocalStorage = safeStorage('localStorage')

  const [isCollapsed, setIsCollapsed] = useState<boolean>(false)

  useEffect(() => {
    const storedState = localStorage.getItem(createAnnotationLocalStorageKey(annotation))
    if (storedState !== null) {
      // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/set-state-in-effect
      setIsCollapsed(JSON.parse(storedState))
    }
  }, [annotation])

  const handleToggleCollapsed = () => {
    ssrSafeWindow?.requestAnimationFrame(() => {
      safeLocalStorage.setItem(createAnnotationLocalStorageKey(annotation), JSON.stringify(!isCollapsed))
    })
    setIsCollapsed((prevIsCollapsed: boolean) => !prevIsCollapsed)
  }
  return {isCollapsed, handleToggleCollapsed}
}

import {clsx} from 'clsx'

import {DiffAnnotationLevels} from '../../conversation-types'
import styles from './AnnotationIcon.module.css'

export type AnnotationIconProps = {
  annotationLevel: DiffAnnotationLevels
  className?: string
}

/**
 * Renders an svg corresponding to the given annotation level
 */
export function AnnotationIcon({annotationLevel, ...rest}: AnnotationIconProps) {
  const {icon, ...annotationData} = AnnotationPresentationMap[annotationLevel]
  return buildAnnotationIcon({...icon, ...annotationData, ...rest})
}

/**
 * Renders an annotation icon that fades in
 */
export function AnimatedAnnotationIcon({className, ...props}: AnnotationIconProps) {
  return <AnnotationIcon {...props} className={clsx(styles.AnimatedAnnotationIcon, className)} />
}

function buildAnnotationIcon({
  fill,
  stroke,
  ariaLabel,
  svgHtml,
  className,
}: {
  fill: string
  stroke: string
  ariaLabel: string
  svgHtml: React.ReactElement
  className?: string
}): React.ReactElement {
  return (
    <svg
      aria-label={ariaLabel}
      className={className}
      fill={fill}
      height="24"
      stroke={stroke}
      strokeLinecap="round"
      strokeLinejoin="round"
      strokeWidth="2"
      style={{boxShadow: 'none'}}
      viewBox="0 0 24 24"
      width="24"
      xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg"
    >
      {svgHtml}
    </svg>
  )
}

    ariaLabel: string
    primaryColor: string
    primaryColorClass: string
    borderColor: string
    icon: {

      stroke: string
      svgHtml: React.ReactElement
    }
  }
} = {
  [DiffAnnotationLevels.Failure]: {
    priority: 1,
    ariaLabel: 'Check failure',
    primaryColor: 'danger.fg',
    primaryColorClass: 'fgColor-danger',
    borderColor: 'borderColor-danger-emphasis',
    icon: {
      fill: 'var(--bgColor-danger-emphasis, var(--color-danger-emphasis))',
      stroke: 'var(--fgColor-onEmphasis, var(--color-fg-on-emphasis))',
      svgHtml: (
        <>
          <circle cx="12" cy="12" r="10" />
          <line x1="15" x2="9" y1="9" y2="15" />
          <line x1="9" x2="15" y1="9" y2="15" />
        </>
      ),
    },
  },
  [DiffAnnotationLevels.Warning]: {
    priority: 2,
    ariaLabel: 'Check warning',
    primaryColor: 'attention.fg',
    primaryColorClass: 'fgColor-attention',
    borderColor: 'borderColor-attention-emphasis',
    icon: {
      fill: 'var(--bgColor-attention-emphasis, var(--color-attention-emphasis))',
      stroke: 'var(--fgColor-onEmphasis, var(--color-fg-on-emphasis))',
      svgHtml: (
        <>
          <circle cx="12" cy="12" r="10" />
          <line x1="12" x2="12" y1="8" y2="12" />
          <line x1="12" x2="12" y1="16" y2="16" />
        </>
      ),
    },
  },
  [DiffAnnotationLevels.Notice]: {
    priority: 3,
    ariaLabel: 'Check notice',
    primaryColor: 'fg.muted',
    primaryColorClass: 'fgColor-muted',
    borderColor: 'fgColor-muted',
    icon: {
      fill: 'var(--bgColor-neutral-emphasis, var(--color-neutral-emphasis))',
      stroke: 'var(--fgColor-onEmphasis, var(--color-fg-on-emphasis))',
      svgHtml: (
        <>
          <circle cx="12" cy="12" r="10" />
          <line x1="12" x2="12" y1="16" y2="12" />
          <line x1="12" x2="12" y1="8" y2="8" />
        </>
      ),
    },
  },
}

import {debounce} from '@github/mini-throttle'
import {announce} from '@github-ui/aria-live'

import {DragAndDropResources} from './strings'
import {type DragAndDropItemBase, DragAndDropMoveOptions} from './types'

const emptyAnnouncement = () => ''

/**
 * Announces a message to screen readers at a slowed-down rate. This is useful when you want to announce
 * drag and drop movements to screen readers but you don't want to overwhelm the user with too many announcements
 * in rapid succession. Do not use this function if interactions that trigger announcements do not happen rapidly.
 */
export const debounceAnnouncement = debounce((announcement: string) => {
  announce(announcement, {assertive: true})
}, 100)

/*
 * When passed into dnd-kit's DNDContext default announcements will be turned off.
 * This is useful and should only be used when you want to provide your own announcements.
 */
export const defaultAnnouncementsOff = {
  onDragStart: emptyAnnouncement,
  onDragOver: emptyAnnouncement,
  onDragMove: emptyAnnouncement,
  onDragEnd: emptyAnnouncement,
  onDragCancel: emptyAnnouncement,
}

export function successfulMoveDialogAnnouncement({
  newIndex,
  items,
  title,
  ...rest
}: {
  newIndex: number
  currentIndex: number
  items: DragAndDropItemBase[]
  title: string
  moveAction: DragAndDropMoveOptions
}) {
  const itemBeforeTitle = items[newIndex - 1]?.title
  const itemAfterTitle = items[newIndex + 1]?.title

  const message = getMoveDialogMessage({
    equalMessage: DragAndDropResources.successfulNoMove(title),
    firstPositionMessage: DragAndDropResources.successfulFirstMove(title),
    lastPositionMessage: DragAndDropResources.successfulLastMove(title),
    betweenBeforeMessage: DragAndDropResources.successfulMove(title, itemBeforeTitle, items[newIndex]?.title),
    betweenAfterMessage: DragAndDropResources.successfulMove(title, items[newIndex]?.title, itemAfterTitle),
    newIndex,
    items,
    ...rest,
  })

  announce(message, {assertive: true})
}

export function getMoveDialogMessage({
  equalMessage,
  firstPositionMessage,
  lastPositionMessage,
  betweenBeforeMessage,
  betweenAfterMessage,
  newIndex,
  currentIndex,
  items,
  moveAction,
}: {
  equalMessage: string
  firstPositionMessage: string
  lastPositionMessage: string
  betweenBeforeMessage: string
  betweenAfterMessage: string
  newIndex: number
  currentIndex: number
  items: DragAndDropItemBase[]
  moveAction: DragAndDropMoveOptions
}) {
  if (newIndex === currentIndex) {
    return equalMessage
  } else if (newIndex === 0 && moveAction !== DragAndDropMoveOptions.AFTER) {
    return firstPositionMessage
  } else if (newIndex === items.length - 1 && moveAction !== DragAndDropMoveOptions.BEFORE) {
    return lastPositionMessage
  } else {
    switch (moveAction) {
      case DragAndDropMoveOptions.BEFORE:
        return betweenBeforeMessage
      case DragAndDropMoveOptions.AFTER:
        return betweenAfterMessage
      case DragAndDropMoveOptions.ROW:
        if (newIndex < currentIndex) {
          return betweenBeforeMessage
        } else {
          return betweenAfterMessage
        }
    }
  }
}

export function successfulMoveAnnouncement({
  newIndex,
  currentIndex,
  items,
  title,
}: {
  newIndex: number
  currentIndex: number
  items: DragAndDropItemBase[]
  title: string
}) {
  const itemIds = items.map(item => item.id)
  if (newIndex === currentIndex) {
    announce(DragAndDropResources.successfulNoMove(title), {assertive: true})
  } else if (newIndex === 0) {
    announce(DragAndDropResources.successfulFirstMove(title), {assertive: true})
  } else if (newIndex === items.length - 1) {
    announce(DragAndDropResources.successfulLastMove(title), {assertive: true})
  } else {
    const isBefore = newIndex <= currentIndex
    const itemA = isBefore ? itemIds[newIndex - 1] : itemIds[newIndex + 1]
    const itemB = itemIds[newIndex]
    const itemATitle = items.find(item => item.id === itemA)?.title ?? ''
    const itemBTitle = items.find(item => item.id === itemB)?.title ?? ''

    announce(DragAndDropResources.successfulMove(title, itemATitle, itemBTitle), {assertive: true})
  }
}

import {useAnalytics} from '@github-ui/use-analytics'
import {Button, Label} from '@primer/react'
import {type RefObject, useCallback, useRef} from 'react'
import {
  useSuggestionInBatch,
  useSuggestionBatchActions,
  useIsSuggestionBatchPending,
  useSuggestionBatch,
  usePullRequestBasePath,
  useComparisonInfo,
} from '@github-ui/pull-requests/pull-request-store-selectors'
import {useFeatureFlag} from '@github-ui/react-core/use-feature-flag'
import type {SuggestedChange} from '../../../conversation-types'
import {saveActiveBatchToStorage} from '@github-ui/pull-requests/helpers/suggestion-batch-storage'

export function ApplyOrAddToBatch({
  onOpenDialog,
  suggestedChange,
  canAddToBatch = true,
}: {
  onOpenDialog: (returnFocusRef?: RefObject<HTMLElement | null>) => void
  suggestedChange: SuggestedChange
  canAddToBatch?: boolean
}) {
  const {sendAnalyticsEvent} = useAnalytics()
  const returnFocusRef = useRef<HTMLButtonElement>(null)

  const isPendingInBatch = useSuggestionInBatch({
    commentDatabaseId: suggestedChange.commentDatabaseId || 0,
    suggestionIndex: suggestedChange.suggestionIndex,
  })
  const isSuggestionBatchPending = useIsSuggestionBatchPending()
  const batchSuggestedChangesEnabled = useFeatureFlag('batch_suggested_changes')

  const {addSuggestionToBatch, removeSuggestionFromBatch} = useSuggestionBatchActions()
  const suggestionBatch = useSuggestionBatch()
  const pullRequestBasePath = usePullRequestBasePath()
  const comparisonInfo = useComparisonInfo()

  const handleAddToBatch = useCallback(() => {
    if (!suggestedChange.commentDatabaseId) {
      return
    }
    const suggestionId = {
      commentDatabaseId: suggestedChange.commentDatabaseId,
      suggestionIndex: suggestedChange.suggestionIndex,
      threadId: suggestedChange.threadId,
      path: suggestedChange.path,
      lineRange: suggestedChange.lineRange,
    }
    addSuggestionToBatch(suggestionId)
    saveActiveBatchToStorage(pullRequestBasePath, comparisonInfo.fullDiff.headOid, [
      ...suggestionBatch.activeBatch,
      suggestionId,
    ])

    sendAnalyticsEvent('comments.add_suggested_change_to_batch', 'ADD_SUGGESTED_CHANGE_TO_BATCH_BUTTON')
  }, [
    addSuggestionToBatch,
    suggestedChange.commentDatabaseId,
    suggestedChange.suggestionIndex,
    suggestedChange.threadId,
    suggestedChange.path,
    suggestedChange.lineRange,
    suggestionBatch,
    pullRequestBasePath,
    comparisonInfo,
    sendAnalyticsEvent,
  ])

  const handleRemoveFromBatch = useCallback(() => {
    if (!suggestedChange.commentDatabaseId) {
      return
    }
    const suggestionId = {
      commentDatabaseId: suggestedChange.commentDatabaseId,
      suggestionIndex: suggestedChange.suggestionIndex,
    }
    removeSuggestionFromBatch(suggestionId)
    saveActiveBatchToStorage(
      pullRequestBasePath,
      comparisonInfo.fullDiff.headOid,
      suggestionBatch.activeBatch.filter(
        id =>
          !(
            id.commentDatabaseId === suggestionId.commentDatabaseId &&
            id.suggestionIndex === suggestionId.suggestionIndex
          ),
      ),
    )

    sendAnalyticsEvent('comments.remove_suggested_change_from_batch', 'REMOVE_SUGGESTED_CHANGE_FROM_BATCH_BUTTON')
  }, [
    removeSuggestionFromBatch,
    suggestedChange.commentDatabaseId,
    suggestedChange.suggestionIndex,
    suggestionBatch,
    pullRequestBasePath,
    comparisonInfo,
    sendAnalyticsEvent,
  ])

  const handleApplySuggestion = useCallback(() => {
    onOpenDialog(returnFocusRef)
  }, [onOpenDialog])

  return (
    <div className="d-flex flex-row flex-items-center">
      {batchSuggestedChangesEnabled && isPendingInBatch ? (
        <>
          <Label variant="attention">Pending in batch</Label>
          <div className="ml-auto">
            <Button variant="danger" onClick={handleRemoveFromBatch}>
              Remove from batch
            </Button>
          </div>
        </>
      ) : (
        <div className="d-flex gap-2 ml-auto flex-wrap-reverse">
          {!isSuggestionBatchPending && (
            <Button variant="default" onClick={handleApplySuggestion} ref={returnFocusRef}>
              Apply suggestion
            </Button>
          )}
          {batchSuggestedChangesEnabled && canAddToBatch && (
            <Button variant="primary" onClick={handleAddToBatch}>
              Add suggestion to batch
            </Button>
          )}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  )
}

import {useAnalytics} from '@github-ui/use-analytics'
import useIsMounted from '@github-ui/use-is-mounted'
import {ScopedCommands} from '@github-ui/ui-commands'
import {FocusKeys} from '@primer/behaviors'
import {FormControl, Spinner, Textarea, TextInput, useFocusZone, IconButton, CounterLabel} from '@primer/react'
import type {DialogProps} from '@primer/react/experimental'
import {Dialog} from '@primer/react/experimental'
import {type PropsWithChildren, useCallback, useReducer, useState} from 'react'
import {FileIcon, XIcon} from '@primer/octicons-react'
import {VerifiedHTMLBox} from '@github-ui/safe-html/VerifiedHTML'
import type {SafeHTMLString} from '@github-ui/safe-html'
import {DiscardBatchConfirmationDialog} from '../DiscardBatchConfirmationDialog'
import type {SuggestionIdentifier, SuggestionDetails} from '../../../conversation-types'

function DialogFooter({children, footerButtons}: PropsWithChildren<Pick<DialogProps, 'footerButtons'>>) {
  const {containerRef: footerRef} = useFocusZone({
    bindKeys: FocusKeys.ArrowHorizontal | FocusKeys.Tab,
    focusInStrategy: 'closest',
  })
  return footerButtons ? (
    <Dialog.Footer ref={footerRef as React.RefObject<HTMLDivElement | null>}>
      <div className="d-flex flex-column">
        <div className="d-flex flex-row gap-2 flex-justify-end">
          <Dialog.Buttons buttons={footerButtons} />
        </div>
        {children}
      </div>
    </Dialog.Footer>
  ) : null
}

type FormState = {
  commitMessage: string
  extendedDescription: string
}

type FormAction = {
  type: ThisType<typeof actions.CHANGE_COMMIT_MESSAGE> | ThisType<typeof actions.CHANGE_EXTENDED_DESCRIPTION>
  payload: string
}

const actions = {
  CHANGE_COMMIT_MESSAGE: 'CHANGE_COMMIT_MESSAGE',
  CHANGE_EXTENDED_DESCRIPTION: 'CHANGE_EXTENDED_DESCRIPTION',
}

const baseErrorMessage = 'There was an error trying to commit changes'

function formReducer(state: FormState, action: FormAction): FormState {
  switch (action.type) {
    case actions.CHANGE_COMMIT_MESSAGE: {
      return {
        ...state,
        commitMessage: action.payload,
      }
    }
    case actions.CHANGE_EXTENDED_DESCRIPTION:
      return {...state, extendedDescription: action.payload}
    default:
      // eslint-disable-next-line no-console
      console.error('Unknown suggested change action.')
      return state
  }
}

function getCommitMessage(authorLogins: string[]) {
  // if there's only one suggestion
  if (authorLogins.length === 1) {
    return `Apply suggestion from @${authorLogins[0]}`
  }

  // if multiple suggestions that all have the same login
  if (new Set(authorLogins).size === 1) {
    return `Apply suggestions from @${authorLogins[0]}`
  }

  // multiple suggestions with different logins
  return `Apply suggestions from code review`
}

export type ApplySuggestionDialogProps = {
  authorLogins: string[]
  batchSize: number
  onClose: () => void
  onCommit: (
    commitMessage: string,
    onError: (error: Error, type?: string, friendlyMessage?: string) => void,
    onCompleted: () => void,
  ) => void
  defaultCommitMessage?: string
  dialogTitle?: string
  suggestions?: SuggestionDetails[]
  onRemoveSuggestion?: (suggestionId: SuggestionIdentifier) => void
  onDiscardBatch?: () => void
  returnFocusRef?: React.RefObject<HTMLElement | null>
}

export function ApplySuggestionDialog({
  authorLogins,
  batchSize,
  onClose,
  onCommit,
  defaultCommitMessage,
  dialogTitle,
  suggestions,
  onRemoveSuggestion,
  onDiscardBatch,
  returnFocusRef,
}: ApplySuggestionDialogProps) {
  const isMounted = useIsMounted()
  const {sendAnalyticsEvent} = useAnalytics()
  const [isSubmitting, setIsSubmitting] = useState(false)
  const [showDiscardConfirmation, setShowDiscardConfirmation] = useState(false)
  const [errorMessage, setErrorMessage] = useState<string | undefined>()
  const [formValues, setFormValues] = useReducer(formReducer, {
    commitMessage: defaultCommitMessage || getCommitMessage(authorLogins),
    extendedDescription: '',
  })
  const handleError = useCallback(
    (_error: Error, _type?: string, friendlyMessage?: string) => {
      if (!isMounted()) return

      let serverErrorMessage = baseErrorMessage
      if (friendlyMessage) serverErrorMessage += `: ${friendlyMessage}`
      else serverErrorMessage += '.'

      setIsSubmitting(false)
      setErrorMessage(serverErrorMessage)
    },
    [isMounted],
  )

  const handleCompleted = useCallback(() => {
    if (!isMounted()) return
    setIsSubmitting(false)
    if (errorMessage) setErrorMessage(undefined)
  }, [errorMessage, isMounted])

  const handleOnCommitClick = useCallback(() => {
    if (isSubmitting) return

    setIsSubmitting(true)
    sendAnalyticsEvent('comments.commit_suggestion', 'COMMIT_SUGGESTION_DIALOG_BUTTON', {batchSize})
    onCommit(`${formValues.commitMessage}\n\n${formValues.extendedDescription}`, handleError, handleCompleted)
  }, [
    batchSize,
    formValues.commitMessage,
    formValues.extendedDescription,
    handleCompleted,
    handleError,
    isSubmitting,
    onCommit,
    sendAnalyticsEvent,
  ])

  const handleDiscardClick = useCallback(() => {
    setShowDiscardConfirmation(true)
  }, [])

  const handleDiscardConfirm = useCallback(() => {
    setShowDiscardConfirmation(false)
    onDiscardBatch?.()
  }, [onDiscardBatch])

  const handleDiscardCancel = useCallback(() => {
    setShowDiscardConfirmation(false)
  }, [])

  const title = batchSize > 1 ? 'Apply suggestions' : 'Apply suggestion'

  const renderFooter = () => {
    if (onDiscardBatch) {
      return (
        <Dialog.Footer>
          <div className="d-flex flex-column width-full">
            <div className="d-flex flex-row flex-justify-between width-full">
              <Dialog.Buttons
                buttons={[{content: 'Discard batch', onClick: handleDiscardClick, buttonType: 'danger'}]}
              />
              <div className="d-flex flex-row gap-2">
                <Dialog.Buttons
                  buttons={[
                    {content: 'Cancel', onClick: onClose},
                    {
                      content: (
                        <span className="d-flex flex-row flex-items-center">
                          Apply suggestions
                          {suggestions && (
                            <CounterLabel className="ml-1 color-fg-inherit">{suggestions.length}</CounterLabel>
                          )}
                          {isSubmitting && <Spinner size="small" className="ml-1" />}
                        </span>
                      ),
                      onClick: handleOnCommitClick,
                      buttonType: 'primary',
                    },
                  ]}
                />
              </div>
            </div>
            {errorMessage && (
              <FormControl.Validation variant="error" className="mt-2 pt-1 flex-justify-end">
                {errorMessage}
              </FormControl.Validation>
            )}
          </div>
        </Dialog.Footer>
      )
    }

    return (
      <DialogFooter
        footerButtons={[
          {content: 'Cancel', onClick: onClose},
          {
            content: (
              <span className="d-flex flex-row flex-items-center">
                Apply Suggestion{isSubmitting && <Spinner size="small" className="ml-1" />}
              </span>
            ),
            onClick: handleOnCommitClick,
            buttonType: 'primary',
          },
        ]}
      >
        {errorMessage && (
          <FormControl.Validation variant="error" className="mt-2 pt-1 flex-justify-end">
            {errorMessage}
          </FormControl.Validation>
        )}
      </DialogFooter>
    )
  }

  return (
    <>
      <ScopedCommands commands={{'github:submit-form': handleOnCommitClick}}>
        <Dialog
          title={dialogTitle || title}
          renderFooter={renderFooter}
          onClose={onClose}
          returnFocusRef={returnFocusRef}
        >
          <FormControl className="mb-2">
            <FormControl.Label required>Commit message</FormControl.Label>
            <TextInput
              block
              name="commit_message"
              onChange={(event: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) =>
                setFormValues({type: actions.CHANGE_COMMIT_MESSAGE, payload: event.target.value})
              }
              value={formValues.commitMessage}
            />
          </FormControl>
          <FormControl>
            <FormControl.Label>Extended description</FormControl.Label>
            <Textarea
              block
              rows={3}
              placeholder="Add an optional description..."
              onChange={(event: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLTextAreaElement>) =>
                setFormValues({type: actions.CHANGE_EXTENDED_DESCRIPTION, payload: event.target.value})
              }
              value={formValues.extendedDescription}
            />
          </FormControl>
          {suggestions && suggestions.length > 0 && (
            <div className="tmp-mb-3 tmp-mt-3">
              <p className="f6 text-bold mb-2">
                Suggestions to apply <CounterLabel>{suggestions.length}</CounterLabel>
              </p>
              <div
                style={{
                  maxHeight: '200px',
                  overflowY: 'auto',
                }}
              >
                {suggestions.map(suggestion => (
                  <div key={suggestion.commentDatabaseId} className="border rounded-2 mb-2">
                    <div
                      className="d-flex flex-items-center flex-justify-between p-2"
                      style={{
                        transition: 'background-color 0.1s',
                      }}
                      onMouseEnter={e => (e.currentTarget.style.backgroundColor = 'var(--bgColor-muted, #f6f8fa)')}
                      onMouseLeave={e => (e.currentTarget.style.backgroundColor = 'transparent')}
                    >
                      <FileIcon className="mr-2" size={16} />
                      <span
                        className="flex-1 text-mono f6"
                        style={{
                          overflow: 'hidden',
                          textOverflow: 'ellipsis',
                          whiteSpace: 'nowrap',
                        }}
                      >
                        {suggestion.path}
                      </span>
                      {onRemoveSuggestion && (
                        <IconButton
                          aria-label={`Remove suggestion from ${suggestion.path}`}
                          icon={XIcon}
                          size="small"
                          variant="invisible"
                          onClick={() =>
                            onRemoveSuggestion({
                              commentDatabaseId: suggestion.commentDatabaseId,
                              suggestionIndex: suggestion.suggestionIndex,
                            })
                          }
                        />
                      )}
                    </div>
                    <div className="p-2">
                      <VerifiedHTMLBox
                        className="markdown-body"
                        comment-testid={`Comment body html for comment`}
                        /* eslint-disable-next-line @github-ui/github-monorepo/no-cast-as-safe-html-string -- HTML is sanitized server-side. TODO: Move type assertion to data ingress point. */
                        html={suggestion.bodyHTML as SafeHTMLString}
                      />
                    </div>
                  </div>
                ))}
              </div>
            </div>
          )}
        </Dialog>
      </ScopedCommands>
      <DiscardBatchConfirmationDialog
        isOpen={showDiscardConfirmation}
        onConfirm={handleDiscardConfirm}
        onCancel={handleDiscardCancel}
        suggestionsCount={suggestions?.length || batchSize}
      />
    </>
  )
}

import type {DiffEntry} from '../../../conversation-types'
import {IconButton} from '@primer/react'
import {StaticUnifiedDiffPreview} from '../StaticUnifiedDiffPreview'
import styles from './AutomatedReviewDiffEntry.module.css'
import {useState} from 'react'
import {clsx} from 'clsx'
import {ChevronDownIcon, ChevronRightIcon} from '@primer/octicons-react'

export function AutomatedReviewDiffEntry({diffEntry}: {diffEntry: DiffEntry}) {
  const [isCollapsed, setIsCollapsed] = useState(false)
  const onToggleCollapsed = () => setIsCollapsed(!isCollapsed)

  return (
    <div key={diffEntry.path} className="d-flex flex-column overflow-hidden">
      <div
        className={clsx(
          'd-flex flex-items-center tmp-px-3 py-2 text-small gap-2 text-mono',
          styles.diffEntryHeader,
          isCollapsed && 'rounded-2',
        )}
      >
        <IconButton
          aria-label={isCollapsed ? 'Open review comment' : 'Close review comment'}
          icon={isCollapsed ? ChevronRightIcon : ChevronDownIcon}
          size="small"
          variant="invisible"
          onClick={onToggleCollapsed}
        />
        {diffEntry.path}
      </div>
      {!isCollapsed && (
        <StaticUnifiedDiffPreview
          // TODO(@aliceclv): pass tabSize dynamically
          tabSize={4}
          className={clsx('m-0 border-bottom borderColor-default overflow-hidden', styles.automatedReviewDiff)}
          diffTableClassName="borderColor-default"
          diffLines={diffEntry.diffLines}
        />
      )}
    </div>
  )
}

import {CopilotCodeReviewFeedback} from '@github-ui/copilot-code-review-feedback/CopilotCodeReviewFeedback'
import {useAutomatedReviewFeedback} from '../../../hooks/use-automated-review-feedback'

export interface AutomatedReviewFeedbackProps {
  automatedCommentId: string
  feedbackOptions: Array<{label: string; value: string}>
  basePath: string
}

export function AutomatedReviewFeedback({automatedCommentId, feedbackOptions, basePath}: AutomatedReviewFeedbackProps) {
  const {feedbackPath} = useAutomatedReviewFeedback(automatedCommentId, basePath)

  return (
    <div data-testid={`automated-review-feedback-${automatedCommentId}`}>
      <CopilotCodeReviewFeedback
        commentId={automatedCommentId}
        feedbackPath={feedbackPath}
        feedbackOptions={feedbackOptions}
        showNegativeFeedbackModal={false}
      />
    </div>
  )
}

import {SuggestionStates, type Suggestion, type SuggestionState} from '../../../conversation-types'
import {
  AutomatedReviewSuggestionApplied,
  AutomatedReviewSuggestionError,
  AutomatedReviewSuggestionOutdated,
  AutomatedReviewSuggestionPending,
  AutomatedReviewSuggestionPresent,
} from './AutomatedReviewSuggestionStates'

export interface AutomatedReviewSuggestionProps {
  automatedCommentId: string
  basePath: string
  commentAuthorLogin?: string
  commentDatabaseId?: number | null
  commentTitle?: string
  feedbackOptions?: Array<{label: string; value: string}>
  isClassicExperience?: boolean
  suggestion?: Suggestion
  suggestionState?: SuggestionState
}

const SuggestionStateComponents = {
  [SuggestionStates.Pending]: AutomatedReviewSuggestionPending,
  [SuggestionStates.Error]: AutomatedReviewSuggestionError,
  [SuggestionStates.Outdated]: AutomatedReviewSuggestionOutdated,
  [SuggestionStates.Applied]: AutomatedReviewSuggestionApplied,
  [SuggestionStates.Present]: AutomatedReviewSuggestionPresent, // Default suggestion component
} as const

export function AutomatedReviewSuggestion({
  automatedCommentId,
  basePath,
  commentAuthorLogin,
  commentDatabaseId,
  commentTitle,
  feedbackOptions,
  isClassicExperience,
  suggestion,
  suggestionState,
}: AutomatedReviewSuggestionProps) {
  if (!suggestionState || suggestionState === SuggestionStates.NoSuggestion) {
    return null
  }

  const SuggestionComponent = SuggestionStateComponents[suggestionState]

  if (!SuggestionComponent) {
    return null
  }

  return (
    <SuggestionComponent
      automatedCommentId={automatedCommentId}
      basePath={basePath}
      commentTitle={commentTitle}
      commentAuthorLogin={commentAuthorLogin}
      commentDatabaseId={commentDatabaseId}
      feedbackOptions={feedbackOptions}
      isClassicExperience={isClassicExperience}
      suggestion={suggestion}
    />
  )
}

import {Button, CounterLabel, Label, Spinner, Tooltip} from '@primer/react'
import {AutomatedReviewDiffEntry} from './AutomatedReviewDiffEntry'
import React, {useState, useRef, useCallback, useMemo} from 'react'
import {isFeatureEnabled} from '@github-ui/feature-flags'
import {ApplySuggestionDialog} from '../CodeSuggestionActions/ApplySuggestionDialog'
import {useApplyAutomatedSuggestionMutation} from '@github-ui/pull-requests/use-apply-automated-suggestion-mutation'
import type {AutomatedReviewSuggestionProps} from './AutomatedReviewSuggestion'
import {FoldIcon, UnfoldIcon} from '@primer/octicons-react'
import type {Suggestion, SuggestedChangeLineRange} from '../../../conversation-types'
import styles from './AutomatedReviewSuggestionStates.module.css'
import {
  useSuggestionInBatch,
  useSuggestionBatchActions,
  useIsSuggestionBatchPending,
  useSuggestionBatch,
  usePullRequestBasePath,
  useComparisonInfo,
} from '@github-ui/pull-requests/pull-request-store-selectors'
import {saveActiveBatchToStorage} from '@github-ui/pull-requests/helpers/suggestion-batch-storage'
import {
  getLineRangesFromDiffEntries,
  validateSuggestedChangeCanBeAddedToBatch,
} from '../../../helpers/suggested-changes'
import {CodeSuggestionUnavailable} from '../CodeSuggestionActions/CodeSuggestionUnavailable'

interface AutomatedReviewSuggestionBaseProps {
  suggestion: Suggestion
  actions?: React.ReactNode
}

function AutomatedReviewSuggestionBase({suggestion, actions}: AutomatedReviewSuggestionBaseProps) {
  const diffEntries = suggestion.diffEntries || []
  const suggestedChangesCount = diffEntries.length || 0

  return (
    <div
      className={`d-flex flex-column color-fg-default ${styles.suggestion}`}
      data-testid="automated-review-suggestion"
    >
      {/* Metadata */}
      {suggestedChangesCount > 0 && (
        <div className="d-flex flex-items-center flex-justify-between text-small color-fg-muted pt-1">
          <div>
            Suggested changeset <CounterLabel scheme="secondary">{suggestedChangesCount}</CounterLabel>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Diffs */}
      {diffEntries.map(diffEntry => {
        return <AutomatedReviewDiffEntry key={diffEntry.path} diffEntry={diffEntry} />
      })}

      {actions}
    </div>
  )
}

export function AutomatedReviewSuggestionPending(_props: AutomatedReviewSuggestionProps) {
  return (
    <div className={styles.suggestionPending} data-testid="automated-review-suggestion-pending">
      <Spinner size="medium" />
      Loading suggestion...
    </div>
  )
}

// TODO(@aliceclv): we store the suggestion_error_message in the DB, we can display it here
export function AutomatedReviewSuggestionError(_props: AutomatedReviewSuggestionProps) {
  return (
    <div className={styles.suggestionError} data-testid="automated-review-suggestion-error">
      <h3 className={styles.suggestionErrorTitle}>Sorry, we could not generate an autofix suggestion.</h3>
      <p className="color-fg-muted">
        Try pushing a new commit or if the problem persists{' '}
        <a className="fgColor-link" href="https://support.github.com/">
          <u>contact support</u>
        </a>
        .
      </p>
    </div>
  )
}

export function AutomatedReviewSuggestionOutdated({suggestion, isClassicExperience}: AutomatedReviewSuggestionProps) {
  const Actions = (
    <div className="d-flex flex-items-center flex-justify-between gap-1">
      <span className="color-fg-muted text-small">Unable to commit as this autofix suggestion is now outdated.</span>

      {isClassicExperience ? (
        <Button size="small" variant="primary" disabled>
          Commit suggestion
        </Button>
      ) : (
        <Tooltip text="This autofix suggestion is outdated">
          <Button variant="default" inactive>
            Apply suggestion
          </Button>
        </Tooltip>
      )}
    </div>
  )
  return suggestion && <AutomatedReviewSuggestionBase suggestion={suggestion} actions={Actions} />
}

export function AutomatedReviewSuggestionApplied({suggestion}: AutomatedReviewSuggestionProps) {
  const [isFolded, setIsFolded] = useState(true)
  const fold = () => setIsFolded(!isFolded)

  return (
    <div className={styles.suggestionApplied}>
      <div className="d-flex flex-justify-between flex-items-center">
        <span className={styles.suggestionAppliedText}>This autofix suggestion was applied.</span>
        <Button
          variant="invisible"
          onClick={fold}
          leadingVisual={isFolded ? UnfoldIcon : FoldIcon}
          size="small"
          className="color-fg-muted"
        >
          {isFolded ? 'Show' : 'Hide'} autofix suggestion
        </Button>
      </div>
      {!isFolded && suggestion && <AutomatedReviewSuggestionBase suggestion={suggestion} />}
    </div>
  )
}

export function AutomatedReviewSuggestionPresent({
  automatedCommentId,
  basePath,
  commentAuthorLogin,
  commentDatabaseId,
  commentTitle,
  isClassicExperience,
  suggestion,
}: AutomatedReviewSuggestionProps) {
  const [isApplySuggestionDialogOpen, setIsApplySuggestionDialogOpen] = useState(false)
  const openDialog = useCallback(() => setIsApplySuggestionDialogOpen(true), [])
  const closeDialog = useCallback(() => setIsApplySuggestionDialogOpen(false), [])

  const diffEntries = suggestion?.diffEntries || []
  const suggestedChangesCount = diffEntries.length || 0
  const applyButtonRef = useRef<HTMLButtonElement>(null)
  // Note(@aliceclv): this is optimized for Universe ship & code quality
  // We should have a default commit message passed from the backend when adapting the component to code scanning & CCR
  const defaultCommitMessage = `Potential fix for pull request finding${commentTitle ? ` '${commentTitle}'` : ''}`
  const {mutate: applyAutomatedSuggestion} = useApplyAutomatedSuggestionMutation(automatedCommentId, basePath)

  const handleCommitSuggestion = useCallback(
    (
      commitMessage: string,
      onError: (error: Error, type?: string, friendlyMessage?: string) => void,
      onCompleted: () => void,
    ) => {
      applyAutomatedSuggestion(
        {message: commitMessage},
        {
          onSuccess: () => {
            closeDialog()
            onCompleted()
          },
          onError: (error: Error) => {
            onError(error, 'applyAutomatedSuggestion', 'Failed to commit suggested changes')
          },
        },
      )
    },
    [applyAutomatedSuggestion, closeDialog],
  )

  if (!suggestion) return null

  const actions = (
    <>
      {isClassicExperience ? (
        <RailsExperienceButtons applyButtonRef={applyButtonRef} onApplyButtonClick={openDialog} />
      ) : (
        // commentDatabaseId shouldn't ever be null here, but we add this check to satisfy TypeScript
        commentDatabaseId != null && (
          <ReactExperienceButtons
            commentDatabaseId={commentDatabaseId}
            applyButtonRef={applyButtonRef}
            onApplyButtonClick={openDialog}
            suggestion={suggestion}
          />
        )
      )}

      {isApplySuggestionDialogOpen && suggestedChangesCount > 0 && (
        <ApplySuggestionDialog
          authorLogins={commentAuthorLogin ? [commentAuthorLogin] : []}
          batchSize={suggestedChangesCount}
          defaultCommitMessage={defaultCommitMessage}
          onClose={closeDialog}
          onCommit={handleCommitSuggestion}
          dialogTitle={isClassicExperience ? 'Commit suggestion' : 'Apply suggestion'}
          returnFocusRef={applyButtonRef}
        />
      )}
    </>
  )

  return <AutomatedReviewSuggestionBase suggestion={suggestion} actions={actions} />
}

function isBatchButtonEnabled() {
  return isFeatureEnabled('batch_suggested_changes') && isFeatureEnabled('copilot_code_review_batch_apply_suggestions')
}

function RailsExperienceButtons({
  applyButtonRef,
  onApplyButtonClick,
}: {
  applyButtonRef: React.RefObject<HTMLButtonElement | null>
  onApplyButtonClick: () => void
}) {
  const showBatchButton = isBatchButtonEnabled()

  return (
    <div className="d-flex gap-2 flex-items-center flex-justify-end flex-wrap">
      <Button
        data-testid="commit-suggestion-button"
        size="small"
        variant="primary"
        ref={applyButtonRef}
        onClick={onApplyButtonClick}
      >
        Commit suggestion
      </Button>
      {showBatchButton && (
        <Tooltip text="Batching autofix suggestions is only available in the new Files changed experience">
          <Button data-testid="add-suggestion-to-batch-button" size="small" inactive>
            Add suggestion to batch
          </Button>
        </Tooltip>
      )}
    </div>
  )
}

function ReactExperienceButtons({
  commentDatabaseId,
  applyButtonRef,
  onApplyButtonClick,
  suggestion,
}: {
  commentDatabaseId: number
  applyButtonRef: React.RefObject<HTMLButtonElement | null>
  onApplyButtonClick: () => void
  suggestion: Suggestion | undefined
}) {
  const showApplyButton = !useIsSuggestionBatchPending()
  const showBatchButton = isBatchButtonEnabled() && commentDatabaseId != null

  const suggestionId = useMemo(() => ({commentDatabaseId, suggestionIndex: undefined}), [commentDatabaseId])
  const isPendingInBatch = useSuggestionInBatch(suggestionId)

  const suggestionBatch = useSuggestionBatch()
  const pathLineRanges = useMemo(
    () => getLineRangesFromDiffEntries(suggestion?.diffEntries || []),
    [suggestion?.diffEntries],
  )
  const {isValid: canAddToBatch, reason} = useMemo(
    () => validateSuggestedChangeCanBeAddedToBatch({pathLineRanges}, suggestionBatch.activeBatch),
    [pathLineRanges, suggestionBatch.activeBatch],
  )

  const canAddToOrRemoveFromBatch = isPendingInBatch || canAddToBatch
  return (
    <>
      {showBatchButton && !canAddToOrRemoveFromBatch && (
        <CodeSuggestionUnavailable
          reason={reason ?? 'Unable to commit due to other pending changes affecting this line.'}
          className="m-0"
        />
      )}
      <div className="d-flex gap-2 flex-items-center flex-justify-end flex-wrap">
        {showApplyButton && (
          <Button
            data-testid="commit-suggestion-button"
            variant="default"
            ref={applyButtonRef}
            onClick={onApplyButtonClick}
          >
            Apply suggestion
          </Button>
        )}
        {showBatchButton && canAddToOrRemoveFromBatch && (
          <BatchButton commentDatabaseId={commentDatabaseId} pathLineRanges={pathLineRanges} />
        )}
      </div>
    </>
  )
}

function BatchButton({
  commentDatabaseId,
  pathLineRanges,
}: {
  commentDatabaseId: number
  pathLineRanges: Array<{path: string; lineRange: SuggestedChangeLineRange}>
}) {
  const suggestionId = useMemo(() => ({commentDatabaseId, suggestionIndex: undefined}), [commentDatabaseId])
  const isPendingInBatch = useSuggestionInBatch(suggestionId)

  const suggestionBatch = useSuggestionBatch()
  const {addSuggestionToBatch, removeSuggestionFromBatch} = useSuggestionBatchActions()

  const pullRequestBasePath = usePullRequestBasePath()
  const comparisonInfo = useComparisonInfo()

  const batchedSuggestion = useMemo(() => ({...suggestionId, pathLineRanges}), [suggestionId, pathLineRanges])

  const handleAddToBatch = useCallback(() => {
    addSuggestionToBatch(batchedSuggestion)
    saveActiveBatchToStorage(pullRequestBasePath, comparisonInfo.fullDiff.headOid, [
      ...suggestionBatch.activeBatch,
      batchedSuggestion,
    ])
  }, [addSuggestionToBatch, batchedSuggestion, suggestionBatch, pullRequestBasePath, comparisonInfo])

  const handleRemoveFromBatch = useCallback(() => {
    removeSuggestionFromBatch(suggestionId)
    saveActiveBatchToStorage(
      pullRequestBasePath,
      comparisonInfo.fullDiff.headOid,
      suggestionBatch.activeBatch.filter(item => item.commentDatabaseId !== commentDatabaseId),
    )
  }, [removeSuggestionFromBatch, suggestionId, commentDatabaseId, suggestionBatch, pullRequestBasePath, comparisonInfo])

  if (isPendingInBatch) {
    return (
      <div className="d-flex flex-items-center flex-justify-between flex-1">
        <Label variant="attention">Pending in batch</Label>
        <Button data-testid="remove-suggestion-from-batch-button" variant="danger" onClick={handleRemoveFromBatch}>
          Remove from batch
        </Button>
      </div>
    )
  }

  return (
    <Button data-testid="add-suggestion-to-batch-button" variant="primary" onClick={handleAddToBatch}>
      Add suggestion to batch
    </Button>
  )
}

import {RuleSeverityBadge} from '@github-ui/code-scanning-shared/components/RuleSeverityBadge'
import {SecuritySeverityBadge} from '@github-ui/code-scanning-shared/components/SecuritySeverityBadge'
// eslint-disable-next-line @github-ui/github-monorepo/restrict-relay-imports
import type {ActivityHeaderHeadingProps} from '@github-ui/commenting/ActivityHeader'
// eslint-disable-next-line @github-ui/github-monorepo/restrict-relay-imports
import {ActivityHeader, CommentAuthorTypes} from '@github-ui/commenting/ActivityHeader'
// eslint-disable-next-line @github-ui/github-monorepo/restrict-relay-imports
import {CommentActions} from '@github-ui/commenting/CommentActions'
// eslint-disable-next-line @github-ui/github-monorepo/restrict-relay-imports
import {VALUES} from '@github-ui/commenting/Values'
import {noop} from '@github-ui/noop'
import type {ReactionContent, ReactionViewerGroup} from '@github-ui/reaction-viewer/ReactionGroupsUtils'
import {ReactionViewerBase} from '@github-ui/reaction-viewer/ReactionViewerBase'
import type {SafeHTMLString} from '@github-ui/safe-html'
import {UnsafeHTMLText} from '@github-ui/safe-html/UnsafeHTML'
import {VerifiedHTMLText} from '@github-ui/safe-html/VerifiedHTML'
import {ssrSafeLocation, ssrSafeWindow} from '@github-ui/ssr-utils'
// eslint-disable-next-line no-restricted-imports
import {useToastContext} from '@github-ui/toast/ToastContext'
import {AlertIcon, StopIcon} from '@primer/octicons-react'
import {Flash, LabelGroup} from '@primer/react'
import {useRef, useState} from 'react'
import {useCommentAnchor} from '../../hooks/use-comment-anchor'
import type {Comment} from '../../conversation-types'
import styles from './AutomatedReviewThreadComment.module.css'
import {AutomatedReviewSuggestion} from './AutomatedReview/AutomatedReviewSuggestion'
import {Blankslate} from '@primer/react/experimental'
import {AutomatedReviewFeedback} from './AutomatedReview/AutomatedReviewFeedback'
import {CopilotApplyChangesButton} from '@github-ui/copilot-apply-changes-button/CopilotApplyChangesButton'
import {useReactToCommentMutationWithCallback} from '@github-ui/pull-requests/use-react-to-comment-mutation'

type CommentState = 'editing' | 'hidden' | 'visible'

export interface AutomatedReviewThreadCommentProps {
  anchorPrefix?: string
  basePath: string
  comment: Comment
  /**
   * If true, the comment will scroll itself into view and show a blue border
   * when the component renders with a hash that matches the comment.
   */
  isAnchorable?: boolean
  isInDialogMode?: boolean | undefined
  isOutdated?: boolean
  onQuoteReply?: (quotedText?: string) => void
  originalDiffPathUri?: string | null
  /**
   * If true, the "Apply changes" button will be shown, allowing Copilot to
   * automatically apply suggested changes from the code review comment.
   * This is determined server-side based on feature flags, user permissions,
   * and whether the comment contains a suggestion.
   */
  showApplyChangesButton?: boolean
  threadId: string
  threadPositionNumber?: number
  footnoteTextHTML?: string
  /**
   * If true, uses classic experience labels (e.g., "Commit suggestion" instead of "Apply suggestion")
   * and shows the batch suggestions button as inactive.
   */
  isClassicExperience?: boolean
}

function formatBotAuthorLogin(login?: string): string | undefined {
  if (!login) {
    return undefined
  }

  // Change the format of bots (eg.: `github-code-quality[bot]` to `github-code-quality`)
  return login.replace(/\[bot\]$/i, '')
}

export function AutomatedReviewThreadComment({
  anchorPrefix = 'r',
  basePath,
  comment,
  isAnchorable = false,
  isClassicExperience,
  isInDialogMode,
  isOutdated,
  onQuoteReply = noop,
  originalDiffPathUri,
  showApplyChangesButton = false,
  threadId,
  threadPositionNumber,
  footnoteTextHTML,
}: AutomatedReviewThreadCommentProps): React.ReactElement {
  const {addToast} = useToastContext()
  const [isMinimized, setIsMinimized] = useState(comment.isHidden)
  const [visibilityErrorMessage] = useState('')
  const [reactionGroups, setReactionGroups] = useState<ReactionViewerGroup[] | undefined>(comment.reactionGroups)

  const commentBodyRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null)
  const containerRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null)
  const reactionButtonRef = useRef<HTMLButtonElement>(null)
  const {
    id: automatedCommentId,
    title,
    message,
    severity,
    securitySeverity,
    suggestion,
    suggestionState,
    feedbackOptions,
    showFeedbackButtons: showFeedbackButtonsInput,
    showAiNote,
    source,
  } = comment.automatedComment || {}

  // this is needed because any properties coming from comment.automatedComment can be null
  // and cannot be used directly in AutomatedReviewFeedback
  const showFeedbackButtons = showFeedbackButtonsInput && automatedCommentId && feedbackOptions

  const showFootBar = showFeedbackButtons || showAiNote || footnoteTextHTML || reactionGroups

  const isCopilotAuthoredPr = source === 'copilot'

  const onReactSuccessCallback = (data: {reactionGroups: ReactionViewerGroup[]}) => {
    setReactionGroups(data.reactionGroups)
  }
  const {mutate: reactToCommentMutation} = useReactToCommentMutationWithCallback(basePath, onReactSuccessCallback)

  const onReact = (reaction: ReactionContent, viewerHasReacted: boolean) => {
    if (!comment.databaseId) throw new Error('Comment not found')

    setTimeout(() => reactionButtonRef.current?.focus(), 0)
    reactToCommentMutation(
      {
        commentDatabaseId: comment.databaseId,
        threadId,
        reaction,
        viewerHasReacted,
      },
      {
        onError: () => {
          // eslint-disable-next-line @github-ui/dotcom-primer/toast-migration
          addToast({
            type: 'error',
            message: 'Failed to react to comment',
          })
        },
      },
    )
  }

  // TODO(@aliceclv): figure out when the `.id` value gets populated to understand if it's a safe fallback
  // The unique identifier for this comment in the DOM
  const commentId = comment.databaseId?.toString() || comment.id
  // The anchor portion of the URL that will target this specific comment when navigating to it
  // This is used to scroll the comment into view and highlight it when directly linked
  const commentAnchor = commentId ? `${anchorPrefix}${commentId}` : undefined
  // Note(@aliceclv): Comment is not editable
  const commentState: CommentState = isMinimized ? 'hidden' : 'visible'

  // The full URL including anchor that points directly to this comment within the current page
  const pageScopedCommentHref =
    commentAnchor && ssrSafeLocation
      ? new URL(`${ssrSafeLocation.pathname}#${commentAnchor}`, ssrSafeLocation.origin).toString()
      : ''

  useCommentAnchor({
    isAnchorable,
    commentId,
    commentAnchor,
    containerRef,
  })

  const commentActivityProps = {
    headingProps: {as: 'h3'} as ActivityHeaderHeadingProps,
    avatarUrl: comment.author?.avatarUrl ?? VALUES.ghostUser.avatarUrl,
    comment: {
      ...comment,
      bodyForClipboard: comment.body,
      url: pageScopedCommentHref,
      referenceText: comment.reference.text ?? `#${comment.reference.number}`,
      // Note(@aliceclv): none of these actions are allowed in the context of an automated comment
      viewerCanMinimize: false,
      viewerCanUpdate: false,
      viewerCanDelete: false,
      viewerCanBlockFromOrg: false,
      viewerCanUnblockFromOrg: false,
      viewerCanReport: false,
      viewerCanReportToMaintainer: false,
      viewerCanReferenceInIssue: false,
      viewerCanQuoteReply: false,
    },
    commentRef: containerRef,
    editHistoryComponent: undefined,
    onBlock: undefined,
    onUnblock: undefined,
    onMinimize: setIsMinimized,
    onSuccessfulBlock: () => {
      //not ideal, but this is the easiest way to get all of the comments to reflect the newly updated state
      setTimeout(() => ssrSafeWindow?.location.reload(), 800)
    },
    isMinimized,
    navigate: noop,
    onReplySelect: onQuoteReply,
    isReply: false,
    forceInlineAvatar: true,
