    containerStyle: {},
    originalDiffPathUri,
    isOutdated,
    // Comment actions are null as they are not allowed for automated comments
    editComment: () => null,
    deleteComment: () => null,
    hideComment: () => null,
    unhideComment: () => null,

    // Comment author props
    commentAuthorSlug: undefined,
    commentAuthorType: CommentAuthorTypes.Bot,
    commentAuthorLogin: formatBotAuthorLogin(comment.author?.login) ?? VALUES.ghostUser.login,
    hideProfileLink: true,
  }

  const getCommentAriaLabel = (): string => {
    if (threadPositionNumber !== undefined) {
      return `Comment ${threadPositionNumber}`
    } else {
      return 'Comment'
    }
  }

  const securitySeverityBadge = securitySeverity ? <SecuritySeverityBadge severity={securitySeverity} /> : null
  const ruleSeverityBadge = severity ? <RuleSeverityBadge severity={severity} /> : null

  return (
    <div
      ref={containerRef}
      id={isAnchorable ? commentAnchor : undefined}
      className={styles.automatedComment}
      style={{scrollMarginTop: isAnchorable ? '120px' : 0}}
      data-first-thread-comment
      data-marker-navigation-comment-thread-id={threadId}
      data-marker-navigation-comment-id={comment.id}
      data-testid="automated-review-comment"
      // When aria-label and role is set while still in gridcell mode, the browser doesn't the respect the
      // aria-hidden"true" and we end up with the aria-labels leaking into the accessible name of the gridcell,
      // which is not what we want. So we should only set these attributes while in dialog mode.
      {...(isInDialogMode
        ? {
            role: 'document',
            'aria-roledescription': 'comment',
            'aria-label': getCommentAriaLabel(),
          }
        : {})}
    >
      <div className={styles.automatedComment__headerContainer}>
        <ActivityHeader
          {...commentActivityProps}
          commentBody={comment.body}
          isInDialogMode={isInDialogMode}
          actions={<CommentActions {...commentActivityProps} />}
          badges={
            <LabelGroup>
              {securitySeverityBadge}
              {ruleSeverityBadge}
            </LabelGroup>
          }
          isOutdated={isOutdated}
        />
      </div>
      {visibilityErrorMessage && (
        <Flash variant="danger" className="tmp-m-3">
          <StopIcon className="mr-2" />
          {visibilityErrorMessage}
        </Flash>
      )}
      <div className={styles.automatedComment__bodyContainer}>
        {commentState === 'visible' && (
          <div className={styles.automatedComment__body}>
            <div className={styles.automatedComment__content}>
              {title && (
                <div className="d-flex flex-items-center">
                  <h3 className={styles.automatedComment__title}>{title}</h3>
                </div>
              )}
              <VerifiedHTMLText
                ref={commentBodyRef}
                className={`${styles.automatedComment__message} markdown-body`}
                comment-testid={`Comment message html for automated comment ${commentId}`}
                /* eslint-disable-next-line @github-ui/github-monorepo/no-cast-as-safe-html-string -- HTML is sanitized server-side. TODO: Move type assertion to data ingress point. */
                html={message as SafeHTMLString}
              />
            </div>
            {automatedCommentId && (
              <AutomatedReviewSuggestion
                commentTitle={title}
                commentAuthorLogin={comment.author?.login}
                commentDatabaseId={comment.databaseId}
                feedbackOptions={feedbackOptions}
                isClassicExperience={isClassicExperience}
                suggestion={suggestion}
                suggestionState={suggestionState}
                automatedCommentId={automatedCommentId}
                basePath={basePath}
              />
            )}
            {showApplyChangesButton && (
              <CopilotApplyChangesButton
                commentPermalink={comment.url}
                isCopilotAuthoredPr={isCopilotAuthoredPr}
                commentCount={1}
              />
            )}
            {showFootBar && (
              <div className="d-flex flex-items-center flex-justify-between gap-2 mb-1">
                {reactionGroups && (
                  <ReactionViewerBase
                    ref={reactionButtonRef}
                    reactionGroups={reactionGroups}
                    onReact={onReact}
                    canReact={comment.viewerCanReact}
                  />
                )}
                {showFeedbackButtons && (
                  <AutomatedReviewFeedback
                    automatedCommentId={automatedCommentId}
                    basePath={basePath}
                    feedbackOptions={feedbackOptions}
                  />
                )}
                {footnoteTextHTML || showAiNote ? (
                  <div className="text-small color-fg-muted">
                    {footnoteTextHTML ? (
                      <UnsafeHTMLText html={footnoteTextHTML} />
                    ) : (
                      'Copilot uses AI. Check for mistakes.'
                    )}
                  </div>
                ) : null}
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

export function AutomatedReviewThreadCommentErrorFallback() {
  return (
    <div className={`position-relative`}>
      <Blankslate border>
        <Blankslate.Visual>
          <AlertIcon size={24} className="fgColor-muted tmp-mt-3 tmp-mb-3" />
        </Blankslate.Visual>
        <Blankslate.Heading>
          <strong>There was a problem loading this review comment</strong>
        </Blankslate.Heading>
        <div className="mb-n2">
          <Blankslate.Description>Refresh the page or try again later</Blankslate.Description>
        </div>
        <Blankslate.SecondaryAction href="https://www.githubstatus.com/">GitHub status</Blankslate.SecondaryAction>
      </Blankslate>
    </div>
  )
}

import type {DirectoryItem, ReposFileTreeData} from '@github-ui/code-view-types'

interface FlatPathsResponse {
  paths: string[]
  directories: string[]
}

export function buildTreeFromPaths(response: FlatPathsResponse): ReposFileTreeData {
  const {paths, directories} = response
  const treeData: ReposFileTreeData = {}
  const directorySet = new Set(directories)

  // Initialize root directory
  treeData[''] = {
    items: [],
    totalCount: 0,
  }

  // Sort directories first to ensure consistent order
  const sortedDirectories = [...directories].sort()

  // First, process all directories
  for (const dirPath of sortedDirectories) {
    const parentPath = dirPath.substring(0, dirPath.lastIndexOf('/')) || ''
    const dirName = dirPath.substring(dirPath.lastIndexOf('/') + 1)

    // Create directory item
    const dirItem: DirectoryItem = {
      name: dirName,
      path: dirPath,
      contentType: 'directory',
    }

    // Ensure parent directory exists
    if (!treeData[parentPath]) {
      treeData[parentPath] = {
        items: [],
        totalCount: 0,
      }
    }

    // Add directory to parent's items
    treeData[parentPath].items.push(dirItem)

    // Initialize this directory's entry
    if (!treeData[dirPath]) {
      treeData[dirPath] = {
        items: [],
        totalCount: 0,
      }
    }
  }

  // Sort paths to ensure consistent order of files
  const sortedPaths = paths.filter(p => !directorySet.has(p)).sort()

  // Process all files
  for (const filePath of sortedPaths) {
    const parentPath = filePath.substring(0, filePath.lastIndexOf('/')) || ''
    const fileName = filePath.substring(filePath.lastIndexOf('/') + 1)

    // Create file item
    const fileItem: DirectoryItem = {
      name: fileName,
      path: filePath,
      contentType: 'file',
    }

    // Ensure parent directory exists
    if (!treeData[parentPath]) {
      treeData[parentPath] = {
        items: [],
        totalCount: 0,
      }
    }

    // Add file to parent's items
    treeData[parentPath].items.push(fileItem)
  }

  // Sort items in each directory
  for (const dirPath in treeData) {
    const dirData = treeData[dirPath]
    if (!dirData) continue

    dirData.items.sort((a, b) => {
      // Directories come before files
      if (a.contentType === 'directory' && b.contentType !== 'directory') return -1
      if (a.contentType !== 'directory' && b.contentType === 'directory') return 1
      // Sort by name within same type
      return a.name.localeCompare(b.name)
    })
  }

  // Calculate recursive totalCount for each directory
  const calculateTotalCount = (path: string): number => {
    const dir = treeData[path]
    if (!dir) return 0

    // Count direct children plus recursive count of subdirectories
    return dir.items.reduce((total, item) => {
      if (item.contentType === 'directory') {
        return total + 1 + calculateTotalCount(item.path)
      }
      return total + 1
    }, 0)
  }

  // Update totalCount for each directory
  for (const dirPath in treeData) {
    const dirData = treeData[dirPath]
    if (!dirData) continue

    dirData.totalCount = calculateTotalCount(dirPath)
  }

  return treeData
}

import {CopilotDiffChatContextMenu} from '@github-ui/copilot-code-chat/CopilotDiffChatContextMenu'
// eslint-disable-next-line @github-ui/github-monorepo/restrict-relay-imports
import type {FileDiffReference} from '@github-ui/copilot-chat/utils/copilot-chat-types'
import {
  CommentDiscussionIcon,
  CopyIcon,
  FoldDownIcon,
  FoldUpIcon,
  LinkIcon,
  MultiSelectIcon,
} from '@primer/octicons-react'
import {ActionList} from '@primer/react'
import {useCallback, type MouseEvent} from 'react'

import useExpandHunk from '../../hooks/use-expand-hunk'
import type {ClientDiffLine, DiffLine, DiffSide} from '../../types'
import {StartConversationContextMenuItems} from '../StartConversationContextMenuItems'
import {KeybindingHint} from '@primer/react/experimental'
import {CommentsPreference} from '@github-ui/diff-view-settings/types'
import {copyText} from '@github-ui/copy-to-clipboard'
import {anchorLinkForSelection, copilotSelectedDiffLineRange} from '../../helpers/line-helpers'
import {DiffFeature, type DiffAnchor, type SimpleDiffLine} from '@github-ui/diffs/types'
import {useGetSelectedLineRange, useHiddenFeatures} from '../../stores/selectors'
import type {HunkData} from '../../helpers/hunk-data-helpers'
import type {DiffAnnotation, Thread, ViewerData} from '../../conversation-types'

type CellContextMenuProps = {
  currentHunk: HunkData | undefined
  diffAnchor: DiffAnchor
  diffLine: ClientDiffLine
  showStartConversation: boolean
  shouldDisplayCollapseComments?: boolean
  copilotChatReference?: FileDiffReference
  handleViewMarkersSelection: (markerLoc?: DiffSide) => void
  isLeftSide: boolean
  isSplit: boolean
  startConversationCurrentLine: () => void
  startConversationWithSuggestedChange: () => void
  handleCopyCode?: () => void
  nextHunk: HunkData | undefined
  previousHunk: HunkData | undefined
  threads: Thread[]
  annotations: DiffAnnotation[]
  viewerData: ViewerData
}

export function CellContextMenu(props: CellContextMenuProps) {
  return (
    <>
      <MarkerListItems {...props} />
      {props.viewerData.shouldDisplayComments && (
        <CopilotListItems
          diffAnchor={props.diffAnchor}
          diffLine={props.diffLine}
          fileDiffReference={props.copilotChatReference}
          isLeftSide={props.isLeftSide}
        />
      )}
      <CopyContentListItems handleCopyCode={props.handleCopyCode} />
      <SelectAllListItem diffAnchor={props.diffAnchor} />
      {props.viewerData.shouldDisplayComments && (
        <CopyAnchorLink diffAnchor={props.diffAnchor} diffLine={props.diffLine} />
      )}
      <HunkListItems currentHunk={props.currentHunk} nextHunk={props.nextHunk} previousHunk={props.previousHunk} />
    </>
  )
}

function CopyAnchorLink({diffLine, diffAnchor}: {diffAnchor: DiffAnchor; diffLine: ClientDiffLine}) {
  const getSelectedLineRange = useGetSelectedLineRange()
  const line = diffLine as SimpleDiffLine

  const copyAnchorLink = useCallback(() => {
    const lineRange = getSelectedLineRange()
    const link = anchorLinkForSelection({line, range: lineRange, fileAnchor: diffAnchor})
    if (link) copyText(link)
  }, [diffAnchor, getSelectedLineRange, line])

  return (
    <ActionList.Item
      onSelect={copyAnchorLink}
      onMouseDown={(event: MouseEvent) => {
        // We need to prevent the window's Selection object from being reset on mouse down if user has selected text.
        // This is to prevent the browser from resetting the selection before onSelect event callback is called.
        if (window.getSelection()?.toString() !== '') event.preventDefault()
      }}
    >
      <ActionList.LeadingVisual>
        <LinkIcon />
      </ActionList.LeadingVisual>
      Copy link
      <ActionList.TrailingVisual>
        <KeybindingHint keys="Mod+Alt+y" />
      </ActionList.TrailingVisual>
    </ActionList.Item>
  )
}

const CopilotListItems: React.FC<{
  diffAnchor: DiffAnchor
  diffLine: ClientDiffLine
  fileDiffReference?: FileDiffReference
  isLeftSide: boolean
}> = props => {
  const getSelectedLineRange = useGetSelectedLineRange()
  const selectedLineRange = getSelectedLineRange()

  const copilotSelectedDiffRowRange = copilotSelectedDiffLineRange(
    selectedLineRange,
    props.diffLine,
    props.isLeftSide,
    props.diffAnchor,
  )

  if (!props.fileDiffReference) return null

  return (
    <CopilotDiffChatContextMenu
      showDivider
      selectedRange={copilotSelectedDiffRowRange}
      fileDiffReference={props.fileDiffReference}
    />
  )
}

function SelectAllListItem({diffAnchor}: {diffAnchor: DiffAnchor}) {
  const handleSelectAllSelection = () => {
    // We need to wait one click for the context menu to close before we allow for the event to occur inside of the table instead of context menu
    setTimeout(() => {
      document
        .querySelector(`table[data-diff-anchor="${diffAnchor}"]`)
        ?.dispatchEvent(new KeyboardEvent('keydown', {key: 'a', code: 'KeyA', ctrlKey: true}))
    })
  }

  return (
    <ActionList.Item onSelect={handleSelectAllSelection}>
      <ActionList.LeadingVisual>
        <MultiSelectIcon />
      </ActionList.LeadingVisual>
      Select all
      <ActionList.TrailingVisual>
        <KeybindingHint keys="Mod+a" />
      </ActionList.TrailingVisual>
    </ActionList.Item>
  )
}

function MarkerListItems({
  diffLine,
  handleViewMarkersSelection,
  isLeftSide,
  isSplit,
  shouldDisplayCollapseComments,
  showStartConversation,
  startConversationCurrentLine,
  startConversationWithSuggestedChange,
  threads,
  annotations,
  viewerData,
}: Omit<CellContextMenuProps, 'copilotChatReference'>) {
  const hasAnnotations = annotations.length > 0

  if (!showStartConversation && !threads && !hasAnnotations) {
    return null
  }

  return (
    <>
      {viewerData.shouldDisplayComments && showStartConversation && (
        <StartConversationContextMenuItems
          diffLine={diffLine}
          handleStartConversation={startConversationCurrentLine}
          handleStartConversationWithSuggestedChange={startConversationWithSuggestedChange}
          isLeftSide={isLeftSide}
          isSplit={isSplit}
        />
      )}
      <ViewMarkerListItems
        threads={threads}
        annotations={annotations}
        diffLine={diffLine}
        handleViewMarkersSelection={handleViewMarkersSelection}
        isLeftSide={isLeftSide}
        isSplit={isSplit}
        shouldDisplayCollapseComments={shouldDisplayCollapseComments}
        viewerData={viewerData}
      />
      <ActionList.Divider />
    </>
  )
}

function ViewMarkerListItems({
  diffLine,
  handleViewMarkersSelection,
  isLeftSide,
  isSplit,
  threads,
  annotations,
  shouldDisplayCollapseComments,
  viewerData,
}: {
  diffLine: ClientDiffLine
  handleViewMarkersSelection: (markerLoc?: DiffSide) => void
  isLeftSide: boolean
  isSplit: boolean
  threads: Thread[]
  annotations: DiffAnnotation[]
  shouldDisplayCollapseComments?: boolean
  viewerData: ViewerData
}) {
  if (viewerData.commentsPreference === CommentsPreference.Visible) {
    return null
  }

  if (!isSplit) {
    return (
      <UnifiedDiffMarkerListItem
        threads={threads}
        annotations={annotations}
        diffLine={diffLine}
        onSelect={() => handleViewMarkersSelection()}
        shouldDisplayCollapseComments={shouldDisplayCollapseComments}
      />
    )
  }

  return (
    <SplitDiffMarkersListItems
      threads={threads}
      annotations={annotations}
      handleViewMarkersSelection={handleViewMarkersSelection}
      isLeftSide={isLeftSide}
      shouldDisplayCollapseComments={shouldDisplayCollapseComments}
    />
  )
}

function SplitDiffMarkersListItems({
  handleViewMarkersSelection,
  isLeftSide,
  shouldDisplayCollapseComments,
  threads,
  annotations,
}: {
  handleViewMarkersSelection: (markerLoc: DiffSide) => void
  isLeftSide: boolean
  shouldDisplayCollapseComments?: boolean
  threads: Thread[]
  annotations: DiffAnnotation[]
}) {
  const hasThreads = threads.length > 0
  const hasAnnotations = annotations.length > 0
  if (isLeftSide) {
    return (
      <>
        {hasThreads && (
          <MarkerListItem
            text={shouldDisplayCollapseComments ? 'Collapse comments' : 'Expand comments'}
            onSelect={() => handleViewMarkersSelection('LEFT')}
          />
        )}
      </>
    )
  }

  return (
    <>
      {(hasThreads || hasAnnotations) && (
        <MarkerListItem
          text={shouldDisplayCollapseComments ? 'Collapse comments' : 'Expand comments'}
          onSelect={() => handleViewMarkersSelection('RIGHT')}
        />
      )}
    </>
  )
}

function UnifiedDiffMarkerListItem({
  diffLine,
  onSelect,
  threads,
  annotations,
  shouldDisplayCollapseComments,
}: {
  diffLine: ClientDiffLine
  onSelect: () => void
  threads: Thread[]
  annotations: DiffAnnotation[]
  shouldDisplayCollapseComments?: boolean
}) {
  const line = diffLine as DiffLine
  const hasMarkers = line && (threads.length > 0 || annotations.length > 0)
  if (!hasMarkers) return null
  return (
    <MarkerListItem
      text={shouldDisplayCollapseComments ? 'Collapse comments' : 'Expand comments'}
      onSelect={() => onSelect()}
    />
  )
}

function MarkerListItem({onSelect, text}: {onSelect: () => void; text: string}) {
  return (
    <ActionList.Item onSelect={onSelect}>
      <ActionList.LeadingVisual>
        <CommentDiscussionIcon />
      </ActionList.LeadingVisual>
      {text}
    </ActionList.Item>
  )
}

function CopyContentListItems({handleCopyCode}: {handleCopyCode?: () => void}) {
  return (
    <ActionList.Item
      onSelect={handleCopyCode}
      onMouseDown={(event: MouseEvent) => {
        // We need to prevent the window's Selection object from being reset on mouse down if user has selected text.
        // This is to prevent the browser from resetting the selection before onSelect event callback is called.
        if (window.getSelection()?.toString() !== '') event.preventDefault()
      }}
    >
      <ActionList.LeadingVisual>
        <CopyIcon />
      </ActionList.LeadingVisual>
      Copy
      <ActionList.TrailingVisual>
        <KeybindingHint keys="Mod+c" />
      </ActionList.TrailingVisual>
    </ActionList.Item>
  )
}

function HunkListItems({
  currentHunk,
  nextHunk,
  previousHunk,
}: {
  currentHunk: HunkData | undefined
  nextHunk: HunkData | undefined
  previousHunk: HunkData | undefined
}) {
  const hiddenFeatures = useHiddenFeatures()

  const {canExpandStartOfHunk, canExpandEndOfHunk} = useExpandHunk({currentHunk, nextHunk, previousHunk})

  if (!canExpandStartOfHunk && !canExpandEndOfHunk && !previousHunk && !nextHunk) return null

  return (
    <>
      <ActionList.Divider />
      {/** If exanding diff lines is a hidden feature, we don't render expanding hunk list items */}
      {!hiddenFeatures.has(DiffFeature.expandLines) ? (
        <ExpandHunksListItems currentHunk={currentHunk} nextHunk={nextHunk} previousHunk={previousHunk} />
      ) : null}
    </>
  )
}

function ExpandHunksListItems({
  currentHunk,
  nextHunk,
  previousHunk,
}: {
  currentHunk: HunkData | undefined
  nextHunk: HunkData | undefined
  previousHunk: HunkData | undefined
}) {
  const {canExpandStartOfHunk, expandEndOfHunk, expandStartOfHunk, canExpandEndOfHunk} = useExpandHunk({
    currentHunk,
    nextHunk,
    previousHunk,
  })

  return (
    <>
      {canExpandStartOfHunk && (
        <ActionList.Item onSelect={expandStartOfHunk}>
          <ActionList.LeadingVisual>
            <FoldUpIcon />
          </ActionList.LeadingVisual>
          Expand above
        </ActionList.Item>
      )}
      {canExpandEndOfHunk && (
        <ActionList.Item onSelect={expandEndOfHunk}>
          <ActionList.LeadingVisual>
            <FoldDownIcon />
          </ActionList.LeadingVisual>
          Expand below
        </ActionList.Item>
      )}
    </>
  )
}

import {ArrowRightIcon, TriangleDownIcon} from '@primer/octicons-react'
import {ActionList, ActionMenu, Button, Dialog, FormControl, Radio, RadioGroup, TextInput} from '@primer/react'
import {useCallback, useRef, useState} from 'react'
import {useGetStartNewCommentOnLine} from '../../stores/selectors'
import {FileLevelCode} from '../../stores/slices/move-new-comment'
import type {CommentLineChangeConfig} from './AddCommentEditor'
import type {DiffLineKey, FileKey, LineKey} from '../../types'
import type {DiffSide} from '../../conversation-types'

export type LineNumberOption = 'line' | 'lineRange' | 'file'

export function ChangeCommentLineNumberButton({
  ariaLabelId,
  currentLineNumberText,
  setCommentLineChangeConfig,
  baseEndLineNumber,
  diffSide,
  baseStartLineNumber,
}: {
  ariaLabelId?: string
  currentLineNumberText: string
  setCommentLineChangeConfig: (config: CommentLineChangeConfig) => void
  diffSide: DiffSide
  baseEndLineNumber?: number
  baseStartLineNumber?: number
}) {
  const [isDialogOpen, setIsDialogOpen] = useState(false)
  const [selectedOption, setSelectedOption] = useState<LineNumberOption>(() => {
    if (baseStartLineNumber && baseEndLineNumber) {
      return 'lineRange'
    }
    if (baseEndLineNumber) {
      return 'line'
    }
    return 'file'
  })
  const [isLeftSelected, setIsLeftSelected] = useState(diffSide === 'LEFT')
  const [startLineNumber, setStartLineNumber] = useState(baseStartLineNumber ? String(baseStartLineNumber) : '')
  const [endLineNumber, setEndLineNumber] = useState(baseEndLineNumber ? String(baseEndLineNumber) : '')
  const [errorText, setErrorText] = useState<string>('')
  const getStartNewCommentOnLine = useGetStartNewCommentOnLine()
  const buttonRef = useRef<HTMLButtonElement>(null)

  const submit = useCallback(() => {
    const endLineData = getStartNewCommentOnLine(
      getLineKey(isLeftSelected, Number(endLineNumber), selectedOption === 'file'),
    )
    const startLineData = getStartNewCommentOnLine(
      getLineKey(isLeftSelected, Number(startLineNumber), selectedOption === 'file'),
    )
    const startConvoOnNewLine = endLineData?.startNewComment
    const endDiffLineKey = endLineData?.diffLineKey
    const startDiffLineKey = startLineData?.diffLineKey
    if (
      !validateLineNumberInput(selectedOption === 'lineRange' ? startLineNumber : undefined, endLineNumber) &&
      selectedOption !== 'file'
    ) {
      setErrorText('Please enter a valid line number or range.')
    } else {
      if (startConvoOnNewLine && endDiffLineKey) {
        // there is a valid line to start a new comment on because the line key had data associated with it
        const commentLineChangeConfig = buildCommentLineChangeConfig({
          isLeftSide: isLeftSelected,
          startLineNumber: selectedOption === 'lineRange' ? Number(startLineNumber) : undefined,
          endDiffLineKey,
          endLineNumber: Number(endLineNumber),
          isFileLevelComment: selectedOption === 'file',
          startDiffLineKey: selectedOption === 'lineRange' ? startDiffLineKey : undefined,
          startConversationOnNewLine: startConvoOnNewLine,
          closeLineMovingDialog: () => setIsDialogOpen(false),
          setErrorMessage: message => setErrorText(message),
        })
        setCommentLineChangeConfig(commentLineChangeConfig)
      } else {
        setErrorText('Please enter a valid line number or range.')
      }
    }
  }, [
    getStartNewCommentOnLine,
    isLeftSelected,
    endLineNumber,
    selectedOption,
    startLineNumber,
    setCommentLineChangeConfig,
  ])

  return (
    <div className="ml-1">
      <Button
        aria-labelledby={ariaLabelId}
        aria-description="Select to change location"
        ref={buttonRef}
        type="button"
        size="small"
        onClick={() => setIsDialogOpen(true)}
        trailingAction={TriangleDownIcon}
      >
        {currentLineNumberText}
      </Button>
      {isDialogOpen && (
        <Dialog
          onClose={() => setIsDialogOpen(false)}
          returnFocusRef={buttonRef}
          title="Add comment on"
          width="medium"
          footerButtons={[
            {
              buttonType: 'default',
              content: 'Cancel',
              onClick: () => {
                setIsDialogOpen(false)
              },
            },
            {
              buttonType: 'primary',
              content: 'Save',
              onClick: submit,
            },
          ]}
        >
          <RadioGroup
            name="line-number-selection"
            onChange={value => {
              setSelectedOption(value as LineNumberOption)
            }}
          >
            <RadioGroup.Label />
            <FormControl>
              <Radio value="line" defaultChecked={selectedOption === 'line'} />
              <FormControl.Label>Line number</FormControl.Label>
              {selectedOption === 'line' && (
                <FormControl.Caption className="d-flex align-items-center">
                  <MoveCommentLineNumberDropdown
                    isLeftSelected={isLeftSelected}
                    setIsLeftSelected={setIsLeftSelected}
                  />
                  <TextInput
                    autoComplete="off"
                    className="ml-1"
                    aria-label="Line number"
                    name="lineNumber"
                    value={endLineNumber}
                    onChange={e => setEndLineNumber(e.target.value)}
                    onKeyDown={e => {
                      // eslint-disable-next-line @github-ui/ui-commands/no-manual-shortcut-logic
                      if (e.key === 'Enter') {
                        submit()
                      }
                    }}
                  />
                </FormControl.Caption>
              )}
            </FormControl>
            <FormControl>
              <Radio value="lineRange" defaultChecked={selectedOption === 'lineRange'} />
              <FormControl.Label>Line range</FormControl.Label>
              {selectedOption === 'lineRange' && (
                <FormControl.Caption className="d-flex align-items-center">
                  <MoveCommentLineNumberDropdown
                    isLeftSelected={isLeftSelected}
                    setIsLeftSelected={setIsLeftSelected}
                  />
                  <TextInput
                    autoComplete="off"
                    className={'ml-1'}
                    aria-label="Line number"
                    name="lineNumber"
                    value={startLineNumber}
                    onChange={e => setStartLineNumber(e.target.value)}
                    onKeyDown={e => {
                      // eslint-disable-next-line @github-ui/ui-commands/no-manual-shortcut-logic
                      if (e.key === 'Enter') {
                        submit()
                      }
                    }}
                  />
                  <ArrowRightIcon className="mt-2 mx-2" />
                  <TextInput
                    autoComplete="off"
                    className="ml-1"
                    aria-label="Line number"
                    name="lineNumber"
                    value={endLineNumber}
                    onChange={e => setEndLineNumber(e.target.value)}
                    onKeyDown={e => {
                      // eslint-disable-next-line @github-ui/ui-commands/no-manual-shortcut-logic
                      if (e.key === 'Enter') {
                        submit()
                      }
                    }}
                  />
                </FormControl.Caption>
              )}
            </FormControl>
            <FormControl>
              <Radio value="file" defaultChecked={selectedOption === 'file'} />
              <FormControl.Label>File</FormControl.Label>
            </FormControl>
            {errorText && <RadioGroup.Validation variant="error">{errorText}</RadioGroup.Validation>}
          </RadioGroup>
        </Dialog>
      )}
    </div>
  )
}

const leftText = 'Left (L)'
const rightText = 'Right (R)'

function MoveCommentLineNumberDropdown({
  isLeftSelected,
  setIsLeftSelected,
}: {
  isLeftSelected: boolean
  setIsLeftSelected: (value: boolean) => void
}) {
  const textToUse = isLeftSelected ? leftText : rightText
  return (
    <ActionMenu>
      <ActionMenu.Button>{textToUse}</ActionMenu.Button>
      <ActionMenu.Overlay>
        <ActionList>
          <ActionList.Item
            onSelect={() => {
              setIsLeftSelected(true)
            }}
          >
            {leftText}
          </ActionList.Item>
          <ActionList.Item
            onSelect={() => {
              setIsLeftSelected(false)
            }}
          >
            {rightText}
          </ActionList.Item>
        </ActionList>
      </ActionMenu.Overlay>
    </ActionMenu>
  )
}

function getLineKey(isLeftSide: boolean, lineNumber: number, isFile: boolean): LineKey | FileKey {
  if (isFile) {
    return FileLevelCode
  }
  const sideIdentifier = isLeftSide ? 'L' : 'R'
  return `${sideIdentifier}${lineNumber}`
}

function buildCommentLineChangeConfig(args: {
  isLeftSide: boolean
  startLineNumber: number | undefined
  endDiffLineKey: DiffLineKey
  endLineNumber: number
  isFileLevelComment: boolean
  setErrorMessage: (message: string) => void
  closeLineMovingDialog: () => void
  startDiffLineKey: DiffLineKey | undefined
  startConversationOnNewLine: () => void
}): CommentLineChangeConfig {
  return {
    diffSide: args.isLeftSide ? 'LEFT' : 'RIGHT',
    startLineNumber: args.startLineNumber,
    endLineNumber: args.endLineNumber,
    isFileLevelComment: args.isFileLevelComment,
    startDiffLineKey: args.startDiffLineKey,
    endDiffLineKey: args.endDiffLineKey,
    setErrorMessage: args.setErrorMessage,
    startConversationOnNewLine: args.startConversationOnNewLine,
    closeLineMovingDialog: args.closeLineMovingDialog,
  }
}

function validateLineNumberInput(startLine: string | undefined, endLine: string | undefined): boolean {
  const startLineNumber = Number(startLine)
  const endLineNumber = Number(endLine)
  return (
    Number.isInteger(endLineNumber) &&
    endLineNumber > 0 &&
    (startLine === undefined ||
      (Number.isInteger(startLineNumber) && startLineNumber > 0 && startLineNumber <= endLineNumber))
  )
}

import {ChatImage} from '@github-ui/copilot-chat/components/ChatImage'
import type {CopilotChatMode} from '@github-ui/copilot-chat/utils/copilot-chat-types'
import {sendEvent} from '@github-ui/hydro-analytics'
import {memo, useCallback, useEffect} from 'react'

import styles from './ChatImageAttachment.module.css'
import {useContentPreview} from './ContentPreview/ContentPreviewContext'

export interface ChatImageAttachmentProps {
  messageId: string
  src?: string
  alt: string
  name: string
  mode: CopilotChatMode
  squareView?: boolean
}

const ChatImageAttachmentUnmemoized = ({messageId, src, alt, name, mode, squareView}: ChatImageAttachmentProps) => {
  const {openPreviewPane, openItem, updateItem} = useContentPreview()

  // Since name isn't guaranteed to be unique, we append the message ID as a unique identifier
  const id = `image:${src}` as const

  const onClick = useCallback(
    (event: React.MouseEvent<HTMLAnchorElement>) => {
      if (event.metaKey || event.ctrlKey) return

      sendEvent('dotcom_chat.activate', {target: 'BROWSER_IMAGE_OPENED', mode: 'immersive'})

      openItem(id)
      openPreviewPane()

      event.preventDefault()
    },
    [id, openPreviewPane, openItem],
  )

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!src) return

    updateItem({
      altText: alt,
      id,
      messageId,
      name,
      type: 'image',
      url: src,
    })
  }, [id, alt, messageId, name, src, updateItem])

  // If src is undefined, don't render anything
  if (!src) {
    return null
  }

  return (
    <div className={styles.imageAttachment}>
      <ChatImage src={src} alt={alt} onClick={onClick} mode={mode} useOverlay squareView={squareView} />
    </div>
  )
}

export const ChatImageAttachment = memo(ChatImageAttachmentUnmemoized)

import {type RefObject, useCallback, useMemo, useRef, useState} from 'react'
import {useSubmitSuggestedChangesMutation} from '@github-ui/pull-requests/use-submit-suggested-changes-mutation'
import {useFeatureFlag} from '@github-ui/react-core/use-feature-flag'

import {useDialogStateContext} from '../../contexts/DialogStateContext'

import type {SuggestedChange} from '../../conversation-types'
import {ApplyOrAddToBatch} from './CodeSuggestionActions/ApplyOrAddToBatch'
import {ApplySuggestionDialog} from './CodeSuggestionActions/ApplySuggestionDialog'
import {CodeSuggestionUnavailable} from './CodeSuggestionActions/CodeSuggestionUnavailable'
import {validateSuggestedChangeCanBeAddedToBatch} from '../../helpers/suggested-changes'
import {useComparisonInfo, useSuggestionBatch} from '@github-ui/pull-requests/pull-request-store-selectors'

export function CodeSuggestionActions({suggestedChange}: {suggestedChange: SuggestedChange}) {
  const {setIsDialogOpen} = useDialogStateContext()
  const [isApplySuggestionsDialogOpen, setIsApplySuggestionsDialogOpen] = useState(false)
  const returnFocusRef = useRef<RefObject<HTMLElement | null> | undefined>(undefined)
  const batchSuggestedChangesEnabled = useFeatureFlag('batch_suggested_changes')
  const suggestionBatch = useSuggestionBatch()

  const isPendingInBatch =
    batchSuggestedChangesEnabled &&
    suggestedChange.commentDatabaseId !== undefined &&
    suggestedChange.commentDatabaseId !== null &&
    suggestionBatch.activeBatch.some(
      id =>
        id.commentDatabaseId === suggestedChange.commentDatabaseId &&
        id.suggestionIndex === suggestedChange.suggestionIndex,
    )

  // Validate using the batch which now stores threadId, path, and lineRange
  const {isValid, reason} = validateSuggestedChangeCanBeAddedToBatch(
    {threadId: suggestedChange.threadId, path: suggestedChange.path, lineRange: suggestedChange.lineRange},
    suggestionBatch.activeBatch,
  )

  const changesToApply = useMemo(() => [suggestedChange], [suggestedChange])

  const authorLogins = changesToApply.map((change: SuggestedChange) => change.authorLogin)

  const setDialogOpen = useCallback(
    (isDialogOpen: boolean) => {
      setIsApplySuggestionsDialogOpen(isDialogOpen)
      setIsDialogOpen?.(isDialogOpen)
    },
    [setIsDialogOpen],
  )

  const closeDialog = useCallback(() => {
    setDialogOpen(false)

    // manually return focus to the button that opened the dialog
    if (returnFocusRef.current) {
      const ref = returnFocusRef.current.current
      setTimeout(() => ref?.focus())
    }
  }, [setDialogOpen])

  const openDialog = useCallback(
    (returnFocusTo?: RefObject<HTMLElement | null>) => {
      setDialogOpen(true)

      // store a ref to an element we will return focus to when the dialog closes
      returnFocusRef.current = returnFocusTo
    },
    [setDialogOpen, returnFocusRef],
  )

  const {mutate: submitSuggestions} = useSubmitSuggestedChangesMutation()
  const {
    fullDiff: {headOid},
  } = useComparisonInfo()
  const handleCommit = useCallback(
    (
      commitMessage: string,
      onError: (error: Error, type?: string, friendlyMessage?: string) => void,
      onCompleted: () => void,
    ) => {
      submitSuggestions(
        {changes: changesToApply, message: commitMessage, currentOid: headOid},
        {
          onSuccess: () => {
            closeDialog()
            onCompleted()
          },
          onError: (error: Error) => {
            onError(error, 'submitSuggestedChanges', 'Failed to submit suggested changes')
          },
        },
      )
    },
    [changesToApply, closeDialog, headOid, submitSuggestions],
  )

  return (
    <div className="border-top p-2">
      <ApplyOrAddToBatch suggestedChange={suggestedChange} onOpenDialog={openDialog} canAddToBatch={isValid} />

      {!isPendingInBatch && !isValid && reason && <CodeSuggestionUnavailable reason={reason} className="m-0" />}

      {isApplySuggestionsDialogOpen && (
        <ApplySuggestionDialog
          authorLogins={authorLogins}
          batchSize={changesToApply.length}
          onClose={closeDialog}
          onCommit={handleCommit}
        />
      )}
    </div>
  )
}

export function CodeSuggestionUnavailable({reason, className = 'm-2'}: {reason: string; className?: string}) {
  return (
    <div className={className}>
      <small className="fgColor-muted">{reason}</small>
    </div>
  )
}

import {type AvailableCommand, useAvailableCommands} from '@github-ui/copilot-chat/hooks/use-available-commands'
import {ModeSupportName, type StarterEntry} from '@github-ui/copilot-chat/utils/copilot-chat-types'

import {CommandPill} from './CommandPill'
import styles from './CommandStarters.module.css'
import {getStarterGroups} from './starter-groups'
import {StarterGroupWithMenu} from './StarterGroupWithMenu'

type CommandsProps = {
  onSelect: (command: AvailableCommand) => void
  onIceBreakerSelect: (command: StarterEntry) => void
  iceBreakersExperimentEnabled?: boolean
  paidUser?: boolean
  activeModeName?: string
}

export const CommandStarters = ({
  onSelect,
  onIceBreakerSelect,
  iceBreakersExperimentEnabled,
  paidUser,
  activeModeName,
}: CommandsProps) => {
  const {availableCommands} = useAvailableCommands({enabled: true})

  // Legacy starters to show in the icebreakers experiment
  // show only 'git' and 'pull request' for paid users, and also 'write code' for non-paid users
  const legacyStarters = getStarterGroups(true, activeModeName === ModeSupportName.Task).filter(group => {
    if (group.id === 'git-group' || group.id === 'pull-requests-group') return true
    if (!paidUser && group.id === 'write-code-group') return true
    if (activeModeName === ModeSupportName.Task) return true
    return false
  })

  // Filter commands based on feature flags and user plan
  const filteredCommands = iceBreakersExperimentEnabled
    ? availableCommands.filter(command => {
        if (activeModeName === ModeSupportName.Task) return false
        if (command.key === 'ask') return false
        if (!paidUser && command.key === 'spark') return false
        return true
      })
    : availableCommands

  if (filteredCommands.length === 0 && legacyStarters.length === 0) {
    return null
  }

  return (
    <div className={styles.container}>
      <ul className={styles.list}>
        {filteredCommands.map(command => (
          <li key={command.key}>
            <CommandPill icon={command.icon} onClick={() => onSelect(command)} isNew={command.isNew}>
              {command.name}
            </CommandPill>
          </li>
        ))}
        {/* When the experiment FF is enabled, we want to render the legacy icebreakers */}
        {iceBreakersExperimentEnabled &&
          legacyStarters.map(group => (
            <StarterGroupWithMenu key={group.id} group={group} onEntrySelect={onIceBreakerSelect} />
          ))}
      </ul>
    </div>
  )
}

import type {ChatInputRef} from '@github-ui/copilot-chat/components/ChatInput'
import {type AvailableCommand, useAvailableCommands} from '@github-ui/copilot-chat/hooks/use-available-commands'
import {extractChatCommandType} from '@github-ui/copilot-chat/utils/chat-commands-helpers'
import type {CopilotChatCommandType, StarterEntry} from '@github-ui/copilot-chat/utils/copilot-chat-types'
import {sendEvent} from '@github-ui/hydro-analytics'
import {useNavigate} from '@github-ui/use-navigate'

import {CommandStarters} from './CommandStarters'
import {CommandSuggestions} from './CommandSuggestions'

interface CommandStartersAndSuggestionsProps {
  chatCommand: CopilotChatCommandType | null
  chatInputRef: React.RefObject<ChatInputRef | null>
  textAreaRef: React.RefObject<HTMLTextAreaElement | null>
  containerRef: React.RefObject<HTMLElement | null>
  iceBreakersExperimentEnabled?: boolean
  paidUser?: boolean
  activeModeName?: string
}

export function CommandStartersAndSuggestions({
  chatCommand,
  chatInputRef,
  containerRef,
  textAreaRef,
  iceBreakersExperimentEnabled,
  paidUser,
  activeModeName,
}: CommandStartersAndSuggestionsProps) {
  const {availableCommands, availableCommandKeys} = useAvailableCommands({enabled: true})
  const navigate = useNavigate()

  const handleCommandSelect = (command: AvailableCommand) => {
    sendEvent('dotcom_chat.activate', {target: 'COMMAND_STARTER_SELECTED', mode: 'immersive', command: command.key})
    if (command.enabled?.() === false && command.disabledAction) {
      command.disabledAction(navigate)
      return
    }
    chatInputRef.current?.setText(text => {
      // Replace the existing command with the new one
      const foundCommand = extractChatCommandType(text, availableCommandKeys)

      if (foundCommand) {
        return text.replace(`/${foundCommand}`, `/${command.key}`)
      }

      return `/${command.key} ${text}`
    })

    textAreaRef.current?.focus()
  }

  const handleSuggestionSelect = (command: CopilotChatCommandType, suggestion: string) => {
    sendEvent('dotcom_chat.activate', {
      target: 'COMMAND_SUGGESTION_SELECTED',
      mode: 'immersive',
      command,
      suggestion,
      iceBreakerFFEnabled: iceBreakersExperimentEnabled,
    })
    chatInputRef.current?.setText(`/${command} ${suggestion}`.trim())
    textAreaRef.current?.focus()
  }

  const handleIceBreakerSelect = (command: StarterEntry) => {
    sendEvent('dotcom_chat.activate', {
      target: 'COMMAND_SUGGESTION_SELECTED',
      mode: 'immersive',
      command: command.id,
      suggestion: command.message,
      iceBreakerFFEnabled: iceBreakersExperimentEnabled,
    })
    chatInputRef.current?.setText(`${command.message}`.trim())
    textAreaRef.current?.focus()
  }

  const suggestions = chatCommand ? availableCommands.find(command => command.key === chatCommand)?.suggestions : null
  const hasSuggestions = suggestions && suggestions.length > 0

  return (
    <>
      {/* only show suggestions if they exist, otherwise default to command starters */}
      {chatCommand && hasSuggestions && (
        <CommandSuggestions
          key={chatCommand}
          chatCommand={chatCommand}
          suggestions={suggestions}
          onSelect={handleSuggestionSelect}
          anchorRef={containerRef}
          textAreaRef={textAreaRef}
        />
      )}
      <>
        <h2 className="sr-only">Chat Commands</h2>
        <CommandStarters
          onSelect={handleCommandSelect}
          onIceBreakerSelect={handleIceBreakerSelect}
          iceBreakersExperimentEnabled={iceBreakersExperimentEnabled}
          paidUser={paidUser}
          activeModeName={activeModeName}
        />
      </>
    </>
  )
}

import type {CopilotChatCommandType} from '@github-ui/copilot-chat/utils/copilot-chat-types'
import {getAnchoredPosition} from '@primer/behaviors'
import {ActionList, Overlay} from '@primer/react'
import {useCallback, useEffect, useRef, useState} from 'react'

type CommandSuggestionsProps = {
  chatCommand: CopilotChatCommandType
  suggestions: string[]
  onSelect: (command: CopilotChatCommandType, suggestion: string) => void
  anchorRef: React.RefObject<HTMLElement | null>
  textAreaRef: React.RefObject<HTMLTextAreaElement | null>
}

export const CommandSuggestions = ({
  chatCommand,
  suggestions,
  onSelect,
  anchorRef,
  textAreaRef,
}: CommandSuggestionsProps) => {
  const [top, setTop] = useState<number>(0)
  const [left, setLeft] = useState<number>(0)
  const [isOpen, setIsOpen] = useState(false)
  const [anchorWidth, setAnchorWidth] = useState<number | undefined>(undefined)
  const [selectedIndex, setSelectedIndex] = useState<number>(-1)
  const [textAreaValue, setTextAreaValue] = useState<string>('')
  const resizeObserverRef = useRef<ResizeObserver | null>(null)
  const overlayRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null)

  // Filter suggestions based on text
  const visibleSuggestions = suggestions.filter(suggestion => {
    const fullSuggestion = `/${chatCommand} ${suggestion}`.toLowerCase()
    const lowerTextAreaValue = textAreaValue.toLowerCase().trim()
    return fullSuggestion.includes(lowerTextAreaValue) && fullSuggestion !== lowerTextAreaValue
  })

  const isVisible = isOpen && visibleSuggestions.length > 0

  const updatePosition = useCallback(() => {
    if (overlayRef.current && anchorRef.current) {
      const {top: topPos, left: leftPos} = getAnchoredPosition(overlayRef.current, anchorRef.current, {
        align: 'start',
        side: 'outside-bottom',
        anchorOffset: 4,
      })
      setTop(topPos)
      setLeft(leftPos)
    }
  }, [anchorRef])

  useEffect(() => {
    // Open on the initial render only if the textArea has focus.
    if (textAreaRef.current === document.activeElement) {
      // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/set-state-in-effect
      setIsOpen(true)
    }
  }, [textAreaRef])

  // Track textarea value changes
  useEffect(() => {
    const textArea = textAreaRef.current
    if (!textArea) return

    const handleInput = () => {
      setTextAreaValue(textArea.value)
    }

    // Set initial value
    setTextAreaValue(textArea.value)

    textArea.addEventListener('input', handleInput)

    return () => {
      textArea.removeEventListener('input', handleInput)
    }
  }, [textAreaRef])

  // Measure and track anchor width dynamically using ResizeObserver
  useEffect(() => {
    const currentAnchor = anchorRef.current
    if (!isVisible || !currentAnchor) return

    const updateLayout = () => {
      if (anchorRef.current) {
        const rect = anchorRef.current.getBoundingClientRect()
        setAnchorWidth(rect.width)
        updatePosition()
      }
    }

    // Initial measurement
    updateLayout()

    // Create observer only if it doesn't exist
    if (!resizeObserverRef.current) {
      resizeObserverRef.current = new ResizeObserver(updateLayout)
    }

    // Watch for size changes
    resizeObserverRef.current.observe(currentAnchor)

    return () => {
      // Unobserve when effect cleans up, but keep the observer instance
      if (resizeObserverRef.current) {
        resizeObserverRef.current.unobserve(currentAnchor)
      }
    }
  }, [isVisible, anchorRef, updatePosition])

  const shouldIgnoreInteraction = useCallback(
    (target: Node | null): boolean => {
      if (!target) return false
      // Ignore if interaction is within textarea or overlay
      return (textAreaRef.current?.contains(target) ?? false) || (overlayRef.current?.contains(target) ?? false)
    },
    [textAreaRef, overlayRef],
  )

  useEffect(() => {
    const textArea = textAreaRef.current
    if (!textArea) return

    const handleFocusOut = (event: FocusEvent) => {
      // Don't close if focus moved to overlay or stayed in textarea
      if (shouldIgnoreInteraction(event.relatedTarget as Node | null)) {
        return
      }
      // Close the overlay when focus leaves the textarea
      setIsOpen(false)
    }

    textArea.addEventListener('blur', handleFocusOut)

    return () => {
      textArea.removeEventListener('blur', handleFocusOut)
    }
  }, [textAreaRef, shouldIgnoreInteraction])

  // Reopen overlay when textarea is focused and there's a chat command
  useEffect(() => {
    const textArea = textAreaRef.current
    if (!textArea) return

    const handleFocus = () => {
      if (chatCommand) {
        setIsOpen(true)
      }
    }

    textArea.addEventListener('focus', handleFocus)

    return () => {
      textArea.removeEventListener('focus', handleFocus)
    }
  }, [textAreaRef, chatCommand])

  const closeAndReset = useCallback(() => {
    setIsOpen(false)
    setSelectedIndex(-1)
  }, [])

  // Reset selectedIndex and unlock suggestions when printable characters are typed
  useEffect(() => {
    const textArea = textAreaRef.current
    if (!textArea || !isVisible) return

    const handleKeyDown = (event: KeyboardEvent) => {
      // Check if it's a printable character (length 1 and not a modifier key) or delete/backspace
      if (
        (event.key.length === 1 && !event.ctrlKey && !event.metaKey && !event.altKey && !event.isComposing) ||
        event.key === 'Backspace' ||
        event.key === 'Delete'
      ) {
        setSelectedIndex(-1)
      }
    }

    textArea.addEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown)

    return () => {
      textArea.removeEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown)
    }
  }, [textAreaRef, isVisible])

  // Handle arrow key navigation on textarea
  useEffect(() => {
    const textArea = textAreaRef.current
    if (!textArea || !isVisible) return

    const handleArrowNavigation = (direction: 'up' | 'down', prevIndex: number) => {
      const newIndex =
        direction === 'down'
          ? prevIndex === -1
            ? 0
            : Math.min(prevIndex + 1, visibleSuggestions.length - 1)
          : prevIndex === -1
            ? visibleSuggestions.length - 1
            : Math.max(prevIndex - 1, 0)

      const suggestion = visibleSuggestions[newIndex]
      if (suggestion) {
        onSelect(chatCommand, suggestion)
      }
      return newIndex
    }

    const handleKeyDown = (event: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if (event.key === 'ArrowDown' || event.key === 'ArrowUp') {
        event.preventDefault()
        const direction = event.key === 'ArrowDown' ? 'down' : 'up'
        setSelectedIndex(prev => handleArrowNavigation(direction, prev))
      }
    }

    textArea.addEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown)

    return () => {
      textArea.removeEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown)
    }
  }, [textAreaRef, isVisible, visibleSuggestions, chatCommand, onSelect])

  const onClickOutside = useCallback(
    (event: MouseEvent | TouchEvent) => {
      // Don't close if clicking on the textarea or overlay
      if (event.target instanceof Node && shouldIgnoreInteraction(event.target)) {
        return
      }
      closeAndReset()
    },
    [shouldIgnoreInteraction, closeAndReset],
  )

  const onItemSelect = useCallback(
    (suggestion: string) => {
      onSelect(chatCommand, suggestion)
      setSelectedIndex(-1)
    },
    [chatCommand, onSelect],
  )

  if (!isVisible) {
    return null
  }

  return (
    <Overlay
      top={top}
      left={left}
      onClickOutside={onClickOutside}
      onEscape={closeAndReset}
      returnFocusRef={anchorRef}
      aria-label="Command suggestions"
      style={anchorWidth ? {width: `${anchorWidth}px`} : undefined}
      data-testid="command-suggestions-overlay"
      ref={overlayRef}
      preventFocusOnOpen
    >
      <ActionList>
        {visibleSuggestions.map((suggestion, index) => {
          return (
            <ActionList.Item
              key={suggestion}
              size="large"
              onSelect={() => onItemSelect(suggestion)}
              active={index === selectedIndex}
            >
              <ActionList.LeadingVisual>
                <span className="fgColor-muted">/{chatCommand}</span>
              </ActionList.LeadingVisual>
              {suggestion}
            </ActionList.Item>
          )
        })}
      </ActionList>
    </Overlay>
  )
}

import {AlertIcon} from '@primer/octicons-react'
import {Blankslate} from '@primer/react/experimental'

export function CommentErrorFallback() {
  return (
    <div className={`position-relative`}>
      <Blankslate border>
        <Blankslate.Visual>
          <AlertIcon size={24} className="fgColor-muted tmp-mt-3 tmp-mb-3" />
        </Blankslate.Visual>
        <Blankslate.Heading>
          <strong>Comments cannot be loaded right now</strong>
        </Blankslate.Heading>
        <div className="mb-n2">
          <Blankslate.Description>Refresh the page or try again later</Blankslate.Description>
        </div>
        <Blankslate.SecondaryAction href="https://www.githubstatus.com/">GitHub status</Blankslate.SecondaryAction>
      </Blankslate>
    </div>
  )
}

import {clsx} from 'clsx'
import {GitHubAvatar} from '@github-ui/github-avatar'
import {AvatarStack} from '@primer/react'
import type {CommentAuthor} from '../../types'
import {AnimatedAnnotationIcon, AnnotationIcon, AnnotationPresentationMap} from '../conversations/AnnotationIcon'
import type {DiffAnnotation, DiffAnnotationLevels, Thread, ViewerData} from '../../conversation-types'
import {useEffect, type MutableRefObject} from 'react'
import styles from './CommentIndicator.module.css'
import {VALUES} from '../../constants'

const sizing = {
  compact: {
    avatarSize: 18,
  },
  relaxed: {
    avatarSize: 20,
  },
}

const ghostUser = VALUES.ghostUser

/**
 * Indicates whether a line has annotations, threads, and comments
 * Displays at most 3 avatars/icons as well as the total comment count across threads and annotations
 *
 */
export default function CommentIndicator({
  shouldAnimateRef,
  lineSpacingPreference = 'relaxed',
  threads,
  annotations,
}: {
  shouldAnimateRef?: MutableRefObject<boolean>
  lineSpacingPreference: ViewerData['lineSpacingPreference']
  threads: Thread[]
  annotations: DiffAnnotation[]
}) {
  // Reset the animation state after the first render so that the animation doesn't play on subsequent renders
  useEffect(() => {
    if (shouldAnimateRef?.current) {
      shouldAnimateRef.current = false
    }
  }, [shouldAnimateRef])

  const {avatarSize} = sizing[lineSpacingPreference]

  const totalCommentsCount = threads.length
  const totalAnnotationsCount = annotations.length
  const totalCommentsAndAnnotationsCount = totalCommentsCount + totalAnnotationsCount
  if (totalCommentsAndAnnotationsCount === 0) {
    return null
  }

  const commentAuthors: {[key: string]: CommentAuthor} = {}
  const annotationsInfo: Array<{id: string; level: DiffAnnotationLevels}> = []

  annotations.map(annotation => {
    if (annotation?.annotationLevel) {
      annotationsInfo.push({id: annotation.id, level: annotation.annotationLevel})
    }
  })

  threads.map(thread =>
    thread?.commentsData?.comments?.map(comment => {
      if (comment?.author) {
        commentAuthors[comment?.author.login] = comment.author
      } else if (ghostUser?.login) {
        commentAuthors[ghostUser.login] = ghostUser
      }
    }),
  )

  const annotationIcons = annotationsInfo
    .sort((annotationA, annotationB) => {
      const priorityA = AnnotationPresentationMap[annotationA.level]?.priority || 0
      const priorityB = AnnotationPresentationMap[annotationB.level]?.priority || 0
      return priorityA < priorityB ? -1 : 1
    })
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/refs
    .map(annotation => {
      if (shouldAnimateRef?.current) {
        return <AnimatedAnnotationIcon key={annotation.id} annotationLevel={annotation.level} />
      }
      return <AnnotationIcon key={annotation.id} annotationLevel={annotation.level} />
    })

  const commentAuthorAvatars = Object.keys(commentAuthors).map(authorName => {
    const authorLogin = commentAuthors[authorName]?.login
    const authorAvatarUrl = commentAuthors[authorName]?.avatarUrl ?? (ghostUser?.avatarUrl || ' ')
    return <GitHubAvatar key={authorLogin} alt={authorLogin} size={20} src={authorAvatarUrl} />
  })

  // Display max of 3 stacked avatars/icons
  const avatarStackChildren = [...annotationIcons, ...commentAuthorAvatars].slice(0, 3)
  const indicatorText = totalCommentsAndAnnotationsCount > 9 ? '9+' : `${totalCommentsAndAnnotationsCount}`

  return (
    <div className={clsx('d-flex', 'flex-justify-between', 'flex-items-center', styles.commentIndicatorContainer)}>
      <AvatarStack
        disableExpand
        size={avatarSize}
        className={clsx(styles.AvatarStack, lineSpacingPreference === 'relaxed' ? styles.Relaxed : styles.Compact)}
      >
        {avatarStackChildren}
      </AvatarStack>
      <span className={clsx('fgColor-default', 'ml-1', 'pointer', 'commentIndicatorText', styles.textStyles)}>
        {indicatorText}
      </span>
    </div>
  )
}

import {CommentBox, type CommentBoxHandle, type CommentBoxProps} from '@github-ui/comment-box/CommentBox'
import type React from 'react'

import {useComparisonInfo, usePullRequest, useRepository} from '@github-ui/pull-requests/pull-request-store-selectors'
import type {RepoSubject} from '@github-ui/comment-box/subject'

export type ConversationCommentBoxProps = Omit<CommentBoxProps, 'children' | 'body'> & {
  children?: React.ReactNode
  label: string
  value: string
  ref?: React.Ref<CommentBoxHandle>
}

export const ConversationCommentBox = ({ref, children, ...rest}: ConversationCommentBoxProps) => {
  const pullRequest = usePullRequest()
  const {baseRepository: repository} = useRepository()
  const comparisonInfo = useComparisonInfo()
  const subject: RepoSubject = {
    type: 'pull_request',
    id: {
      id: pullRequest.id,
    },
    pullRequestNumber: pullRequest.number,
    repository: {
      databaseId: repository.id,
      nwo: `${repository.ownerLogin}/${repository.name}`,
      slashCommandsEnabled: false, // TODO - follow up on this
    },
    comparison: {
      startCommitOid: comparisonInfo.effectiveRange.baseOid,
      endCommitOid: comparisonInfo.effectiveRange.headOid,
    },
  }

  return (
    <CommentBox
      {...rest}
      ref={ref}
      placeholder="Leave a comment"
      actions={children}
      subject={subject}
      showLabel={false}
    />
  )
}

ConversationCommentBox.displayName = 'ConversationCommentBox'

import type {DirectoryItem} from '@github-ui/code-view-types'
import type {TreeItem} from '@github-ui/repos-file-tree-view'
import {buildTreeFromPaths} from './build-tree-from-paths'

export interface FlatPathsResponse {
  paths: string[]
  directories: string[]
}

function convertTreeDataToTreeItems(
  treeData: Record<string, {items: DirectoryItem[]}>,
  path: string,
): Array<TreeItem<DirectoryItem>> {
  const directory = treeData[path]
  if (!directory) return []

  const items = directory.items.map(item => ({
    data: item,
    items: item.contentType === 'directory' ? convertTreeDataToTreeItems(treeData, item.path) : [],
  }))

  // Sort items: directories first, then alphabetical within each type
  return items.sort((a, b) => {
    if (a.data.contentType === 'directory' && b.data.contentType !== 'directory') return -1
    if (a.data.contentType !== 'directory' && b.data.contentType === 'directory') return 1
    return a.data.name.localeCompare(b.data.name)
  })
}

export function convertFlatPathsToTreeItems(response: FlatPathsResponse): Array<TreeItem<DirectoryItem>> {
  // First convert flat paths to ReposFileTreeData structure
  const treeData = buildTreeFromPaths(response)
  // Then recursively convert the tree data to TreeItem array starting from root
  return convertTreeDataToTreeItems(treeData, '')
}

import type {CopilotChatThread, CopilotSpaceId} from '@github-ui/copilot-chat/utils/copilot-chat-types'

export function copilotSpaceIdFromThread(thread?: CopilotChatThread | null): CopilotSpaceId | null {
  if (!thread || !thread.customCopilotID || !thread.customCopilotOwner) return null

  return {
    id: thread.customCopilotID,
    owner: thread.customCopilotOwner,
  }
}

import type {CopilotSpace} from '@github-ui/copilot-chat/utils/copilot-chat-types'
import {getCopilotSpacePath, SLUG_ERROR_MESSAGE} from '@github-ui/copilot-chat/utils/custom-copilots-helpers'
import {SpaceDialogFooter} from './SpaceDialogFooter'
import {useAutoSpaceName} from '../hooks'
import {useUpsertCopilotSpace} from '../hooks/use-upsert-copilot-space'
import type {CopilotSpaceRepositoryBackedResource} from '../types'
import {sendEvent} from '@github-ui/hydro-analytics'
import {UnsafeHTMLText} from '@github-ui/safe-html/UnsafeHTML'
import {useNavigate} from '@github-ui/use-navigate'
import {Banner, Dialog, FormControl, Stack, TextInput, useConfirm} from '@primer/react'
import {useCallback, useEffect, useRef, useState} from 'react'
import type {OwnerItem} from './SpacesOwnerDropdown'
import {copilotLocalStorage} from '@github-ui/copilot-chat/utils/copilot-local-storage'

import styles from './CreateSpaceDialog.module.css'
import {SpacesOwnerDropdown} from './SpacesOwnerDropdown'
import {resourcesForDuplication} from '../utils/resources-for-duplication'

export function CreateSpaceDialog({
  dialogTitle = 'New Space',
  closeDialog,
  initialSpace,
  onSubmit,
  returnFocusRef,
}: {
  dialogTitle?: string
  closeDialog: () => void
  initialSpace?: CopilotSpace
  onSubmit?: () => void
  returnFocusRef?: React.RefObject<HTMLElement | null> | null
}) {
  const navigate = useNavigate()

  const nameInputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null)
  const defaultName = useAutoSpaceName()
  const [name, setName] = useState(initialSpace?.name ?? defaultName)
  const [owner, setOwner] = useState<OwnerItem | null>(null)
  const [errorMessages, setErrorMessages] = useState<Record<string, string>>({})
  const [genericErrorSavingSpace, setGenericErrorSavingSpace] = useState(false)
  const {upsertCopilotSpace, isPending: isSaving} = useUpsertCopilotSpace()
  const confirm = useConfirm()

  const clearErrors = useCallback(
    (fields: string[]) => {
      setGenericErrorSavingSpace(false)
      const updatedErrorMessages = {...errorMessages}
      for (const field of fields) {
        if (updatedErrorMessages[field]) {
          delete updatedErrorMessages[field]
        }
      }
      setErrorMessages(updatedErrorMessages)
    },
    [errorMessages],
  )
  const dropdownOnSelect = useCallback((newOwner: OwnerItem) => setOwner(newOwner), [])

  // Track when the v3 create dialog was opened: https://github.com/github/copilot-experiences/issues/6823
  useEffect(() => {
    sendEvent('copilot_spaces.create_space_v3_dialog_opened', {})
  }, [])

  // Announce validation errors to screen readers
  useEffect(() => {
    // When there is an error message with the name, focus the name input so the screen reader user knows where the error is and
    // so the screen reader announces the associated validation message.
    nameInputRef.current?.focus()
  }, [errorMessages.name, errorMessages.slug])

  useEffect(() => {
    if (initialSpace) {
      // If an initial space is being provided we want to focus instead of highlight since the use may not want to
      // change the whole name. They may want to leave it and just set a different owner, for example.
      nameInputRef.current?.focus()
    } else {
      // If the initialSpace is not provided that means we use an auto-generated name. We want to select the input so
      // that the user can quickly set their own name
      nameInputRef.current?.select()
    }
  }, [initialSpace])

  const saveSpace = async (): Promise<void> => {
    if (isSaving) return

    let resourcesToSave = initialSpace?.resources ? [...initialSpace.resources] : []
    let resourcesThatWillNotTransfer: CopilotSpaceRepositoryBackedResource[] = []

    // If duplicating a space
    if (initialSpace && owner) {
      const duplicationResult = resourcesForDuplication(initialSpace.resources || [], owner)
      resourcesToSave = duplicationResult.resourcesToSave
      resourcesThatWillNotTransfer = duplicationResult.resourcesThatWillNotTransfer
    }

    // Treat the save as confirmed unless some resource will not transfer
    let confirmed = true
    if (resourcesThatWillNotTransfer.length > 0) {
      // Returns a comma separated list of owner logins from resources that will not transfer. E.g. "github, vercel, and primer"
      const ownerLoginsAsSentence = new Intl.ListFormat().format(
        // Make it a Set first so that owner logins are unique. Then convert back to Array for formatting
        Array.from(
          new Set(
            resourcesThatWillNotTransfer.map(r => r.ownerLogin).filter((login): login is string => login !== undefined),
          ),
        ),
      )
      const content = `Private resources owned by <strong>${ownerLoginsAsSentence}</strong> will not be duplicated to the new space.`
      confirmed = await confirm({
        title: 'Some resources will not be duplicated',
        // Allows the <strong> tags to be rendered as HTML but will still escape unsafe HTML if present.
        content: <UnsafeHTMLText html={content} />,
        cancelButtonContent: 'Never mind',
        confirmButtonContent: 'Continue',
        confirmButtonType: 'primary',
      })
    }

    if (!confirmed) return

    try {
      const newSpace = await upsertCopilotSpace({
        name,
        ownerId: owner?.id,
        ownerType: owner?.type,
        resources: resourcesToSave,
        generalInstructions: initialSpace?.generalInstructions,
      })

      setErrorMessages({})
      setGenericErrorSavingSpace(false)
      // First close the dialog to prevent conditions where we navigate before the dialog is closed and the dialog pops
      // open again when going back to the space's page.
      closeDialog()
      onSubmit?.()
      // Store the newly created space as the last selected space
      copilotLocalStorage.setLastSelectedSpace(newSpace)
      // navigate to the newly created space
      const path = getCopilotSpacePath(newSpace)
      return navigate(path)
    } catch (errors) {
      // If errors is an object with field-specific errors, use those
      if (errors && typeof errors === 'object' && Object.keys(errors).length > 0) {
        setGenericErrorSavingSpace(false)
        setErrorMessages(errors as Record<string, string>)
      } else {
        // Otherwise, show a generic error banner (e.g., 500 errors)
        setErrorMessages({}) // Clear previously set field errors
        setGenericErrorSavingSpace(true)
      }
    }
  }
  const handleCancel = () => {
    closeDialog()
    // Return focus to the button that opened the dialog
    setTimeout(() => returnFocusRef?.current?.focus(), 0)
  }

  return (
    <Dialog
      position={{narrow: 'fullscreen'}}
      title={dialogTitle}
      onClose={handleCancel}
      width="large"
      aria-label={dialogTitle}
      className={styles.createSpaceDialog}
      renderFooter={() => (
        <SpaceDialogFooter
          onCancel={handleCancel}
          onSave={saveSpace}
          isLoading={isSaving}
          disabled={!owner}
          saveButtonText="Create Space"
        />
      )}
    >
      <form
        onSubmit={async e => {
          e.preventDefault()
          e.stopPropagation()
          await saveSpace()
        }}
      >
        {genericErrorSavingSpace && (
          <Banner
            className="tmp-mb-3"
            variant="critical"
            title="Error saving space"
            hideTitle
            description="Something went wrong while saving."
          />
        )}
        <Stack>
          <FormControl>
            <FormControl.Label required>Space name</FormControl.Label>
            <TextInput
              ref={nameInputRef}
              autoFocus
              block
              data-testid="copilot-space-name-input"
              name="copilot-space-name"
              value={name}
              onChange={e => {
                setName(e.target.value)
                clearErrors(['name', 'slug'])
              }}
            />
            <FormControl.Caption>Choose a name that describes your project or use case</FormControl.Caption>
            {errorMessages.name ? (
              <FormControl.Validation variant="error">
                <span>Name {errorMessages.name}</span>
              </FormControl.Validation>
            ) : errorMessages.slug ? (
              <FormControl.Validation variant="error">
                <span>{SLUG_ERROR_MESSAGE}</span>
              </FormControl.Validation>
            ) : null}
          </FormControl>
          <SpacesOwnerDropdown onSelect={dropdownOnSelect} />
        </Stack>
      </form>
    </Dialog>
  )
}

import {Dialog} from '@primer/react/experimental'

interface DiscardBatchConfirmationDialogProps {
  /**
   * Whether the dialog is open
   */
  isOpen: boolean
  /**
   * Callback when the user confirms the discard action
   */
  onConfirm: () => void
  /**
   * Callback when the user cancels or closes the dialog
   */
  onCancel: () => void
  /**
   * The number of suggestions in the batch
   */
  suggestionsCount: number
}

export function DiscardBatchConfirmationDialog({
  isOpen,
  onConfirm,
  onCancel,
  suggestionsCount,
}: DiscardBatchConfirmationDialogProps) {
  if (!isOpen) {
    return null
  }

  return (
    <Dialog
      title="Discard batch?"
      onClose={onCancel}
      footerButtons={[
        {content: 'Cancel', onClick: onCancel},
        {content: 'Discard', buttonType: 'danger', onClick: onConfirm},
      ]}
      width="medium"
    >
      <p>
        <span>Are you sure you want to discard this batch? This will remove all {suggestionsCount} </span>
        <span>{suggestionsCount === 1 ? 'suggestion' : 'suggestions'}</span>
        <span> from the batch.</span>
      </p>
    </Dialog>
  )
}

import {
  type Activators,
  type CollisionDescriptor,
  type CollisionDetection,
  KeyboardCode,
  type KeyboardCodes,
  KeyboardSensor,
  type KeyboardSensorOptions,
} from '@dnd-kit/core'
import {restrictToFirstScrollableAncestor, restrictToVerticalAxis} from '@dnd-kit/modifiers'

export const defaultKeyboardCodes: KeyboardCodes = {
  start: [KeyboardCode.Space, KeyboardCode.Enter],
  cancel: [KeyboardCode.Esc],
  end: [KeyboardCode.Space, KeyboardCode.Enter],
}

export const modifiers = [restrictToVerticalAxis, restrictToFirstScrollableAncestor]

export class CustomKeyboardSensor extends KeyboardSensor {
  static override activators: Activators<KeyboardSensorOptions> = [
    {
      eventName: 'onKeyDown' as const,
      // eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/unbound-method
      handler: (event: React.KeyboardEvent, {keyboardCodes = defaultKeyboardCodes, onActivation}, {active}) => {
        const {code} = event.nativeEvent

        if (keyboardCodes.start.includes(code)) {
          const activator = active.activatorNode.current

          if (activator && event.target !== activator) {
            return false
          }
          event.preventDefault()

          onActivation?.({event: event.nativeEvent})

          return true
        }

        return false
      },
    },
    {
      eventName: 'onClick' as const,
      // eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/unbound-method
      handler: (event: React.MouseEvent, {onActivation}) => {
        event.preventDefault()
        const keyboardEvent = new KeyboardEvent('keydown', {
          key: 'Enter',
          code: 'Enter',
        })
        onActivation?.({event: keyboardEvent})

        return true
      },
    },
  ]
}

/**
 * Works like `closestCenter` but along the vertical axis only. This is important for roadmap, where widths are
 * infinite and the horizontal axis is not meaningful.
 */
export const closestVerticalCenter: CollisionDetection = ({collisionRect, droppableRects, droppableContainers}) => {
  const collisionRectCenter = (collisionRect.top + collisionRect.bottom) / 2

  const collisions: CollisionDescriptor[] = []

  for (const droppableContainer of droppableContainers) {
    const {id} = droppableContainer
    const rect = droppableRects.get(id)

    if (rect) {
      const droppableRectCenter = (rect.top + rect.bottom) / 2
      const distBetween = Math.abs(collisionRectCenter - droppableRectCenter)

      collisions.push({id, data: {droppableContainer, value: distBetween}})
    }
  }

  return collisions.sort((a, b) => a.data.value - b.data.value)
}

import DragAndDropContainer from './DragAndDropContainer'
import {DragAndDropItem} from './DragAndDropItem'
import {DragAndDropMoveDialog} from './DragAndDropMoveDialog'
import {DragAndDropTrigger} from './DragAndDropTrigger'
import {MoveDialog} from './MoveDialog/MoveDialog'
import {MoveDialogForm} from './MoveDialog/MoveDialogForm'
import {MoveDialogTrigger} from './MoveDialog/MoveDialogTrigger'

/**
 * Wrapper for the sortable list and item components.
 */
export const DragAndDrop = Object.assign(DragAndDropContainer, {
  Item: DragAndDropItem,
  DragTrigger: DragAndDropTrigger,
  MoveDialog,
  MoveDialogForm,
  MoveDialogTrigger,
  DragAndDropMoveDialog,
})

import {
  closestCorners,
  DndContext,
  type DragEndEvent,
  type DragMoveEvent,
  DragOverlay,
  type DragStartEvent,
  type KeyboardCodes,
  type KeyboardSensorOptions,
  MouseSensor,
  TouchSensor,
  useSensor,
  useSensors,
} from '@dnd-kit/core'
import {restrictToHorizontalAxis, restrictToVerticalAxis} from '@dnd-kit/modifiers'
import {
  horizontalListSortingStrategy,
  SortableContext,
  sortableKeyboardCoordinates,
  verticalListSortingStrategy,
} from '@dnd-kit/sortable'
import {announce} from '@github-ui/aria-live'
import {ssrSafeDocument} from '@github-ui/ssr-utils'
import {testIdProps} from '@github-ui/test-id-props'
import {useClientValue} from '@github-ui/use-client-value'
import {usePrefersReducedMotion} from '@github-ui/use-prefers-reduced-motion'
import {useLocalStorage} from '@github-ui/use-safe-storage/local-storage'
import {clsx} from 'clsx'
import {type HTMLAttributes, type Ref, type RefObject, useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useState} from 'react'
import {createPortal} from 'react-dom'

import {DragAndDropContext} from '../context/DragAndDropContext'
import {useBodyClass} from '../hooks/use-body-class'
import {useRootElement} from '../hooks/use-root-element'
import {debounceAnnouncement, defaultAnnouncementsOff, successfulMoveAnnouncement} from '../utils/announcements'
import {closestVerticalCenter, CustomKeyboardSensor} from '../utils/dnd-utils'
import {DragAndDropResources} from '../utils/strings'
import type {DragAndDropDirection, DragDropMetadata, OnDropArgs} from '../utils/types'
import styles from './DragAndDropContainer.module.css'
import {DragAndDropMoveDialog} from './DragAndDropMoveDialog'
import {
  HideKeyboardSpecificInstructionsDialogKey,
  KeyboardSpecificInstructionsDialog,
} from './KeyboardSpecificInstructionsDialog'

export interface DragAndDropProps<T extends string | number, K extends {id: T; title: string}> extends Omit<
  HTMLAttributes<HTMLUListElement>,
  'onDrop'
> {
  /**
   * Array of ids and titles of the items
   */
  items: K[]
  /**
   * Direction of the list, defaults to vertical
   */
  direction?: DragAndDropDirection
  /**
   * Callback called when a drop occurs
   */
  onDrop: (args: OnDropArgs<T>) => void
  /**
   * Component type to render for drag and drop container
   */
  as?: React.ElementType
  /**
   * Render function for overlay
   */
  renderOverlay: (item: K, index: number) => React.ReactNode
  /**
   * Keyboard codes to use for drag and drop
   */
  keyboardCodes?: KeyboardCodes
  /**
   * Enabled touch support for drag and drop.
   */
  enableTouch?: boolean
}

/**
 * A container for a list of sortable items. This component is responsible for
 * managing the drag and drop state and passing it down to the items.
 *
 * @param props DragAndDropProps
 */
const DragAndDropContainer = <T extends string | number, K extends {id: T; title: string}>(
  {
    onDrop,
    items,
    direction = 'vertical',
    children,
    as: As = 'ul',
    renderOverlay,
    keyboardCodes,
    enableTouch = false,
    ...htmlULprops
  }: DragAndDropProps<T, K>,
  ref: React.Ref<HTMLElement>,
) => {
  const [isDragging, setIsDragging] = useState(false)
  const [overIndex, setOverIndex] = useState(0)
  const [hasMoved, setHasMoved] = useState(false)
  const [dragIndex, setDragIndex] = useState(0)
  const [overId, setOverId] = useState<T | null>(null)
  const [isDialogOpen, setIsDialogOpen] = useState(false)
  const [doNotShowAgain] = useLocalStorage(HideKeyboardSpecificInstructionsDialogKey, false)
  const [isKeyboardDragging, setIsKeyboardDragging] = useState(false)
  const [moveDialogItem, setMoveDialogItem] = useState<{
    title: string
    index: number
    returnFocusRef: RefObject<HTMLElement | null>
  } | null>(null)
  const itemIds = items.map(item => item.id)
  const titleMap = useMemo(() => {
    return items.reduce(
      (map, item) => {
        map[item.id] = item.title
        return map
      },
      {} as Record<T, string>,
    )
  }, [items])

  useBodyClass('is-dragging', isDragging)
  useBodyClass(styles.isKeyboardDragging ?? '', isKeyboardDragging)
  const [lastFocusedItem, setLastFocusedItem] = useState<HTMLElement | null>(null)
  const prefersReducedMotion = usePrefersReducedMotion()
  const onInstructionsClose = useCallback(() => {
    setIsDialogOpen(false)
  }, [])

  const handleDragStart = useCallback(
    (event: DragStartEvent) => {
      if (!event.active) return
      announce(DragAndDropResources.instructions(titleMap[event.active.id as T] ?? event.active.id), {
        assertive: true,
      })
      const dragMetadata = event.active.data.current?.metadata as DragDropMetadata<T>
      setOverId(dragMetadata?.id ?? null)
      setDragIndex((event.active.data.current?.sortable.index as number) ?? 0)
      setOverIndex((event.active.data.current?.sortable.index as number) ?? 0)

      setIsDragging(true)
    },
    [titleMap],
  )

  const handleDragMove = useCallback(
    (event: DragMoveEvent) => {
      const overMetadata = event.over?.data.current?.metadata as DragDropMetadata<T>
      const over = overMetadata?.id ?? null
      setOverId(over)

      if (!hasMoved) {
        setHasMoved(true)
      }

      if (event.over && event.over.data.current) {
        const positionIndex: number = event.over.data.current.sortable.index

        setOverIndex(positionIndex)
      }
    },
    [hasMoved],
  )

  /* Announce the drag item's current location when it changes */
  useEffect(() => {
    if (isDragging && hasMoved) {
      if (overIndex === 0) {
        debounceAnnouncement(DragAndDropResources.firstItemInList)
      } else if (overIndex === itemIds.length - 1) {
        debounceAnnouncement(DragAndDropResources.lastItemInList)
      } else {
        const isBefore = overIndex <= dragIndex
        const originalPlacement = overIndex === dragIndex
        const itemA = isBefore ? (itemIds[overIndex - 1] as T) : (itemIds[overIndex + 1] as T)
        const itemB = originalPlacement ? (itemIds[overIndex + 1] as T) : (itemIds[overIndex] as T)
        debounceAnnouncement(DragAndDropResources.movedBetween(titleMap[itemA], titleMap[itemB]))
      }
    }
  }, [isDragging, hasMoved, overIndex, titleMap, itemIds, dragIndex])

  const handleDragCancel = useCallback(
    (event: DragEndEvent) => {
      announce(`${titleMap[event.active.id as T] ?? event.active.id} not moved.`, {assertive: true})
      setIsDragging(false)
      setIsKeyboardDragging(false)
      setHasMoved(false)
      setTimeout(() => {
        document.activeElement?.scrollIntoView({
          behavior: prefersReducedMotion ? 'instant' : 'smooth',
          block: 'nearest',
        })
      }, 1)
    },
    [titleMap, prefersReducedMotion],
  )

  const handleDragEnd = useCallback(
