      />
    </div>
  )
}

/**
 * Recursively filters an object, replacing all leaf values with '[FILTERED]'
 * while preserving the structure (keys, nesting, array shapes).
 * The 'errors' key is preserved without filtering as it contains useful debugging information.
 */
export function filterSensitiveData(obj: unknown): unknown {
  if (obj === null || obj === undefined) {
    return obj
  }

  if (Array.isArray(obj)) {
    return obj.map(item => {
      if (item === null || item === undefined) {
        return item
      }
      if (typeof item === 'object') {
        return filterSensitiveData(item)
      }
      return '[FILTERED]'
    })
  }

  if (typeof obj === 'object') {
    const filtered: Record<string, unknown> = {}
    for (const key in obj) {
      if (Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(obj, key)) {
        const value = (obj as Record<string, unknown>)[key]
        // Preserve 'errors' key without filtering - it's useful for debugging
        if (key === 'errors') {
          filtered[key] = value
        } else if (value === null || value === undefined) {
          filtered[key] = value
        } else if (typeof value === 'object') {
          filtered[key] = filterSensitiveData(value)
        } else {
          filtered[key] = '[FILTERED]'
        }
      }
    }
    return filtered
  }

  return obj
}

import {useRef, type ReactNode} from 'react'
import type {PrunedIconButtonProps} from '../ExpandableHunkHeaderDiffLine'
import {cellIdFrom, rowIdFrom} from '../../helpers/line-helpers'
import {HunkKebabIcon} from './HunkKebabIcon'
import {VerifiedHTMLDiv} from '@github-ui/safe-html/VerifiedHTML'
import type {SafeHTMLString} from '@github-ui/safe-html'
import type {DiffAnchor} from '@github-ui/diffs/types'
import {useGridCellButtonProps} from '../../hooks/use-grid-cell-button-props'
import type {DiffLine} from '../../types'
import {lineAnchorFrom, lineOrientationFrom} from '@github-ui/diffs/diff-line-helpers'

/**
 * Renders a hunk header cell
 */
export function HunkCell({
  diffAnchor,
  diffLine,
  isSplit,
  renderHunkButton,
}: {
  diffAnchor: DiffAnchor
  diffLine: DiffLine
  isSplit: boolean
  renderHunkButton?: (additionalProps: PrunedIconButtonProps) => ReactNode | null
}) {
  const cellRef = useRef<HTMLTableCellElement>(null)
  const [cellProps, buttonProps] = useGridCellButtonProps(cellRef)
  const rowId = rowIdFrom(diffAnchor, diffLine, diffLine)
  const cellId = cellIdFrom(rowId, 0)
  const lineAnchor = lineAnchorFrom(diffAnchor, lineOrientationFrom(diffLine.type), diffLine.blobLineNumber)

  return (
    <td
      ref={cellRef}
      colSpan={4}
      data-grid-cell-id={cellId}
      data-line-anchor={lineAnchor}
      role="gridcell"
      style={{
        userSelect: 'none',
        position: 'relative',
        backgroundColor: 'var(--bgColor-accent-muted, var(--color-accent-subtle))',
        flexGrow: 1,
      }}
      tabIndex={-1}
      valign="top"
      className="diff-hunk-cell focusable-grid-cell left-side"
      {...cellProps}
    >
      <div className="d-flex flex-row">
        {renderHunkButton?.(buttonProps) ?? <HunkKebabIcon isSplit={isSplit} />}
        <code className="diff-text-cell hunk">
          {/* Explicitly mark html as safe because it is server-sanitized */}
          <VerifiedHTMLDiv
            className="diff-text-inner color-fg-muted"
            // eslint-disable-next-line @github-ui/github-monorepo/no-cast-as-safe-html-string -- HTML is sanitized server-side. TODO: Move type assertion to data ingress point.
            html={diffLine.html as SafeHTMLString}
          />
        </code>
      </div>
    </td>
  )
}

import {useMutation, useQueryClient, useQuery, keepPreviousData, useInfiniteQuery} from '@github-ui/react-query'
import type {InfiniteData} from '@github-ui/react-query'
import {
  COPILOT_SPACES_QUERY_KEY,
  COPILOT_SPACES_COUNT_QUERY_KEY,
  copilotSpacesCategoryQueryKey,
  copilotSpacesQueryKey,
  deleteCopilotSpace,
  fetchCopilotSpace,
  fetchCopilotSpaces,
  fetchMenuActions,
  fetchPaginatedCopilotSpaces,
  fetchSpacesCount,
  fetchVisibilitySettings,
  searchCopilotSpaces,
  DEFAULT_PER_PAGE,
} from '../utils/fetch'
import type {PaginatedCopilotSpacesResult} from '../utils/fetch'
import type {
  CopilotSpaceCategory,
  CopilotSpaceId,
  CopilotSpace,
  CopilotSpacePayload,
  IndexCopilotSpace,
} from '@github-ui/copilot-chat/utils/copilot-chat-types'
import {useChatManager} from '@github-ui/copilot-chat/CopilotChatManagerContext'
import {useMemo, useCallback} from 'react'
import {useChatStateValue} from '@github-ui/copilot-chat/CopilotChatContext'
import {ssrSafeWindow} from '@github-ui/ssr-utils'
import {getCopilotSpacePath} from '@github-ui/copilot-chat/utils/custom-copilots-helpers'

export class CopilotSpaceNotFoundError extends Error {
  constructor(message = 'Copilot Space not found') {
    super(message)
    this.name = 'CopilotSpaceNotFoundError'
  }
}

export class CopilotSpaceSSOError extends Error {
  protectedOrganizations: string[]
  constructor(protectedOrganizations: string[], message = 'Copilot Space SSO error') {
    super(message)
    this.protectedOrganizations = protectedOrganizations
    this.name = 'CopilotSpaceSSOError'
  }
}

export function useDeleteCopilotSpace() {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient()
  const manager = useChatManager()
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: async (item: CopilotSpaceId) => {
      const result = await deleteCopilotSpace(item)
      // Ensure result is not an error type before returning
      if (!result.ok) {
        throw new Error(result.error)
      }

      // remove the deleted space from the chat manager state
      manager.dispatch({type: 'REMOVE_CUSTOM_COPILOT', customCopilotId: item})
      // Only return the payload or a safe object
      return {status: result.status, ok: true, payload: result.payload}
    },
    onSuccess: async () => {
      await queryClient.invalidateQueries({queryKey: COPILOT_SPACES_QUERY_KEY})
    },
  })
}

/**
 * Hook for fetching custom copilots by category from the server.
 * @param category - The category of spaces to fetch (starred, user_owned, org_owned, shared_with_user)
 * @param enabled - Whether to enable the fetching
 * @param options - Additional query options
 */
export function useFetchCopilotSpacesByCategory(
  category: CopilotSpaceCategory,
  enabled: boolean,
  options: {throwOnError?: boolean} = {},
) {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: copilotSpacesCategoryQueryKey(category),
    queryFn: () => fetchCopilotSpaces(category),
    enabled,
    staleTime: 5 * 60 * 1000, // 5 minutes
    throwOnError: options.throwOnError,
  })
}

/**
 * Hook for fetching filtered custom copilots by category from the server.
 * @param category - The category of spaces to fetch (starred, user_owned, org_owned, shared_with_user)
 * @param query - The search query string to filter spaces by
 * @param enabled - Whether to enable the fetching
 * @param options - Additional query options
 */
export function useFetchFilteredCopilotSpacesByCategory(
  category: CopilotSpaceCategory,
  query: string,
  enabled: boolean,
  options: {throwOnError?: boolean} = {},
) {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: [...copilotSpacesCategoryQueryKey(category), 'searchTerm', query],
    queryFn: () => searchCopilotSpaces(category, query),
    enabled,
    staleTime: 5 * 60 * 1000, // 5 minutes
    throwOnError: options.throwOnError,
    // Keep previous search results visible while new query loads to prevent flickering
    placeholderData: keepPreviousData,
  })
}

/**
 * Hook for fetching paginated custom copilots by category using cursor-based pagination.
 * Uses useInfiniteQuery to support loading more pages via Link header cursors.
 *
 * @param category - The category of spaces to fetch (starred, user_owned, org_owned, shared_with_user)
 * @param enabled - Whether to enable the fetching
 * @param options - Additional query options
 * @param options.perPage - Number of items per page (default: 30, max: 100)
 * @param options.throwOnError - Whether to throw errors to the nearest error boundary
 * @returns Infinite query result with pages of spaces and pagination controls
 */
export function useFetchPaginatedCopilotSpacesByCategory(
  category: CopilotSpaceCategory,
  enabled: boolean,
  options: {perPage?: number; throwOnError?: boolean} = {},
) {
  const {perPage = DEFAULT_PER_PAGE, throwOnError} = options

  return useInfiniteQuery<
    PaginatedCopilotSpacesResult,
    Error,
    InfiniteData<PaginatedCopilotSpacesResult>,
    unknown[],
    string | null
  >({
    queryKey: [...copilotSpacesCategoryQueryKey(category), 'paginated', perPage],
    queryFn: async ({pageParam}) => {
      return fetchPaginatedCopilotSpaces({
        type: category,
        perPage,
        cursor: pageParam,
      })
    },
    initialPageParam: null,
    getNextPageParam: lastPage => lastPage.pagination.nextCursor,
    getPreviousPageParam: firstPage => firstPage.pagination.prevCursor,
    enabled,
    staleTime: 5 * 60 * 1000, // 5 minutes
    throwOnError,
  })
}

/**
 * Helper hook that flattens paginated spaces data into a single array.
 * Useful for components that need all loaded spaces in a flat list.
 *
 * @param paginatedData - InfiniteData from useFetchPaginatedCopilotSpacesByCategory
 * @returns Flattened array of all loaded spaces
 */
export function useFlattenedPaginatedSpaces(
  paginatedData: InfiniteData<PaginatedCopilotSpacesResult> | undefined,
): IndexCopilotSpace[] {
  return useMemo(() => {
    if (!paginatedData) return []
    return paginatedData.pages.flatMap(page => page.spaces)
  }, [paginatedData])
}

/**
 * The default hook for fetching all custom copilots from the server (without category filter).
 * @deprecated This function fetches all spaces in a single call, which doesn't support independent tab loading.
 * Use useFetchCopilotSpacesByCategory for fetching specific categories, or useManagedFetchCopilotSpaces
 * for automatic fetching of all categories with independent loading. This function may be removed in a future release.
 */
export function useFetchCopilotSpaces(enabled: boolean, options: {throwOnError?: boolean} = {}) {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: COPILOT_SPACES_QUERY_KEY,
    queryFn: () => fetchCopilotSpaces(),
    enabled,
    staleTime: 5 * 60 * 1000, // 5 minutes
    throwOnError: options.throwOnError,
  })
}

function hasCopilotSpaceData(payload: CopilotSpacePayload): payload is CopilotSpace {
  return (payload as CopilotSpace).id !== undefined
}

export function useFetchCopilotSpace(customCopilotId: CopilotSpaceId | null) {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: copilotSpacesQueryKey(customCopilotId),
    queryFn: async () => {
      const response = await fetchCopilotSpace(customCopilotId)
      if (!response.ok) {
        if (response.status === 404) {
          throw new CopilotSpaceNotFoundError()
        }
        throw new Error(response.error)
      }
      if (!hasCopilotSpaceData(response.payload)) {
        throw new CopilotSpaceSSOError(response.payload.protectedOrganizations)
      }
      return response.payload
    },
    enabled: !!customCopilotId,
  })
}

// We split up the customCopilotId because the linter wants each parameter used in the query key,
// but directly passing the whole object in seems to key on object identity and lead to lots of
// redundant API calls.
export function useFetchVisibilitySettings(copilotSpaceOwner: string, copilotSpaceNumber: number) {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: ['visibility-settings', copilotSpaceOwner, copilotSpaceNumber],
    queryFn: async () => {
      return fetchVisibilitySettings({owner: copilotSpaceOwner, id: copilotSpaceNumber})
    },
    enabled: copilotSpaceOwner !== '' && copilotSpaceNumber !== -1,
    staleTime: 5 * 60 * 1000, // 5 minutes
  })
}

export function useFetchMenuActions(copilotSpaceOwner: string, copilotSpaceNumber: number) {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: ['menu-actions', copilotSpaceOwner, copilotSpaceNumber],
    queryFn: async () => {
      return fetchMenuActions({owner: copilotSpaceOwner, id: copilotSpaceNumber})
    },
    enabled: copilotSpaceOwner !== '' && copilotSpaceNumber !== -1,
    staleTime: 5 * 60 * 1000, // 5 minutes
  })
}

/**
 * Hook for fetching the count of spaces for each category.
 * @param enabled - Whether to enable the fetching
 * @returns Query result with the spaces counts
 */
export function useFetchSpacesCount(enabled: boolean) {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: COPILOT_SPACES_COUNT_QUERY_KEY,
    queryFn: fetchSpacesCount,
    enabled,
    staleTime: 5 * 60 * 1000, // 5 minutes
  })
}

/**
 * Hook for fetching all categorized spaces with their loading states.
 * This hook fetches spaces by category and returns both the categorized data
 * and the loading state for each category.
 * @param enabled - Whether to enable the fetching of categorized spaces
 * @param options - Additional options
 * @param options.individualAccessEnabled - Whether individual access is enabled (controls user_owned and shared_with_user fetching)
 * @returns Object containing categorizedSpaces and categorizedLoading
 */
export function useFetchCategorizedSpaces(enabled: boolean, options: {individualAccessEnabled?: boolean} = {}) {
  const {individualAccessEnabled = true} = options

  const {data: starredCopilotSpaces, isLoading: isStarredCopilotSpacesLoading} = useFetchCopilotSpacesByCategory(
    'starred',
    enabled,
  )
  const {data: userOwnedCopilotSpaces, isLoading: isUserOwnedCopilotSpacesLoading} = useFetchCopilotSpacesByCategory(
    'user_owned',
    enabled && individualAccessEnabled,
  )
  const {data: orgOwnedCopilotSpaces, isLoading: isOrgOwnedCopilotSpacesLoading} = useFetchCopilotSpacesByCategory(
    'org_owned',
    enabled,
  )
  const {data: sharedWithUserCopilotSpaces, isLoading: isSharedWithUserCopilotSpacesLoading} =
    useFetchCopilotSpacesByCategory('shared_with_user', enabled && individualAccessEnabled)

  const categorizedSpaces = useMemo(
    () => ({
      starred: starredCopilotSpaces,
      userOwned: userOwnedCopilotSpaces,
      orgOwned: orgOwnedCopilotSpaces,
      sharedWithUser: sharedWithUserCopilotSpaces,
    }),
    [starredCopilotSpaces, userOwnedCopilotSpaces, orgOwnedCopilotSpaces, sharedWithUserCopilotSpaces],
  )

  const categorizedLoading = useMemo(
    () => ({
      starred: isStarredCopilotSpacesLoading,
      userOwned: isUserOwnedCopilotSpacesLoading,
      orgOwned: isOrgOwnedCopilotSpacesLoading,
      sharedWithUser: isSharedWithUserCopilotSpacesLoading,
    }),
    [
      isStarredCopilotSpacesLoading,
      isUserOwnedCopilotSpacesLoading,
      isOrgOwnedCopilotSpacesLoading,
      isSharedWithUserCopilotSpacesLoading,
    ],
  )

  return {categorizedSpaces, categorizedLoading}
}

/**
 * Hook for fetching all categorized spaces with pagination support.
 * Each category is fetched independently using cursor-based pagination.
 *
 * @param enabled - Whether to enable the fetching of categorized spaces
 * @param options - Additional options
 * @param options.perPage - Number of items per page for each category (default: 30, max: 100)
 * @param options.individualAccessEnabled - Whether individual access is enabled (controls user_owned and shared_with_user fetching)
 * @returns Object containing categorizedSpaces data, loading states, pagination states, and fetch functions
 */
export function useFetchPaginatedCategorizedSpaces(
  enabled: boolean,
  options: {perPage?: number; individualAccessEnabled?: boolean} = {},
) {
  const {perPage = DEFAULT_PER_PAGE, individualAccessEnabled = true} = options

  const starredQuery = useFetchPaginatedCopilotSpacesByCategory('starred', enabled, {perPage})
  const userOwnedQuery = useFetchPaginatedCopilotSpacesByCategory('user_owned', enabled && individualAccessEnabled, {
    perPage,
  })
  const orgOwnedQuery = useFetchPaginatedCopilotSpacesByCategory('org_owned', enabled, {perPage})
  const sharedWithUserQuery = useFetchPaginatedCopilotSpacesByCategory(
    'shared_with_user',
    enabled && individualAccessEnabled,
    {perPage},
  )

  // Flatten spaces from each category
  const starredSpaces = useFlattenedPaginatedSpaces(starredQuery.data)
  const userOwnedSpaces = useFlattenedPaginatedSpaces(userOwnedQuery.data)
  const orgOwnedSpaces = useFlattenedPaginatedSpaces(orgOwnedQuery.data)
  const sharedWithUserSpaces = useFlattenedPaginatedSpaces(sharedWithUserQuery.data)

  const categorizedSpaces = useMemo(
    () => ({
      starred: starredSpaces,
      userOwned: userOwnedSpaces,
      orgOwned: orgOwnedSpaces,
      sharedWithUser: sharedWithUserSpaces,
    }),
    [starredSpaces, userOwnedSpaces, orgOwnedSpaces, sharedWithUserSpaces],
  )

  const categorizedLoading = useMemo(
    () => ({
      starred: starredQuery.isLoading,
      userOwned: userOwnedQuery.isLoading,
      orgOwned: orgOwnedQuery.isLoading,
      sharedWithUser: sharedWithUserQuery.isLoading,
    }),
    [starredQuery.isLoading, userOwnedQuery.isLoading, orgOwnedQuery.isLoading, sharedWithUserQuery.isLoading],
  )

  const categorizedFetchingMore = useMemo(
    () => ({
      starred: starredQuery.isFetchingNextPage,
      userOwned: userOwnedQuery.isFetchingNextPage,
      orgOwned: orgOwnedQuery.isFetchingNextPage,
      sharedWithUser: sharedWithUserQuery.isFetchingNextPage,
    }),
    [
      starredQuery.isFetchingNextPage,
      userOwnedQuery.isFetchingNextPage,
      orgOwnedQuery.isFetchingNextPage,
      sharedWithUserQuery.isFetchingNextPage,
    ],
  )

  const categorizedHasMore = useMemo(
    () => ({
      starred: starredQuery.hasNextPage,
      userOwned: userOwnedQuery.hasNextPage,
      orgOwned: orgOwnedQuery.hasNextPage,
      sharedWithUser: sharedWithUserQuery.hasNextPage,
    }),
    [starredQuery.hasNextPage, userOwnedQuery.hasNextPage, orgOwnedQuery.hasNextPage, sharedWithUserQuery.hasNextPage],
  )

  const fetchMoreStarred = useCallback(() => {
    if (starredQuery.hasNextPage && !starredQuery.isFetchingNextPage) {
      void starredQuery.fetchNextPage()
    }
  }, [starredQuery])

  const fetchMoreUserOwned = useCallback(() => {
    if (userOwnedQuery.hasNextPage && !userOwnedQuery.isFetchingNextPage) {
      void userOwnedQuery.fetchNextPage()
    }
  }, [userOwnedQuery])

  const fetchMoreOrgOwned = useCallback(() => {
    if (orgOwnedQuery.hasNextPage && !orgOwnedQuery.isFetchingNextPage) {
      void orgOwnedQuery.fetchNextPage()
    }
  }, [orgOwnedQuery])

  const fetchMoreSharedWithUser = useCallback(() => {
    if (sharedWithUserQuery.hasNextPage && !sharedWithUserQuery.isFetchingNextPage) {
      void sharedWithUserQuery.fetchNextPage()
    }
  }, [sharedWithUserQuery])

  const categorizedFetchMore = useMemo(
    () => ({
      starred: fetchMoreStarred,
      userOwned: fetchMoreUserOwned,
      orgOwned: fetchMoreOrgOwned,
      sharedWithUser: fetchMoreSharedWithUser,
    }),
    [fetchMoreStarred, fetchMoreUserOwned, fetchMoreOrgOwned, fetchMoreSharedWithUser],
  )

  return {
    categorizedSpaces,
    categorizedLoading,
    categorizedFetchingMore,
    categorizedHasMore,
    categorizedFetchMore,
  }
}

/**
 * Hook for fetching all filtered categorized spaces with their loading states.
 * This hook fetches spaces by category and search term and returns both the filtered + categorised data
 * and the loading state for each category.
 * @param enabled - Whether to enable the fetching of categorized spaces
 * @param query - The search query string
 * @param options - Additional options
 * @param options.individualAccessEnabled - Whether individual access is enabled (controls user_owned and shared_with_user fetching)
 * @returns Object containing categorizedSpaces and categorizedLoading
 */
export function useFetchFilteredCategorizedSpaces(
  enabled: boolean,
  query: string,
  options: {individualAccessEnabled?: boolean} = {},
) {
  const {individualAccessEnabled = true} = options

  const {data: starredCopilotSpaces, isLoading: isStarredCopilotSpacesLoading} =
    useFetchFilteredCopilotSpacesByCategory('starred', query, enabled)
  const {data: userOwnedCopilotSpaces, isLoading: isUserOwnedCopilotSpacesLoading} =
    useFetchFilteredCopilotSpacesByCategory('user_owned', query, enabled && individualAccessEnabled)
  const {data: orgOwnedCopilotSpaces, isLoading: isOrgOwnedCopilotSpacesLoading} =
    useFetchFilteredCopilotSpacesByCategory('org_owned', query, enabled)
  const {data: sharedWithUserCopilotSpaces, isLoading: isSharedWithUserCopilotSpacesLoading} =
    useFetchFilteredCopilotSpacesByCategory('shared_with_user', query, enabled && individualAccessEnabled)
  const filteredCategorizedSpaces = useMemo(
    () => ({
      starred: starredCopilotSpaces,
      userOwned: userOwnedCopilotSpaces,
      orgOwned: orgOwnedCopilotSpaces,
      sharedWithUser: sharedWithUserCopilotSpaces,
    }),
    [starredCopilotSpaces, userOwnedCopilotSpaces, orgOwnedCopilotSpaces, sharedWithUserCopilotSpaces],
  )

  const filteredCategorizedLoading = useMemo(
    () => ({
      starred: isStarredCopilotSpacesLoading,
      userOwned: isUserOwnedCopilotSpacesLoading,
      orgOwned: isOrgOwnedCopilotSpacesLoading,
      sharedWithUser: isSharedWithUserCopilotSpacesLoading,
    }),
    [
      isStarredCopilotSpacesLoading,
      isUserOwnedCopilotSpacesLoading,
      isOrgOwnedCopilotSpacesLoading,
      isSharedWithUserCopilotSpacesLoading,
    ],
  )

  return {filteredCategorizedSpaces, filteredCategorizedLoading}
}

/**
 * Custom copilots are enabled for:
 * 1. users with the :copilot_custom_copilots feature flag, which some will be individually opted into
 * 2. The following types of users with the :copilot_custom_copilots_feature_preview feature flag:
 *    a. users with Copilot Individual (CI) licenses, for whom preview features are always enabled
 *    b. CB and CE users whose orgs have opted into preview features
 *    c. Copilot Free users, signified by `has_limited_access` on their plan
 * This values is serialized down from the copilot_chat_helper and put into the chat state.
 */
export function useCustomCopilotsEnabled() {
  return useChatStateValue('customCopilotsEnabled')
}

/**
 * Hook that returns the current number of Copilot spaces.
 *
 * @returns The total count of existing Copilot spaces
 */
export function useNumberOfSpaces(): number {
  const copilotSpaces = useChatStateValue('customCopilots')
  const copilotSpacesCount = copilotSpaces?.length ?? 0
  return copilotSpacesCount
}

/* Generates a default name based on the current number of existing spaces.
 * The default name follows the pattern "New Space {count + 1}" where count is the number of existing spaces.
 *
 * @returns the default name as a string
 */
export function useAutoSpaceName(): string {
  const copilotSpacesCount = useNumberOfSpaces()
  const defaultName = useMemo(() => `New Space ${copilotSpacesCount + 1}`, [copilotSpacesCount])
  return defaultName
}

/** Hook to generate a report URL to Github support for a Copilot Space.
 * @param selectedCopilotSpaceId - The ID of the selected Copilot Space, or null if none is selected.
 * @returns The constructed report URL or undefined if not applicable.
 */
export function useReportUrl(selectedCopilotSpaceId: CopilotSpaceId | null): string | undefined {
  const reportUrl = useMemo(() => {
    if (!selectedCopilotSpaceId) return undefined
    const {owner} = selectedCopilotSpaceId
    const host = ssrSafeWindow?.location.origin
    if (!host) return undefined

    const spaceUrl = getCopilotSpacePath(selectedCopilotSpaceId)
    const fullUrl = `${host}${spaceUrl}`
    const query = `content_url=${encodeURIComponent(fullUrl)}&report=${owner}+(user)`
    const reportTargetUrl = `${host}/contact/report-content?${query}`

    return reportTargetUrl
  }, [selectedCopilotSpaceId])

  return reportUrl
}

import {sendEvent} from '@github-ui/hydro-analytics'
import {createContext, type ReactNode, use, useMemo} from 'react'

import type {MarkdownViewMode} from '../types'

type ViewSwitchTrigger = 'view_switcher' | 'keyboard_shortcut' | 'programmatic' | 'slash_commands_hint'
type ToolbarInteractionTrigger = 'toolbar' | 'keyboard'
type AutocompleteState = 'loading' | 'empty' | 'results'
type FileUploadPhase = 'selected' | 'succeeded' | 'failed'

type SuggestionKind = 'string' | 'object' | 'null'

type HybridEditorTelemetryOptions = {
  featureFlagEnabled: boolean
  editorId?: string
  surface?: string
}

type TrackViewSwitchArgs = {
  from?: MarkdownViewMode | null
  to: MarkdownViewMode
  trigger: ViewSwitchTrigger
}

type TrackToolbarInteractionArgs = {
  control: string
  action?: string
  trigger: ToolbarInteractionTrigger
  isActive?: boolean
  keybinding?: string
  handled?: boolean
}

type TrackKeyboardShortcutArgs = {
  commandId: string
  shortcut: string
  handled: boolean
}

type TrackAutocompleteShownArgs = {
  triggerChar: string
  queryLength: number
  suggestionState: AutocompleteState
  suggestionCount?: number
}

type TrackAutocompleteSelectedArgs = {
  triggerChar: string
  insertedLength: number
  multiStep: boolean
  suggestionKind: SuggestionKind
}

type TrackFileUploadArgs = {
  phase: FileUploadPhase
  mimeType: string
  sizeBytes: number
  mediaKind?: 'image' | 'video' | 'other'
  errorType?: 'upload' | 'dimension' | 'unknown'
}

export interface HybridEditorTelemetry {
  trackViewSwitch(args: TrackViewSwitchArgs): void
  trackToolbarInteraction(args: TrackToolbarInteractionArgs): void
  trackKeyboardShortcut(args: TrackKeyboardShortcutArgs): void
  trackAutocompleteShown(args: TrackAutocompleteShownArgs): void
  trackAutocompleteSelected(args: TrackAutocompleteSelectedArgs): void
  trackFileUpload(args: TrackFileUploadArgs): void
}

const EVENT_PREFIX = 'hybrid_markdown_editor'

const noopTelemetry: HybridEditorTelemetry = {
  trackViewSwitch: () => {},
  trackToolbarInteraction: () => {},
  trackKeyboardShortcut: () => {},
  trackAutocompleteShown: () => {},
  trackAutocompleteSelected: () => {},
  trackFileUpload: () => {},
}

const HybridEditorTelemetryContext = createContext<HybridEditorTelemetry>(noopTelemetry)

export function useHybridEditorTelemetry(): HybridEditorTelemetry {
  return use(HybridEditorTelemetryContext)
}

type HybridEditorTelemetryProviderProps = {
  options: HybridEditorTelemetryOptions
  children: ReactNode
}

export function HybridEditorTelemetryProvider({options, children}: HybridEditorTelemetryProviderProps) {
  const telemetry = useMemo(() => createHybridEditorTelemetry(options), [options])

  return <HybridEditorTelemetryContext value={telemetry}>{children}</HybridEditorTelemetryContext>
}

type TelemetryPropertyValue = string | number | boolean | null | undefined

function createHybridEditorTelemetry({
  featureFlagEnabled,
  editorId,
  surface,
}: HybridEditorTelemetryOptions): HybridEditorTelemetry {
  const baseProperties: Record<string, TelemetryPropertyValue> = {
    ['feature_flag_markdown_experience_hybrid_editor']: featureFlagEnabled,
  }

  if (editorId) {
    baseProperties['editor_id'] = editorId
  }

  if (surface) {
    baseProperties.surface = surface
  }

  const sendWithBaseProperties = (eventName: string, properties: Record<string, TelemetryPropertyValue>) => {
    const payload: Record<string, TelemetryPropertyValue> = {
      ...baseProperties,
      ...properties,
    }

    try {
      sendEvent(eventName, payload)
    } catch (error) {
      if (error instanceof ReferenceError && typeof error.message === 'string' && error.message.includes('telemetry')) {
        return
      }

      throw error
    }
  }

  const trackHybridEvent = (event: string, properties: Record<string, TelemetryPropertyValue>) => {
    sendWithBaseProperties(`${EVENT_PREFIX}.${event}`, properties)
  }

  return {
    trackViewSwitch: ({from, to, trigger}) => {
      sendWithBaseProperties('markdown_editor.select_view', {
        mode: to,
        ['from_mode']: from ?? 'unknown',
        trigger,
        ['did_change']: from ? from !== to : true,
      })
    },
    trackToolbarInteraction: ({control, action, trigger, isActive, keybinding, handled}) => {
      trackHybridEvent('toolbar_interaction', {
        control,
        action,
        trigger,
        ['is_active']: isActive ?? null,
        keybinding,
        handled: handled ?? null,
      })
    },
    trackKeyboardShortcut: ({commandId, shortcut, handled}) => {
      trackHybridEvent('keyboard_shortcut', {
        command: commandId,
        shortcut,
        handled,
      })
    },
    trackAutocompleteShown: ({triggerChar, queryLength, suggestionState, suggestionCount}) => {
      trackHybridEvent('autocomplete_shown', {
        ['trigger_char']: triggerChar,
        ['query_length']: queryLength,
        ['suggestion_state']: suggestionState,
        ['suggestion_count']: suggestionCount ?? null,
      })
    },
    trackAutocompleteSelected: ({triggerChar, insertedLength, multiStep, suggestionKind}) => {
      trackHybridEvent('autocomplete_selected', {
        ['trigger_char']: triggerChar,
        ['inserted_length']: insertedLength,
        ['multi_step']: multiStep,
        ['suggestion_kind']: suggestionKind,
      })
    },
    trackFileUpload: ({phase, mimeType, sizeBytes, mediaKind, errorType}) => {
      trackHybridEvent('file_upload', {
        phase,
        ['mime_type']: mimeType,
        ['size_bytes']: sizeBytes,
        ['media_kind']: mediaKind ?? 'other',
        ['error_type']: errorType ?? null,
      })
    },
  }
}

export function createNoopHybridEditorTelemetry(): HybridEditorTelemetry {
  return noopTelemetry
}

export type {
  AutocompleteState,
  FileUploadPhase,
  HybridEditorTelemetryOptions,
  SuggestionKind,
  ToolbarInteractionTrigger,
  TrackAutocompleteSelectedArgs,
  TrackAutocompleteShownArgs,
  TrackFileUploadArgs,
  TrackKeyboardShortcutArgs,
  TrackToolbarInteractionArgs,
  TrackViewSwitchArgs,
  ViewSwitchTrigger,
}

import {clsx} from 'clsx'
import {useRef} from 'react'

import {useInlineCommentDialogModeInteractiveElements} from '../../hooks/use-inline-comment-dialog-mode-interactive-elements'
import {Annotation} from './Annotation'
import styles from './InlineAnnotation.module.css'

interface InlineAnnotationProps {
  annotation: DiffAnnotation
  gridCellId: string
  isFirstMarker?: boolean
}

/**
 * Inline wrapper for the Annotation component
 * Used within inline markers to display annotations in the context of code
 */
export function InlineAnnotation({annotation, gridCellId, isFirstMarker}: InlineAnnotationProps) {
  const annotationRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null)
  const isInDialogMode = useDialogModeIsEnabled(gridCellId)

  useInlineCommentDialogModeInteractiveElements({
    commentSubjectType: 'pull request',
    gridCellId,
    markerRef: annotationRef,
  })

  return (
    <div
      id={`annotation_${annotation.databaseId}`}
      ref={annotationRef}
      className={clsx(
        'border rounded-2 color-border-default color-shadow-small overflow-hidden',
        isFirstMarker ? 'mb-1' : 'mb-2',
        styles.inlineAnnotation,
      )}
      data-level={annotation.annotationLevel}
      data-testid={`annotation-${annotation.id}`}
      data-marker-id={`${annotation.id}`}
      // eslint-disable-next-line jsx-a11y/no-noninteractive-tabindex
      tabIndex={isInDialogMode ? 0 : -1}
    >
      <Annotation annotation={annotation} />
    </div>
  )
}

import type {AutomatedComment, Thread} from '../../conversation-types'
import type {FileKey, LineKey} from '../../types'
import {useDismissAutomatedReviewThreadMutation} from '@github-ui/pull-requests/use-dismiss-automated-review-thread-mutation'
import {useReopenAutomatedReviewThreadMutation} from '@github-ui/pull-requests/use-reopen-automated-review-thread-mutation'
import {BaseAutomatedReviewCommentActions} from '@github-ui/automated-review-comment-actions/base-automated-review-comment-actions'

export function InlineAutomatedReviewThreadActions({
  automatedComment,
  thread,
  filePath,
  lineMarkersKey,
  setErrorMessage,
  setIsCollapsed,
  safeLocalStorage,
}: {
  automatedComment: AutomatedComment
  thread: Thread
  filePath: string
  lineMarkersKey: LineKey | FileKey
  setErrorMessage: (message: string) => void
  setIsCollapsed: (isCollapsed: boolean) => void
  safeLocalStorage: {removeItem: (key: string) => void}
}) {
  const {
    mutate: dismissAutomatedReviewThreadMutation,
    isPending: dismissIsPending,
    error: dismissError,
  } = useDismissAutomatedReviewThreadMutation(filePath, lineMarkersKey)

  const handleDismissAutomatedReviewThread = (
    {reason, resolutionNote}: {reason: string; resolutionNote?: string},
    {onSuccess}: {onSuccess: () => void},
  ) => {
    dismissAutomatedReviewThreadMutation(
      {automatedReviewCommentId: automatedComment.id, threadId: thread.id, reason, resolutionNote},
      {
        onError: () => setErrorMessage(`Failed to dismiss`),
        onSuccess: () => {
          onSuccess()
          setIsCollapsed(true)
          safeLocalStorage.removeItem(`reviewThreadIsCollapsed_${thread.id}`)
        },
      },
    )
  }

  const {mutate: reopenAutomatedReviewThreadMutation, isPending: reopenIsPending} =
    useReopenAutomatedReviewThreadMutation(filePath, lineMarkersKey)
  const handleReopenAutomatedReviewThread = () => {
    reopenAutomatedReviewThreadMutation(
      {automatedReviewCommentId: automatedComment.id, threadId: thread.id},
      {
        onError: () => setErrorMessage(`Failed to reopen`),
        onSuccess: () => {
          setIsCollapsed(false)
          safeLocalStorage.removeItem(`reviewThreadIsCollapsed_${thread.id}`)
        },
      },
    )
  }

  return (
    <BaseAutomatedReviewCommentActions
      automatedComment={automatedComment}
      dismissError={dismissError}
      isButtonDisabled={dismissIsPending || reopenIsPending}
      onDismiss={handleDismissAutomatedReviewThread}
      onReopen={handleReopenAutomatedReviewThread}
      className="p-2 border-top"
    />
  )
}

  [DiffAnnotationLevels.Failure]: 0,

  [DiffAnnotationLevels.Notice]: 2,
}

export interface InlineMarkersProps extends Pick<InlineReviewThreadProps, 'batchingEnabled' | 'viewerData'> {
  annotations: DiffAnnotation[]
  cellRef: RefObject<HTMLTableCellElement | null>
  dispatchMarkersStatus: React.Dispatch<MarkersStatusAction>
  conversationListThreads: Thread[]
  fileAnchor?: string
  filePath: string
  gridCellId: string
  lineMarkersKey: LineKey
  gutterSizeOffset: string
  inlineMarkersRef: RefObject<HTMLDivElement | null>
  isRowSelected: boolean
  diffLine: DiffLine
  lineType: DiffLineType
  onCloseConversationList: () => void
  onCloseFocusMode: () => void
  onDeleteLineComment: () => void
  shouldListenForEscapeKey?: boolean
  shouldStartNewConversationWithSuggestedChange?: boolean
  threadsConnectionId?: string
  ghostUser?: CommentAuthor
  children?: React.ReactNode
}

export function InlineMarkers(props: InlineMarkersProps) {
  return (
    <DialogStateProvider>
      <InlineMarkersInternal {...props} />
    </DialogStateProvider>
  )
}

function InlineMarkersInternal({
  annotations,
  cellRef,
  diffLine,
  dispatchMarkersStatus,
  children,
  conversationListThreads,
  inlineMarkersRef,
  isRowSelected,
  lineMarkersKey,
  fileAnchor,
  filePath,
  gridCellId,
  gutterSizeOffset,
  lineType,
  onCloseConversationList,
  onCloseFocusMode,
  onDeleteLineComment,
  threadsConnectionId,
  shouldStartNewConversationWithSuggestedChange,
  ghostUser,
  shouldListenForEscapeKey,
  ...rest
}: InlineMarkersProps) {
  const isInDialogMode = useDialogModeIsEnabled(gridCellId)
  const {disableDialogMode, enableDialogMode} = useDialogModeActions()
  const enterDialogMode = useEnterDialogMode(gridCellId, cellRef, dispatchMarkersStatus)
  const suggestedChangesConfig = useSuggestedChanges(diffLine, shouldStartNewConversationWithSuggestedChange)
  // Create a ref for the markers container
  const markersContainerRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null)

  // Sort annotations by level: Failure > Warning > Notice

  const sortedAnnotations = useMemo(() => {
    return [...annotations].sort((a, b) => {
      return annotationLevelPriority[a.annotationLevel] - annotationLevelPriority[b.annotationLevel]
    })
  }, [annotations])

  const markers: Array<Thread | GenericMarkerNavEl> = useMemo(() => {
    if (children) {
      return [...conversationListThreads, {id: 'new-comment'} as GenericMarkerNavEl, ...sortedAnnotations]
    }

    return [...conversationListThreads, ...sortedAnnotations]
  }, [children, conversationListThreads, sortedAnnotations])

  // prevent events bubbling up to the parent elements (e.g Cell, DiffFileHeader, etc...)
  // Note: we do need the bubble up the onKeyDown event, since that exits dialog mode from the cell
  const handleStopPropagation = (event: React.MouseEvent | React.KeyboardEvent | React.FocusEvent) =>
    event.stopPropagation()

  const handleOnKeyDown = (e: React.KeyboardEvent) => {
    const eventTargetNode = e.target as Node
    const eventTargetElement = e.target as HTMLElement
    const closestGridCell = eventTargetElement.closest(`[data-grid-cell-id=${gridCellId}]`) as HTMLElement | undefined
    // Handle escape press on the row
    // eslint-disable-next-line @github-ui/ui-commands/no-manual-shortcut-logic
    if (shouldListenForEscapeKey && closestGridCell?.contains(eventTargetNode) && e?.key === 'Escape') {
      onCloseFocusMode()
      disableDialogMode()
      closestGridCell?.focus()
      return
    }
    //when the user has the final element in the focus loop focused and hits tab, we want to wrap them back
    //around to the beginning and focus the first element in the loop, which is the collapse button
    if (
      closestGridCell?.contains(eventTargetNode) &&
      eventTargetElement.getAttribute('data-exit-dialog-mode-button') === 'true' &&
      !e?.shiftKey &&
      // eslint-disable-next-line @github-ui/ui-commands/no-manual-shortcut-logic
      e?.key === 'Tab'
    ) {
      //prevent the default behavior and instead wrap around to focus the first collapse button
      e.preventDefault()
      ;(closestGridCell?.querySelector('[data-is-first-collapse-button="true"]') as HTMLElement)?.focus()
      return
    }

    //when the user has the first element in the focus loop focused and hits shift+tab, we want to wrap them back
    //around to the end and focus the last element in the loop, which is the exit dialog button
    if (
      closestGridCell?.contains(eventTargetNode) &&
      eventTargetElement.getAttribute('data-is-first-collapse-button') === 'true' &&
      e?.shiftKey &&
      // eslint-disable-next-line @github-ui/ui-commands/no-manual-shortcut-logic
      e?.key === 'Tab'
    ) {
      //prevent the default behavior and instead wrap around to focus the close button
      e.preventDefault()
      ;(closestGridCell?.querySelector('[data-exit-dialog-mode-button="true"]') as HTMLElement)?.focus()
      return
    }
  }

  const handleOnFocus = (event: React.FocusEvent) => {
    handleStopPropagation(event)
    if (!isInDialogMode) {
      enableDialogMode(gridCellId)
    }
  }

  const handleClickOutside = useCallback(
    (e: MouseEvent) => {
      if (!e.target) return
      if (!isInDialogMode) return

      const targetIsNotInsideInlineMarkersDialog = !(e.target as HTMLElement).closest('[data-inline-markers]')

      if (targetIsNotInsideInlineMarkersDialog) {
        disableDialogMode()
      }
    },
    [disableDialogMode, isInDialogMode],
  )

  useEffect(() => {
    ssrSafeDocument?.addEventListener('mousedown', handleClickOutside)
    return () => ssrSafeDocument?.removeEventListener('mousedown', handleClickOutside)
  }, [handleClickOutside])

  const selectedMarkerId = useSelectedMarkerId()
  const selectedMarkerIdRef = useRef<string | null>(null)

  /**
   * If navigating to a review comment or annotation, ensure the marker is visible
   * by entering dialog mode. This handles both initial page load with a hash
   * and navigation from other UI elements like ThreadPreview.
   */
  useEffect(() => {
    if (selectedMarkerId && selectedMarkerIdRef.current !== selectedMarkerId) {
      const threadIds = conversationListThreads.map(l => l.id)
      const annotationsIds = annotations.map(l => l.id)

      if (threadIds.includes(selectedMarkerId) || annotationsIds.includes(selectedMarkerId)) {
        selectedMarkerIdRef.current = selectedMarkerId
        enterDialogMode({shouldFocusFirstMarker: false}, selectedMarkerId)
      }
    }
  }, [selectedMarkerId, enterDialogMode, conversationListThreads, annotations])

  // Use our marker navigation hook
  useMarkerNavigation({
    containerRef: markersContainerRef,
    markers,
    disabled: !isInDialogMode, // Only enable marker navigation when in dialog mode
  })

  return (
    // eslint-disable-next-line jsx-a11y/no-static-element-interactions
    <div
      className={clsx('d-flex pt-1', isInDialogMode ? styles.markersDialogActive : '')}
      style={{marginRight: `-${gutterSizeOffset}`}}
      aria-hidden={isInDialogMode ? 'false' : 'true'}
      onFocus={handleOnFocus}
      onClick={handleStopPropagation}
      onKeyDown={handleOnKeyDown}
      onKeyUp={handleStopPropagation}
      onMouseDown={handleStopPropagation}
      onMouseUp={handleStopPropagation}
      data-inline-markers
      ref={inlineMarkersRef}
    >
      <div className={styles.markersWrapper} ref={markersContainerRef}>
        {conversationListThreads.map((thread, i) => (
          <div
            key={`review-thread-${thread.id}`}
            className={clsx(
              'mt-1 border rounded-2 color-border-default color-shadow-small',
              i === markers.length - 1 ? 'mb-1' : 'mb-2',
            )}
            data-first-marker={i === 0}
            data-marker-id={`${thread.id}`} // Add data-marker-id attribute for keyboard navigation
            // eslint-disable-next-line jsx-a11y/no-noninteractive-tabindex
            tabIndex={isInDialogMode ? 0 : -1} // Make the marker focusable
          >
            <InlineReviewThread
              threadPositionNumber={i + 1}
              isFirstThread={i === 0}
              filePath={filePath}
              lineMarkersKey={lineMarkersKey}
              fileAnchor={fileAnchor}
              gridCellId={gridCellId}
              onDeleteLineComment={onDeleteLineComment}
              threadId={thread.id}
              threadsConnectionId={threadsConnectionId}
              suggestedChangesConfig={suggestedChangesConfig}
              ghostUser={ghostUser}
              {...rest}
            />
          </div>
        ))}
        {children}
        {sortedAnnotations.map((annotation, i) => {
          const isFirstMarker = i === 0 && annotation === markers[0]
          return (
            <InlineAnnotation
              key={`annotation-${annotation.id}`}
              annotation={annotation}
              gridCellId={gridCellId}
              isFirstMarker={isFirstMarker}
            />
          )
        })}
      </div>
      {isInDialogMode && (
        <IconButton
          icon={UndoIcon}
          className={clsx(
            styles.closeMarkersDialogButton,
            'ml-2 position-relative',
            conversationListThreads.length === 0 ? 'mt-2' : 'mt-1',
          )}
          aria-label="Return to code"
          data-exit-dialog-mode-button={'true'}
          variant="default"
          size="small"
          // Using onMouseUp instead of onClick to custom handle onKeyDown events
          onMouseUp={() => {
            onCloseFocusMode()
            disableDialogMode()
            cellRef.current?.focus()
          }}
          onKeyDown={e => {
            // eslint-disable-next-line @github-ui/ui-commands/no-manual-shortcut-logic
            if (e.key === 'Enter' || e.code === 'Space') {
              onCloseFocusMode()
              disableDialogMode()
              cellRef.current?.focus()
            }
          }}
        />
      )}
    </div>
  )
}

import {ariaLabelDate} from '@github-ui/aria-label-date'
import type {LineRange} from '@github-ui/diffs/types'
import {ErrorBoundary} from '@github-ui/react-core/error-boundary'
import safeStorage from '@github-ui/safe-storage'
import {useAnalytics} from '@github-ui/use-analytics'
import {ChevronDownIcon, ChevronRightIcon, StopIcon} from '@primer/octicons-react'
import {Button, Flash, IconButton, Label, Spinner} from '@primer/react'
import {clsx} from 'clsx'
import type {PropsWithChildren} from 'react'
import {Suspense, useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState} from 'react'

import {CommentErrorFallback} from './CommentErrorFallback'
import {useInlineCommentDialogModeInteractiveElements} from '../../hooks/use-inline-comment-dialog-mode-interactive-elements'
import type {
  CommentAuthor,
  ConfigureSuggestedChangesImplementation,
  Thread,
  ThreadPositioning,
} from '../../conversation-types'
import {AutomatedCommentSource, CommentReviewVariantType} from '../../conversation-types'
import styles from './InlineReviewThread.module.css'
import type {ReviewThreadProps} from './ReviewThread'
import {ReviewThread} from './ReviewThread'
import {useDialogModeActions, useDialogModeIsEnabled} from '../../stores/selectors'
import {useThread} from '@github-ui/pull-requests/pull-request-store-selectors'
import {useResolveThreadMutation} from '@github-ui/pull-requests/use-resolve-thread-mutation'
import {useUnresolveThreadMutation} from '@github-ui/pull-requests/use-unresolve-thread-mutation'
import type {LineKey} from '../../types'
import {InlineAutomatedReviewThreadActions} from './InlineAutomatedReviewThreadActions'
import {isLoggedIn} from '@github-ui/client-env'

function Emphasis({children}: PropsWithChildren) {
  return <span className="text-semibold fgColor-default">{children}</span>
}

function ThreadBanner({thread}: {thread?: Thread}) {
  if (typeof thread === 'undefined') return null

  const positioning = thread.positioning ?? thread.subject // support both for backwards compatability

  if (typeof positioning === 'undefined') return null

  if (!positioning.endDiffSide || !positioning.endLine) return null

  if (!positioning.startLine || !positioning.startDiffSide || positioning.startLine === positioning.endLine) {
    return SingleLineThreadBanner({positioning})
  } else {
    return MultineLineThreadBanner({positioning})
  }
}

function SingleLineThreadBanner({positioning}: {positioning: Omit<ThreadPositioning, 'isOutdated' | 'path'>}) {
  const diffSideLetter = positioning.endDiffSide === 'LEFT' ? 'L' : 'R'
  return (
    <h2 className={styles.inlineReviewThreadHeading}>
      Comment on line{' '}
      <Emphasis>
        {diffSideLetter}
        {positioning.endLine ?? ''}
      </Emphasis>
    </h2>
  )
}

function MultineLineThreadBanner({positioning}: {positioning: Omit<ThreadPositioning, 'isOutdated' | 'path'>}) {
  const startSideString = positioning.startDiffSide === 'LEFT' ? 'L' : 'R'
  const endSideString = positioning.endDiffSide === 'LEFT' ? 'L' : 'R'
  return (
    <h2 className={styles.inlineReviewThreadHeading}>
      Comment on lines{' '}
      <Emphasis>
        {startSideString}
        {positioning.startLine ?? ''}
      </Emphasis>{' '}
      to{' '}
      <Emphasis>
        {endSideString}
        {positioning.endLine ?? ''}
      </Emphasis>
    </h2>
  )
}

export interface InlineReviewThreadProps extends Pick<ReviewThreadProps, 'batchingEnabled' | 'viewerData'> {
  threadId: string
  fileAnchor?: string
  filePath: string
  lineMarkersKey: LineKey
  gridCellId: string
  onDeleteLineComment: () => void
  threadsConnectionId?: string
  ghostUser?: CommentAuthor
  isFirstThread?: boolean
  threadPositionNumber?: number
  suggestedChangesConfig?: ConfigureSuggestedChangesImplementation
}

/**
 * The InlineReviewThread opens from the ActionBar when the user selects a conversation.
 */
export function InlineReviewThread({
  fileAnchor,
  filePath,
  gridCellId,
  onDeleteLineComment,
  threadId,
  threadPositionNumber,
  lineMarkersKey,
  threadsConnectionId,
  isFirstThread,
  ghostUser,
  suggestedChangesConfig,
  ...rest
}: InlineReviewThreadProps) {
  const thread = useThread(threadId, filePath, lineMarkersKey)

  const {mutate: resolveThreadMutation} = useResolveThreadMutation(filePath, lineMarkersKey)
  const handleResolveThread = () => {
    if (!thread) return

    resolveThreadMutation(
      {threadId: thread.id},
      {
        onError: () => setErrorMessage('Failed to resolve thread'),
        onSuccess: () => {
          setIsCollapsed(true)
          safeLocalStorage.removeItem(`reviewThreadIsCollapsed_${threadId}`)
        },
      },
    )

    sendAnalyticsEvent('comments.resolve_thread', 'RESOLVE_CONVERSATION_BUTTON')
  }

  const reviewThreadRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null)
  const safeLocalStorage = safeStorage('localStorage')
  const isResolved = thread?.isResolved
  const [errorMessage, setErrorMessage] = useState('')
  const [isCollapsed, setIsCollapsed] = useState<boolean>(isResolved ?? false)
  const isInDialogMode = useDialogModeIsEnabled(gridCellId)
  const {disableDialogMode, enableDialogMode} = useDialogModeActions()

  useEffect(() => {
    const storedState = localStorage.getItem(`reviewThreadIsCollapsed_${threadId}`)
    if (storedState !== null) {
      setIsCollapsed(JSON.parse(storedState))
    } else if (isResolved) {
      setIsCollapsed(true)
    }
  }, [isResolved, threadId])

  const handleToggleCollapsed = () => {
    window.requestAnimationFrame(() => {
      safeLocalStorage.setItem(`reviewThreadIsCollapsed_${threadId}`, JSON.stringify(!isCollapsed))
    })
    setIsCollapsed((prevIsCollapsed: boolean) => !prevIsCollapsed)
  }

  useInlineCommentDialogModeInteractiveElements({
    commentSubjectType: 'pull request',
    gridCellId,
    markerRef: reviewThreadRef,
    isThread: true,
    thread,
  })

  const config = useMemo(() => {
    const positioning = thread?.positioning ?? thread?.subject // support both for backwards compatibility in the interim

    if (!positioning) {
      return undefined
    }

    if (
      fileAnchor &&
      thread &&
      positioning.startDiffSide &&
      positioning.endDiffSide &&
      positioning.startLine &&
      positioning.endLine &&
      suggestedChangesConfig?.configureSuggestedChangesFromLineRange
    ) {
      const startLine = positioning.startLine ?? positioning.endLine
      const startDiffSide = positioning.startDiffSide ?? positioning.endDiffSide

      const threadRowRange: LineRange = {
        diffAnchor: fileAnchor,
        endLineNumber: positioning.endLine,
        endOrientation: positioning.endDiffSide === 'LEFT' ? 'left' : 'right',
        startLineNumber: startLine,
        startOrientation: startDiffSide === 'LEFT' ? 'left' : 'right',
        firstSelectedLineNumber: startLine,
        firstSelectedOrientation: startDiffSide === 'LEFT' ? 'left' : 'right',
      }
      return suggestedChangesConfig?.configureSuggestedChangesFromLineRange(threadRowRange)
    }

    return undefined
  }, [fileAnchor, thread, suggestedChangesConfig])

  const {sendAnalyticsEvent} = useAnalytics()

  const {mutate: unresolveThreadMutation} = useUnresolveThreadMutation(filePath, lineMarkersKey)
  const handleUnresolveThread = () => {
    if (!thread) return

    unresolveThreadMutation(
      {threadId: thread.id},
      {
        onError: () => setErrorMessage('Failed to unresolve thread'),
        onSuccess: () => {
          setIsCollapsed(false)
          safeLocalStorage.removeItem(`reviewThreadIsCollapsed_${thread.id}`)
        },
      },
    )

    sendAnalyticsEvent('comments.unresolve_thread', 'RESOLVE_CONVERSATION_BUTTON')
  }

  const isAutomatedReviewThread =
    thread?.commentsData?.comments[0]?.reviewVariantType === CommentReviewVariantType.Automated
  const automatedComment = isAutomatedReviewThread ? thread.commentsData?.comments[0]?.automatedComment : null
  const dismissableAutomatedComment = automatedComment && automatedComment.dismissable
  const licenseComplianceAutomatedComment =
    automatedComment && automatedComment.source === AutomatedCommentSource.LicenseCompliance

  if (!thread) return null

  const hasComments = (thread.commentsData?.comments?.length ?? 0) > 0

  if (!hasComments) return null

  const isThreadResolved = !!thread.isResolved
  const userCanResolve = isLoggedIn()

  const isResolveable =
    userCanResolve && thread.commentsData?.comments?.some(comment => comment.state?.toUpperCase() !== 'PENDING')

  const createdAt = thread.commentsData?.comments?.[0]?.createdAt
  const commentAuthorLogin = thread.commentsData?.comments?.[0]?.author?.login ?? 'Unknown Author'

  let resolveButtonLabel = `${isResolved ? 'Unresolve' : 'Resolve'} comment`
  if (createdAt) {
    const createdAtFormatted = ariaLabelDate(new Date(createdAt))
    resolveButtonLabel = `${
      isResolved ? 'Unresolve' : 'Resolve'
    } ${commentAuthorLogin}'s comment, ${createdAtFormatted}`
  }

  return (
    <ErrorBoundary boundaryName="InlineReviewThread" fallback={<CommentErrorFallback />}>
      <div className="rounded-2 bgColor-default" ref={reviewThreadRef}>
        <div className={clsx('px-1', styles.ReviewThreadContainer)}>
          <IconButton
            aria-label={isCollapsed ? 'Expand comment' : 'Collapse comment'}
            icon={isCollapsed ? ChevronRightIcon : ChevronDownIcon}
            size="small"
            variant="invisible"
            onClick={handleToggleCollapsed}
            data-is-first-collapse-button={isFirstThread}
          />
          <ThreadBanner thread={thread} />
          <div className={styles.ResolvableContainer}>
            {thread.positioning?.isOutdated && <Label variant="secondary">Outdated</Label>}

            {isThreadResolved && !isAutomatedReviewThread && <Label variant="secondary">Resolved</Label>}
            {automatedComment?.isDismissed && <Label variant="secondary">Dismissed</Label>}
            {automatedComment?.isFixed && <Label variant="secondary">Fixed</Label>}
          </div>
        </div>
        <Suspense
          fallback={
            <div className={styles.ThreadLoading}>
              <Spinner />
              <p>Loading comments</p>
            </div>
          }
        >
          {errorMessage && (
            <Flash variant="danger" className="m-2">
              <StopIcon className="mr-2" />
              {errorMessage}
            </Flash>
          )}
          {!isCollapsed && (
            <>
              <ReviewThread
                lineMarkersKey={lineMarkersKey}
                disableDialogMode={disableDialogMode}
                enableDialogMode={enableDialogMode}
                filePath={filePath}
                gridCellId={gridCellId}
                onDeleteLineComment={onDeleteLineComment}
                isInlineComment
                isInDialogMode={isInDialogMode}
                thread={thread}
                threadPositionNumber={threadPositionNumber}
                threadsConnectionId={threadsConnectionId}
                suggestedChangesConfig={config}
                shouldLimitHeight={false}
                {...rest}
                ghostUser={ghostUser}
              />
              {isResolveable && !dismissableAutomatedComment && !licenseComplianceAutomatedComment && (
                <div className="d-flex flex-items-center p-2 border-top flex-wrap">
                  <Button
                    aria-label={resolveButtonLabel}
                    onClick={isThreadResolved ? handleUnresolveThread : handleResolveThread}
                    className="mr-2"
                  >
                    {isResolved ? 'Unresolve comment' : 'Resolve comment'}
                  </Button>
                  {isThreadResolved && thread.resolvedBy && (
                    <span className="fgColor-muted f6">
                      <b>{thread.resolvedBy}</b> marked this comment as resolved
                    </span>
                  )}
                </div>
              )}
              {dismissableAutomatedComment && (
                <InlineAutomatedReviewThreadActions
                  automatedComment={automatedComment}
                  thread={thread}
                  filePath={filePath}
                  lineMarkersKey={lineMarkersKey}
                  setErrorMessage={setErrorMessage}
                  setIsCollapsed={setIsCollapsed}
                  safeLocalStorage={safeLocalStorage}
                />
              )}
            </>
          )}
        </Suspense>
      </div>
    </ErrorBoundary>
  )
}

import type {KeyboardCodes} from '@dnd-kit/core'
import {useLocalStorage} from '@github-ui/use-safe-storage/local-storage'
import {Button, Checkbox, FormControl} from '@primer/react'
import type {DialogProps} from '@primer/react/experimental'
import {Dialog, KeybindingHint} from '@primer/react/experimental'
import {clsx} from 'clsx'
import {Fragment} from 'react'

import {defaultKeyboardCodes} from '../utils/dnd-utils'
import {DragAndDropResources} from '../utils/strings'
import type {DragAndDropDirection} from '../utils/types'
import styles from './KeyboardSpecificInstructionsDialog.module.css'

type KeyboardSpecificInstructionsDialogProps = {
  /**
   * Whether the dialog is open
   */
  isOpen: boolean
  /**
   * Callback called when the dialog is closed
   */
  onClose: () => void
  /**
   * The direction of the list
   */
  direction: DragAndDropDirection
  /**
   * Optional. Custom keyboard codes to use for drag and drop
   */
  keyboardCodes?: KeyboardCodes
}

type KeyboardSpecificInstructionsFooterProps = Pick<KeyboardSpecificInstructionsDialogProps, 'onClose'> & DialogProps

/*
 * Key used to store the user preference to stop showing the keyboard instructions dialog
 */
export const HideKeyboardSpecificInstructionsDialogKey = 'hideKeyboardSpecificInstructions'

const KeyboardSpecificInstructionsFooter = ({onClose}: KeyboardSpecificInstructionsFooterProps) => {
  const [doNotShowAgain, setDoNotShowAgain] = useLocalStorage(HideKeyboardSpecificInstructionsDialogKey, false)
  return (
    <div className={clsx(styles.footerContainer)}>
      <div className={clsx(styles.footerForm)}>
        <FormControl>
          <Checkbox checked={doNotShowAgain} onChange={() => setDoNotShowAgain(!doNotShowAgain)} />
          <FormControl.Label>{`Don't show this again`}</FormControl.Label>
        </FormControl>
      </div>
      <div className={clsx(styles.footerButton)}>
        <Button onClick={onClose}>Close</Button>
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

/* Dialog component that displays keyboard instructions to perform drag and drop actions.
 * Using the Primer Dialog component.
 *
 * @param props KeyboardSpecificInstructionsDialogProps
 */
export const KeyboardSpecificInstructionsDialog = ({
  isOpen,
  onClose,
  direction,
  keyboardCodes = defaultKeyboardCodes,
}: KeyboardSpecificInstructionsDialogProps) => {
  if (!isOpen) return null

  const onKeyDown = (e: React.KeyboardEvent) => {
    e.stopPropagation()
    if (e.code === 'Escape') {
      onClose()
    }
  }

  return (
    // eslint-disable-next-line jsx-a11y/no-static-element-interactions
    <div onKeyDown={onKeyDown}>
      <Dialog
        title="How to move objects via keyboard"
        subtitle="This navigation is only available when move mode is activated."
        onClose={onClose}
        renderFooter={() => (
          <Dialog.Footer className="d-flex">
            <KeyboardSpecificInstructionsFooter onClose={onClose} />
          </Dialog.Footer>
        )}
      >
        <table className={clsx(styles.instructionContainer)}>
          <thead className="sr-only">
            <tr>
              <th className="text=bold">Action</th>
              <th className="text=bold">Keyboard Shortcut</th>
            </tr>
          </thead>
          <tbody>
            <tr className={clsx(styles.instruction)}>
              <td>{DragAndDropResources.cancelDrag}</td>
              <td>
                {keyboardCodes.cancel.map((key, index) => (
                  <Fragment key={key}>
                    {index > 0 && <span> / </span>}
                    <KeybindingHint keys={key} />
                  </Fragment>
                ))}
              </td>
            </tr>
            <tr className={clsx(styles.instruction)}>
              <td>{DragAndDropResources.movePosition}</td>
              <td>
                <KeybindingHint keys={direction === 'vertical' ? 'up' : 'left'} />
                <span> / </span>
                <KeybindingHint keys={direction === 'vertical' ? 'down' : 'right'} />
              </td>
            </tr>
            <tr className={clsx(styles.instruction, 'border-bottom-0')}>
              <td>{DragAndDropResources.endDrag}</td>
              <td>
                {keyboardCodes.end.map((key, index) => (
                  <Fragment key={key}>
                    {index > 0 && <span> / </span>}
                    <kbd className="bgColor-default box-shadow-none fgColor-muted">{key.toLowerCase()}</kbd>
                  </Fragment>
                ))}
              </td>
            </tr>
          </tbody>
        </table>
      </Dialog>
    </div>
  )
}

/***
 * STATUS: Experimental - This component is not ready for production
 * The Accessibility Team is actively working on this component.
 * Please reach out to #accessibility with any questions.
 */

import {announce} from '@github-ui/aria-live'
import {testIdProps} from '@github-ui/test-id-props'
import {AlertIcon, InfoIcon, TriangleDownIcon} from '@primer/octicons-react'
import {
  Button,
  Flash,
  FormControl,
  SelectPanel,
  type SelectPanelItemInput,
  type SelectPanelProps,
  useOnOutsideClick,
} from '@primer/react'
import {Dialog} from '@primer/react/experimental'
import {clsx} from 'clsx'
import {type FormEvent, useId, useRef, useState} from 'react'

import {DragAndDropResources} from '../../utils/strings'
import styles from './MoveDialog.module.css'
import type {FormProps} from './MoveDialogForm'
import {MoveDialogForm} from './MoveDialogForm'

const MOVE_DIALOG_TITLE_ID = 'move-dialog-title'
export interface MoveDialogBaseProps {
  /**
   * The props for the form that is rendered in the dialog
   */
  formProps: FormProps
  /**
   * Callback to close the dialog
   */
  closeDialog: () => void
  /**
   * Optional: label for the submit button
   */
  submitButtonLabel?: string
  /**
   *  Optional: The label for the dialog
   */
  title?: string
  /**
   *  Optional: Callback to submit the dialog
   */
  onSubmit?: (event: FormEvent) => void | Promise<void>
  /**
   * Optional: The ref to return focus to when the dialog is closed
   */
  returnFocusRef?: React.RefObject<HTMLElement | null>
}

export interface MoveDialogPropsSingleSelect {
  /**
   * The selected item that the user is moving
   */
  selectedItem: {
    value: string
  }
  multiSelectItems?: false
}

export interface MoveDialogPropsMultiSelect {
  multiSelectItems: true
  /**
   * Note that the selected items may be derived from the select panel's "selected" props
   */
  selectPanelProps: SelectPanelProps
}

export type MoveDialogProps = MoveDialogBaseProps & (MoveDialogPropsSingleSelect | MoveDialogPropsMultiSelect)

/**
 * A dialog that allows the user to move an item to a new position in the list.
 *
 * @param props MoveDialogProps
 */
export const MoveDialog = ({
  closeDialog,
  onSubmit: onSubmitProp,
  formProps,
  title,
  submitButtonLabel,
  returnFocusRef,
  ...rest
}: MoveDialogProps) => {
  // TODO: pass setIsInvalid to the form
  const [isInvalidOption, setIsInvalidOption] = useState(false)
  const [showErrorDialog, setShowErrorDialog] = useState(false)

  let singleSelectedItemValue = ''
  let firstSelectedMultiselectItem: SelectPanelItemInput | undefined

  // We can't destructure `multiSelectItems` out of `rest` in the props type signature because TS then can't figure out if
  // it should expect `selectedItems` or `selectedItem` in the resulting object without type coercion.
  if (!rest.multiSelectItems) {
    singleSelectedItemValue = rest.selectedItem.value
  } else {
    const initiallySelectedItems = rest.selectPanelProps.selected as SelectPanelItemInput[]
    firstSelectedMultiselectItem = initiallySelectedItems[0]
  }
  const [helperText, setHelperText] = useState('')

  const helperTextId = useId()

  const containerRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null)

  // Dialog doesn't use Overlay or call `useOnOutsideClick` under the hood. This means that
  // it doesn't get registered as an overlay in the stack, which means it doesn't intercept
  // and stop propagating clicks. This means that if there are any open `Overlay`s underneath
  // this `Dialog`, clicks in this dialog will register as clicks outside of those overlays,
  // causing them to try to close.
  // We can prevent this by registering a fake `useOnClickOutside` hook to make this look like
  // a regular `Overlay`. Clicks inside will no-op and the hook will prevent them from propagating
  // to other overlays; clicks outside need to have `preventDefault` called on them to stop them
  // from propagating.
  useOnOutsideClick({containerRef, onClickOutside: e => e.preventDefault()})

  const onSubmit = async (event: FormEvent) => {
    event.preventDefault() // prevent page reload
    // checkVal
    if (isInvalidOption) {
      const invalidField = containerRef.current?.querySelector('[aria-invalid="true"]')
      if (invalidField) (invalidField as HTMLInputElement).focus()
      return
    }
    await onSubmitProp?.(event)
  }

  const onSelectedChange = (selected: SelectPanelItemInput[]) => {
    if (!rest.multiSelectItems) return
    if (selected[0] !== firstSelectedMultiselectItem) {
      setShowErrorDialog(true)
    } else {
      rest.selectPanelProps.onSelectedChange?.(selected as (SelectPanelItemInput | undefined) & SelectPanelItemInput[])
    }
  }

  const renderAnchor = (buttonProps: React.HTMLAttributes<HTMLElement>) => {
    const {children, ...otherProps} = buttonProps
    return (
      <Button trailingAction={TriangleDownIcon} block {...otherProps}>
        {children}
      </Button>
    )
  }

  let selectPanelProps: SelectPanelProps
  if (rest.multiSelectItems) {
    selectPanelProps = {
      ...rest.selectPanelProps,
      selected: rest.selectPanelProps.selected as SelectPanelItemInput[],
      onSelectedChange,
      renderAnchor,
    }
  }

  return (
    <>
      <Dialog
        title={
          <span className={clsx(styles.title)} id={MOVE_DIALOG_TITLE_ID} {...testIdProps(MOVE_DIALOG_TITLE_ID)}>
            {title ?? (rest.multiSelectItems ? 'Move selected items' : 'Move selected item')}
          </span>
        }
        onClose={() => {
          closeDialog()
          if (!rest.multiSelectItems) {
            announce(DragAndDropResources.cancelMove(singleSelectedItemValue), {assertive: true})
          } else {
            const selectedItems = rest.selectPanelProps.selected as SelectPanelItemInput[]
            announce(
              DragAndDropResources.cancelMoveMultiple(selectedItems[0]?.text ?? '', selectedItems?.length ?? 0),
              {
                assertive: true,
              },
            )
          }
        }}
        width="large"
        ref={containerRef}
        renderBody={() => (
          // By default there is no way to wrap the footer and body in one element, so we
          // have to custom-render the footer inside the body. Otherwise we can't use `submit`
          // button to submit the form
          <form
            onSubmit={onSubmit}
            {...testIdProps('move-dialog-form')}
            // Stopping blur propagation fixes a bug where the table thinks the cell editor was
            // blurred when it was really an input inside this dialog.
            onBlur={e => e.stopPropagation()}
            className={clsx(styles.form)}
            noValidate
            aria-labelledby={MOVE_DIALOG_TITLE_ID}
          >
            <Dialog.Body className={clsx(styles.dialogBody)}>
              <div className={clsx(styles.dialogTitle)}>
                {rest.multiSelectItems ? (
                  <FormControl required>
                    <FormControl.Label requiredIndicator={false}>Item(s)</FormControl.Label>
                    <SelectPanel {...selectPanelProps} />
                  </FormControl>
                ) : (
                  <>
                    <span className="text-bold pb-1">Item</span>
                    <span>{singleSelectedItemValue}</span>
                  </>
                )}
              </div>
              <MoveDialogForm
                formProps={formProps}
                setHelperText={setHelperText}
                setIsInvalidOption={setIsInvalidOption}
                helperTextId={helperTextId}
              />
              <Flash
                aria-live="assertive"
                variant={isInvalidOption ? 'danger' : 'default'}
                id={helperTextId}
                {...testIdProps('drag-and-drop-move-dialog-flash')}
              >
                {isInvalidOption ? <AlertIcon /> : <InfoIcon />}
                {helperText}
              </Flash>
            </Dialog.Body>
            <Dialog.Footer className="p-2">
              <Button type="submit" variant="primary" {...testIdProps('drag-and-drop-move-dialog-move-item-button')}>
                {submitButtonLabel ?? 'Move'}
              </Button>
            </Dialog.Footer>
          </form>
        )}
        returnFocusRef={returnFocusRef}
      />
      {showErrorDialog && (
        <Dialog
          width="large"
          title="Missing selection"
          onClose={() => {
            if (rest.multiSelectItems) {
              setShowErrorDialog(false)
            }
          }}
        >
          <Dialog.Body className={clsx(styles.dialogBody)}>
            The first item you selected cannot be deselected when performing a move.
          </Dialog.Body>
          <Dialog.Footer className="p-2">
            <Button
              type="submit"
              variant="primary"
              onClick={() => {
                if (rest.multiSelectItems) {
                  setShowErrorDialog(false)
                }
              }}
            >
              I understand
            </Button>
          </Dialog.Footer>
        </Dialog>
      )}
    </>
  )
}

import {DatePicker, type SingleDatePickerProps} from '@github-ui/date-picker'
import {TriangleDownIcon} from '@primer/octicons-react'
import {
  Button,
  FormControl,
  Select,
  SelectPanel,
  type SelectPanelProps,
  type SelectProps,
  TextInput,
  type TextInputProps,
} from '@primer/react'
import {clsx} from 'clsx'
import React from 'react'
import {type ChangeEvent, createContext, type ReactElement, use, useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useState} from 'react'

import styles from './MoveDialogForm.module.css'

export const MoveDialogContext = createContext<{
  formLabel: string
  onChange: (helperText: string, isInValid: boolean) => void
  helperTextId?: string
}>({formLabel: '', onChange: () => {}})

export type Action = {
  /**
   * The value and label of the action
   */
  value: string
  /**
   * The input that is rendered when the action is selected
   */
  renderInput: ReactElement // TODO figure out how to limit types
}

export interface FormProps {
  /**
   * The movements that a user can take based on the type of action
   * For example, if the type of action is "reorder" then a action might be
   * "move item first"
   */
  actions: Action[]
  /**
   * Optional: Callback to set the validity of the form
   */
  onActionChange?: (e: ChangeEvent<HTMLSelectElement>) => void
  /**
   * Optional: The label of the action select
   */
  actionsLabel?: string
}

interface MoveDialogFormProps {
  /**
   * Callback to set the validity of the form
   */
  setIsInvalidOption: (isInvalid: boolean) => void
  /**
   * Callback to set the helper text of the form
   */
  setHelperText: (helperText: string) => void
  /**
   * The form option props
   */
  formProps: FormProps
  /***
   * The id of the helper text. Used to associate the helper text with the form input.
   */
  helperTextId: string
}

/**
 * A form that allows the user to move an item to a new position in the list.
 *
 * @param props InputProps
 */
export function MoveDialogForm(props: MoveDialogFormProps) {
  const {formProps, setIsInvalidOption, setHelperText, helperTextId} = props
  const {actions, onActionChange, actionsLabel} = formProps
  const [selectedAction, setSelectedAction] = useState(actions[0]?.value)

  const onChange = useCallback(
    (event: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLSelectElement>) => {
      onActionChange?.(event)
      const action = actions.find(a => a.value === event.target.value)
      if (action) {
        setSelectedAction(action.value)
      }
    },
    [actions, onActionChange],
  )

  return (
    <MoveDialogContext
      value={useMemo(
        () => ({
          formLabel: selectedAction ?? '',
          onChange: (helperText, isInValid) => {
            setHelperText(helperText)
            setIsInvalidOption(isInValid)
          },
          helperTextId,
        }),
        [selectedAction, setHelperText, helperTextId, setIsInvalidOption],
      )}
    >
      <div className={clsx(styles.form)}>
        {actions.length > 1 && (
          <FormControl required>
            <FormControl.Label>{actionsLabel ?? 'Action'}</FormControl.Label>
            <Select block onChange={onChange}>
              {actions.map(action => (
                <Select.Option key={action.value} value={action.value}>
                  {action.value}
                </Select.Option>
              ))}
            </Select>
          </FormControl>
        )}
        {actions.find(a => a.value === selectedAction)?.renderInput}
      </div>
    </MoveDialogContext>
  )
}

type OptionProps = {
  helperText: string
  isInvalid?: boolean
}

export const SingleSelect = ({
  ref,
  ...props
}: OptionProps & Omit<SelectProps, 'ref'> & {ref?: React.Ref<HTMLSelectElement>}) => {
  const {helperText, isInvalid, ...rest} = props
  const {formLabel, onChange: parentOnChange, helperTextId} = use(MoveDialogContext)

  useEffect(() => {
    parentOnChange(helperText ?? '', !!isInvalid)
  }, [helperText, isInvalid, parentOnChange])

  return (
    <FormControl required>
      <FormControl.Label>{formLabel}</FormControl.Label>
      <Select
        block
        aria-invalid={isInvalid}
        validationStatus={isInvalid ? 'error' : undefined}
        aria-describedby={helperTextId}
        ref={ref}
        {...rest}
      />
    </FormControl>
  )
}

SingleSelect.displayName = 'MoveDialogForm.SingleSelect'

// we only want to show form validation after `onSubmit` is called
export const Text = ({
  ref,
  ...props
}: OptionProps & Omit<TextInputProps, 'ref'> & {ref?: React.Ref<HTMLInputElement>}) => {
  const {isInvalid, helperText, ...rest} = props
  const {formLabel, onChange: parentOnChange, helperTextId} = use(MoveDialogContext)

  useEffect(() => {
    parentOnChange(helperText, !!isInvalid)
  }, [helperText, isInvalid, parentOnChange])

  return (
    <FormControl required>
      <FormControl.Label>{formLabel}</FormControl.Label>
      <TextInput
        block
        aria-invalid={isInvalid}
        validationStatus={isInvalid ? 'error' : undefined}
        aria-describedby={helperTextId}
        ref={ref}
        {...rest}
      />
    </FormControl>
  )
}

Text.displayName = 'MoveDialogForm.Text'

export function MultiSelect(props: SelectPanelProps & OptionProps) {
  const {isInvalid, helperText, ...rest} = props
  const {formLabel, onChange: parentOnChange} = use(MoveDialogContext) // TODO: helperTextId

  useEffect(() => {
    parentOnChange(helperText, !!isInvalid)
  }, [helperText, isInvalid, parentOnChange])

  const renderAnchor = (buttonProps: React.HTMLAttributes<HTMLElement>) => {
    const {children, ...otherProps} = buttonProps
    return (
      <Button trailingAction={TriangleDownIcon} block {...otherProps}>
        {children}
      </Button>
    )
  }

  const selectPanelProps = {
    ...rest,
    renderAnchor,
  }

  // TODO: Figure out how to handle the aria-describedby
  return (
    <FormControl required>
      <FormControl.Label>{formLabel}</FormControl.Label>
      <SelectPanel {...selectPanelProps} />
    </FormControl>
  )
}

export function DateInput(props: SingleDatePickerProps & OptionProps) {
  const {isInvalid, helperText, ...datePickerProps} = props
  const {formLabel, onChange: parentOnChange, helperTextId} = use(MoveDialogContext)

  const defaultDatePickerProps: Partial<SingleDatePickerProps> = {
    anchor: 'button',
    anchorClassName: styles.datePickerAnchor,
    compressedHeader: false,
    confirmation: false,
    confirmUnsavedClose: true,
    dateFormat: 'long',
    placeholder: formLabel,
    showTodayButton: true,
    showClearButton: false,
    variant: 'single',
    weekStartsOn: 'Sunday',
  }

  useEffect(() => {
    parentOnChange(helperText, !!isInvalid)
  }, [helperText, isInvalid, parentOnChange])

  const merged = {...defaultDatePickerProps, ...datePickerProps}

  return (
    <FormControl required>
      <FormControl.Label>{formLabel}</FormControl.Label>
      <DatePicker {...merged} aria-describedby={helperTextId} fieldName={formLabel} />
    </FormControl>
  )
}

MoveDialogForm.SingleSelect = SingleSelect
MoveDialogForm.Text = Text
MoveDialogForm.MultiSelect = MultiSelect
MoveDialogForm.Date = DateInput
// Convenience alias
MoveDialogForm.SingleSelectOption = Select.Option

import React, {useCallback} from 'react'

import {useDragAndDrop} from '../../hooks/use-drag-and-drop'
import {useDragAndDropItem} from '../../hooks/use-drag-and-drop-item'

export interface MoveDialogTriggerProps<T> {
  /**
   * The clickable component that will trigger the move dialog to open
   */
  Component: T
  /**
   * Optional: The ref to return focus to after the move dialog is closed
   */
  returnFocusRef?: React.RefObject<HTMLElement | null>
}

/**
 * A trigger component that opens the move dialog when clicked.
 *
 * @param props MoveDialogTriggerProps
 */
export const MoveDialogTrigger = <T extends React.ElementType>({
  ref,
  Component,
  returnFocusRef: externalReturnFocusRef,
  ...props
}: MoveDialogTriggerProps<T> & React.ComponentProps<T> & {ref?: React.Ref<HTMLElement>}) => {
  const {title, index} = useDragAndDropItem()
  const {items, openMoveDialog} = useDragAndDrop()

  const openDialog = useCallback(() => {
    const returnFocusRef = (externalReturnFocusRef ?? ref) as React.RefObject<HTMLElement | null>
    openMoveDialog(title, index, returnFocusRef)
  }, [openMoveDialog, title, index, externalReturnFocusRef, ref])

  if (items.length === 1) return null

  return <Component {...props} onClick={openDialog} onSelect={openDialog} ref={ref} />
}

MoveDialogTrigger.displayName = 'MoveDialogTrigger'

import {Button, Dialog, Flash, FormControl, IconButton, Stack} from '@primer/react'
import {ArrowLeftIcon, XIcon} from '@primer/octicons-react'
import {useState, useEffect, useCallback, useRef} from 'react'
import type {GitHubFileFormData, ResourceCounts} from '../types'
import type {CopilotChatRepo} from '@github-ui/copilot-chat/utils/copilot-chat-types'
import type {TreeItem} from '@github-ui/repos-file-tree-view'
import {fetchRepo} from '../utils/fetch-repo'
import type {DirectoryItem} from '@github-ui/code-view-types'
import {useTreeList} from '@github-ui/code-view-shared/hooks/use-tree-list'
import {convertFlatPathsToTreeItems} from '../utils/convert-tree-data'
import {FileTreePicker} from './file-tree-picker/FileTreePicker'
import {RepoSelectPanel} from '@github-ui/copilot-chat/components/RepoSelectPanel'
import {CurrentRepositoryProvider, type Repository} from '@github-ui/current-repository'
import {FileQueryProvider} from '@github-ui/code-view-shared/contexts/FileQueryContext'
