import {CopilotSettingsProvider} from '@github-ui/code-view-shared/contexts/CopilotSettingsContext'
import {EditPermissionsProvider} from '@github-ui/code-view-shared/contexts/EditPermissionsContext'
import {RefInfoProvider} from '@github-ui/code-view-shared/contexts/RefInfoContext'
import {PathProvider} from '@github-ui/code-view-shared/contexts/PathContext'
import {ActionProvider} from '@github-ui/code-view-shared/contexts/ActionContext'
import {AllShortcutsEnabledProvider} from '@github-ui/code-view-shared/contexts/AllShortcutsEnabledContext'
import {testIdProps} from '@github-ui/test-id-props'
import FileResultsList from './files-search/FileResultsList'

import styles from './MultiFilePicker.module.css'

import {sendEvent} from '@github-ui/hydro-analytics'
import {isSupportedFileType} from '../utils/supported-file-types'

interface Props {
  formData: GitHubFileFormData[]
  onCancel: () => void
  onSaveFiles: (data: GitHubFileFormData[]) => Promise<void> | void
  findFileWorkerPath: string
  attachmentButtonRef: React.RefObject<HTMLButtonElement | null>
  owner?: string
  resourceCounts?: ResourceCounts
  initialRepo?: CopilotChatRepo | null
  onRepoSelect?: (repo: CopilotChatRepo | null) => void
  // Whether to hide the back button in the dialog header of the file picker.
  hideBackButton?: boolean
  allowFolderReferences?: boolean
  maxFileCount?: number
}

interface FilePickerFormProps {
  currentRepo: CopilotChatRepo
  localFormData: GitHubFileFormData[]
  setLocalFormData: (data: GitHubFileFormData[]) => void
  fetchError: string | null
  onFileCountChange?: (count: number) => void
  expandedPath: string
  resourceCounts?: ResourceCounts
  allowFolderReferences?: boolean
  maxFileCount?: number
  onRejectionMessage?: (message: string | null) => void
  onDisplayCountChange?: (count: number) => void
  onFilteredFilesChange?: (files: string[]) => void
}

function createFormDataEntry(
  path: string,
  type: 'github_file' | 'github_folder',
  currentRepo: CopilotChatRepo,
): GitHubFileFormData {
  return {
    id: `${currentRepo.id}${type === 'github_folder' ? '-folder' : ''}-${path}`,
    repositoryId: currentRepo.id,
    nwo: `${currentRepo.ownerLogin}/${currentRepo.name}`,
    filePath: path,
    // FilePicker currently only supports the default branch
    sha: currentRepo.defaultBranch,
    type,
  }
}

function processFileSelection(
  selectedItems: Set<string>,
  allDirectories: Set<string>,
  currentRepo: CopilotChatRepo,
  shouldAutoFolderize: boolean,
  effectiveMaxFileCount: number,
  allowFolderReferences: boolean,
): GitHubFileFormData[] {
  const selectedFolders = Array.from(selectedItems).filter(path => allDirectories.has(path))
  const selectedFiles = Array.from(selectedItems).filter(path => !allDirectories.has(path))

  if (!allowFolderReferences) {
    return selectedFiles.map(path => createFormDataEntry(path, 'github_file', currentRepo))
  }

  const formData: GitHubFileFormData[] = []
  const processedPaths = new Set<string>()

  for (const folder of selectedFolders) {
    if (selectedFolders.some(other => other !== folder && folder.startsWith(`${other}/`))) {
      continue
    }

    const filesInFolder = selectedFiles.filter(file => file.startsWith(`${folder}/`))

    if (allowFolderReferences && filesInFolder.length === 0) {
      formData.push(createFormDataEntry(folder, 'github_folder', currentRepo))
      continue
    }
    if (allowFolderReferences && filesInFolder.length >= effectiveMaxFileCount) {
      formData.push(createFormDataEntry(folder, 'github_folder', currentRepo))
      for (const file of filesInFolder) {
        processedPaths.add(file)
      }
    } else {
      for (const file of filesInFolder) {
        formData.push(createFormDataEntry(file, 'github_file', currentRepo))
        processedPaths.add(file)
      }
    }
  }

  const uncoveredFiles = selectedFiles.filter(file => !processedPaths.has(file))

  if (!shouldAutoFolderize || !allowFolderReferences) {
    for (const file of uncoveredFiles) {
      formData.push(createFormDataEntry(file, 'github_file', currentRepo))
    }
  } else {
    const filesByDirectory = new Map<string, string[]>()

    for (const file of uncoveredFiles) {
      const lastSlash = file.lastIndexOf('/')
      const parentDir = lastSlash > 0 ? file.substring(0, lastSlash) : ''

      if (!filesByDirectory.has(parentDir)) {
        filesByDirectory.set(parentDir, [])
      }
      filesByDirectory.get(parentDir)?.push(file)
    }

    for (const [parentDir, files] of filesByDirectory) {
      if (parentDir === '') {
        for (const file of files) {
          formData.push(createFormDataEntry(file, 'github_file', currentRepo))
        }
      } else {
        formData.push(createFormDataEntry(parentDir, 'github_folder', currentRepo))
      }
    }
  }

  return formData
}

function truncatePathsByLength(path: string, maxLength = 30): string {
  return path.length <= maxLength ? path : `...${path.slice(-maxLength)}`
}

function FilteredFilesWarning({files, error}: {files: string[]; error?: boolean}) {
  if (files.length === 0) return null

  if (files.length === 1 && files[0]) {
    const message = error ? "This file can't be submitted: " : 'This file was filtered out: '
    return (
      <>
        {message} <strong>{truncatePathsByLength(files[0])}</strong>. Only supported file types are allowed.
      </>
    )
  }

  const displayFiles = files.slice(0, 3)
  const remainingCount = files.length - 3
  const message = error ? "These files can't be submitted: " : 'These files were filtered out: '

  return (
    <>
      <span>{message}</span>
      {displayFiles.map((path, index) => (
        <span key={path}>
          {index > 0 && <span>, </span>}
          <strong>{truncatePathsByLength(path)}</strong>
        </span>
      ))}
      {remainingCount > 0 && <span>, and {remainingCount} more</span>}
      <span>. Only supported file types are allowed.</span>
    </>
  )
}

function FilePickerForm({
  currentRepo,
  setLocalFormData,
  fetchError,
  onFileCountChange,
  expandedPath,
  resourceCounts,
  allowFolderReferences,
  maxFileCount,
  onRejectionMessage,
  onDisplayCountChange,
  onFilteredFilesChange,
}: FilePickerFormProps) {
  const [selectedItems, setSelectedItems] = useState<Set<string>>(() => new Set())
  const [allDirectories, setAllDirectories] = useState<Set<string>>(() => new Set())

  const effectiveMaxFileCount = maxFileCount ?? 50

  // call useTreeList
  const {list, directories, loading} = useTreeList(currentRepo.commitOID, true)

  const [rootItems, setRootItems] = useState<Array<TreeItem<DirectoryItem>>>([])

  // Update tree items when list or directories change
  useEffect(() => {
    const response = {paths: list, directories}
    const items = convertFlatPathsToTreeItems(response)
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/set-state-in-effect
    setRootItems(items)
    setAllDirectories(new Set(directories))
  }, [list, directories, setRootItems, currentRepo.commitOID])

  // Track selected files and update form data
  useEffect(() => {
    const selectedFiles = Array.from(selectedItems).filter(path => !allDirectories.has(path))
    const selectedDirectories = Array.from(selectedItems).filter(path => allDirectories.has(path))

    onFileCountChange?.(selectedFiles.length)

    let shouldAutoFolderize = false

    if (allowFolderReferences && selectedFiles.length > 0) {
      const selectedFolderPrefixes = selectedDirectories.map(f => `${f}/`)
      const uncoveredFiles = selectedFiles.filter(
        path => !selectedFolderPrefixes.some(prefix => path.startsWith(prefix)),
      )

      const hasGroupableFiles = uncoveredFiles.some(path => path.includes('/'))
      shouldAutoFolderize = uncoveredFiles.length > effectiveMaxFileCount && hasGroupableFiles
    }

    const result = processFileSelection(
      selectedItems,
      allDirectories,
      currentRepo,
      shouldAutoFolderize,
      effectiveMaxFileCount,
      allowFolderReferences ?? false,
    )
    setLocalFormData(result)

    if (onDisplayCountChange) {
      onDisplayCountChange(result.length)
    }
  }, [
    selectedItems,
    allDirectories,
    currentRepo,
    setLocalFormData,
    onFileCountChange,
    onDisplayCountChange,
    allowFolderReferences,
    effectiveMaxFileCount,
  ])

  const getItemUrl = useCallback(
    (item: DirectoryItem) =>
      `/${`${currentRepo.name}/${currentRepo.ownerLogin}`}/tree/${currentRepo.refInfo.name}/${item.path}`,
    [currentRepo],
  )

  const handleSelectionChange = useCallback(
    (newSelectedItems: Set<string>) => {
      // Filter out unsupported file types from newly selected items
      const newlySelectedPaths = Array.from(newSelectedItems).filter(path => !selectedItems.has(path))
      const unsupportedFiles: string[] = []
      const filteredPaths: string[] = []

      // Check newly selected paths for unsupported file types
      for (const path of newlySelectedPaths) {
        const isDirectory = allDirectories.has(path)
        if (isSupportedFileType(path, isDirectory)) {
          filteredPaths.push(path)
        } else {
          unsupportedFiles.push(path)
        }
      }

      // Build the final filtered selection set
      const filteredSelection = new Set<string>()

      // Add existing selected items that are still in newSelectedItems
      for (const path of selectedItems) {
        if (newSelectedItems.has(path)) {
          filteredSelection.add(path)
        }
      }

      // Add newly selected valid paths
      for (const path of filteredPaths) {
        filteredSelection.add(path)
      }

      // Notify parent about filtered files
      if (unsupportedFiles.length > 0) {
        onFilteredFilesChange?.(unsupportedFiles)
      } else if (filteredSelection.size <= selectedItems.size) {
        // User is deselecting items, clear the filtered files message
        onFilteredFilesChange?.([])
      }

      if (filteredSelection.size <= selectedItems.size) {
        setSelectedItems(filteredSelection)
        onRejectionMessage?.(null)
        return
      }

      if (!allowFolderReferences) {
        const selectedFiles = Array.from(filteredSelection).filter(path => !allDirectories.has(path))

        const capacity = resourceCounts
          ? resourceCounts.maxResourceCount - resourceCounts.currentResourceCount
          : effectiveMaxFileCount

        if (selectedFiles.length > capacity) {
          const errorMessage = resourceCounts
            ? `${resourceCounts.currentResourceCount + selectedFiles.length} / ${
                resourceCounts.maxResourceCount
              } possible sources selected, add fewer files.`
            : `Up to ${effectiveMaxFileCount} files can be added at a time.`

          onRejectionMessage?.(errorMessage)
          setSelectedItems(filteredSelection)
          return
        }
        onRejectionMessage?.(null)
        setSelectedItems(filteredSelection)
        return
      }

      const selectedFiles = Array.from(filteredSelection).filter(path => !allDirectories.has(path))
      const selectedFolderPrefixes = Array.from(filteredSelection)
        .filter(path => allDirectories.has(path))
        .map(f => `${f}/`)

      const uncoveredFiles = selectedFiles.filter(
        path => !selectedFolderPrefixes.some(prefix => path.startsWith(prefix)),
      )

      const hasGroupableFiles = uncoveredFiles.some(path => path.includes('/'))
      const shouldAutoFolderize = uncoveredFiles.length > effectiveMaxFileCount && hasGroupableFiles

      const resultingFormData = processFileSelection(
        filteredSelection,
        allDirectories,
        currentRepo,
        shouldAutoFolderize,
        effectiveMaxFileCount,
        allowFolderReferences,
      )

      const attachmentCount = resultingFormData.length

      if (attachmentCount > effectiveMaxFileCount) {
        setSelectedItems(filteredSelection)
        return
      }

      setSelectedItems(filteredSelection)
    },
    [
      selectedItems,
      effectiveMaxFileCount,
      allDirectories,
      allowFolderReferences,
      onRejectionMessage,
      currentRepo,
      resourceCounts,
      onFilteredFilesChange,
    ],
  )

  return (
    <div className={styles.Box}>
      <FileTreePicker
        rootItems={rootItems}
        expandedPath={expandedPath}
        selectedItemRef={() => {}}
        setRootItems={setRootItems}
        navigateOnClick={false}
        directoryNavigateOnClick={false}
        selectedItems={selectedItems}
        onSelectionChange={handleSelectionChange}
        loading={loading || false}
        fetchError={!!fetchError}
        getItemUrl={getItemUrl}
        allowFolderReferences={Boolean(allowFolderReferences)}
        sortDirectoryItems={items => items.sort((a, b) => a.data.name.localeCompare(b.data.name))}
      />
    </div>
  )
}

function CloseButton({onClose}: {onClose: () => void}) {
  return <IconButton icon={XIcon} aria-label="Close" onClick={onClose} variant="invisible" />
}

export function MultiFilePicker({
  formData,
  onCancel,
  onSaveFiles,
  findFileWorkerPath,
  attachmentButtonRef,
  owner,
  resourceCounts,
  initialRepo,
  onRepoSelect,
  hideBackButton = false,
  allowFolderReferences = false,
  maxFileCount = 50,
}: Props) {
  const [repoDetailsCache, setRepoDetailsCache] = useState<Record<number, CopilotChatRepo>>({})
  const [fetchError, setFetchError] = useState<string | null>(null)
  const [currentRepo, setCurrentRepo] = useState<CopilotChatRepo | null>(initialRepo || null)
  const [openRepoPicker, setOpenRepoPicker] = useState(true)
  const [localFormData, setLocalFormData] = useState<GitHubFileFormData[]>(formData)
  const [isSaving, setIsSaving] = useState(false)
  const [savingError, setSavingError] = useState<{errorMessage: string; filePaths: string[]} | null>(null)
  const [expandedPath, setExpandedPath] = useState('')
  const [rejectionMessage, setRejectionMessage] = useState<string | null>(null)
  const [displayCount, setDisplayCount] = useState(0)
  const [selectedFileCount, setSelectedFileCount] = useState(0)
  const [filteredFiles, setFilteredFiles] = useState<string[]>([])

  const selectingRepo = useRef(false)

  const fetchRepoAndUpdateState = async (repoID: number): Promise<void> => {
    selectingRepo.current = true

    let payload: CopilotChatRepo

    if (repoDetailsCache[repoID]) {
      payload = repoDetailsCache[repoID]
      setCurrentRepo(payload)
      onRepoSelect?.(payload)
      return
    }

    try {
      payload = await fetchRepo(repoID)

      setRepoDetailsCache(prev => ({
        ...prev,
        [repoID]: payload,
      }))

      setCurrentRepo(payload)
      onRepoSelect?.(payload)
      setOpenRepoPicker(false)
    } catch (error) {
      const errorMessage = error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'An unknown error occurred'
      setFetchError(errorMessage)
    }
    selectingRepo.current = false
  }

  const onSelectRepo = () => {
    onRepoSelect?.(null)
    setCurrentRepo(null)
    setLocalFormData([])
    setOpenRepoPicker(true)
  }

  const validateTotalResourceCount = resourceCounts !== undefined
  const countToValidate = validateTotalResourceCount ? selectedFileCount : displayCount
  const capacity = validateTotalResourceCount
    ? resourceCounts.maxResourceCount - resourceCounts.currentResourceCount
    : maxFileCount

  const fileCountExceeded = countToValidate > capacity

  const handleSave = async () => {
    setIsSaving(true)

    try {
      if (fileCountExceeded) {
        return
      }

      sendEvent('copilot-spaces.click', {target: 'SAVED_FILES_FROM_FILE_PICKER'})
      await onSaveFiles(localFormData)
    } catch (error: unknown) {
      const errorMessageObj = {errorMessage: 'An unknown error occurred', filePaths: [] as string[]}

      if (error && typeof error === 'object' && 'base' in error && typeof error.base === 'string') {
        if (error.base.startsWith('unsupported_file_type') && 'files' in error) {
          errorMessageObj.filePaths = error.files as string[]
        } else if (error.base.startsWith('The size of the space exceeds the current limit')) {
          errorMessageObj.errorMessage = 'The size of the space exceeds the current limit'
        } else {
          errorMessageObj.errorMessage = error.base
        }
      }

      setSavingError(errorMessageObj)
    } finally {
      setIsSaving(false)
    }
  }

  useEffect(() => {
    setSavingError(null)
  }, [localFormData])

  const errorMessage = fileCountExceeded
    ? validateTotalResourceCount
      ? `${resourceCounts.currentResourceCount + selectedFileCount} / ${
          resourceCounts.maxResourceCount
        } possible sources selected, add fewer files.`
      : `Up to ${maxFileCount} items can be added at a time.`
    : rejectionMessage

  const hasError = fileCountExceeded || Boolean(rejectionMessage)

  return (
    <>
      <RepoSelectPanel
        selectionVariant="instant"
        open={!currentRepo && openRepoPicker}
        onOpenChange={newOpen => {
          // Ignore while loading repo data in order to avoid flash
          if (!selectingRepo.current) {
            setOpenRepoPicker(newOpen)
            onCancel()
          }
        }}
        selectedRepoIds={new Set()}
        onSelectRepo={async (repoId: number) => {
          if (!repoId) return
          setLocalFormData([])
          sendEvent('copilot-spaces.click', {target: 'SELECTED_REPO_FROM_REPO_PICKER'})
          await fetchRepoAndUpdateState(repoId)
        }}
        cancelReturnFocusRef={attachmentButtonRef}
        submitReturnFocusRef={attachmentButtonRef}
        error={fetchError ?? undefined}
        ownerDisplayLogin={owner}
        includeForks
      />

      {currentRepo && (
        <Dialog
          onClose={onCancel}
          title={`Add files from ${currentRepo.ownerLogin}/${currentRepo.name}`}
          width="large"
          position={{narrow: 'fullscreen'}}
          renderHeader={({onClose, dialogLabelId}) => {
            return (
              <Dialog.Header>
                <div className={styles.dialogHeader}>
                  <Dialog.Title id={dialogLabelId}>
                    <Stack align="center" direction="horizontal" gap="none">
                      {!hideBackButton && (
                        <Stack.Item>
                          <IconButton
                            icon={ArrowLeftIcon}
                            aria-label="Back"
                            onClick={onSelectRepo}
                            variant="invisible"
                          />
                        </Stack.Item>
                      )}
                      <Stack.Item grow className="mx-1">
                        Select folders and files in {currentRepo.ownerLogin}/{currentRepo.name}
                      </Stack.Item>
                      <Stack.Item>
                        <CloseButton onClose={() => onClose('close-button')} />
                      </Stack.Item>
                    </Stack>
                  </Dialog.Title>
                </div>
                <Dialog.Subtitle className="mt-0">
                  <CurrentRepositoryProvider repository={currentRepo as unknown as Repository}>
                    <FileQueryProvider>
                      <PathProvider path="test">
                        <ActionProvider action="tree">
                          <CopilotSettingsProvider copilotAccessAllowed>
                            <RefInfoProvider
                              refInfo={{
                                name: currentRepo.refInfo.name,
                                canEdit: true,
                                listCacheKey: 'anything',
                                currentOid: currentRepo.commitOID,
                              }}
                            >
                              <EditPermissionsProvider
                                canEdit
                                canEditOnDefaultBranch={false}
                                fileExistsOnDefault={false}
                              >
                                <AllShortcutsEnabledProvider allShortcutsEnabled={false}>
                                  <FileResultsList
                                    commitOid={currentRepo.commitOID}
                                    findFileWorkerPath={findFileWorkerPath}
                                    onItemSelected={(path?: string) => {
                                      if (path) {
                                        setExpandedPath(path)
                                      }
                                    }}
                                    config={{
                                      enableOverlay: true,
                                      disableNavigation: true,
                                      searchPlaceholder: 'Search for files or folders',
                                      actionText: 'Search for files or folders',
                                    }}
                                  />
                                </AllShortcutsEnabledProvider>
                              </EditPermissionsProvider>
                            </RefInfoProvider>
                          </CopilotSettingsProvider>
                        </ActionProvider>
                      </PathProvider>
                    </FileQueryProvider>
                  </CurrentRepositoryProvider>
                </Dialog.Subtitle>
              </Dialog.Header>
            )
          }}
          renderFooter={() => (
            <Dialog.Footer>
              <div className="d-flex flex-justify-between flex-items-center width-full">
                <div className="d-flex flex-items-center">
                  {errorMessage && <FormControl.Validation variant="error">{errorMessage}</FormControl.Validation>}
                </div>
                <div className="d-flex flex-row flex-justify-end gap-2">
                  <Button onClick={onCancel}>Cancel</Button>
                  <Button
                    {...testIdProps('save-resource-button')}
                    variant="primary"
                    onClick={handleSave}
                    count={
                      isSaving
                        ? undefined
                        : (validateTotalResourceCount ? selectedFileCount : displayCount) || undefined
                    }
                    loading={isSaving}
                    disabled={hasError}
                  >
                    Add
                  </Button>
                </div>
              </div>
            </Dialog.Footer>
          )}
          renderBody={() => (
            <div className="d-flex flex-column">
              {fetchError && <span>{fetchError}</span>}
              {savingError && (
                <div className="position-sticky top-0 bgColor-default flash-banner">
                  <Flash full variant="danger">
                    {savingError.filePaths.length > 0 ? (
                      <FilteredFilesWarning files={savingError.filePaths} error />
                    ) : (
                      <span>{savingError.errorMessage}</span>
                    )}
                  </Flash>
                </div>
              )}
              {filteredFiles.length > 0 && (
                <div className="position-sticky top-0 bgColor-default flash-banner">
                  <Flash full variant="warning">
                    <FilteredFilesWarning files={filteredFiles} />
                  </Flash>
                </div>
              )}
              <CurrentRepositoryProvider repository={currentRepo as unknown as Repository}>
                <FileQueryProvider>
                  <PathProvider path="test">
                    <ActionProvider action="tree">
                      <CopilotSettingsProvider copilotAccessAllowed>
                        <RefInfoProvider
                          refInfo={{
                            name: currentRepo.refInfo.name,
                            canEdit: true,
                            listCacheKey: 'anything',
                            currentOid: currentRepo.commitOID,
                          }}
                        >
                          <EditPermissionsProvider canEdit canEditOnDefaultBranch={false} fileExistsOnDefault={false}>
                            <FilePickerForm
                              expandedPath={expandedPath}
                              currentRepo={currentRepo}
                              localFormData={localFormData}
                              setLocalFormData={setLocalFormData}
                              onFileCountChange={setSelectedFileCount}
                              onDisplayCountChange={setDisplayCount}
                              fetchError={fetchError}
                              resourceCounts={resourceCounts}
                              allowFolderReferences={allowFolderReferences}
                              maxFileCount={maxFileCount}
                              onRejectionMessage={setRejectionMessage}
                              onFilteredFilesChange={setFilteredFiles}
                            />
                          </EditPermissionsProvider>
                        </RefInfoProvider>
                      </CopilotSettingsProvider>
                    </ActionProvider>
                  </PathProvider>
                </FileQueryProvider>
              </CurrentRepositoryProvider>
            </div>
          )}
          className={styles.Dialog}
        />
      )}
    </>
  )
}

import {
  getAPITaskParamsFromAttempt,
  TaskCreationFailedBannerList,
} from '@github-ui/agent-sessions/components/TaskCreationFailedBanner'
import {ConsecutiveCAPI401sProvider} from '@github-ui/agent-sessions/contexts/ConsecutiveCAPI401sContext'
import {useIntentDetectionContext} from '@github-ui/agent-sessions/contexts/IntentDetectionContext'
import type {DropdownRepository} from '@github-ui/agent-sessions/hooks/useCopilotRepoFetching'
import type {IntentDetectionMode} from '@github-ui/agent-sessions/types/intent-detection-mode'
import type {SelectedAgent} from '@github-ui/agent-sessions/types/session'
import {shouldLinkToRepoTaskView} from '@github-ui/agent-sessions/utils/generate-task-path'
import {uuid} from '@github-ui/agent-sessions/utils/uuid'
import type {ChatInputRef} from '@github-ui/copilot-chat/components/ChatInput'
import CopilotIconAnimation from '@github-ui/copilot-chat/components/CopilotIconAnimation'
import CopilotSuggestions from '@github-ui/copilot-chat/components/CopilotSuggestions'
import {LegalDisclaimer} from '@github-ui/copilot-chat/components/LegalDisclaimer'
import {EMBEDDED_MENU_PORTAL_CLASS, MenuPortalContainer} from '@github-ui/copilot-chat/components/PortalContainerUtils'
import {useEntitlement} from '@github-ui/copilot-chat/components/quota/EntitlementContext'
import {isPaidUser} from '@github-ui/copilot-chat/utils/copilot-chat-entitlement'
import {type CopilotChatCommandType, ModeSupportName} from '@github-ui/copilot-chat/utils/copilot-chat-types'
import {copilotFeatureFlags} from '@github-ui/copilot-chat/utils/copilot-feature-flags'
import {copilotLocalStorage} from '@github-ui/copilot-chat/utils/copilot-local-storage'
// eslint-disable-next-line no-restricted-imports
import {useChatState} from '@github-ui/copilot-chat/utils/CopilotChatContext'
import {useChatManager} from '@github-ui/copilot-chat/utils/CopilotChatManagerContext'
import {AgentsList} from '@github-ui/dashboard-lists/components/AgentsList'
import {isFeatureEnabled} from '@github-ui/feature-flags'
import {sendEvent} from '@github-ui/hydro-analytics'
import ProductAnnouncement from '@github-ui/product-announcement/components/ProductAnnouncement'
import {useFeatureFlag} from '@github-ui/react-core/use-feature-flag'
import {useNavigate} from '@github-ui/use-navigate'
import {clsx} from 'clsx'
import {useCallback, useEffect, useRef, useState} from 'react'

import {useFailedTasks} from '../../contexts/FailedTasksContext'
import {ChatInputContainer} from '../ChatInputContainer'
import {CopilotEmptyStateAnimation} from '../CopilotEmptyStateAnimation'
import {CommandStartersAndSuggestions} from './CommandStartersAndSuggestions'
import styles from './NewConversation.module.css'
import {NewConversationBanners} from './NewConversationBanners'
import {
  getProductAnnouncementId,
  handleProductAnnouncementBannerTracking,
  handleProductAnnouncementModalTracking,
} from './product-announcement'
import {TasksInChatInput} from './TasksInChatInput'

interface NewConversationProps {
  textAreaRef: React.RefObject<HTMLTextAreaElement | null>
  handleUserSubmit: (content: string) => Promise<void>
  nextMessageIndex: number
  /** Force icon-only mode in the toolbar, useful for container-based responsive behavior */
  forceIconOnly?: boolean
  /** Whether this is the embedded immersive view (shows URL-based suggestions instead of command starters) */
  isEmbedded?: boolean
}

export function NewConversation({
  textAreaRef,
  handleUserSubmit,
  nextMessageIndex,
  forceIconOnly,
  isEmbedded,
}: NewConversationProps) {
  const state = useChatState()
  const manager = useChatManager()
  const containerRef = useRef<HTMLFormElement>(null)
  const hasSubmittedChatRef = useRef(false)

  // Intent detection POC
  const isUsingIntentDetection = isFeatureEnabled('copilot_intent_detection')
  const {setTaskCreationRequest} = useIntentDetectionContext()
  const navigate = useNavigate()

  const {chatQuotaExceeded, isLicensedLimited} = useEntitlement()
  const isInputDisabled = isLicensedLimited && chatQuotaExceeded
  const [chatCommand, setChatCommand] = useState<CopilotChatCommandType | null>(null)
  const [activeModeName, setActiveModeName] = useState<string>(() => {
    const savedModeName = copilotLocalStorage.getActiveMode()
    return savedModeName ?? ModeSupportName.Ask
  })

  const chatInputRef = useRef<ChatInputRef>(null)

  const productAnnouncementId = getProductAnnouncementId()
  const {plan: copilotPlan} = useEntitlement()

  const isMissionControlEnabled = state.agentsEnabled
  const isTaskListV2Enabled = useFeatureFlag('copilot_agent_task_list_v2')
  const linkToSessionsInRepo = useFeatureFlag('copilot_mission_control_link_to_sessions_in_repo')
  const linkToSessionsInHyperspace = useFeatureFlag('copilot_mission_control_link_to_hyperspace')
  const showProPlusAnimation = useFeatureFlag('copilot_pro_plus_animation')

  const {failedTasks, setFailedTasks, createTask} = useFailedTasks()

  const handleRetry = useCallback(
    (attemptIndex: number) => {
      const attempt = failedTasks[attemptIndex]
      if (attempt) {
        setFailedTasks(prev => prev.filter((_, index) => index !== attemptIndex))
        createTask(getAPITaskParamsFromAttempt(attempt))
      }
    },
    [failedTasks, setFailedTasks, createTask],
  )

  const handleDismiss = useCallback(
    (attemptIndex: number) => {
      setFailedTasks(prev => prev.filter((_, index) => index !== attemptIndex))
    },
    [setFailedTasks],
  )

  const handleSubmit = useCallback(
    async (content: string) => {
      hasSubmittedChatRef.current = true
      await handleUserSubmit(content)
    },
    [handleUserSubmit],
  )

  const handleIntentDetectionSubmit = async (
    content: string,
    selectedRepo: DropdownRepository | null,
    selectedBranch: string,
    selectedAgent: SelectedAgent,
    selectedMode?: IntentDetectionMode,
    modelId?: string,
    createPullRequest?: boolean,
  ): Promise<void> => {
    const trimmedContent = content.trim()
    if (trimmedContent === '' || !selectedBranch || selectedBranch.trim() === '') return
    if (selectedRepo) {
      const clientGeneratedId = uuid()
      setTaskCreationRequest({
        nwo: selectedRepo.nameWithOwner,
        problemStatement: trimmedContent,
        baseRef: selectedBranch,
        model: modelId,
        agentId: selectedAgent.topLevelAgentId,
        customAgent: selectedAgent.subAgentSlug,
        createPullRequest,
        mode: selectedMode,
        clientGeneratedId,
      })

      navigate(`/copilot/t/${selectedRepo.nameWithOwner}/new`)
    } else {
      throw new Error('Intent detection requires a repository selection.')
    }
    return Promise.resolve()
  }

  useEffect(() => {
    // Log feature flag values when NewConversation first renders
    sendEvent('dotcom_chat.activate', {
      target: 'NEW_CONVERSATION_VIEW_LOADED',
      defaultToTask: copilotFeatureFlags.defaultToTaskInput,
      defaultToTaskControl: copilotFeatureFlags.defaultToTaskControl,
      askModeDropdown: copilotFeatureFlags.askModeDropdown,
    })

    // Log bounce event if user leaves without submitting a chat
    return () => {
      if (!hasSubmittedChatRef.current) {
        sendEvent('dotcom_chat.activate', {
          target: 'NEW_CONVERSATION_BOUNCE',
          mode: 'immersive',
          bounceToUrl: document.location.href,
          defaultToTask: copilotFeatureFlags.defaultToTaskInput,
          defaultToTaskControl: copilotFeatureFlags.defaultToTaskControl,
        })
      }
    }
  }, [])

  // Regenerate suggestions for embedded mode when component mounts (e.g., new conversation)
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!isEmbedded) return

    const suggestionContext = state.context?.[0] ?? state.currentRepository
    if (suggestionContext) {
      manager.generateSuggestions(suggestionContext)
    }

    return () => {
      manager.clearSuggestions()
    }
  }, [isEmbedded, manager, state.context, state.currentRepository, state.selectedThreadID])

  const iceBreakersExperimentEnabled = copilotFeatureFlags.copilotIcebreakersExperimentHyperspace

  return (
    <>
      <div className={clsx(styles.scrollContainer, {[styles.embeddedScrollContainer]: isEmbedded})}>
        {!isEmbedded && (
          <MenuPortalContainer className={state.mode === 'assistive' ? EMBEDDED_MENU_PORTAL_CLASS : undefined} />
        )}
        <div className={clsx(styles.container, {[styles.embeddedContainer]: isEmbedded})}>
          {isEmbedded && (
            <div className={styles.embeddedDisclaimer}>
              <LegalDisclaimer />
            </div>
          )}
          <div className={styles.banner}>
            <NewConversationBanners />
          </div>
          <div
            className={clsx(styles.main, {
              [styles.withTaskList]: isMissionControlEnabled && isTaskListV2Enabled,
              [styles.embeddedMain]: isEmbedded,
            })}
          >
            <div className={clsx(styles.content, {[styles.embeddedContent]: isEmbedded})}>
              <div className={clsx(styles.innerContainer, {[styles.embeddedInnerContainer]: isEmbedded})}>
                <h1 className="sr-only">Copilot Chat</h1>

                <div
                  className={clsx(styles.inputAndStartersContainer, {
                    [styles.embeddedInputContainer]: isEmbedded,
                  })}
                >
                  {showProPlusAnimation ? <CopilotEmptyStateAnimation /> : <CopilotIconAnimation hidden />}

                  {/* For embedded, show suggestions above input */}
                  {isEmbedded && !isInputDisabled && !isUsingIntentDetection && (
                    <div className={styles.embeddedSuggestions}>
                      <CopilotSuggestions suggestionKind="initial" />
                    </div>
                  )}

                  {/* For non-embedded, render the input inside the scroll container */}
                  {!isEmbedded &&
                    (!isUsingIntentDetection ? (
                      <ChatInputContainer
                        chatInputRef={chatInputRef}
                        textAreaRef={textAreaRef}
                        handleUserSubmit={handleSubmit}
                        nextMessageIndex={nextMessageIndex}
                        onChatCommandChange={setChatCommand}
                        isNewConversation
                        containerRef={containerRef}
                        slashCommandsEnabled
                        showDisabledInputOnQuotaExceeded
                        onClickMode={setActiveModeName}
                        activeModeName={activeModeName}
                        forceIconOnly={forceIconOnly}
                      />
                    ) : (
                      <TasksInChatInput handleIntentDetectionSubmit={handleIntentDetectionSubmit} />
                    ))}

                  {/* For non-embedded, show command starters below input */}
                  {!isEmbedded &&
                    !isInputDisabled &&
                    !isUsingIntentDetection &&
                    copilotFeatureFlags.chatInputCommands && (
                      <div className={styles.commandStartersContainer}>
                        <CommandStartersAndSuggestions
                          chatCommand={chatCommand}
                          chatInputRef={chatInputRef}
                          textAreaRef={textAreaRef}
                          containerRef={containerRef}
                          iceBreakersExperimentEnabled={iceBreakersExperimentEnabled}
                          paidUser={isPaidUser(copilotPlan)}
                          activeModeName={activeModeName}
                        />
                      </div>
                    )}
                </div>

                {productAnnouncementId && (
                  <ProductAnnouncement
                    id="copilot-immersive-v1"
                    announcementId={productAnnouncementId}
                    shouldShowTabCtas={isPaidUser(copilotPlan)}
                    shouldShowMainCta={!isPaidUser(copilotPlan)}
                    useLocalStorage
                    onBannerEvent={handleProductAnnouncementBannerTracking}
                    onModalEvent={handleProductAnnouncementModalTracking}
                  />
                )}

                <TaskCreationFailedBannerList
                  attempts={failedTasks}
                  onRetry={handleRetry}
                  onDismiss={handleDismiss}
                  className={styles.failedTasksContainer}
                />

                {isMissionControlEnabled && isTaskListV2Enabled && (
                  <div className={styles.agentListContainer}>
                    <ConsecutiveCAPI401sProvider>
                      <AgentsList
                        showViewAll
                        title="Recent agent sessions"
                        initialResultCount={5}
                        linkToSessionsInRepo={shouldLinkToRepoTaskView(
                          linkToSessionsInRepo,
                          linkToSessionsInHyperspace,
                        )}
                      />
                    </ConsecutiveCAPI401sProvider>
                  </div>
                )}
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
          {!isEmbedded && (
            <div className={styles.footer}>
              <LegalDisclaimer />
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    </>
  )
}

import {useEntitlement} from '@github-ui/copilot-chat/components/quota/EntitlementContext'
import {CopilotLicenseType} from '@github-ui/copilot-chat/utils/copilot-chat-types'

import styles from '../ChatInputBanners.module.css'
import {SkuSuccessBanner} from '../SkuSuccessBanner/SkuSuccessBanner'
import {useSkuSuccessBanners} from '../SkuSuccessBanner/use-sku-success-banners'

export function NewConversationBanners() {
  const entitlement = useEntitlement()
  const licenseType = entitlement.licenseType
  const {activeSku, visibility} = useSkuSuccessBanners()

  // Question: What happens when not LicensedFull?
  const showTrialBanner = activeSku === 'trial' && licenseType === CopilotLicenseType.LicensedFull
  const showOtherSkuBanner = activeSku && activeSku !== 'trial'

  return (
    <>
      {showTrialBanner && visibility.trial && (
        <SkuSuccessBanner skuType={activeSku} className={styles.banner} onDismiss={visibility.trial.onDismiss} />
      )}

      {showOtherSkuBanner && visibility[activeSku] && (
        <SkuSuccessBanner skuType={activeSku} className={styles.banner} onDismiss={visibility[activeSku].onDismiss} />
      )}
    </>
  )
}

/**
 * Parses the RFC 5988 Link header to extract the URL for a given relation.
 * Expected format: `<url>; rel="next"`
 */
export function parseLinkHeader(linkHeader: string | null, rel: string): string | null {
  if (!linkHeader) return null
  if (!rel) return null

  // Escape special regex characters in the rel parameter for safety
  const escapedRel = rel.replace(/[.*+?^${}()|[\]\\]/g, '\\$&')

  // Match patterns like: <url>; rel="next" or <url>; rel=next
  const regex = new RegExp(`<([^>]+)>;\\s*rel=["']?${escapedRel}["']?`, 'i')
  const match = linkHeader.match(regex)
  return match?.[1] ?? null
}

import type {CopilotSpaceResource} from '../types'

export function resourceMetadataToRailsPayload(resource: CopilotSpaceResource) {
  switch (resource.type) {
    case 'repository':
      return {
        repository_id: resource.repositoryId,
      }
    case 'github_file':
      return {
        repository_id: resource.repositoryId,
        file_path: resource.filePath,
        sha: resource.sha,
      }
    case 'free_text':
      return {
        text: resource.text,
        name: resource.name,
      }
    case 'github_issue':
    case 'github_pull_request':
      return {
        repository_id: resource.repositoryId,
        number: resource.number,
      }
    case 'uploaded_text_file':
      return {name: resource.name}
    case 'media_content':
      return {
        media_type: resource.mediaType,
        name: resource.name,
        url: resource.url,
        height: resource.height,
        width: resource.width,
      }
    default:
      throw new Error(`Unsupported resource`)
  }
}

import {isFeatureEnabled} from '@github-ui/feature-flags'
import {sendEvent} from '@github-ui/hydro-analytics'
import type {
  ProductAnnouncementBannerEvent,
  ProductAnnouncementModalEvent,
} from '@github-ui/product-announcement/components/ProductAnnouncement.types'

export const getProductAnnouncementId = () => {
  if (isFeatureEnabled('hyperspace_nudges_universe25')) return 'universe25'

  return ''
}

export const handleProductAnnouncementBannerTracking = (event: ProductAnnouncementBannerEvent) => {
  switch (event) {
    case 'displayed':
      sendEvent('view', {
        category: 'universe_banner',
        action: 'banner_view',
        label: 'banner_universe_loggedIn',
      })
      break
    case 'clicked':
      sendEvent('click', {
        category: 'universe_banner',
        action: 'banner_click_openModal',
        label: 'banner_universe_loggedIn',
      })
      break
    case 'dismissed':
      sendEvent('click', {
        category: 'universe_banner',
        action: 'banner_dismiss',
        label: 'banner_universe_loggedIn',
      })
      break
  }
}

export const handleProductAnnouncementModalTracking = (
  event: ProductAnnouncementModalEvent,
  data?: Record<string, unknown>,
) => {
  switch (event) {
    case 'opened':
      sendEvent('view', {
        category: 'universe_modal',
        action: 'modal_view',
        label: 'modal_universe_loggedIn',
      })
      break
    case 'closed':
      sendEvent('click', {
        category: 'universe_modal',
        action: 'modal_close',
        label: 'modal_universe_loggedIn',
      })
      break
    case 'tab_clicked':
      sendEvent('click', {
        category: 'universe_modal',
        action: 'modal_nav_click',
        label: 'modal_universe_loggedIn',
        navItem: data?.tabName as string,
      })
      break
    case 'main_cta_clicked':
      sendEvent('click', {
        category: 'universe_modal',
        action: 'modal_primary_cta_left_click',
        label: 'modal_universe_loggedIn',
      })
      break
    case 'see_all_clicked':
      sendEvent('click', {
        category: 'universe_modal',
        action: 'modal_seeAllAnnouncements_left_click',
        label: 'modal_universe_loggedIn',
      })
      break
    case 'tab_cta_1_clicked':
      sendEvent('click', {
        category: 'universe_modal',
        action: 'modal_primary_cta_right_click',
        label: 'modal_universe_loggedIn',
        navItem: data?.tabName as string,
        ctaType: 'primary_right',
        ctaUrl: data?.url as string,
        userStatus: 'logged_in',
        positionIndex: data?.tabIndex as number,
      })
      break
    case 'tab_cta_2_clicked':
      sendEvent('click', {
        category: 'universe_modal',
        action: 'modal_secondary_cta_right_click',
        label: 'modal_universe_loggedIn',
        navItem: data?.tabName as string,
        ctaType: 'secondary_right',
        ctaUrl: data?.url as string,
        userStatus: 'logged_in',
        positionIndex: data?.tabIndex as number,
      })
      break
    case 'tab_link_clicked':
      sendEvent('click', {
        category: 'universe_modal',
        action: 'modal_link_right_click',
        label: 'modal_universe_loggedIn',
        navItem: data?.tabName as string,
        ctaType: 'learn_more_right',
        ctaUrl: data?.url as string,
        userStatus: 'logged_in',
        positionIndex: data?.tabIndex as number,
      })
      break
    case 'tab_description_link_clicked':
      sendEvent('click', {
        category: 'universe_modal',
        action: 'modal_description_link_right_click',
        label: 'modal_universe_loggedIn',
        navItem: data?.tabName as string,
        ctaType: 'link',
        ctaUrl: data?.url as string,
        userStatus: 'logged_in',
        positionIndex: data?.tabIndex as number,
      })
      break
  }
}

import {useEntitlement} from '@github-ui/copilot-chat/components/quota/EntitlementContext'
import {ENABLE_ADDITIONAL_REQUESTS_URL, UPGRADE_PLAN_URL} from '@github-ui/copilot-chat/utils/copilot-chat-helpers'
import {CopilotPlan} from '@github-ui/copilot-chat/utils/copilot-chat-types'
import {copilotFeatureFlags} from '@github-ui/copilot-chat/utils/copilot-feature-flags'
import {copilotLocalStorage} from '@github-ui/copilot-chat/utils/copilot-local-storage'
import {sendEvent} from '@github-ui/hydro-analytics'
import {Banner, LinkButton} from '@primer/react'
import {useEffect, useState} from 'react'

import styles from './QuotaExceededBanner.module.css'

export function useQuotaExceededBannerVisibility() {
  const {
    isLicensedLimited,
    chatQuotaRemaining,
    premiumChatQuotaRemaining,
    premiumInteractionsQuotaExceeded,
    overagesEnabled,
  } = useEntitlement()
  const [isVisible, setIsVisible] = useState<boolean>(() => {
    return copilotLocalStorage.getQuotaExceededBannerFlag() ?? false
  })

  useEffect(() => {
    if (copilotFeatureFlags.premiumRequestQuotasEnabled && chatQuotaRemaining !== undefined) {
      const existingFlag = copilotLocalStorage.getQuotaExceededBannerFlag()

      if (copilotFeatureFlags.premiumRequestQuotasEnabled && !overagesEnabled && premiumInteractionsQuotaExceeded) {
        if (existingFlag === null) {
          copilotLocalStorage.setQuotaExceededBannerFlag(true)
          // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/set-state-in-effect
          setIsVisible(true)
        } else {
          setIsVisible(existingFlag)
        }
      } else {
        copilotLocalStorage.removeQuotaExceededBannerFlag()
        setIsVisible(false)
      }
    }
  }, [chatQuotaRemaining, premiumChatQuotaRemaining, overagesEnabled, premiumInteractionsQuotaExceeded])

  const handleDismiss = () => {
    copilotLocalStorage.setQuotaExceededBannerFlag(false)
    setIsVisible(false)
  }

  return {
    visible:
      isVisible &&
      !isLicensedLimited &&
      chatQuotaRemaining !== undefined &&
      copilotFeatureFlags.premiumRequestQuotasEnabled,
    onDismiss: handleDismiss,
  }
}

interface QuotaExceededBannerProps {
  onDismiss: () => void
}

export function QuotaExceededBanner({onDismiss}: QuotaExceededBannerProps) {
  const {plan, canUpgradePlan, resetDate} = useEntitlement()

  let description = null
  let primaryAction = null

  if (plan === CopilotPlan.IndividualFree) {
    description = `You have used all requests available this month. Upgrade your plan to increase your limit. Limit resets on ${resetDate}.`
    primaryAction = (
      <LinkButton
        as="a"
        href={UPGRADE_PLAN_URL}
        onClick={() => {
          sendEvent('dotcom_chat.activate', {
            target: 'QUOTA_METER_BANNER_LINK_UPGRADE',
            mode: 'immersive',
          })
        }}
      >
        Upgrade
      </LinkButton>
    )
  } else if (plan === CopilotPlan.IndividualPro || plan === CopilotPlan.IndividualProPlus) {
    if (canUpgradePlan) {
      description = `You have used all premium requests available this month. Upgrade your plan to increase your limit. Limit resets on ${resetDate}.`
      primaryAction = (
        <LinkButton
          as="a"
          href={UPGRADE_PLAN_URL}
          onClick={() => {
            sendEvent('dotcom_chat.activate', {
              target: 'QUOTA_METER_BANNER_LINK_UPGRADE',
              mode: 'immersive',
            })
          }}
        >
          Upgrade
        </LinkButton>
      )
    } else {
      description = `You have used all premium requests available this month. Enable additional requests to get more. Limit resets on ${resetDate}.`
      primaryAction = (
        <LinkButton
          as="a"
          href={ENABLE_ADDITIONAL_REQUESTS_URL}
          onClick={() => {
            sendEvent('dotcom_chat.activate', {
              target: 'QUOTA_METER_BANNER_LINK_OVERAGES',
              mode: 'immersive',
            })
          }}
        >
          Enable additional requests
        </LinkButton>
      )
    }
  } else if (plan === CopilotPlan.Business || plan === CopilotPlan.Enterprise) {
    description = `You have reached your monthly limit for premium requests. Ask your admin to increase your limit. Limit resets on ${resetDate}.`
  }

  return (
    <div className={styles.container}>
      <Banner
        variant="upsell"
        title="Limit reached"
        description={description}
        hideTitle
        primaryAction={primaryAction}
        onDismiss={() => {
          onDismiss()
          sendEvent('dotcom_chat.activate', {
            target: 'QUOTA_EXCEEDED_BANNER_DISMISS',
            mode: 'immersive',
          })
        }}
      />
    </div>
  )
}

import {useEntitlement} from '@github-ui/copilot-chat/components/quota/EntitlementContext'
import {
  ENABLE_ADDITIONAL_REQUESTS_URL,
  MANAGE_BILLING_URL,
  PREMIUM_QUOTA_TRIGGER,
  UPGRADE_PLAN_URL,
} from '@github-ui/copilot-chat/utils/copilot-chat-helpers'
import {copilotFeatureFlags} from '@github-ui/copilot-chat/utils/copilot-feature-flags'
import {copilotLocalStorage} from '@github-ui/copilot-chat/utils/copilot-local-storage'
import {sendEvent} from '@github-ui/hydro-analytics'
import {Banner, Link, LinkButton} from '@primer/react'
import {useEffect, useState} from 'react'

const triggerValue = copilotFeatureFlags.premiumRequestQuotasEnabled ? PREMIUM_QUOTA_TRIGGER : 10

export function useQuotaMeterBannerVisibility() {
  const {
    isLicensedLimited,
    chatQuotaRemaining,
    premiumChatQuotaRemaining,
    premiumInteractionsQuotaExceeded,
    overagesEnabled,
  } = useEntitlement()
  const [isVisible, setIsVisible] = useState<boolean>(() => {
    return copilotLocalStorage.getMeterBannerFlag() ?? false
  })

  useEffect(() => {
    if ((isLicensedLimited || copilotFeatureFlags.premiumRequestQuotasEnabled) && chatQuotaRemaining !== undefined) {
      const existingFlag = copilotLocalStorage.getMeterBannerFlag()

      if (
        (chatQuotaRemaining > 0 && chatQuotaRemaining <= triggerValue) ||
        (!isLicensedLimited && premiumChatQuotaRemaining > 0 && premiumChatQuotaRemaining <= triggerValue) ||
        (!isLicensedLimited && premiumInteractionsQuotaExceeded && overagesEnabled)
      ) {
        if (existingFlag === null) {
          copilotLocalStorage.setMeterBannerFlag(true)
          // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/set-state-in-effect
          setIsVisible(true)
        } else {
          setIsVisible(existingFlag)
        }
      } else {
        copilotLocalStorage.removeMeterBannerFlag()
        setIsVisible(false)
      }
    }
  }, [
    isLicensedLimited,
    chatQuotaRemaining,
    premiumChatQuotaRemaining,
    overagesEnabled,
    premiumInteractionsQuotaExceeded,
  ])

  const handleDismiss = () => {
    copilotLocalStorage.setMeterBannerFlag(false)
    setIsVisible(false)
  }

  return {
    visible:
      isVisible &&
      chatQuotaRemaining !== undefined &&
      (isLicensedLimited || copilotFeatureFlags.premiumRequestQuotasEnabled),
    onDismiss: handleDismiss,
  }
}

interface QuotaMeterBannerProps {
  onDismiss: () => void
  className?: string
}

export function QuotaMeterBanner({onDismiss, className}: QuotaMeterBannerProps) {
  const {isLicensedLimited, canUpgradePlan, canPurchaseAdditionalQuota, premiumInteractionsQuotaExceeded, resetDate} =
    useEntitlement()

  const description = isLicensedLimited
    ? `You have used ${100 - triggerValue}% of your free requests this month.`
    : !premiumInteractionsQuotaExceeded
      ? `You have used ${100 - triggerValue}% of your premium responses this month. ${
          canUpgradePlan
            ? 'Upgrade to increase your limit.'
            : 'Enable additional requests to get more usage after the limit is reached.'
        }`
      : `You have used all premium requests available this month. You will be charged per additional premium request until the limit resets on ${resetDate}.`

  const primaryAction =
    canUpgradePlan && !premiumInteractionsQuotaExceeded ? (
      <LinkButton
        as="a"
        href={UPGRADE_PLAN_URL}
        onClick={() => {
          sendEvent('dotcom_chat.activate', {
            target: 'QUOTA_METER_BANNER_LINK_UPGRADE',
            mode: 'immersive',
          })
        }}
      >
        Upgrade
      </LinkButton>
    ) : canPurchaseAdditionalQuota && !premiumInteractionsQuotaExceeded ? (
      <LinkButton
        as="a"
        href={ENABLE_ADDITIONAL_REQUESTS_URL}
        onClick={() => {
          sendEvent('dotcom_chat.activate', {
            target: 'QUOTA_METER_BANNER_LINK_OVERAGES',
            mode: 'immersive',
          })
        }}
      >
        Enable additional requests
      </LinkButton>
    ) : (
      canPurchaseAdditionalQuota && (
        <LinkButton
          as="a"
          href={MANAGE_BILLING_URL}
          onClick={() => {
            sendEvent('dotcom_chat.activate', {
              target: 'QUOTA_METER_BANNER_LINK_MANAGE_BILLING',
              mode: 'immersive',
            })
          }}
        >
          Manage billing
        </LinkButton>
      )
    )

  const secondaryAction =
    canUpgradePlan && canPurchaseAdditionalQuota && !premiumInteractionsQuotaExceeded ? (
      <Link
        as="a"
        href={ENABLE_ADDITIONAL_REQUESTS_URL}
        onClick={() => {
          sendEvent('dotcom_chat.activate', {
            target: 'QUOTA_METER_BANNER_LINK_OVERAGES',
            mode: 'immersive',
          })
        }}
      >
        Enable additional requests
      </Link>
    ) : null

  return (
    <div className={className}>
      <Banner
        variant="info"
        title="Limit reached soon"
        description={description}
        hideTitle
        primaryAction={primaryAction}
        secondaryAction={secondaryAction}
        onDismiss={() => {
          onDismiss()
          sendEvent('dotcom_chat.activate', {
            target: 'QUOTA_METER_BANNER_DISMISS',
            mode: 'immersive',
          })
        }}
      />
    </div>
  )
}

import {RepoSelectPanel} from '@github-ui/copilot-chat/components/RepoSelectPanel'
import {useChatManager} from '@github-ui/copilot-chat/CopilotChatManagerContext'
import {useLastUsedRepository} from '@github-ui/copilot-chat/hooks/use-last-used-repository'
import {isFileReference} from '@github-ui/copilot-chat/utils/copilot-chat-helpers'
import type {CopilotChatRepo} from '@github-ui/copilot-chat/utils/copilot-chat-types'
import {copilotFeatureFlags} from '@github-ui/copilot-chat/utils/copilot-feature-flags'
import {RefSelector} from '@github-ui/ref-selector'
import {usePreviousValue} from '@github-ui/use-previous-value'
import {
  ChevronDownIcon,
  CodeIcon,
  GitPullRequestIcon,
  IssueOpenedIcon,
  SidebarCollapseIcon,
} from '@primer/octicons-react'
import {Button, IconButton, UnderlineNav} from '@primer/react'
import {clsx} from 'clsx'
import {useCallback, useEffect, useRef, useState} from 'react'

import type {PreviewableContent} from '../ContentPreview/content-preview-types'
import {useContentPreview} from '../ContentPreview/ContentPreviewContext'
import {FileTab} from './FileTab'
import styles from './ResourcePanel.module.css'

interface ResourcePanelProps {
  className?: string
}

export function ResourcePanel({className}: ResourcePanelProps) {
  const {closeResourcePanel} = useContentPreview()
  const [repoPanelOpen, setRepoPanelOpen] = useState(false)
  const {fetchError, loading, selectedBranch, selectedRepo, setSelectedBranch, setSelectedRepo} = useSelectedRepo()
  const [selectedTab, setSelectedTab] = useState<'files' | 'pull-requests' | 'issues'>('files')
  const slimLineTopTabs = copilotFeatureFlags.copilotWorkbenchSlimLineTopTabs

  const repoPanelAnchorRef = useRef<HTMLButtonElement>(null)
  const repoName = selectedRepo ? `${selectedRepo.ownerLogin}/${selectedRepo.name}` : 'Select Repo'

  return (
    <div
      className={clsx(styles.resourcePanel, {[styles.slimLineTopTabs]: slimLineTopTabs}, className)}
      data-testid="resource-panel"
    >
      <div className={styles.resourcePanelHeader}>
        <div className={styles.resourcePanelHeaderRow}>
          <Button
            ref={repoPanelAnchorRef}
            onClick={() => setRepoPanelOpen(true)}
            variant="invisible"
            trailingVisual={ChevronDownIcon}
            className={styles.resourcePanelRepoButton}
          >
            {repoName}
          </Button>
          <RepoSelectPanel
            selectionVariant="instant"
            open={repoPanelOpen}
            onOpenChange={setRepoPanelOpen}
            selectedRepoIds={new Set()}
            // eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/require-await
            onSelectRepo={async (repoId: number) => {
              if (!repoId) return
              setSelectedRepo(repoId)
            }}
            submitReturnFocusRef={repoPanelAnchorRef}
            cancelReturnFocusRef={repoPanelAnchorRef}
            anchorRef={repoPanelAnchorRef}
            includeForks
          />
          <div className="d-flex flex-justify-end">
            <IconButton
              size="small"
              icon={SidebarCollapseIcon}
              aria-label="Close resource panel"
              onClick={closeResourcePanel}
              variant="invisible"
            />
          </div>
        </div>
        <div className={styles.resourcePanelHeaderRow}>
          {selectedRepo && (
            <RefSelector
              cacheKey="anything"
              canCreate={false}
              closeOnSelect
              currentCommitish={selectedBranch ?? selectedRepo.defaultBranch}
              defaultBranch={selectedRepo.defaultBranch}
              onSelectItem={ref => {
                setSelectedBranch(ref)
              }}
              owner={selectedRepo.ownerLogin}
              repo={selectedRepo.name}
              selectedRefType="branch"
            />
          )}
        </div>
        <UnderlineNav aria-label="Resource panel navigation">
          <UnderlineNav.Item
            aria-current={selectedTab === 'files' ? 'location' : undefined}
            aria-label="Files"
            onSelect={e => {
              setSelectedTab('files')
              e.preventDefault()
            }}
            className={styles.resourcePanelNavItem}
          >
            <CodeIcon />
          </UnderlineNav.Item>
          <UnderlineNav.Item
            aria-current={selectedTab === 'pull-requests' ? 'location' : undefined}
            aria-label="Pull requests"
            onSelect={e => {
              setSelectedTab('pull-requests')
              e.preventDefault()
            }}
            className={styles.resourcePanelNavItem}
          >
            <GitPullRequestIcon />
          </UnderlineNav.Item>
          <UnderlineNav.Item
            aria-current={selectedTab === 'issues' ? 'location' : undefined}
            aria-label="Issues"
            onSelect={e => {
              setSelectedTab('issues')
              e.preventDefault()
            }}
            className={styles.resourcePanelNavItem}
          >
            <IssueOpenedIcon />
          </UnderlineNav.Item>
        </UnderlineNav>
      </div>
      <div className={styles.resourcePanelContent}>
        {selectedTab === 'files' ? (
          <FileTab
            fetchError={fetchError}
            loading={loading}
            selectedBranch={selectedBranch}
            selectedRepo={selectedRepo}
          />
        ) : (
          <div className={styles.resourcePanelPlaceholder}>Coming soon...</div>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

function repoFromPreviewableContent(item: PreviewableContent | undefined): number | null {
  if (item?.type === 'file' && isFileReference(item.reference)) {
    return item.reference.repoID
  }
  return null
}

function useSelectedRepo() {
  const manager = useChatManager()
  const {items, selectedItem} = useContentPreview()
  const previousSelectedItem = usePreviousValue(selectedItem)
  const [lastUsedRepository, setLastUsedRepository] = useLastUsedRepository()
  const [fetchError, setFetchError] = useState<string>('')
  const [selectedRepo, setSelectedRepo] = useState<{
    branch: string | undefined
    repo: CopilotChatRepo | null
    repoID: number
  }>(() => {
    const selected = selectedItem && items.get(selectedItem)
    const repoID = repoFromPreviewableContent(selected)
    return repoID
      ? {repo: null, repoID, branch: undefined}
      : lastUsedRepository
        ? {repo: lastUsedRepository, repoID: lastUsedRepository.id, branch: lastUsedRepository.defaultBranch} // TODO: extend lastUsedRepository to store last used branch
        : {repo: null, repoID: -1, branch: undefined}
  })

  const selectRepo = useCallback(
    (repoID: number, branch?: string) => {
      // if we have the repo cached, use it so there's no flicker while loading
      const cached = manager.service.getCachedRepo(repoID)
      if (cached) {
        setSelectedRepo({repo: cached, repoID, branch: branch ?? cached.defaultBranch})
        setLastUsedRepository(cached)
      } else {
        setSelectedRepo({repo: null, repoID, branch})
      }
    },
    [manager.service, setLastUsedRepository],
  )

  const selectBranch = useCallback((branch: string) => {
    setSelectedRepo(prev => ({...prev, branch}))
  }, [])

  // if the selected item has changed and it's associated with a different repo, update selectedRepo
  if (previousSelectedItem !== selectedItem && selectedItem) {
    const selected = items.get(selectedItem)
    const newRepoID = repoFromPreviewableContent(selected)
    if (newRepoID && newRepoID !== selectedRepo.repoID) {
      selectRepo(newRepoID)
    }
  }

  useEffect(() => {
    // fetch repo if we have an id but no repo
    const fetchRepo = async (repoId: number) => {
      const result = await manager.service.fetchRepo(repoId)
      if (result.ok && result.payload) {
        setSelectedRepo(sr => ({
          repo: result.payload,
          repoID: result.payload.id,
          branch: sr.branch ?? result.payload.defaultBranch,
        }))
        setLastUsedRepository(result.payload)
      } else if (result.error) {
        setFetchError(result.error)
      }
    }
    if (selectedRepo.repoID > 0 && !selectedRepo.repo) {
      void fetchRepo(selectedRepo.repoID)
    }
  }, [manager, selectedRepo, setLastUsedRepository])

  return {
    fetchError,
    loading: selectedRepo.repoID > 0 && !selectedRepo.repo,
    selectedBranch: selectedRepo.branch,
    selectedRepo: selectedRepo.repo,
    setSelectedBranch: selectBranch,
    setSelectedRepo: selectRepo,
  }
}

import type {CopilotSpaceResource, CopilotSpaceRepositoryBackedResource} from '../types'
import type {OwnerItem} from '../components/SpacesOwnerDropdown'

interface ResourcesForDuplicationResult {
  resourcesToSave: CopilotSpaceResource[]
  resourcesThatWillNotTransfer: CopilotSpaceRepositoryBackedResource[]
}

/**
 * Determines which resources can be saved when duplicating a space to a new owner
 * and which will not transfer due to access restrictions.
 *
 * Private resources owned by a different organization/user than the target owner
 * will not be transferable if the target owner is an Organization.
 *
 * If the target owner is an individual user, all resources can be transferred.
 *
 * @param resources - The resources from the original space
 * @param targetOwner - The new owner for the duplicated space
 * @returns An object containing resources that can be saved and those that cannot transfer
 */
export function resourcesForDuplication(
  resources: CopilotSpaceResource[],
  targetOwner: OwnerItem,
): ResourcesForDuplicationResult {
  const resourcesToSave: CopilotSpaceResource[] = []
  const resourcesThatWillNotTransfer: CopilotSpaceRepositoryBackedResource[] = []

  if (targetOwner.type === 'Organization' && !targetOwner.allowsPrivateReposFromOtherOrgs) {
    // If the target owner is an organization that does NOT allow private repos from other orgs, we need to check for organization-level access
    for (const resource of resources) {
      // Check if this is a repository-backed resource with ownership and privacy info
      if ('ownerId' in resource && 'private' in resource && resource.private && resource.ownerId !== targetOwner.id) {
        // Private resource owned by different owner - cannot transfer
        resourcesThatWillNotTransfer.push(resource)
      } else {
        // Resource can be saved
        resourcesToSave.push(resource)
      }
    }

    return {
      resourcesToSave,
      resourcesThatWillNotTransfer,
    }
  } else {
    // If the target owner is a user then they can save all resources, even if the resource is org-owned and private.
    // This is because the resources in the space are already filtered to what the current user can see, so we can safely
    // assume duplicating the current user will preserve access to all resources.
    return {
      resourcesToSave: resources,
      resourcesThatWillNotTransfer: [],
    }
  }
}

// eslint-disable-next-line @github-ui/github-monorepo/restrict-relay-imports
import {CompactCommentButton} from '@github-ui/commenting/CompactCommentButton'
// eslint-disable-next-line @github-ui/github-monorepo/restrict-relay-imports
import {IDS} from '@github-ui/commenting/DomElements'
import {ssrSafeWindow} from '@github-ui/ssr-utils'
import {useAnalytics} from '@github-ui/use-analytics'
import {Banner} from '@primer/react'
import {useRef, useState} from 'react'
import {useCreateReplyCommentMutation} from '@github-ui/pull-requests/use-create-reply-comment-mutation'

import {anchorComment} from '../../helpers/anchor-helpers'
import {generateSuggestedChangeLineRangeFromDiffThread} from '../../helpers/suggested-changes'
import {type CommentAuthor, CommentReviewVariantType, type Thread} from '../../conversation-types'
import type {AddCommentEditorProps} from './AddCommentEditor'
import {AddCommentEditor} from './AddCommentEditor'
import {AutomatedReviewThreadComment, AutomatedReviewThreadCommentErrorFallback} from './AutomatedReviewThreadComment'
import styles from './ReviewThread.module.css'
import {ReviewThreadComment, type ReviewThreadCommentProps} from './ReviewThreadComment'
import type {FileKey, LineKey} from '../../types'
import {ErrorBoundary} from '@github-ui/react-core/error-boundary'
import {usePullRequestBasePath, useSelectedRange} from '@github-ui/pull-requests/pull-request-store-selectors'

export interface ReviewThreadProps
  extends
    Pick<
      ReviewThreadCommentProps,
      'disableDialogMode' | 'enableDialogMode' | 'gridCellId' | 'isInDialogMode' | 'viewerData'
    >,
    Pick<AddCommentEditorProps, 'batchingEnabled' | 'suggestedChangesConfig'> {
  commentAnchorPrefix?: string
  filePath: string
  isInlineComment: boolean
  onDeleteLineComment?: () => void
  thread: Thread
  lineMarkersKey: LineKey | FileKey
  threadPositionNumber?: number
  threadsConnectionId?: string
  shouldLimitHeight?: boolean
  ghostUser?: CommentAuthor
}

/**
 * Renders a given review thread's comments along with a reply comment box.
 *
 * TODO:
 * - Support paginated loading of large review threads.
 * - Better support for deleted comment authors.
 */
export function ReviewThread({
  batchingEnabled,
  commentAnchorPrefix,
  lineMarkersKey,
  disableDialogMode,
  enableDialogMode,
  filePath,
  gridCellId,
  isInlineComment,
  isInDialogMode,
  onDeleteLineComment,
  thread,
  threadPositionNumber,
  shouldLimitHeight = true,
  suggestedChangesConfig,
  viewerData,
  ghostUser,
}: ReviewThreadProps) {
  const [isReplying, setIsReplying] = useState(false)
  const [quoteReplyText, setQuoteReplyText] = useState<string | undefined>(undefined)
  const {sendAnalyticsEvent} = useAnalytics()
  const basePath = usePullRequestBasePath()

  const replyButtonRef = useRef<HTMLButtonElement>(null)
  const replyContainerRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null)

  const {mutate: addReplyMutation} = useCreateReplyCommentMutation(filePath, lineMarkersKey)
  const {baseOid: startOid, headOid: endOid} = useSelectedRange()

  const addReplyComment = ({
    commentText,
    onCompleted,
    onError,
    submitBatch,
  }: {
    commentText: string
    onCompleted?: (commentDatabaseId?: number) => void
    onError: (error: Error) => void
    submitBatch?: boolean
  }) => {
    const handleCompleted = (commentDatabaseId: number | null | undefined) => {
      if (commentDatabaseId) anchorComment(commentDatabaseId.toString())
      onCompleted?.()
    }

    const lastComment = thread.commentsData?.comments[thread.commentsData?.comments.length - 1]
    const inReplyTo = lastComment?.databaseId
    addReplyMutation(
      {
        text: commentText,
        submitBatch,
        inReplyTo,
        path: filePath,
        comparisonEndOid: endOid,
        comparisonStartOid: startOid,
      },
      {
        onError,
        onSuccess: data => handleCompleted(data.comment.databaseId),
      },
    )

    sendAnalyticsEvent('comments.add', 'ADD_COMMENT_BUTTON')
  }

  const cancelComment = () => {
    setIsReplying(false)
    setQuoteReplyText(undefined)
    sendAnalyticsEvent('comments.cancel_thread_reply', 'CANCEL_REVIEW_THREAD_BUTTON')
    ssrSafeWindow?.requestAnimationFrame(() => replyButtonRef.current?.focus())
  }

  const onQuoteReply = (text?: string) => {
    setQuoteReplyText(text)
    setIsReplying(true)
  }

  if (!thread.commentsData || thread.commentsData.comments === null || thread.commentsData.comments.length < 1)
    return null
  const lineRange = generateSuggestedChangeLineRangeFromDiffThread(thread)
  const applySuggestedChangesValidationData =
    thread.subjectType?.toLowerCase() === 'line'
      ? {
          lineRange,
        }
      : undefined

  const handleReplyContainerFocus = (e: React.FocusEvent) => {
    // Only handle focus logic if a user is on the reply container's first <div> element.
    if (replyContainerRef.current !== e.target) return

    if (isReplying) {
      // Focus on the textarea element inside of the reply container
      replyContainerRef.current?.querySelector('textarea')?.focus()
    } else {
      // Focus on the Write a reply button.
      replyButtonRef.current?.focus()
    }
  }

  const isAutomatedComment = (comment: (typeof thread.commentsData.comments)[0], index: number) => {
    return comment.reviewVariantType === CommentReviewVariantType.Automated && index === 0
  }

  return (
    <div data-testid="review-thread">
      <div className={styles.ReviewThreadContainer} data-limit-height={shouldLimitHeight}>
        {thread.commentsData.comments.map((comment, index) => {
          if (isAutomatedComment(comment, index)) {
            return (
              <ErrorBoundary
                key={comment.id}
                fallback={<AutomatedReviewThreadCommentErrorFallback />}
                boundaryName="AutomatedReviewThreadComment"
              >
                <AutomatedReviewThreadComment
                  key={comment.id}
                  anchorPrefix={commentAnchorPrefix}
                  basePath={basePath}
                  comment={comment}
                  isAnchorable
                  isInDialogMode={isInDialogMode}
                  isOutdated={thread.positioning?.isOutdated}
                  onQuoteReply={onQuoteReply}
                  threadId={thread.id}
                  threadPositionNumber={threadPositionNumber}
                />
              </ErrorBoundary>
            )
          }
          return (
            <ReviewThreadComment
              lineMarkersKey={lineMarkersKey}
              isAnchorable
              index={index}
              threadPositionNumber={threadPositionNumber}
              isFirstComment={index === 0}
              isInlineComment={isInlineComment}
              isLastChild={index === (thread.commentsData?.comments.length ?? 0) - 1}
              key={comment.id}
              anchorPrefix={commentAnchorPrefix}
              comment={comment}
              commentConnectionId={thread.commentsData?.__id}
              disableDialogMode={disableDialogMode}
              enableDialogMode={enableDialogMode}
              filePath={filePath}
              gridCellId={gridCellId}
              isInDialogMode={isInDialogMode}
              isOutdated={thread.positioning?.isOutdated}
              threadId={thread.id}
              onDeleteLineComment={onDeleteLineComment}
              onQuoteReply={onQuoteReply}
              threadCommentCount={thread.commentsData?.comments.length}
              isThreadResolved={!!thread.isResolved}
              suggestedChangesConfig={suggestedChangesConfig}
              applySuggestedChangesValidationData={applySuggestedChangesValidationData}
              viewerData={viewerData}
              ghostUser={ghostUser}
            />
          )
        })}
        {thread.reviewCommentsLimitExceeded && (
          <Banner
            aria-label="Warning"
            title="Warning"
            variant="warning"
            hideTitle
            description={`Only the first ${(thread.reviewCommentsLimit || 0) - 1} replies are currently being shown.`}
            className="tmp-m-3"
          />
        )}
        {thread.viewerCanReply && (
          <div
            id={IDS.issueCommentComposer}
            className="rounded-bottom-2 p-2 bgColor-inset"
            data-marker-navigation-comment-thread-id={thread.id}
            data-marker-navigation-comment-id={`${thread.id}-reply-comment`}
            data-marker-navigation-thread-reply
            // eslint-disable-next-line jsx-a11y/no-noninteractive-tabindex
            tabIndex={isInDialogMode ? 0 : -1}
            onFocus={handleReplyContainerFocus}
            ref={replyContainerRef}
          >
            {isReplying && (
              <AddCommentEditor
                startLineNumber={lineRange?.startLineNumber}
                lineNumber={lineRange?.endLineNumber}
                batchingEnabled={batchingEnabled}
                condensed={false}
                fileLevelComment
                filePath={filePath}
                focusOnMount
                isReplying
                onCancelComment={cancelComment}
                quotedText={quoteReplyText}
                threadId={thread.id}
                onAddComment={addReplyComment}
                suggestedChangesConfig={suggestedChangesConfig}
              />
            )}
            {!isReplying && (
              <CompactCommentButton
                ref={replyButtonRef}
                onClick={() => {
                  sendAnalyticsEvent('comments.start_thread_reply', 'REPLY_TO_THREAD_INPUT_BUTTON')
                  setIsReplying(true)
                }}
              >
                Write a reply
              </CompactCommentButton>
            )}
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

import {CommentBoxButton, type CommentBoxHandle} from '@github-ui/comment-box/CommentBox'
// eslint-disable-next-line @github-ui/github-monorepo/restrict-relay-imports
import type {ActivityHeaderHeadingProps} from '@github-ui/commenting/ActivityHeader'
// eslint-disable-next-line @github-ui/github-monorepo/restrict-relay-imports
import {ActivityHeader, CommentAuthorTypes} from '@github-ui/commenting/ActivityHeader'
// eslint-disable-next-line @github-ui/github-monorepo/restrict-relay-imports
import {CommentActions} from '@github-ui/commenting/CommentActions'
// eslint-disable-next-line @github-ui/github-monorepo/restrict-relay-imports
import {VALUES} from '@github-ui/commenting/Values'
import {CopilotCodeReviewFeedback} from '@github-ui/copilot-code-review-feedback/CopilotCodeReviewFeedback'
import {isFeatureEnabled} from '@github-ui/feature-flags'
import {noop} from '@github-ui/noop'
import type {ReactionContent} from '@github-ui/reaction-viewer/ReactionGroupsUtils'
import {ReactionViewerBase} from '@github-ui/reaction-viewer/ReactionViewerBase'
import type {SafeHTMLString} from '@github-ui/safe-html'
import {VerifiedHTMLBox} from '@github-ui/safe-html/VerifiedHTML'
import {ssrSafeLocation, ssrSafeWindow} from '@github-ui/ssr-utils'
// eslint-disable-next-line no-restricted-imports
import {useToastContext} from '@github-ui/toast/ToastContext'
import {StopIcon} from '@primer/octicons-react'
import {Banner, Flash, ConfirmationDialog, Link} from '@primer/react'
import {clsx} from 'clsx'
import {useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState} from 'react'
import {useBlockUserFromOrgMutation} from '@github-ui/pull-requests/use-block-user-from-org-mutation'
import {useDeleteReviewCommentMutation} from '@github-ui/pull-requests/use-delete-review-comment-mutation'
import {useHideCommentMutation} from '@github-ui/pull-requests/use-hide-comment-mutation'
import {useReactToCommentMutation} from '@github-ui/pull-requests/use-react-to-comment-mutation'
import {useUnblockUserFromOrgMutation} from '@github-ui/pull-requests/use-unblock-user-from-org-mutation'
import {useUnhideCommentMutation} from '@github-ui/pull-requests/use-unhide-comment-mutation'
import {useUpdateReviewCommentMutation} from '@github-ui/pull-requests/use-update-review-comment-mutation'
// eslint-disable-next-line @github-ui/github-monorepo/restrict-relay-imports
import {RelayEnvironmentProvider} from 'react-relay/hooks'

import {useCommentAnchor} from '../../hooks/use-comment-anchor'
import {usePersistedDiffCommentData} from '../../hooks/use-persisted-comment-data'
import type {
  ApplySuggestedChangesValidationData,
  Comment,
  CommentAuthor,
  SuggestedChangesConfiguration,
  ViewerData,
} from '../../conversation-types'
import {validateSuggestedChange} from '../../util/validate-suggested-change'
import {ConversationCommentBox} from './ConversationCommentBox'
import styles from './ReviewThreadComment.module.css'
import {SuggestedChangeView} from './SuggestedChangeView'
import type {DisableDialogModeFn, EnterDialogModeFn} from '../../stores/slices/dialog-mode'
import type {FileKey, LineKey} from '../../types'
/* eslint-disable-next-line @github-ui/github-monorepo/restrict-relay-imports --
   preexisting violation https://github.com/github/react-platform/issues/1135 */
import {relayEnvironmentWithMissingFieldHandlerForNode} from '@github-ui/relay-environment'
