import {ScopedCommands} from '@github-ui/ui-commands'
// eslint-disable-next-line @github-ui/github-monorepo/restrict-relay-imports
import {selectQuoteFromComment} from '@github-ui/commenting/quotes'
import {usePullRequestBasePath} from '@github-ui/pull-requests/pull-request-store-selectors'

type CommentState = 'editing' | 'hidden' | 'visible'

export interface ReviewThreadCommentProps {
  anchorPrefix?: string
  comment: Comment
  commentConnectionId?: string
  disableDialogMode?: DisableDialogModeFn
  enableDialogMode?: EnterDialogModeFn
  filePath: string
  gridCellId?: string | undefined
  hideActions?: boolean
  index?: number
  /**
   * If true, the comment will scroll itself into view and show a blue border
   * when the component renders with a hash that matches the comment.
   */
  isAnchorable?: boolean
  isFirstComment?: boolean
  isInlineComment: boolean
  isInDialogMode?: boolean | undefined
  isLastChild?: boolean
  isOutdated?: boolean
  isThreadResolved: boolean
  lineMarkersKey: LineKey | FileKey
  onDeleteLineComment?: () => void
  onQuoteReply?: (quotedText?: string) => void
  suggestedChangesConfig?: SuggestedChangesConfiguration
  applySuggestedChangesValidationData?: ApplySuggestedChangesValidationData
  threadCommentCount?: number
  threadId: string
  threadPositionNumber?: number
  viewerData?: ViewerData
  ghostUser?: CommentAuthor
  originalDiffPathUri?: string | null
  headingLevel?: 'h1' | 'h2' | 'h3' | 'h4' | 'h5' | 'h6'
}

export function ReviewThreadComment({
  isAnchorable = false,
  index = 0,
  isInlineComment,
  isLastChild,
  isOutdated,
  isThreadResolved,
  lineMarkersKey,
  anchorPrefix = 'r',
  comment,
  disableDialogMode,
  enableDialogMode,
  filePath,
  gridCellId,
  hideActions,
  isFirstComment = false,
  isInDialogMode,
  onDeleteLineComment,
  onQuoteReply = noop,
  threadCommentCount,
  threadId,
  threadPositionNumber,
  suggestedChangesConfig,
  applySuggestedChangesValidationData,
  viewerData,
  ghostUser = VALUES.ghostUser,
  originalDiffPathUri,
  headingLevel = 'h3',
}: ReviewThreadCommentProps): React.ReactElement {
  const {addToast} = useToastContext()
  const [isEditing, setIsEditing] = useState(false)
  const [isMinimized, setIsMinimized] = useState(comment.isHidden)
  const [deleteConfirmationVisible, setDeleteConfirmationVisible] = useState(false)
  const [bodyText, setBodyText] = useState(comment.body)
  const [isSubmitting, setIsSubmitting] = useState(false)
  const [isDeleting, setIsDeleting] = useState(false)
  const [errorMessage, setErrorMessage] = useState<string | undefined>('')
  const basePath = usePullRequestBasePath()
  const {persistCommentToStorage, removePersistedCommentFromStorage} = usePersistedDiffCommentData({
    subjectId: basePath,
    filePath,
    fileLevelComment: comment.subjectType === 'FILE',
    threadId: comment.id,
    handlePersistedCommentExists: ({text}) => {
      if (!text) return
      setBodyText(text)
    },
  })
  const commentBoxRef = useRef<CommentBoxHandle>(null)
  const commentBodyRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null)
  const reactionButtonRef = useRef<HTMLButtonElement>(null)
  const [visibilityErrorMessage, setVisibilityErrorMessage] = useState('')

  const focusCommentBox = () => {
    commentBoxRef.current?.focus()
  }

  useEffect(() => {
    if (isEditing) {
      const timeout = window.setTimeout(focusCommentBox)

      return () => {
        window.clearTimeout(timeout)
      }
    }
  }, [isEditing])

  const {mutate: deleteReviewCommentMutation} = useDeleteReviewCommentMutation({
    filePath,
    lineMarkersKey,
    onSuccess: () => {
      setDeleteConfirmationVisible(false)
      setIsDeleting(false)
      onDeleteLineComment?.()
    },
  })

  const onDelete = async () => {
    // when InlineCommentDialogMode is enabled, keyboard interactions are trapped in that div
    // we disable here so we can use keyboard interactions in the confirm dialog, which is outside the div
    disableDialogMode?.()
    setDeleteConfirmationVisible(true)
  }

  const handleDeleteDialogClose = (gesture: 'confirm' | 'cancel' | 'close-button' | 'escape') => {
    if (gesture === 'confirm') {
      setIsDeleting(true)

      deleteReviewCommentMutation(
        {commentId: comment.id, threadId, filePath},
        {
          onError: () => {
            setDeleteConfirmationVisible(false)
            // eslint-disable-next-line @github-ui/dotcom-primer/toast-migration
            addToast({
              type: 'error',
              message: 'Failed to delete comment',
            })
            setIsDeleting(false)
          },
        },
      )
    } else {
      // when InlineCommentDialogMode is enabled, keyboard interactions are trapped in that div
      // we re-enable here so that keyboard interactions work again after the dialog is closed
      if (gridCellId) enableDialogMode?.(gridCellId)
      setDeleteConfirmationVisible(false)
      setIsDeleting(false)
    }
  }

  const {mutate: hideCommentMutation} = useHideCommentMutation(filePath, lineMarkersKey)

  const onHide = (reason: string) => {
    if (!comment.databaseId) throw new Error('Comment not found')

    hideCommentMutation(
      {commentDatabaseId: comment.databaseId, reason},
      {
        onError: () => {
          setIsSubmitting(false)
          setVisibilityErrorMessage('Failed to hide comment')
        },
        onSuccess: () => setIsMinimized(true),
      },
    )
  }

  const {mutate: unhideCommentMutation} = useUnhideCommentMutation(filePath, lineMarkersKey)

  const onUnhide = () => {
    if (!comment.databaseId) throw new Error('Comment not found')

    unhideCommentMutation(
      {commentDatabaseId: comment.databaseId},
      {
        onError: () => {
          setIsSubmitting(false)
          setVisibilityErrorMessage('Failed to unhide comment')
        },
        onSuccess: () => setIsMinimized(false),
      },
    )
  }

  const {mutate: blockUserFromOrgMutation} = useBlockUserFromOrgMutation()

  const onBlock = (
    duration: string,
    shouldHideComment: boolean,
    hiddenReason: string | undefined,
    organizationLogin: string,
    notifyBlockedUser: boolean,
    userLogin: string,
  ) =>
    blockUserFromOrgMutation(
      {
        duration,
        shouldHideComment,
        hiddenReason,
        organizationLogin,
        notifyBlockedUser,
        userLogin,
      },
      {
        onError: () => setVisibilityErrorMessage('Failed to block user'),
        onSuccess: () => {
          if (shouldHideComment) {
            setIsMinimized(true)
          }
        },
      },
    )

  const {mutate: unblockUserFromOrgMutation} = useUnblockUserFromOrgMutation()

  const onUnblock = (organizationLogin: string, userLogin: string) =>
    unblockUserFromOrgMutation(
      {organizationLogin, userLogin},
      {onError: () => setVisibilityErrorMessage('Failed to unblock user')},
    )

  const {mutate: reactToCommentMutation} = useReactToCommentMutation(filePath, lineMarkersKey)

  const onReact = (reaction: ReactionContent, viewerHasReacted: boolean) => {
    if (!comment.databaseId) throw new Error('Comment not found')

    setTimeout(() => reactionButtonRef.current?.focus(), 0)
    reactToCommentMutation(
      {
        commentDatabaseId: comment.databaseId,
        threadId,
        reaction,
        viewerHasReacted,
      },
      {
        onError: () => {
          setIsSubmitting(false)
          // eslint-disable-next-line @github-ui/dotcom-primer/toast-migration
          addToast({
            type: 'error',
            message: 'Failed to react to comment',
          })
        },
      },
    )
  }

  const onEdit = () => {
    setIsEditing(true)
  }

  const onCancel = () => {
    setIsEditing(false)
    removePersistedCommentFromStorage()
    setBodyText(comment.body)
  }

  const onChange = (newBody: string) => {
    persistCommentToStorage({text: newBody})
    setBodyText(newBody)
    setErrorMessage(undefined)
  }

  const {mutate: updateReviewCommentMutation} = useUpdateReviewCommentMutation({
    filePath,
    lineMarkersKey,
    onSuccess: () => {
      setIsEditing(false)
      setIsSubmitting(false)
      removePersistedCommentFromStorage()
    },
  })

  const onSave = useCallback(() => {
    setIsSubmitting(true)

    const suggestedChangedEvaluation = validateSuggestedChange(
      bodyText,
      suggestedChangesConfig?.sourceContentFromDiffLines ?? '',
    )
    if (!suggestedChangedEvaluation.isValid) {
      setErrorMessage(suggestedChangedEvaluation.errorMessage)
      setIsSubmitting(false)

      return
    }

    updateReviewCommentMutation(
      {commentId: String(comment.databaseId), body: bodyText, bodyVersion: comment.bodyVersion},
      {
        onError: () => {
          setIsSubmitting(false)
          // eslint-disable-next-line @github-ui/dotcom-primer/toast-migration
          addToast({
            type: 'error',
            message: 'Failed to update comment',
          })
        },
      },
    )
  }, [addToast, bodyText, comment, suggestedChangesConfig?.sourceContentFromDiffLines, updateReviewCommentMutation])

  // The unique identifier for this comment in the DOM
  const commentId = comment.databaseId?.toString()

  // The anchor portion of the URL that will target this specific comment when navigating to it
  // This is used to scroll the comment into view and highlight it when directly linked
  const commentAnchor = commentId ? `${anchorPrefix}${commentId}` : undefined

  // The full URL including anchor that points directly to this comment within the current page
  const pageScopedCommentHref =
    commentAnchor && ssrSafeLocation
      ? new URL(`${ssrSafeLocation.pathname}#${commentAnchor}`, ssrSafeLocation.origin).toString()
      : ''
  const containerRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null)

  useCommentAnchor({
    isAnchorable,
    commentId,
    commentAnchor,
    containerRef,
  })

  const isReply = index > 0
  const isNestedReply = index > 1

  const commentState: CommentState = isEditing ? 'editing' : isMinimized ? 'hidden' : 'visible'

  const commentActivityProps = {
    headingProps: {as: headingLevel} as ActivityHeaderHeadingProps,
    avatarUrl: comment.author?.avatarUrl ?? ghostUser.avatarUrl,
    comment: {
      ...comment,
      url: pageScopedCommentHref,
      referenceText: comment.reference.text ?? `#${comment.reference.number}`,
    },
    commentRef: containerRef,
    deleteComment: onDelete,
    editComment: onEdit,
    editHistoryComponent: undefined,
    hideComment: onHide,
    onBlock,
    onUnblock,
    onMinimize: setIsMinimized,
    onSuccessfulBlock: () => {
      //not ideal, but this is the easiest way to get all of the comments to reflect the newly updated state
      setTimeout(() => ssrSafeWindow?.location.reload(), 800)
    },
    isMinimized,
    commentAuthorLogin: comment.author?.login ?? ghostUser.login,
    navigate: noop,
    commentSubjectAuthorLogin: comment.reference?.author?.login ?? '',
    commentSubjectType: 'pull request',
    onReplySelect: onQuoteReply,
    unhideComment: onUnhide,
    hideActions,
    isReply,
    forceInlineAvatar: true,
    containerStyle: {},
    originalDiffPathUri,
    isOutdated,
    commentAuthorSlug: comment.reviewVariantType === 'copilot' ? 'copilot-pull-request-reviewer' : undefined,
    commentAuthorType: comment.reviewVariantType === 'copilot' ? CommentAuthorTypes.Bot : undefined,
  }

  const getCommentAriaLabel = (): string => {
    if (isReply) {
      return threadPositionNumber !== undefined ? `Reply ${index} to Comment ${threadPositionNumber}` : `Reply ${index}`
    } else if (threadPositionNumber !== undefined) {
      return `Comment ${threadPositionNumber}`
    } else {
      return 'Comment'
    }
  }

  const quoteReplyCommand = () => {
    if (isEditing) {
      // Don't trigger a reply when typing 'r' while editing
      return
    }

    const selection = ssrSafeWindow?.getSelection()

    const selectionContext = selection?.anchorNode
      ? {anchorNode: selection.anchorNode, range: selection.getRangeAt(0)}
      : null

    const newValue = selectQuoteFromComment(containerRef?.current, selectionContext, comment.body)
    onQuoteReply(newValue)
  }

  const environment = useMemo(() => relayEnvironmentWithMissingFieldHandlerForNode(), [])

  return (
    <ScopedCommands
      commands={{
        'pull-requests-diff-view:quote-reply': quoteReplyCommand,
      }}
    >
      <div
        ref={containerRef}
        id={isAnchorable ? commentAnchor : undefined}
        // eslint-disable-next-line jsx-a11y/no-noninteractive-tabindex -- tabIndex needed for keyboard navigation in grid
        tabIndex={isInlineComment ? (isInDialogMode ? 0 : -1) : 0}
        data-first-thread-comment={isFirstComment}
        data-marker-navigation-comment-thread-id={threadId}
        data-marker-navigation-comment-id={comment.id}
        // When aria-label and role is set while still in gridcell mode, the browser doesn't the respect the
        // aria-hidden"true" and we end up with the aria-labels leaking into the accessible name of the gridcell,
        // which is not what we want. So we should only set these attributes while in dialog mode.
        {...(isInDialogMode
          ? {
              role: 'document',
              'aria-roledescription': 'comment',
              'aria-label': getCommentAriaLabel(),
            }
          : {})}
        className={clsx(
          styles.ReviewThreadContainer,
          isAnchorable && styles.anchorable,
          commentState === 'hidden' && 'pb-2',
          isReply && styles.isReply,
        )}
      >
        {isNestedReply && (
          <div className={styles.ReviewThreadWrapper} data-is-reply={isReply}>
            <div className={styles.ReviewThreadBox} />
          </div>
        )}
        <div className={styles.ReviewThreadInnerContainer} data-is-nested-reply={isNestedReply}>
          {comment.reviewVariantType === 'code_scanning' &&
            isFeatureEnabled('code_scanning_dfa_degraded_experience_notice') && (
              <Banner variant="info" hideTitle title="Code scanning notice" className="mt-2 tmp-mb-3">
                <Banner.Description>
                  <Link inline href={basePath}>
                    View in Conversation tab
                  </Link>{' '}
                  for the full code scanning experience.
                </Banner.Description>
              </Banner>
            )}
          <ActivityHeader
            {...commentActivityProps}
            commentBody={comment.body}
            threadCommentCount={threadCommentCount}
            isInDialogMode={isInDialogMode}
            actions={
              hideActions ? undefined : (
                <RelayEnvironmentProvider environment={environment}>
                  <CommentActions {...commentActivityProps} />
                </RelayEnvironmentProvider>
              )
            }
            isOutdated={isOutdated}
          />
        </div>
        {visibilityErrorMessage && (
          <Flash variant="danger" className="tmp-m-3">
            <StopIcon className="mr-2" />
            {visibilityErrorMessage}
          </Flash>
        )}
        <div className={styles.ReviewThreadWrapper} data-is-reply={isReply}>
          {isReply && <div className={styles.ReplyWrapper} data-is-last-child={isLastChild} />}

          {commentState === 'editing' && (
            <div className={clsx('flex-1 min-width-0 pr-2 pt-1 pb-2', isReply ? 'pl-0' : 'pl-2')}>
              <ConversationCommentBox
                ref={commentBoxRef}
                label="Update comment"
                value={bodyText}
                onChange={onChange}
                onPrimaryAction={onSave}
                suggestedChangesConfig={suggestedChangesConfig}
                markdownErrorMessage={errorMessage}
              >
                <CommentBoxButton variant="default" onClick={onCancel}>
                  Cancel
                </CommentBoxButton>
                <CommentBoxButton
                  disabled={isSubmitting || !bodyText.length}
                  loading={isSubmitting}
                  variant="primary"
                  onClick={onSave}
                >
                  Update
                </CommentBoxButton>
              </ConversationCommentBox>
            </div>
          )}

          {commentState === 'visible' && (
            <div className={styles.BodyHTMLContainer} data-is-reply={isReply}>
              {comment.bodyHTML && (
                <>
                  <VerifiedHTMLBox
                    ref={commentBodyRef}
                    className={clsx('markdown-body', styles.SafeHTMLBox, styles.SafeHTMLBoxWrapped)}
                    comment-testid={`Comment body html for comment ${comment.id}`}
                    /* eslint-disable-next-line @github-ui/github-monorepo/no-cast-as-safe-html-string -- HTML is sanitized server-side. TODO: Move type assertion to data ingress point. */
                    html={comment.bodyHTML as SafeHTMLString}
                  />
                  {comment.reviewVariantType === 'copilot' && (
                    <p className="text-small color-fg-muted mt-2 mb-1">
                      <a
                        target="_blank"
                        rel="noopener noreferrer"
                        className="Link--inTextBlock"
                        href="https://docs.github.com/en/copilot/responsible-use-of-github-copilot-features/responsible-use-of-github-copilot-code-review"
                      >
                        Copilot
                      </a>{' '}
                      uses AI. Check for mistakes.
                    </p>
                  )}
                  <div className="d-flex flex-direction-column gap-2">
                    {comment.reviewVariantType === 'copilot' && (
                      <div className="mt-2">
                        <CopilotCodeReviewFeedback
                          commentUrl={comment.url}
                          commentId={comment.databaseId?.toString() ?? ''}
                          showNegativeFeedbackModal={false}
                        />
                      </div>
                    )}
                    {comment.reactionGroups && (
                      <div className="d-flex flex-direction-column mt-2">
                        <ReactionViewerBase
                          ref={reactionButtonRef}
                          reactionGroups={comment.reactionGroups}
                          onReact={onReact}
                          canReact={comment.viewerCanReact}
                        />
                      </div>
                    )}
                  </div>
                  {applySuggestedChangesValidationData && (
                    <SuggestedChangeView
                      comment={comment}
                      commentBodyRef={commentBodyRef}
                      filePath={filePath}
                      isOutdated={isOutdated}
                      isThreadResolved={isThreadResolved}
                      databaseId={comment.databaseId}
                      suggestedChangesConfig={suggestedChangesConfig}
                      applySuggestedChangesValidationData={applySuggestedChangesValidationData}
                      viewerCanApplySuggestion={viewerData?.viewerCanApplySuggestion}
                    />
                  )}
                </>
              )}
              {deleteConfirmationVisible && (
                <ConfirmationDialog
                  title="Delete comment?"
                  confirmButtonContent="Delete"
                  confirmButtonType="danger"
                  confirmButtonLoading={isDeleting}
                  onClose={handleDeleteDialogClose}
                >
                  Are you sure you want to delete this comment?
                </ConfirmationDialog>
              )}
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    </ScopedCommands>
  )
}

import {createContext} from 'react'

export const RootElementContext = createContext<HTMLElement | undefined>(undefined)

import {Button} from '@primer/react'

import styles from './SpaceDialogFooter.module.css'

interface SpaceDialogFooterProps {
  onSave: () => void
  onCancel: () => void
  saveButtonText: string
  isLoading?: boolean
  loadingAnnouncement?: string
  disabled?: boolean
}

/**
 * This component renders a standardized footer with two buttons:
 * - A Cancel button that triggers the onCancel callback
 * - A Save button that triggers the onSave callback and can show loading/disabled states
 */
export function SpaceDialogFooter({
  onSave,
  onCancel,
  saveButtonText,
  isLoading = false,
  loadingAnnouncement = '',
  disabled = false,
}: SpaceDialogFooterProps) {
  return (
    <div className={styles.footerContainer}>
      <Button variant="default" onClick={onCancel}>
        Cancel
      </Button>
      <Button
        variant="primary"
        loading={isLoading}
        loadingAnnouncement={loadingAnnouncement}
        onClick={onSave}
        disabled={disabled}
      >
        {saveButtonText}
      </Button>
    </div>
  )
}

import {GitHubAvatar} from '@github-ui/github-avatar'
import {testIdProps} from '@github-ui/test-id-props'
import {verifiedFetchJSON} from '@github-ui/verified-fetch'
import {ActionList, ActionMenu, FormControl, Label} from '@primer/react'
import {useCallback, useEffect, useState} from 'react'

import styles from './SpacesOwnerDropdown.module.css'

export interface OwnerItem {
  name: string
  avatarUrl: string
  displayName: string
  id: number
  type: 'User' | 'Organization'
  allowsPrivateReposFromOtherOrgs?: boolean
}

interface SpacesOwnerDropdownProps {
  onSelect: (owner: OwnerItem) => void
  repoId?: number
}

export function SpacesOwnerDropdown({onSelect, repoId}: SpacesOwnerDropdownProps) {
  interface OwnersResult {
    owners: OwnerItem[]
  }

  const [ownerItems, setOwnerItems] = useState<OwnerItem[]>([])
  const [displayLoadingError, setDisplayLoadingError] = useState(false)
  const [selectedOwner, setSelectedOwner] = useState<OwnerItem | undefined>(undefined)

  const handleSelectOwner = useCallback(
    (owner: OwnerItem) => {
      setSelectedOwner(owner)
      onSelect(owner)
    },
    [onSelect],
  )

  const handleLoadingError = useCallback(() => {
    setOwnerItems([])
    setDisplayLoadingError(true)
  }, [])

  useEffect(() => {
    const loadOwners = async () => {
      try {
        const url = repoId
          ? `/github-copilot/chat/custom_copilots_owners?repo_id=${repoId}`
          : '/github-copilot/chat/custom_copilots_owners'

        const result = await verifiedFetchJSON(url)
        const data = (await result.json()) as OwnersResult
        const owners = data?.owners
        if (!result.ok || !owners) {
          handleLoadingError()
          return
        }
        setOwnerItems(owners)
        if (owners[0]) {
          handleSelectOwner(owners[0])
        }
      } catch {
        handleLoadingError()
      }
    }

    void loadOwners()
  }, [handleLoadingError, handleSelectOwner, repoId])

  const getCaption = () => {
    const screenReaderCaption = (owner: OwnerItem) => {
      return <span className="sr-only">Space owner. Required. Selected owner: @{owner.displayName}.</span>
    }

    if (!selectedOwner) {
      return (
        <span>
          <span className="sr-only">Space owner. Required.</span> Where we will store your space.
        </span>
      )
    } else if (selectedOwner.type === 'User') {
      return (
        <div className="mt-2 d-flex flex-row flex-shrink flex-items-center">
          {screenReaderCaption(selectedOwner)}
          <Label className="mr-1">Private</Label>
          <span {...testIdProps('owner-user-caption')}>Only you will be able to see this space.</span>
        </div>
      )
    } else {
      return (
        <div className="mt-2 d-flex flex-row flex-shrink">
          {screenReaderCaption(selectedOwner)}
          <Label className={styles.orgLabel}>Private</Label>
          <span {...testIdProps('owner-org-caption')}>
            Only visible to you and organization admins. You can share it later.
          </span>
        </div>
      )
    }
  }

  const headerButton = () => {
    const displayName = selectedOwner?.name || 'Choose an owner'
    const buttonAvatar = selectedOwner ? (
      <GitHubAvatar src={selectedOwner.avatarUrl} key={selectedOwner.avatarUrl} />
    ) : undefined

    return (
      <ActionMenu.Button
        data-testid="owner-dropdown-anchor"
        alignContent="start"
        aria-describedby="owner-caption"
        leadingVisual={buttonAvatar}
      >
        {displayName}
      </ActionMenu.Button>
    )
  }

  return (
    <div className="mt-2 mb-1">
      <FormControl>
        <FormControl.Label required>Owner</FormControl.Label>
        <ActionMenu>
          {headerButton()}
          <ActionMenu.Overlay width="large" maxHeight="large" className={styles.ownerDropdownOverlay}>
            <ActionList>
              <ActionList.Group className={styles.ownerListGroup}>
                <ActionList selectionVariant="single">
                  {ownerItems &&
                    ownerItems.map(item => {
                      const {name, avatarUrl, displayName} = item
                      return (
                        <ActionList.Item
                          key={name}
                          selected={name === selectedOwner?.name}
                          onSelect={() => handleSelectOwner(item)}
                          className={styles.ownerListItem}
                        >
                          <ActionList.LeadingVisual>
                            <GitHubAvatar src={avatarUrl} />
                          </ActionList.LeadingVisual>
                          {name}
                          <ActionList.Description>{displayName}</ActionList.Description>
                        </ActionList.Item>
                      )
                    })}
                  {!ownerItems.length && !displayLoadingError && (
                    <ActionList.Item key="fetching-owners" disabled>
                      Fetching owners…
                    </ActionList.Item>
                  )}
                  {displayLoadingError && (
                    <ActionList.Item key="error-fetching-owners" disabled className={styles.errorListItem}>
                      An error occurred while loading owners. Please reopen the dialog to try again.
                    </ActionList.Item>
                  )}
                </ActionList>
              </ActionList.Group>
            </ActionList>
          </ActionMenu.Overlay>
        </ActionMenu>

        <FormControl.Caption id="owner-caption">{getCaption()}</FormControl.Caption>
      </FormControl>
    </div>
  )
}

import type {IndexCopilotSpace} from '@github-ui/copilot-chat/utils/copilot-chat-types'
import {sendEvent} from '@github-ui/hydro-analytics'
import {ActionList, SelectPanel, type SelectPanelItemInput as ItemInput, Button} from '@primer/react'
import {useState, useRef, useEffect, useCallback, useMemo} from 'react'

import {useCustomCopilotsEnabled} from '../hooks'
import {verifiedFetchJSON} from '@github-ui/verified-fetch'

import {COPILOT_PATH} from '@github-ui/copilot-chat/utils/copilot-chat-helpers'

import {useChatManager} from '@github-ui/copilot-chat/CopilotChatManagerContext'
import {SpaceIcon, StarFillIcon, ChevronRightIcon} from '@primer/octicons-react'
import {copilotLocalStorage} from '@github-ui/copilot-chat/utils/copilot-local-storage'

const SEARCH_DEBOUNCE_MS = 350
const MAX_SPACE_NAME_LENGTH = 40

const StarredIcon = () => (
  <span role="img" aria-label="Starred" style={{color: 'var(--button-star-iconColor)'}}>
    <StarFillIcon />
  </span>
)

const getSpaceKey = (space: {owner: string; id: number}) => `${space.owner}-${space.id}`

export function SpacesSelectNew({
  openCreateSpaceDialog,
  setPendingSpace,
  clearSpaceSelection,
  activeCopilotSpaceId,
  readOnly,
}: {
  openCreateSpaceDialog: (show: boolean) => void
  setPendingSpace: (space: {id: number; owner: string}) => void
  clearSpaceSelection: () => void
  activeCopilotSpaceId: {id: number; owner: string} | null
  readOnly: boolean
}) {
  const manager = useChatManager()
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false)
  const debounceTimeoutRef = useRef<NodeJS.Timeout | null>(null)

  const [filter, setFilter] = useState('')
  const [debouncedFilter, setDebouncedFilter] = useState('')
  const [filteredSpaces, setFilteredSpaces] = useState<IndexCopilotSpace[]>([])
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false)
  const [showCreateSpace, setShowCreateSpace] = useState(false)

  useEffect(() => {
    // We only want to show the "Create a space" button in /copilot
    setShowCreateSpace(window.location.pathname.startsWith(COPILOT_PATH))
  }, [])

  const handleMenuOpen = (value: boolean) => {
    if (value) {
      sendEvent('dotcom_chat.activate', {target: 'ATTACHMENT_MENU_COPILOT_SPACES', mode: 'immersive'})
    }
    setOpen(value)
  }

  const handleCreateSpace = () => {
    setOpen(false)
    openCreateSpaceDialog(true)
    sendEvent('dotcom_chat.activate', {
      target: 'ATTACHMENT_MENU_COPILOT_SPACES_CREATE_SPACE',
      mode: 'immersive',
    })
  }

  const handleSpaceSelect = useCallback(
    (space: IndexCopilotSpace) => {
      const isCurrentlySelected =
        activeCopilotSpaceId && space.id === activeCopilotSpaceId.id && space.owner === activeCopilotSpaceId.owner

      if (isCurrentlySelected) {
        clearSpaceSelection()
      } else {
        manager.addReference({...space, type: 'copilot-space'}, 'spaces')
        setPendingSpace({id: space.id, owner: space.owner})
        sendEvent('dotcom_chat.activate', {
          target: 'ATTACHMENT_MENU_COPILOT_SPACES_SELECT_SPACE',
          space: `${space.owner}/${space.id}`,
        })
      }
      setOpen(false)
    },
    [activeCopilotSpaceId, clearSpaceSelection, manager, setPendingSpace],
  )

  const fetchResults = useCallback(async (searchQuery: string) => {
    try {
      const params = new URLSearchParams({
        q: searchQuery,
      })

      const res = await verifiedFetchJSON(`/github-copilot/chat/custom_copilots/search?${params.toString()}`)

      if (!res.ok) throw new Error('Failed to fetch spaces')
      const data = (await res.json()) as IndexCopilotSpace[]
      return data
    } catch {
      return []
    }
  }, [])

  useEffect(() => {
    let cancelled = false
    const fetchAndSetSpaces = async () => {
      if (!open) return
      setLoading(true)

      const spaces = await fetchResults(debouncedFilter)
      if (cancelled) return

      setFilteredSpaces(spaces)
      setLoading(false)
    }
    fetchAndSetSpaces()
    return () => {
      cancelled = true
    }
  }, [fetchResults, debouncedFilter, open])

  useEffect(() => {
    return () => {
      if (debounceTimeoutRef.current) {
        clearTimeout(debounceTimeoutRef.current)
      }
    }
  }, [])

  const onSelectedChange = (selectedItem: ItemInput | undefined) => {
    const selectedSpace = filteredSpaces.find(space => getSpaceKey(space) === selectedItem?.id)
    if (selectedSpace) {
      handleSpaceSelect(selectedSpace)
      copilotLocalStorage.setLastSelectedSpace(selectedSpace)
    }
  }

  const copilotSpacesEnabled = useCustomCopilotsEnabled()

  const items: ItemInput[] = useMemo(
    () =>
      filteredSpaces.map(space => ({
        id: getSpaceKey(space),
        key: getSpaceKey(space),
        text:
          space.name.length > MAX_SPACE_NAME_LENGTH ? `${space.name.slice(0, MAX_SPACE_NAME_LENGTH)}...` : space.name,
        description: space.owner,
        descriptionVariant: 'inline',
        value: getSpaceKey(space),
        trailingVisual: space.starred ? StarredIcon : undefined,
      })),
    [filteredSpaces],
  )

  // If readOnly (if a thread exists) hide the menu. We can't yet assign
  // a space to an existing thread.
  if (!copilotSpacesEnabled || readOnly) {
    return null
  }

  const selected = activeCopilotSpaceId ? items.find(item => item.id === getSpaceKey(activeCopilotSpaceId)) : undefined

  const singleSelectProps = {
    selected,
    onSelectedChange,
  }

  return (
    <>
      <SelectPanel
        align="end"
        title="Select a space"
        subtitle="Recent spaces"
        renderAnchor={props => (
          <ActionList.Item as="button" {...(props as React.ComponentProps<typeof ActionList.Item>)}>
            <ActionList.LeadingVisual>
              <SpaceIcon />
            </ActionList.LeadingVisual>
            Spaces&hellip;
            <ActionList.TrailingVisual>
              <ChevronRightIcon />
            </ActionList.TrailingVisual>
          </ActionList.Item>
        )}
        open={open}
        onOpenChange={handleMenuOpen}
        loading={loading}
        items={items}
        filterValue={filter}
        onFilterChange={(value: string) => {
          setFilter(value)
          if (debounceTimeoutRef.current) clearTimeout(debounceTimeoutRef.current)
          debounceTimeoutRef.current = setTimeout(() => {
            setDebouncedFilter(value)
          }, SEARCH_DEBOUNCE_MS)
        }}
        {...singleSelectProps}
        overlayProps={{
          height: 'auto',
          width: 'large',
          anchorSide: 'outside-right',
        }}
        message={
          filteredSpaces.length === 0 && !loading
            ? {
                title: 'No spaces found',
                body: 'You can create a new space to get started.',
                variant: 'empty',
                action: (
                  <Button variant="default" size="small" onClick={handleCreateSpace}>
                    Create a new space
                  </Button>
                ),
              }
            : undefined
        }
        secondaryAction={
          showCreateSpace && filteredSpaces.length > 0 ? (
            <SelectPanel.SecondaryActionButton onClick={handleCreateSpace}>
              Create a space
            </SelectPanel.SecondaryActionButton>
          ) : undefined
        }
      />
    </>
  )
}

var UNKNOWN_FUNCTION = '<unknown>';
/**
 * This parses the different stack traces and puts them into one format
 * This borrows heavily from TraceKit (https://github.com/csnover/TraceKit)
 */

function parse(stackString) {
  var lines = stackString.split('\n');
  return lines.reduce(function (stack, line) {
    var parseResult = parseChrome(line) || parseWinjs(line) || parseGecko(line) || parseNode(line) || parseJSC(line);

    if (parseResult) {
      stack.push(parseResult);
    }

    return stack;
  }, []);
}
var chromeRe = /^\s*at (.*?) ?\(((?:file|https?|blob|chrome-extension|native|eval|webpack|rsc|<anonymous>|\/|[a-z]:\\|\\\\).*?)(?::(\d+))?(?::(\d+))?\)?\s*$/i;
var chromeEvalRe = /\((\S*)(?::(\d+))(?::(\d+))\)/;

function parseChrome(line) {
  var parts = chromeRe.exec(line);

  if (!parts) {
    return null;
  }

  var isNative = parts[2] && parts[2].indexOf('native') === 0; // start of line

  var isEval = parts[2] && parts[2].indexOf('eval') === 0; // start of line

  var submatch = chromeEvalRe.exec(parts[2]);

  if (isEval && submatch != null) {
    // throw out eval line/column and use top-most line/column number
    parts[2] = submatch[1]; // url

    parts[3] = submatch[2]; // line

    parts[4] = submatch[3]; // column
  }

  return {
    file: !isNative ? parts[2] : null,
    methodName: parts[1] || UNKNOWN_FUNCTION,
    arguments: isNative ? [parts[2]] : [],
    lineNumber: parts[3] ? +parts[3] : null,
    column: parts[4] ? +parts[4] : null
  };
}

var winjsRe = /^\s*at (?:((?:\[object object\])?.+) )?\(?((?:file|ms-appx|https?|webpack|rsc|blob):.*?):(\d+)(?::(\d+))?\)?\s*$/i;

function parseWinjs(line) {
  var parts = winjsRe.exec(line);

  if (!parts) {
    return null;
  }

  return {
    file: parts[2],
    methodName: parts[1] || UNKNOWN_FUNCTION,
    arguments: [],
    lineNumber: +parts[3],
    column: parts[4] ? +parts[4] : null
  };
}

var geckoRe = /^\s*(.*?)(?:\((.*?)\))?(?:^|@)((?:file|https?|blob|chrome|webpack|rsc|resource|\[native).*?|[^@]*bundle)(?::(\d+))?(?::(\d+))?\s*$/i;
var geckoEvalRe = /(\S+) line (\d+)(?: > eval line \d+)* > eval/i;

function parseGecko(line) {
  var parts = geckoRe.exec(line);

  if (!parts) {
    return null;
  }

  var isEval = parts[3] && parts[3].indexOf(' > eval') > -1;
  var submatch = geckoEvalRe.exec(parts[3]);

  if (isEval && submatch != null) {
    // throw out eval line/column and use top-most line number
    parts[3] = submatch[1];
    parts[4] = submatch[2];
    parts[5] = null; // no column when eval
  }

  return {
    file: parts[3],
    methodName: parts[1] || UNKNOWN_FUNCTION,
    arguments: parts[2] ? parts[2].split(',') : [],
    lineNumber: parts[4] ? +parts[4] : null,
    column: parts[5] ? +parts[5] : null
  };
}

var javaScriptCoreRe = /^\s*(?:([^@]*)(?:\((.*?)\))?@)?(\S.*?):(\d+)(?::(\d+))?\s*$/i;

function parseJSC(line) {
  var parts = javaScriptCoreRe.exec(line);

  if (!parts) {
    return null;
  }

  return {
    file: parts[3],
    methodName: parts[1] || UNKNOWN_FUNCTION,
    arguments: [],
    lineNumber: +parts[4],
    column: parts[5] ? +parts[5] : null
  };
}

var nodeRe = /^\s*at (?:((?:\[object object\])?[^\\/]+(?: \[as \S+\])?) )?\(?(.*?):(\d+)(?::(\d+))?\)?\s*$/i;

function parseNode(line) {
  var parts = nodeRe.exec(line);

  if (!parts) {
    return null;
  }

  return {
    file: parts[2],
    methodName: parts[1] || UNKNOWN_FUNCTION,
    arguments: [],
    lineNumber: +parts[3],
    column: parts[4] ? +parts[4] : null
  };
}

export { parse };

import type {DiffAnchor} from '@github-ui/diffs/types'
import {useAnalytics} from '@github-ui/use-analytics'
import {type FocusEvent, useMemo, useRef} from 'react'

import type {StartThreadCommentProps} from './StartThreadComment'
import {StartThreadComment} from './StartThreadComment'
import {useGetSelectedLineRange, useTreeEntries} from '../../stores/selectors'
import type {DiffLine} from '../../types'
import {useSuggestedChanges} from '../../hooks/use-suggested-changes'
import {useStartConversationInfo} from '../../hooks/use-start-conversation-info'
import {useFeatureFlag} from '@github-ui/react-core/use-feature-flag'
import {useCreateReviewCommentMutation} from '@github-ui/pull-requests/use-create-review-comment-mutation'
import {ReviewCommentFactory} from '../../helpers/review-comment-factory'
import {useComparisonInfo, useSelectedRange} from '@github-ui/pull-requests/pull-request-store-selectors'
import {useCommentDialogTitle} from '../../hooks/use-comment-dialog-title'

export interface StartConversationProps extends Pick<
  StartThreadCommentProps,
  'batchingEnabled' | 'fileLevelComment' | 'filePath' | 'focusOnMount' | 'viewerData' | 'threadsConnectionId'
> {
  diffAnchor: DiffAnchor
  diffLine: DiffLine
  isInDialogMode: boolean
  isLeftSide: boolean
  isOpen: boolean
  isRowSelected: boolean
  onCloseCommentDialog: (shouldFocusCell?: boolean) => void
  onCommentCreated?: () => void
  shouldStartNewConversationWithSuggestedChange?: boolean
}

/**
 * Renders a modal AnchoredOverlay for starting a new conversation thread or an in line version
 */
export function StartConversation({
  diffAnchor,
  isInDialogMode,
  isLeftSide,
  isOpen,
  isRowSelected,
  onCloseCommentDialog,
  onCommentCreated,
  diffLine,
  shouldStartNewConversationWithSuggestedChange,
  ...rest
}: StartConversationProps) {
  const {sendAnalyticsEvent} = useAnalytics()
  const newCommentContainerRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null)
  const getSelectedLineRange = useGetSelectedLineRange()

  const startConversationInfo = useStartConversationInfo({
    line: diffLine,
    isLeftSide,
    isRowSelected,
  })

  const suggestedChangesConfig = useSuggestedChanges(diffLine, shouldStartNewConversationWithSuggestedChange)
  const {newTreeEntry, oldTreeEntry} = useTreeEntries()
  const {effectiveRange: effectiveDiffRange} = useComparisonInfo()
  const {baseOid: startOid, headOid: endOid} = useSelectedRange()
  const commentOutsideOfTheDiffEnabled = useFeatureFlag('cotd_ui')
  const addCommentDialogTitle = useCommentDialogTitle(startConversationInfo)

  const {mutate: createReviewCommentMutation} = useCreateReviewCommentMutation()
  const handleAddComment = ({
    onCompleted,
    onError,
    submitBatch,
    text,
  }: {
    onCompleted?: (threadId: string, commentDatabaseId: number | undefined | null) => void
    onError: (error: Error) => void
    submitBatch?: boolean | undefined
    text: string
  }) => {
    if (!diffLine) return

    if (commentOutsideOfTheDiffEnabled) {
      const reviewCommentFactory = new ReviewCommentFactory({
        filePath: rest.filePath,
        newTreeEntry,
        oldTreeEntry,
        effectiveDiffRange,
        submitBatch,
        text,
      })

      const requestData = startConversationInfo.isMultiline
        ? reviewCommentFactory.multilineRequestData({
            startLineNumber: startConversationInfo.startLineNumber as number,
            startSide: startConversationInfo.startSide as 'left' | 'right',
            endLineNumber: startConversationInfo.lineNumber,
            endSide: startConversationInfo.side,
          })
        : reviewCommentFactory.lineRequestData({
            lineNumber: startConversationInfo.lineNumber,
            side: startConversationInfo.side,
          })

      createReviewCommentMutation(requestData, {
        onError,
        onSuccess: data => {
          onCompleted?.(data.thread.id, data.comment?.databaseId)
          onCommentCreated?.()
        },
      })
    } else {
      createReviewCommentMutation(
        {
          text,
          line: startConversationInfo.lineNumber,
          path: rest.filePath,
          side: startConversationInfo.side,
          startSide: startConversationInfo.startSide,
          submitBatch,
          startLine: startConversationInfo.startLineNumber,
          comparisonEndOid: endOid,
          comparisonStartOid: startOid,
        },
        {
          onError,
          onSuccess: data => {
            onCompleted?.(data.thread.id, data.comment?.databaseId)
            onCommentCreated?.()
          },
        },
      )
    }
  }

  const config = useMemo(() => {
    if (isOpen && suggestedChangesConfig?.configureSuggestedChangesFromLineRange) {
      const selectedLineRange = getSelectedLineRange()
      return suggestedChangesConfig.configureSuggestedChangesFromLineRange(
        selectedLineRange,
        suggestedChangesConfig?.shouldStartNewConversationWithSuggestedChange,
      )
    }
    return undefined
  }, [isOpen, suggestedChangesConfig, getSelectedLineRange])

  if (!isOpen) return null

  const handleNewCommentContainerFocus = (e: FocusEvent) => {
    // Only handle focus logic if a user is on the new comment container's first <div> element.
    if (e.target !== newCommentContainerRef.current) return

    // Focus on the textarea element inside of the new comment container
    newCommentContainerRef.current.querySelector('textarea')?.focus()
  }

  return (
    <div
      className="rounded-2 bgColor-default"
      data-marker-navigation-new-thread
      data-marker-id="new-comment" // Add data-marker-id attribute for keyboard navigation
      ref={newCommentContainerRef}
      // eslint-disable-next-line jsx-a11y/no-noninteractive-tabindex
      tabIndex={isInDialogMode ? 0 : -1}
      onFocus={handleNewCommentContainerFocus}
    >
      <StartThreadComment
        onClose={(shouldFocusCell?: boolean) => {
          onCloseCommentDialog(shouldFocusCell)
          sendAnalyticsEvent('comments.cancel_thread_reply', 'CANCEL_REVIEW_THREAD_BUTTON')
        }}
        showOnCloseIcon={false}
        suggestedChangesConfig={config}
        onAddComment={handleAddComment}
        addCommentDialogTitle={addCommentDialogTitle}
        lineNumber={startConversationInfo.lineNumber}
        startLineNumber={startConversationInfo.startLineNumber}
        isLeftSide={startConversationInfo.side === 'left'}
        {...rest}
      />
    </div>
  )
}

import type {StarterGroup} from '@github-ui/copilot-chat/utils/copilot-chat-types'

import starters from '../../data/starters.json'
import taskStarters from '../../data/task-starters.json'
import updatedStarters from '../../data/updated-starters.json'

export function getStarterGroups(useUpdatedStarters = false, useTaskStarters = false): StarterGroup[] {
  return Object.values(
    useUpdatedStarters ? (useTaskStarters ? taskStarters : updatedStarters) : starters,
  ) as StarterGroup[]
}

import type {StarterEntry, StarterGroup} from '@github-ui/copilot-chat/utils/copilot-chat-types'
import {VerifiedHTMLDiv} from '@github-ui/safe-html/VerifiedHTML'
import {ActionList, ActionMenu} from '@primer/react'
import {useRef, useState} from 'react'

import {StarterPill} from './StarterPill'

export function StarterGroupWithMenu({
  group,
  onEntrySelect,
}: {
  group: StarterGroup
  onEntrySelect: (entry: StarterEntry) => void
}) {
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false)
  const buttonRef = useRef<HTMLButtonElement>(null)

  const handleClick = () => {
    setOpen(!open)
  }

  // If there's only one entry, render a simple StarterPill without dropdown
  if (group.entries.length === 1) {
    const singleEntry = group.entries[0]!
    return (
      <li>
        <StarterPill
          ref={buttonRef}
          testId={group.id}
          name={group.name}
          icon={group.icon}
          color={group.color}
          onClick={() => onEntrySelect(singleEntry)}
          dropdown={false}
        />
      </li>
    )
  }

  return (
    <li>
      <ActionMenu open={open} onOpenChange={setOpen}>
        <ActionMenu.Anchor>
          <StarterPill
            ref={buttonRef}
            testId={group.id}
            name={group.name}
            icon={group.icon}
            color={group.color}
            onClick={handleClick}
            dropdown
            open={open}
          />
        </ActionMenu.Anchor>
        <ActionMenu.Overlay style={{width: '260px'}}>
          <ActionList>
            {group.entries.map(entry => (
              <ActionList.Item key={entry.id} onSelect={() => onEntrySelect(entry)}>
                <VerifiedHTMLDiv html={entry.titleHtml} />
              </ActionList.Item>
            ))}
          </ActionList>
        </ActionMenu.Overlay>
      </ActionMenu>
    </li>
  )
}

import {Icon} from '@github-ui/pacer/Icon'
import {TriangleDownIcon} from '@primer/octicons-react'
import React from 'react'

import styles from './StarterPill.module.css'

type StarterPillProps = {
  testId: string
  name: string
  icon: string
  color: string
  onClick: () => void
  dropdown?: boolean
  open?: boolean
  ref?: React.Ref<HTMLButtonElement>
}

export const StarterPill = ({ref, testId, name, icon, color, onClick, dropdown, open}: StarterPillProps) => {
  return (
    <button
      ref={ref}
      className={styles.container}
      onClick={onClick}
      aria-expanded={open}
      data-testid={`starter-${testId}`}
    >
      {Icon && (
        <div className={styles.icon}>
          <Icon icon={icon} color={color} />
        </div>
      )}
      <div className={styles.title}>
        {name}
        {dropdown && (
          <div className={styles.dropdown}>
            <TriangleDownIcon />
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </button>
  )
}

StarterPill.displayName = 'StarterPill'

import {GitHubAvatar} from '@github-ui/github-avatar'
import {XIcon} from '@primer/octicons-react'
import {Heading, IconButton} from '@primer/react'

import {anchorComment} from '../../helpers/anchor-helpers'
import type {AddCommentEditorProps, CommentLineChangeConfig} from './AddCommentEditor'
import {AddCommentEditor} from './AddCommentEditor'
import {ChangeCommentLineNumberButton} from './ChangeCommentLineNumberButton'
import {useState, useId} from 'react'
import {useFeatureFlag} from '@github-ui/react-core/use-feature-flag'
import {useDialogModeActions} from '../../stores/selectors'

const COMMENT_POSITIONING_ID_PREFIX = 'diff-comment-positioning-'

export interface StartThreadCommentProps extends Pick<
  AddCommentEditorProps,
  | 'batchingEnabled'
  | 'fileLevelComment'
  | 'filePath'
  | 'focusOnMount'
  | 'lineNumber'
  | 'startLineNumber'
  | 'suggestedChangesConfig'
> {
  addCommentDialogTitle?: string
  isLeftSide: boolean | undefined
  onAddComment: (args: {
    onCompleted?: (threadId: string, commentDatabaseId: number | undefined | null) => void
    onError: (error: Error) => void
    submitBatch?: boolean | undefined
    text: string
  }) => void
  onClose?: (shouldFocusCell?: boolean) => void
  viewerData: {
    avatarUrl: string
    login: string
  }
  showOnCloseIcon?: boolean
  threadsConnectionId?: string
  setStartLineNumber?: (lineNumber: number | undefined) => void
}

/**
 * The StartThreadComment component is used to render the comment editor when starting a new inline thread.
 */
export function StartThreadComment({
  addCommentDialogTitle,
  isLeftSide,
  fileLevelComment,
  filePath,
  focusOnMount = true,
  lineNumber,
  onAddComment,
  onClose,
  viewerData,
  threadsConnectionId,
  showOnCloseIcon = true,
  ...rest
}: StartThreadCommentProps) {
  const [commentLineChangeConfig, setCommentLineChangeConfig] = useState<CommentLineChangeConfig | undefined>(undefined)
  const moveNewCommentFlagEnabled = useFeatureFlag('move_new_comments_ui')
  // This feature is not current enabled
  // Issue: http://github.com/github/pull-requests/issues/19502
  const moveNewCommentEnabled = moveNewCommentFlagEnabled && false
  const {disableDialogMode} = useDialogModeActions()
  const handleAddComment = ({
    commentText,
    onCompleted,
    onError,
    submitBatch,
  }: {
    commentText: string
    onCompleted?: (threadId: string, commentDatabaseId?: number | undefined | null) => void
    onError: (error: Error) => void
    submitBatch?: boolean
  }) => {
    const handleCompleted = (threadId: string, commentDatabaseId: number | undefined | null) => {
      if (commentDatabaseId) anchorComment(commentDatabaseId.toString())
      onCompleted?.(threadId, commentDatabaseId)
    }

    onAddComment({
      text: commentText,
      onError,
      onCompleted: handleCompleted,
      submitBatch,
    })
  }

  const onCommentClose = () => {
    onClose?.()
    disableDialogMode()
  }
  const commentPositioningId = `${COMMENT_POSITIONING_ID_PREFIX}${useId()}`

  return (
    <div className="px-2 pb-2 pt-1">
      <div className="d-flex flex-justify-between flex-items-center">
        <Heading as="h4" className="f5 ml-1 pt-1 pb-2 d-flex">
          <GitHubAvatar alt={viewerData.login} size={24} src={viewerData.avatarUrl || ''} className="mr-2" />

          {!moveNewCommentEnabled &&
            (fileLevelComment ? (
              <>Add comment on file</>
            ) : (
              <span>{addCommentDialogTitle ? `Add a comment on ${addCommentDialogTitle}` : 'Add a comment'}</span>
            ))}
          {moveNewCommentEnabled && (
            <div className="d-flex flex-items-center" id={commentPositioningId}>
              <div className="pt-1">Add {fileLevelComment ? '' : 'a'} comment on </div>
              <ChangeCommentLineNumberButton
                ariaLabelId={commentPositioningId}
                currentLineNumberText={fileLevelComment ? 'file' : (addCommentDialogTitle ?? '')}
                baseEndLineNumber={lineNumber}
                baseStartLineNumber={rest.startLineNumber}
                diffSide={isLeftSide ? 'LEFT' : 'RIGHT'}
                setCommentLineChangeConfig={setCommentLineChangeConfig}
              />
            </div>
          )}
        </Heading>
        {onClose && showOnCloseIcon && (
          <IconButton variant="invisible" icon={XIcon} onClick={onCommentClose} aria-label="Cancel" />
        )}
      </div>
      <AddCommentEditor
        focusOnMount={focusOnMount}
        condensed={false}
        fileLevelComment={fileLevelComment ?? false}
        filePath={filePath}
        lineNumber={lineNumber}
        onAddComment={handleAddComment}
        onCancelComment={onCommentClose}
        commentLineChangeConfig={commentLineChangeConfig}
        side={isLeftSide ? 'LEFT' : 'RIGHT'}
        {...rest}
      />
    </div>
  )
}

import {getLineNumberWidth} from '@github-ui/diffs/diff-line-helpers'
import {UnifiedDiffLines} from '@github-ui/diffs/DiffParts'
import {clsx} from 'clsx'

import type {StaticDiffLine} from '../../conversation-types'
import styles from './StaticUnifiedDiffPreview.module.css'

type StaticUnifiedDiffPreviewProps = {
  diffLines?: StaticDiffLine[]
  tabSize: number
  className?: string
  diffTableClassName?: string
}

/**
 * Renders a static representation of a unified diff
 * Specifically for use in outdated threads, which return a slightly different set of difflines
 */
export function StaticUnifiedDiffPreview({
  diffLines,
  tabSize,
  className,
  diffTableClassName,
}: StaticUnifiedDiffPreviewProps) {
  if (!diffLines || diffLines.length < 1) {
    return null
  }

  const lineWidth = getLineNumberWidth(diffLines)

  return (
    <div className={clsx(styles.StaticDiffPreview, className)}>
      <UnifiedDiffLines lineWidth={lineWidth} lines={diffLines} tabSize={tabSize} className={diffTableClassName} />
    </div>
  )
}

import {useLayoutEffect, useState, type RefObject} from 'react'
import {createPortal} from 'react-dom'

import {validateSuggestedChangeCanBeApplied} from '../../helpers/suggested-changes'
import {extractSuggestionFromDOM} from '../../helpers/extract-suggestion-content'
import type {
  ApplySuggestedChangesValidationData,
  CommentWithoutFragment,
  SuggestedChange,
  SuggestedChangesConfiguration,
} from '../../conversation-types'
import {CodeSuggestionActions} from './CodeSuggestionActions'
import {CodeSuggestionUnavailable} from './CodeSuggestionActions/CodeSuggestionUnavailable'
import {useHasCommitRange, usePullRequest} from '@github-ui/pull-requests/pull-request-store-selectors'

function useSuggestedChangesRefs(
  commentBodyRef: React.RefObject<HTMLDivElement | null>,
  comment: CommentWithoutFragment,
  filePath: string,
  threadId: number | null | undefined,
  applySuggestedChangesValidationData: ApplySuggestedChangesValidationData,
) {
  const [suggestedChangeRefs, setSuggestedChangeRefs] = useState<SuggestedChangeWithRef[] | undefined>()

  // This useLayoutEffect will search the comment body for div.js-apply-changes elements.
  // The apply changes nodes will be stored in an Array and used as refs with React's createPortal.
  // The rest of the suggested change info will be stored in state to be used for batching or applying suggestions.
  // We choose to use useLayoutEffect here because we want to run this code before the comment body is rendered so we don't
  // flash content.
  useLayoutEffect(() => {
    if (!commentBodyRef.current) return
    if (comment.subjectType?.toUpperCase() !== 'LINE') return

    const renderedApplyChangeElements = [...commentBodyRef.current.getElementsByClassName('js-apply-changes')]
    if (renderedApplyChangeElements.length > 0) {
      const suggestedChanges: SuggestedChangeWithRef[] = []

      for (let i = 0; i < renderedApplyChangeElements.length; i++) {
        const ref = renderedApplyChangeElements[i]
        if (!ref) continue
        const changesBlobEl = ref.closest('div.js-suggested-changes-blob')
        if (!changesBlobEl) continue
        if (!applySuggestedChangesValidationData.lineRange) continue

        const suggestionLines = extractSuggestionFromDOM(changesBlobEl)

        suggestedChanges.push({
          authorLogin: comment.author?.login ?? 'ghost',
          commentId: comment.id,
          commentDatabaseId: comment.databaseId,
          path: filePath,
          ref,
          suggestion: suggestionLines,
          lineRange: applySuggestedChangesValidationData.lineRange,
          threadId,
          suggestionIndex: i,
        })
      }
      // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/set-state-in-effect
      setSuggestedChangeRefs(suggestedChanges)
    }
  }, [
    applySuggestedChangesValidationData.lineRange,
    comment.author?.login,
    comment.bodyHTML,
    comment.databaseId,
    comment.id,
    comment.subjectType,
    commentBodyRef,
    filePath,
    threadId,
  ])

  return suggestedChangeRefs
}

type SuggestedChangeWithRef = SuggestedChange & {ref: Element}

export interface SuggestedChangeViewProps {
  applySuggestedChangesValidationData: ApplySuggestedChangesValidationData
  comment: CommentWithoutFragment
  commentBodyRef: RefObject<HTMLDivElement | null>
  filePath: string
  isOutdated?: boolean
  isThreadResolved: boolean
  suggestedChangesConfig?: SuggestedChangesConfiguration
  databaseId: number | null | undefined
  viewerCanApplySuggestion?: boolean
}

export function SuggestedChangeView({
  applySuggestedChangesValidationData,
  comment,
  commentBodyRef,
  filePath,
  isOutdated,
  isThreadResolved,
  suggestedChangesConfig,
  databaseId,
  viewerCanApplySuggestion,
}: SuggestedChangeViewProps): React.ReactElement {
  const {state: pullRequestState} = usePullRequest()
  const hasCommitRange = useHasCommitRange()

  const suggestedChangeRefs = useSuggestedChangesRefs(
    commentBodyRef,
    comment,
    filePath,
    databaseId,
    applySuggestedChangesValidationData,
  )

  return (
    <>
      {/* Insert suggested change button group inside of each div.js-apply-changes element. */}
      {suggestedChangeRefs?.length
        ? suggestedChangeRefs.map(suggestedChange => {
            const {isValid, reason} = validateSuggestedChangeCanBeApplied({
              suggestedChange,
              hasCommitRange,
              isOutdated,
              isPending: comment.state.toUpperCase() === 'PENDING',
              isResolved: isThreadResolved,
              pullRequestIsClosed:
                pullRequestState === undefined || pullRequestState === 'CLOSED' || pullRequestState === 'MERGED',
              pullRequestIsInMergeQueue: pullRequestState === 'QUEUED',
              userCanApplySuggestion: !!viewerCanApplySuggestion,
              applySuggestedChangesValidationData,
              suggestedChangesConfig,
            })
            if (!isValid && reason) {
              return createPortal(<CodeSuggestionUnavailable reason={reason} />, suggestedChange.ref)
            }
            return createPortal(<CodeSuggestionActions suggestedChange={suggestedChange} />, suggestedChange.ref)
          })
        : null}
    </>
  )
}

import {NewTaskChatInput} from '@github-ui/agent-sessions/components/new-task-chat-input/NewTaskChatInput'
